### [1] [SLVR: Structured Latent Visual Reasoning via Human-like Reasoning Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10563)
*Albert Gao,Bing Xue,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SLVR 训练框架，将多模态推理组织为规划、定位、证据选择、整合四类"类型化隐阶段"，用结构化监督替代显式文本 CoT，在推理时不产生解码开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 回答视觉推理问题时过度依赖语言先验而非视觉证据；文本 CoT 可部分缓解但自回归生成中间步骤增加推理成本；已有隐式推理方法对中间状态编码缺乏可控性，难以分别施加规划、定位与证据选择的监督。

Method: SLVR 先通过掩蔽可泄露答案的文本、以视觉上合理的干扰项做对比，迫使模型依赖图像；再将推理组织为规划、定位、证据选择、整合四个隐阶段，分别以规划文本、边界框、视觉证据、最终理由作为对应阶段监督信号。

Result: 基于 Qwen2.5-VL-7B，在多模态推理基准上一致提升：MMVP +9.4、BLINK Relation +14.2（绝对值），V*、MathVista、ChartQA 亦有改进，且推理时无文本 CoT 解码开销。

Conclusion: 结构化隐式监督可在不引入文本 CoT 解码开销的前提下提升细粒度视觉推理能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often answer visual reasoning questions by relying on linguistic priors rather than task-relevant visual evidence. Textual chain-of-thought reasoning can partially mitigate this issue by encouraging models to decompose visual questions into intermediate evidence-seeking steps, but generating these steps autoregressively increases inference cost. Latent reasoning avoids explicit rationale generation, but existing approaches provide limited control over what intermediate states encode, making it difficult to impose separate supervision for planning, grounding, and evidence selection. We propose Structured Latent Visual Reasoning (SLVR), a training framework that bridges explicit chain-of-thought and latent reasoning by organizing multimodal reasoning into typed latent stages for planning, grounding, evidence selection, and reasoning integration. SLVR first trains the model to rely on the image by masking answer-revealing text and contrasting the correct answer with visually plausible distractors. It then organizes reasoning into latent stages for planning, grounding, evidence selection, and integration, supervising each stage with the corresponding signal: plans, boxes, visual evidence, and final rationales. This gives latent reasoning an explicit functional structure while avoiding generated textual chains at inference time. Built on Qwen2.5-VL-7B, SLVR improves consistently across multimodal reasoning benchmarks, with absolute gains of +9.4 on MMVP and +14.2 on BLINK Relation, as well as improvements on V*, MathVista, and ChartQA. These results suggest that structured latent supervision can improve fine-grained visual reasoning without the decoding overhead of textual CoT. Project page is available \href{ this https URL }{here}.

</details>


### [2] [A Camera-Native Stereo VR180 Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10607)
*Linxuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个自采集立体VR180数据集（1211样本，含原生鱼眼与半等距柱状投影编码及工厂标定），并通过x265重编码实验证明原生鱼眼编码在同视口质量下比半等距柱状编码需更高码率（中位+38%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开VR180资源多来自YouTube等平台，已经过未知流程的拼接、投影与压缩，且缺失镜头标定，无法支撑可复现的研究与编解码分析。

Method: 使用两台Blackmagic URSA Cine Immersive相机采集立体VR180内容，包含636段立体视频（2220.8秒，多为90fps）与575张立体静图；每样本以相机原生Blackmagic RAW、分离双眼原生鱼眼HEVC（8160x7200/眼）和半等距柱状HEVC（7200x7200/眼）三种形式发布，并提供工厂镜头标定、便携式鱼眼/半等距柱状转换工具及AI生成的场景与视觉挑战标注。在24个片段、双眼、4个码率点、9个视方向上用x265重编码进行码率-视口质量对比实验。

Result: 在所有24个片段、视场任意位置，原生鱼眼编码在同视口质量下所需码率均高于半等距柱状编码，中位高出38%。

Conclusion: 该数据集为VR180研究提供了带标定的高质量、多格式一手素材与工具链，并给出鱼眼与半等距柱状投影在压缩效率上的实测量化结论。

Abstract: Immersive VR180 video is increasingly produced with professional stereo fisheye cameras, yet public VR180 research resources are mostly collected from online platforms such as YouTube: already stitched, projected and compressed by unknown pipelines, and without lens calibration. We present a firsthand-captured stereo VR180 dataset recorded with two Blackmagic URSA Cine Immersive cameras. It contains 1,211 samples -- 636 stereo video clips (2,220.8 s, mostly 90 fps) and 575 stereo stills -- each released as camera-native Blackmagic RAW, separate-eye native fisheye HEVC (8160x7200 per eye) and half-equirectangular HEVC (7200x7200 per eye), together with the factory lens calibration, portable fisheye/half-equirectangular conversion tools and AI-generated scene and visual-challenge annotations. Re-encoding the released fisheye and half-equirectangular renders with x265 over 24 clips, both eyes, four rate points and nine viewing directions, native-fisheye coding needed more bitrate than half-equirectangular coding at equal viewport quality for all 24 clips (median +38%), in every part of the field of view. Data: this https URL ; code: this https URL

</details>


### [3] [Seeing Through the Glare: A Multi-Source Benchmark and Ocular-Adaptive Pixel MeanFlow for Eyeglass Reflection Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10703)
*Tao Liu,Youwei Pang,Kailai Zhou,Jiaming Zuo,Hanqi Liu,Wei Ji,Peng-Tao Jiang,Xiaofeng Liu,Weisi Lin,Xiaoqi Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出眼镜反光去除基准 OcuBench 与高效方法 OcuFlow，通过眼部自适应像素 MeanFlow 与原生分辨率频谱保持合成实现细节保留的快速修复，在多种反光条件下取得一致优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼镜反光去除对手机影像、视频会议等以人脸为中心的视觉应用很重要，但反光从轻微光度污染到严重遮挡眼部不等，需要选择性校正且保持身份与自然外观；现有数据集覆盖反光条件有限，限制了对复杂真实场景的泛化与系统评估。

Method: 构建多源基准 OcuBench，含 10,280 可控合成对、732 真实输入伪配对、458 张独立真实测试图，支持配对评估与生成监督之外的评测；提出眼部自适应像素 MeanFlow（pMF）框架 OcuFlow，结合几何自适应表示与单步 pMF 聚焦反光遮挡的眼部区域重建，并用原生分辨率频谱保持合成保留可靠观测细节。

Result: 在多样反光条件下，OcuFlow 在反光去除质量、眼部保真度和效率上均取得一致优势；盲测用户研究中获得 67.32% 的选择率，为次优方法的 6.2 倍。代码与数据集将开源。

Conclusion: OcuBench 与 OcuFlow 为眼镜反光去除提供了更全面的评测与高效高保真方案，具备实际应用价值，代码与数据集将公开。

Abstract: Eyeglass reflection removal is important across smartphone imaging, video conferencing, and other face-centric visual applications. The task is challenging because reflections range from mild photometric contamination to severe ocular occlusion, requiring selective correction and plausible reconstruction without altering identity or natural appearance. Existing datasets cover limited reflection conditions, constraining generalization to complex real-world scenes and systematic evaluation. We introduce \textbf{OcuBench}, a multi-source benchmark comprising 10,280 controllable synthetic pairs, 732 real-input pseudo-pairs, and 458 independent real-world test images, supporting both paired evaluation and assessment beyond generated supervision. We further propose \textbf{OcuFlow}, an ocular-adaptive pixel MeanFlow (pMF) framework for efficient, detail-preserving restoration. It combines geometry-adaptive representation with one-step pMF to focus reconstruction on reflection-obscured ocular regions, together with native-resolution frequency-preserving synthesis to retain reliable observed details. Experiments across diverse reflection conditions demonstrate that OcuFlow achieves consistent advantages in reflection removal quality, ocular fidelity, and efficiency. In a blind user study, it receives $67.32\%$ of selections, $6.2\times$ the next-best share. Both the code and dataset will be released.

</details>


### [4] [LinSlot: Exploiting Linear Representation hypothesis for unsupervised attribute discovery from slot based object representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10722)
*Sanket Gandhi,Utkarsh Giri,Varun Subramanium,Rohan Paul,Parag Singla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于线性表示假设(LRH)的概率模型与块注意力架构，从未标注图像中联合发现物体与属性解耦表示，优化ELBO，提升DCI并支持图像编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Slot-based方法在无监督物体表示学习上成功，但block-slot注意力的方法假定物体表示到属性的均匀因子分解，可能次优，限制了表示质量。因此需要联合发现物体与属性表示。

Method: 利用线性表示假设(LRH)，假设可组合概念在slot表示中是线性可加子空间；提出连接图像、slot(物体)、block(属性)的概率模型，用块注意力连接属性与slot，并在物体与属性空间中加入LRH约束，架构等效优化该图模型的ELBO。

Result: 实验显示：(i)有效发现解耦的物体与属性表示；(ii)在slot空间中获得LRH的经验证据；(iii)由于表示解耦可解释，可进行图像编辑。多个数据集上DCI分数优于SOTA。

Conclusion: LRH可用于引导无监督解耦表示学习，联合建模物体与属性优于均匀因子分解假设，为可解释表示与图像编辑提供基础。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of learning disentangled representations of objects and their attributes from raw, unstructured image data. Slot-based methods have shown considerable success in unsupervised learning of object representations from images. Block-slot attention-based methods extend this framework to attribute representations by assuming a uniform factorization of object representations into attributes, which may be suboptimal and consequently limit the quality of the learned representations. We therefore investigate a framework for jointly discovering object and attribute representations. Our key contribution is leveraging the Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH), which postulates that composable concepts can be represented as linearly additive subspaces in slot representations. Based on this insight, we propose a probabilistic model connecting images, slots (objects), and blocks (attributes). We present an architecture that leverages block attention to connect attribute representations to slots and incorporates LRH in both object and attribute representation spaces. This architecture effectively optimizes the Evidence Lower Bound (ELBO) of the proposed graphical model. Our experiments demonstrate (i) effective discovery of disentangled object and attribute representations, (ii) empirical evidence for LRH in slot space, and (iii) the ability to perform image editing owing to the disentangled and interpretable nature of the learned representations. Our experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate improvements in DCI scores over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [5] [MESSENGER: Memory-Enhanced Sequential Scene Flow Estimation via Autoregressive Next-Frame Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10759)
*Jiuming Liu,Jianing Li,Mengmeng Liu,Hongyang He,Hesheng Wang,Per Ola Kristensson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出记忆增强的序列化场景流估计框架MESSENGER，通过显式记忆缓冲区存储历史流与隐状态并采用自回归式下一帧预测范式，在nuScenes和Argoverse 2长时程外推上取得SOTA，EPE3D分别降低71.6%和67.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景流估计方法存在两类不足：早期成对估计器仅依赖瞬时两帧运动，缺乏长时时间关联且未来外推能力差；近期多帧序列到序列方法虽引入时序建模，但随输入帧数增加计算开销显著上升，且由于运动传播低效导致长时程预测性能退化。

Method: 设计记忆缓冲区，显式存储多段历史流估计与隐状态；对每个输入帧，对时间存储的流和状态进行关联与检索，以"下一帧预测"方式初始化当前帧流；引入不确定性感知重加权模块，过滤不可靠检索结果并缓解误差累积。

Result: 在nuScenes和Argoverse 2上达到SOTA；长时程未来外推任务中EPE3D在nuScenes上降低71.6%，在Argoverse 2上降低67.7%。

Conclusion: 性能优势归因于所设计的自回归预测范式，该范式天然迫使网络基于历史观测逐步学习下一帧分布，从而在保持计算效率的同时实现长时程场景流外推。代码将开源。

Abstract: Scene flow can capture low-level 3D motion displacements in dynamic scenarios. Early pairwise estimators relying on instantaneous two-frame motion lack long-term temporal correlation and also struggle with poor extrapolation ability in future prediction. Although some recent methods attempt to explore multi-frame scene flow estimation in a sequence-to-sequence manner, they typically suffer from heavy computational overhead with increasing input frames and long-horizon prediction degradation due to ineffective motion propagation. To address these problems, we propose a novel memory-enhanced sequential scene flow pipeline, called MESSENGER. To sufficiently mine long-term temporal dependencies naturally within consecutive sequences, a memory buffer is designed by explicitly storing multiple history flow estimates and latent states. For each input frame, the temporally stored flows and states are correlated and retrieved to predict the current initialized flow in a next-frame forecasting manner. Furthermore, we develop an uncertainty-aware reweighting module to filter unreliable retrievals and mitigate accumulated errors. Extensive experiments on nuScenes and Argoverse 2 demonstrate state-of-the-art performance of our MESSENGER, reducing EPE3D by 71.6% on nuScenes and 67.7% on Argoverse 2 in long-horizon future extrapolation. This superiority can be attributed to our designed autoregressive forecasting paradigm, which naturally forces the network to progressively learn the next-frame distribution based on history observations. Code will be released at this https URL .

</details>


### [6] [DOGS: Design-Space Sampling for Prompt-Driven Logo Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10760)
*Ganyu Zou,Chen Dai,Nathan Self,Kevin Piper,Ramachandra Rao Seethiraju,Karthik Shyamsunder,Chang-Tien Lu,Naren Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将Logo的文本到图像提示词优化重构为结构化设计空间采样问题，提出DOGS方法：从真实Logo语料中挖掘类型化图结构设计语法，用GFlowNet按其奖励（可识别性、美学、语料相对原创性）成比例采样设计图，并以封闭词表加解析期过滤实现侵权规避；在两种开源渲染器上优于九个基线，Logo更可识别、更美观、更多样且侵权风险显著更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I提示词优化几乎都采用"文本改写"范式，将短brief扩展成更长的token序列。作者认为这种语言空间表述不适合Logo等结构化视觉设计任务：一句话简报未指定大部分设计决策，这些决策依赖线性序列无法编码的关系型先验，且长token序列为受保护商标的复现留下不可控通道。

Method: 从大规模真实Logo语料中挖掘带类型的图结构设计语法，边记录经验共现；用GFlowNet采样器生成设计图，采样概率正比于由可识别性、美学和语料相对原创性组成的终端奖励；每个槽位仅从封闭的设计级词表中取值，解析阶段移除所有可能引发侵权或有害的token；原创性奖励额外惩罚与已有Logo的接近程度，从构造上把侵权规避内嵌进方法。

Result: 在两种开源渲染器上、对比九个基线，DOGS生成的Logo可识别性和美学性更高、多样性显著更高，且商标侵权倾向大幅降低。

Conclusion: 将Logo提示词从语言空间改写为结构化设计空间采样是可行且更优的范式，图结构语法与GFlowNet奖励采样可在保证视觉质量与多样性的同时，从构造层面抑制商标侵权，为受约束的创意生成任务提供了新思路。

Abstract: Prompt optimization for text-to-image (T2I) generation has been pursued almost entirely as text rewriting, in which a short user brief is expanded into a longer, model-preferred token sequence. We argue that such a language-space formulation is ill-suited to structured visual design tasks such as logo creation, where a one-line brief leaves most design decisions unspecified. These decisions depend on relational priors that a linear sequence cannot encode, and they leave an uncontrolled channel through which protected marks may be reproduced. We therefore recast logo prompting as sampling within a structured design space, and instantiate this idea as DOGS (Design-space prompting with an Originality-aware GFlowNet Sampler). From a large corpus of real-world logos, we mine a typed, graph-structured design grammar whose edges record empirical co-occurrence. A GFlowNet sampler then generates design graphs with probability proportional to a terminal reward that combines recognizability, aesthetics, and corpus-relative originality. Every slot draws only from a closed design-level vocabulary, and any infringement-inducing or harmful token is removed during parsing. The originality reward further penalizes proximity to existing logos, thereby incorporating infringement avoidance into the method by construction. On two open-source renderers and against nine baselines, DOGS produces logos that are more recognizable and aesthetic, substantially more diverse, and far less prone to trademark infringement.

</details>


### [7] [VICO: Visual Environments Co-Evolving for Vision-Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10782)
*Meng Lu,Ligeng Zhu,Olivia Xiao,Yuchen Zhuang,Zihan Wang,Kuncheng Wu,Bangya Liu,Yu Wang,Charles Fleming,Wenqi Shi,Xuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VICO：面向VLM后训练的"演员—环境共演化"RLVR框架，用可验证图像侧结构编辑（场景图/图表表格/掩码）动态重渲染生成难度匹配当前能力的训练样本，替代静态语料与人工标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）已成为VLM后训练标准范式，但普遍假设训练环境静态。随着策略变强，固定任务漂移出学习前沿：部分变得平凡、部分仍不可解，学习信号崩溃。作者主张VLM后训练应让视觉环境与策略共同演化，而非只更新策略本身。

Method: 提出VICO共演化框架，联合训练Actor与EnvRewriter（环境改写器）。EnvRewriter对可验证的图像侧结构进行编辑——场景图、图表表格、以及受保护区域掩码——随后重渲染生成标签仍有效的训练样本；通过基于通过率的奖励将难度校准到Actor当前能力水平。该闭环在无需额外人工标注的情况下持续对齐任务难度与模型能力。

Result: 在涵盖数学推理与视觉接地理解的九个多模态基准上，VICO-8B在域外任务上较基座模型最高提升+5.0%；相较最强的自演化基线与文本编辑共演化基线分别高+4.3%与+8.4%；与图表专用RLVR方法性能相当，但所用标注样本少16–160倍。

Conclusion: 将监督来源从人工标注转向图像编辑共演化，VICO为视觉推理提供了超越静态语料RLVR的可扩展路径，说明环境与Actor协同演化是维持有效学习信号的关键机制。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a standard recipe for post-training vision-language models (VLMs), but it typically assumes a static training environment. As the actor improves, fixed tasks drift out of its learning frontier: many become trivial, others remain unsolvable; and the learning signal collapses. We argue that VLM post-training should evolve the visual environment alongside the actor, not just the actor itself. We propose VICO, a co-evolutionary framework in which an actor and an Environment-as-Rewriter (EnvRewriter) are trained jointly: the EnvRewriter edits verifiable image-side structures, such as scene graphs, chart tables, or protected region masks, and re-renders them to produce label-valid training samples whose difficulty is calibrated to the actor's current ability through a pass-rate-based reward. This loop continuously realigns task difficulty with actor capability without any additional human annotation. Across nine multimodal benchmarks spanning mathematical reasoning and visually grounded understanding, VICO-8B improves over its base model by up to +5.0% on out-of-domain tasks, surpasses the strongest self-evolution and text-editing co-evolution baselines by +4.3% and +8.4% respectively, and stays comparable to chart-specialized RLVR methods using 16-160 times fewer labeled samples. By shifting from human-labeled supervision to image-editing co-evolution, VICO offers a scalable path beyond static-corpus RLVR for visual reasoning.

</details>


### [8] [Velocity Scaling in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10823)
*Youssef Saied,François Fleuret*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出MSE训练的流匹配速度场并不存在速度幅值低估，速度缩放实际是在校正"群体时间滞后"，并据此选择增益将ImageNet-256上的FID从28.0降至12.2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作认为MSE训练会系统性低估速度幅值，因此用增益γ(t)缩放速度场可提升生成质量。作者质疑这一解释，希望厘清速度缩放真正起作用的机制。

Method: 理论上证明MSE训练不产生速度幅值亏损；提出"群体时间滞后"（population time lag）概念，即模型时间t的采样状态更像更早时间的训练状态；并统一解释速度缩放与移动模型时间两种操作，通过在线测量时间滞后来自适应选择增益γ(t)。

Result: 在多种架构与模型规模上验证，时间滞后测量与增益选择一致提升生成质量；ImageNet-256在NFE 25、无引导条件下FID从28.0降至12.2（基于相邻增益FID插值估计）。

Conclusion: 速度缩放的收益来源是对群体时间滞后而非幅值亏损的修正，为流匹配/扩散采样提供新的诊断指标与增益选择准则。

Abstract: Scaling a learned flow-matching velocity field $v_\theta$ by a gain $\gamma(t)$ was recently shown to greatly improve generation quality. Prior work argued that velocity fields trained with mean-squared error (MSE) systematically underestimate velocity magnitude and that scaling corrects this error. We show that MSE training does not create a velocity-magnitude deficit. We find instead that velocity scaling reduces population time lag: sampled states at model time $t$ resemble training states from an earlier time. Velocity scaling and moving model time back are two ways to address this population time lag. Across architectures and model sizes, measuring population time lag and using it to select a gain greatly improves generation quality, reducing FID from 28.0 to 12.2 (estimated by linear interpolation between FID measurements at neighboring gains) on ImageNet-256 at NFE 25 without guidance.

</details>


### [9] [Enabling Preference-driven Unlearning in Few-step Distilled Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10859)
*Gaurav Patel,Jun Fang,Greg Ver Steeg,Qiang Qiu,Sravan Sripada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向少步蒸馏扩散模型的偏好驱动遗忘框架，指出标准DPO及其遗忘变体因生成动力学改变而迁移性差，并给出与少步生成特性对齐的改进偏好优化形式，实现FSD模型中直接概念移除，兼顾少步效率与保留能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步蒸馏（FSD）扩散模型因推理快而被广泛部署，但存在生成有害或非预期内容的安全风险。现有数据驱动遗忘方法依赖多步去噪动力学的隐式假设，在FSD模型上失效；而先在非蒸馏基座上遗忘再重蒸馏的计算与时间开销过大，不实用。

Method: 提出偏好驱动的遗忘框架，重新审视扩散模型的直接偏好优化（DPO）。分析表明围绕噪声预测误差构建的标准DPO及其遗忘衍生方法因生成动力学改变而在FSD模型上迁移性差，故引入与少步生成特性显式对齐的改进偏好优化公式，实现FSD模型中的直接概念移除，同时保持少步效率与对非目标能力的保留。

Result: 主要在身份（identity）与NSFW（裸露）移除任务上评估，并扩展到对象级遗忘；大量实验显示一致且有效的遗忘效果与较强的保留性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为FSD模型中的遗忘提供了实用且具原则性的解决方案，无需重蒸馏即可直接移除概念。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models are increasingly distilled into few-step variants and being deployed to enable fast inference. However, their ability to generate harmful or undesired content poses significant safety risks. Data-driven unlearning methods suppress targeted generations by fine-tuning model weights using specialized unlearning objectives. Crucially, these objectives implicitly rely on multi-step denoising dynamics, an assumption that breaks down for few-step distilled (FSD) models, resulting in ineffective forgetting. Furthermore, performing unlearning on the non-distilled base model and subsequently re-distilling it to obtain an unlearned FSD model incurs substantial computational and time overhead, making it impractical in many settings. Hence, we address this limitation with a preference-driven unlearning framework that revisits Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) for diffusion models. We show that standard DPO and its unlearning derivatives, formulated around noise-prediction error, transfer poorly to FSD models due to their altered generation dynamics. To overcome this, we introduce a modified preference optimization formulation explicitly aligned with the few-step generation properties, enabling direct concept removal in FSD models while preserving few-step efficiency and maintaining strong retention of desirable (non-targeted) capabilities. We evaluate our framework primarily on identity and NSFW (nudity) removal tasks and also extend our method to object-level unlearning. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent and effective forgetting, and strong retention performance, establishing our method as a practical and principled solution for unlearning in FSD models.

</details>


### [10] [SPLIT-RL: Staged Perception-Language Reasoning Training with Claim-Level Advantages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10889)
*Raja Kumar,Rajat Koner,Ritwick Chaudhry,Zhuowei Li,Nishant Sankaran,Yifan Xing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 SPLIT-RL，将视觉推理（VR）与语言推理（LR）分阶段训练，并引入 claim 级优势（CLA-GRPO）以提供细粒度奖励信号，在多个 VLM 上取得一致性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习（如 GRPO）用单一 CoT 与最终答案奖励同时训练视觉与语言推理，使所有 token 共享同一序列级优势，无法区分能力相关的错误来源，导致感知能力可能未被有效优化。

Method: SPLIT-RL：在互不相交的阶段分别训练 VR 与 LR。由于同组 rollout 仅在一个能力维度上存在差异，组相对优势可隔离该能力，每阶段使用特定奖励优化。进一步提出 CLA-GRPO，将 VR 阶段 rollout 分解为原子视觉 claim，基于视觉类型分组形成 claim 级细粒度优势。推理时仍为单次 CoT 调用，与 GRPO 模型一致。

Result: 在 Qwen3-VL（2B 至 30B-A3B）及 InternVL3.5-8B 上，SPLIT-RL 平均准确率较 GRPO 提升 1.4–6.1 分。基于 oracle 的诊断评估显示：仅答案奖励的 GRPO 未改变感知能力，而 SPLIT-RL 同时提升 VR 与 LR。

Conclusion: 分阶段、分能力的 RL 后训练与 claim 级优势分配可有效克服单一序列级优势的局限，在不增加推理开销的前提下提升 VLM 的多模态推理能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language (VL) reasoning requires a model to both extract relevant and accurate information from an image (visual reasoning, VR), and to infer the answer from it (language reasoning, LR). Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards typically trains both through a single chain-of-thought with a final-answer reward. This gives every CoT token the same sequence-level advantage, failing to distinguish capability specific errors. We propose SPLIT-RL, a staged post-training approach that trains VR and LR in disjoint phases. Because a group's rollouts differ along one capability at a time, the group-relative advantage isolates it, and each phase is optimized using phase-specific reward. We further introduce Claim-Level Advantage (CLA-GRPO), which decomposes VR-phase rollouts into atomic visual claims and provides a fine-grained advantage at claim level based on visual-type group formation. Although trained in two phases, trained policy is evaluated like GRPO model, with a single CoT call at inference time. Under this protocol, SPLIT-RL improves average accuracy over GRPO by 1.4-6.1 points across Qwen3-VL models from 2B to 30B-A3B and InternVL3.5-8B. Evaluating each capability using an oracle based diagnostic shows that answer-only GRPO leaves perception unchanged, whereas SPLIT-RL improves both VR and LR.

</details>


### [11] [Less from More: Reinforcing Sparse Video Reasoning from Dense References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10893)
*Wenfang Sun,Yingjun Du,Cees G. M. Snoek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAVER：以稠密视频为训练时参照、稀疏帧为推理输入的后训练框架，用 ground 奖励与可靠性门控参考奖励做强化微调，在时间定位与视频问答基准上以更少帧数达到或超越稠密帧基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频语言模型普遍假设"时间观测越多推理越可靠"，本文质疑该假设，指出真正挑战不是高效处理更多帧，而是在有限时间证据下可靠推理（稀缺帧预算下的证据定位与推理）。

Method: 稠密到稀疏（dense-to-sparse）后训练框架：训练时使用成对的稠密与稀疏视频视图，在强化后训练阶段以时间定位（grounding）奖励与可靠性门控的参考奖励联合优化，使稀疏视图预测保留任务相关的时间证据；仅用 1250 条随机采样的时间定位样本训练，不使用任何视频问答标注。

Result: 在 3 个时间定位基准和 6 个视频问答基准上，跨帧预算一致提升；稀疏帧下可匹配或超越稠密帧 Qwen3.5 基线，且用帧数大幅更少；说明时间定位可作为证据定位代理，使稀疏视频推理能力迁移到更广泛的视频理解任务。

Conclusion: 时间定位任务可作为有效的证据定位代理，用于学习迁移性强的稀疏视频推理；以极少标注、极低帧预算获得与稠密帧基线相当或更好的表现，动摇了"越多帧越可靠"的默认假设。

Abstract: Video-language models commonly assume that more temporal observations lead to more reliable reasoning. We question this assumption and argue that the key challenge is not merely processing more video frames efficiently, but learning to reason reliably under limited temporal evidence. We propose SAVER, a dense-to-sparse post-training framework that uses dense video views as training-time references for sparse-frame inference. During reinforcement post-training, paired dense and sparse views are optimized with grounding rewards and a reliability-gated reference reward, encouraging sparse view predictions to preserve task-relevant temporal evidence. Notably, SAVER is trained only on 1,250 randomly sampled temporal grounding examples, without using any video question answering annotations. Across three temporal grounding benchmarks and six video question-answering benchmarks, SAVER consistently improves performance across frame budgets. In particular, SAVER can match or surpass dense-frame Qwen3.5 baselines while using substantially fewer frames. These results show that temporal grounding can serve as an effective evidence-localization proxy for learning sparse video reasoning that transfers to broader video understanding tasks.

</details>


### [12] [GHARP: Real-time Gaussian Head Animation from Large-scale Reconstruction Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10945)
*Ali Benlalah,Sepehr Johari,Patricia Vitoria,Armin Kappeler,Artem Sevastopolsky,Alexander Jung,Gabriele Fanelli,Kevin Mader,Manuel Breitenstein,Claudia Plüss,Jan Rüegg,Simon Biland,Thomas Etterlin,Dmitry Kostiaev,Mathias Deschler,Brian Amberg,Sebastian Martin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GHARP 通过"离线身份重建 + 在线轻量动画"两阶段解耦，在预训练重建模型的语义潜空间中预测表情残差，并用身体对齐网络消除身体/衣物歧义，实现实时高斯头部动画，在 Ava-256 上达到 SOTA 且比此前方法快 13 倍、高斯数少 8 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表情驱动的 3D 人头动画需要在保真度、质量与运行速度间权衡，移动端实时性尤其困难。同时，所有表情驱动 avatar 方法共有缺陷：表情编码只描述面部，身体姿态与衣物位置未被指定，导致这些区域欠约束，动画网络面对病态映射只能对冲突外观取平均，产生模糊与时间闪烁。

Method: 1) 两阶段解耦：离线 identity 阶段用多视角输入建立主体几何与外观的紧凑固定尺寸规范化高斯表示；运行时 animation 阶段以轻量网络预测表情相关残差。2) 在预训练重建模型的语义结构化潜空间中做动画，使表情变化在空间上保持局部性，残差预测更高效；重建先验提供一致空间布局以融合多视角。3) 引入身体对齐网络，学习将目标图像中的人物身体与输入参考图对齐，从训练信号中移除身体姿态/衣物的歧义。

Result: 在 Ava-256 基准上取得 SOTA 质量；在 A100 GPU 上运行速度最高提升 13 倍，且高斯数量减少 8 倍。

Conclusion: 将身份重建与表情动画解耦，并在重建先验潜空间中做残差建模，是兼顾质量与实时性的有效设计；身体对齐监督可显式消除身体/衣物区域的欠约束问题，为表情驱动 avatar 的固有局限提供通用性解法。

Abstract: We present GHARP (Real-time Gaussian Head Animation from Large-scale Reconstruction Prior), a method that animates 3D human heads in real time from a few input images of a subject and a driving expression signal. We decouple the problem into an identity stage that builds a representation of the subject's geometry and appearance offline, and an animation stage that predicts expression-dependent residuals on top of it at runtime. This separation offers a favorable trade-off with respect to fidelity, quality and runtime: the identity stage can be expensive while the animation stage runs a lightweight network, optimized for mobile devices. Our method performs animation in a semantically structured latent space of a pretrained reconstruction model, where expression changes remain spatially contained, making residual prediction efficient. This reconstruction prior provides a consistent spatial layout, allowing fusion of multiple input views into a compact, fixed-size canonical Gaussian representation. While this two-stage design improves the runtime-quality trade-off, it still inherits a problem common to all expression-driven avatar methods: expression codes describe only the face and thus omit body pose and clothing position, making these regions underspecified in the input. The animation network faces an ill-posed mapping and resorts to averaging over conflicting body appearances, producing blur and temporal flicker. We address this with a body alignment network that learns to align the person's body in the target image with the input reference images, removing the ambiguity from the training signal. Our method achieves state-of-the-art quality on the Ava-256 benchmark while running up to 13x faster on an A100 GPU with 8x fewer Gaussians.

</details>


### [13] [GPU-Accelerated Computation of Persistent Homology for Topological Analysis of Image Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10959)
*Fan Wang,Hubert Wagner,Rezaul Chowdhury,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TopoGPU：面向2D/3D图像立方复形的GPU流式持续同调计算管线，含分层感知离散Morse匹配、并行拓扑排序与V路径奇偶校验算法，平均端到端加速53.24倍（最高198.01倍），并集成到拓扑保持深度网络以降低训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续同调在深度学习中被快速采用，但其计算是网络训练的主要瓶颈，现有方法（如Cubical Ripser）在大规模图像上耗时过高，亟需GPU加速方案。

Method: TopoGPU将输入图像分块流式处理，块内由GPU块网格上的大规模并行核处理，边界关系累积到主机内存后由CPU做边界矩阵约简；提出分层感知离散Morse匹配以保证流式下持续同调不变，并设计并行拓扑排序算法与并行V路径奇偶校验算法在GPU上导出Morse边界。

Result: 在所有评测基准上均优于当前最优方法Cubical Ripser，平均端到端加速53.24倍，最高198.01倍；集成到拓扑保持深度网络后显著降低训练中持续同调计算成本；已开源并提供预编译二进制、Colab笔记本与Docker镜像。

Conclusion: TopoGPU以流式GPU管线结合Morse理论有效缓解了持续同调的计算瓶颈，可实际嵌入拓扑感知深度网络训练流程，具备开源可复现性。

Abstract: In recent years, persistent homology has seen rapid adoption in deep learning, yet its computation remains a major bottleneck in network training. This paper introduces TopoGPU, a GPU streaming pipeline that computes persistence diagrams of cubical complexes induced by 2D and 3D images. TopoGPU streams the input image chunk by chunk, processing each chunk with massively parallel GPU kernels on a grid of GPU blocks; the resulting boundary relations are accumulated in host memory, where the CPU performs the boundary matrix reduction. TopoGPU introduces a stratification-aware discrete Morse matching that provably preserves persistent homology under streaming, together with a parallel topological sorting algorithm and a parallel V-path parity algorithm for deriving Morse boundaries on the GPU. TopoGPU outperforms Cubical Ripser, a state-of-the-art method for persistent homology computation, on every benchmark evaluated, achieving an average end-to-end speedup of 53.24x and a maximum of 198.01x. We further integrate TopoGPU into a topology-preserving deep network, demonstrating that it substantially reduces the cost of persistent homology computation during network training. TopoGPU is open source, with pre-built binaries, Google Colab notebooks, and Docker images available at the project's GitHub page: this https URL .

</details>


### [14] [LVSPM: Long Sequence View Synthesis and Pose Estimation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10960)
*Xi Chen,Yachi Zhang,Linghao Chen,Minghua Liu,Hao Su,Zexiang Xu,Xiaoshuai Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LVSPM：一种联合估计相机位姿并合成新视角的可泛化模型，仅用RGB与位姿监督、结合测试时训练层，在无标定图像集上超越VGGT并在多数据集上取得无位姿NVS的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式三维重建/新视角合成方法在多视图扩展性、严格位姿精度阈值以及大场景退化方面表现不足，且依赖稠密3D真值或标定位姿。作者希望仅凭RGB与位姿监督，构建可泛化、可随视图数扩展、无需位姿输入的联合位姿估计与NVS模型。

Method: 提出LVSPM，联合估计相机位姿并合成新视角；训练仅需RGB图像与位姿监督，不需稠密3D真值；引入测试时训练（TTT）层，使模型可在推理阶段适应并扩展至数百个输入视图。

Result: 在RealEstate10k、Co3Dv2、DL3DV上，16-256视图范围内位姿估计均超过VGGT，严格阈值下优势尤其明显；在实际协议（更多视图覆盖更大场景）下，无位姿NVS达到SOTA质量，PSNR甚至超过依赖位姿的模型，且随场景尺度增大仍保持高质量，而基线方法崩溃。

Conclusion: LVSPM证明无标定、无稠密3D真值条件下，通过测试时训练可实现可泛化、可扩展的联合位姿估计与新视角合成，在大规模无位姿场景下优于现有方法，并提供开源代码。

Abstract: We present LVSPM, a generalizable model that jointly estimates camera poses and synthesizes novel views from uncalibrated image collections. Trained with only RGB images and pose supervision, LVSPM avoids dense 3D ground truth and employs test-time training (TTT) layers to scale seamlessly to hundreds of input views. On RealEstate10k, Co3Dv2, and DL3DV, LVSPM surpasses VGGT in pose estimation across 16-256 views, with especially large margins at strict thresholds. For novel view synthesis under a practical protocol where more views cover larger scenes, LVSPM achieves state-of-the-art pose-free quality---surpassing even pose-dependent models in PSNR---and still maintains high quality as scene scale grows, while baselines collapse. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [15] [Fluid-Gen-Zero: Grounding Pretrained Video Generators in Physics without Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10984)
*Hong Huang,Yuqiu Liu,Chenyu You,Daniel Martin,Chuhang Zou,Wuyang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Fluid-Gen-Zero：免训练、解耦物理推理与外观合成的流体-物体交互视频生成框架，用物理模拟器提供动力学、VLM智能体+潜空间引导桥接，兼容现有视频基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型难以生成物理可信的流体-物体交互（轨迹漂移、流场错误），而纯模拟方法外观不真实；作者希望在不重训练的前提下同时获得物理一致性与高质量外观。

Method: 两层级智能体工作流：生成时规划由VLM智能体解读意图与模拟rollout来组织生成片段；潜空间引导通过区域感知的latent wrapping将模拟信号注入去噪过程。整体为即插即用，无需训练。

Result: 在Tora(CogVideoX)、VACE与WanMove(Wan)上，物体轨迹误差降低26.7%-81.5%，流体fEPE降低67.9%-84.0%，感知质量基本保持；人类偏好研究中同骨干对比胜率55.1%-74.4%，对模拟类方法胜率90.4%-94.2%。并提出了流体-物体交互视频生成基准。

Conclusion: 将动力学交给模拟器、把模拟信号注入预训练视频生成器的潜空间，是免训练实现物理感知视频生成的有效范式，且可跨骨干即插即用。

Abstract: We present Fluid-Gen-Zero, a training-free framework for physics-aware fluid-object interaction video generation that decouples physical reasoning from appearance synthesis. Our key insight is to delegate motion dynamics to a physics simulator while preserving the appearance modeling capacity of pretrained video generators. We bridge these two domains through a two-level agentic workflow: generation-time planning, where a vision-language model (VLM) agent interprets intent and the simulation rollout to organize generation clips, and latent-space guidance, which injects simulation signals into denoising through region-aware latent wrapping. This plug-and-play design is compatible with current video foundation models. We further introduce a benchmark for fluid-object interaction video generation. Across Tora (CogVideoX-based), VACE and WanMove (Wan-based), Fluid-Gen-Zero consistently improves simulation alignment, reducing object trajectory error by 26.7%-81.5% and fluid fEPE (fluid flow endpoint error) by 67.9%-84.0%, while largely preserving perceptual quality. In a human preference study, raters favor Fluid-Gen-Zero in 55.1%-74.4% of same-backbone comparisons across three backbones, and in 90.4%-94.2% of comparisons against simulation-based methods. Code and data will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [16] [Omni-Diffusion-Distill: Few-Step Distillation of Unified Multimodal Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10990)
*Hong Huang,Chenhongyi Yang,Junzhe Sun,Animesh Sinha,Wuyang Chen,Yifan Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Omni-Diffusion-Distill 两阶段蒸馏框架，将统一多模态离散扩散大模型（dLLM）的图像生成与多模态理解能力同时压缩到少步学生模型，推理加速 18.2x/21.2x，并保持 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态 dLLM 用单一架构同时做图像生成和多模态理解，但迭代解码需数十到数百次前向。已有少步蒸馏方法多只针对图像或文本生成之一，如何把完全离散的多模态 dLLM 压缩成单一高效学生模型、同时保留生成与理解能力尚不明确。

Method: 两阶段蒸馏：第一阶段让学生在离散 token 空间重放缓存的教师轨迹以学习跳步解码；第二阶段在学生自身 rollout 的中间状态上精修。生成与理解、图像与文本的蒸馏在离散 token 空间中对齐。另针对两类退化加以补救：用成对碰撞惩罚（pairwise collision penalty）减少并行文本解码的重复，用熵匹配引导（entropy-matched guidance）防止拟合被锐化的教师分布导致图像生成的熵塌缩。

Result: 在解码效率与生成/理解性能权衡上达到 SOTA：图像生成从 128 步降至 8 步，多模态理解从 512 步降至 64 步，分别获得 18.2x 和 21.2x 墙钟加速。该预算下 GenEval 0.828、DPG-Bench 83.0；多模态理解 MM-Vet GPT 评审分 20.0、COCO captioning 57.2（同步数下教师为 28.4 的两倍）。

Conclusion: 统一的离散 token 空间两阶段蒸馏可同时压缩图像生成与多模态理解，配合碰撞惩罚与熵匹配引导能有效缓解统一蒸馏中的退化，为多模态 dLLM 提供高效部署路径。

Abstract: Unified multimodal diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a single architecture for both image generation and multimodal understanding, but their iterative decoding requires tens to hundreds of forward passes. Existing few-step distillation methods largely focus on either image generation or text generation, making it unclear how to compress a fully discrete multimodal dLLM into a single efficient student while preserving both generation and understanding. We introduce Omni-Diffusion-Distill, a unified two-stage distillation framework that retains strong generation and understanding capabilities while substantially reducing the inference cost of a unified multimodal dLLM. Omni-Diffusion-Distill aligns the distillation of both generation and understanding, for both images and text, in the discrete token space. In the first stage, the student is trained to skip decoding steps by replaying cached teacher trajectories, and in the second stage the student is refined on intermediate states along its own rollouts. We further remedy two sources of degradation in unified distillation with a pairwise collision penalty that reduces repetition under parallel text decoding, and entropy-matched guidance that prevents entropy collapse caused by fitting the sharpened teacher distribution in image generation. Omni-Diffusion-Distill achieves state-of-the-art trade-offs between decoding efficiency and generation and understanding performance for multimodal dLLMs, reducing image generation from 128 to 8 decoding steps and multimodal understanding from 512 to 64, giving 18.2x and 21.2x wall-clock speedups. Under these budgets, it scores 0.828 on GenEval and 83.0 on DPG-Bench for text-to-image generation, while reaching GPT judge scores of 20.0 on MM-Vet and 57.2 on COCO captioning (twice the teacher's 28.4 at the same steps) for multimodal understanding.

</details>


### [17] [Region-Aware CLS Token Augmentation for Fine-Grained Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10991)
*Ian de Holanda Cavalcanti Bezerra,Vivek Trivedy,Lucas Pascotti Valem,Longin Jan Latecki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作利用 DINOv2-reg 的语义 token（[CLS] 与 4 个 register token），为每个 token 自动匹配一个"buddy" patch 并提取 N×N 局部 ROI token，再以 ColBERT 式多向量检索框架进行细粒度匹配，在无需外部框或显著性模块的条件下提升细粒度图像检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像检索依赖单一全局语义描述子（如 ViT 的 [CLS] token），将所有语义信息压缩进一个向量会损失细节，尤其在细粒度检索任务中性能受限。作者希望在不引入外部边界框或显著性模块的前提下，显式引入空间区域表示来补充语义 token。

Method: 基于 DINOv2-reg 模型（含 [CLS] 与 4 个寄存器 token，寄存器 token 可涌现式学习物体/部件表示）。对每个"cue" token（[CLS] 及每个 register token），通过匹配找到一个"buddy"图像 patch token，并以其为中心提取 N×N patch 区域，生成一组局部化 ROI token，自动获取感兴趣区域。随后将这些 token 纳入受 ColBERT 启发的多向量检索框架，通过逐 token 对齐实现细粒度匹配，同时避免存储全部 patch embedding 带来的巨大开销。

Result: 实验表明：(1) register token 编码了可补充 [CLS] 的有用细粒度细节；(2) 自动池化的 ROI token 进一步提升了细粒度判别能力；(3) 使用少量 token 的多向量检索优于 DINOv2-reg 单向量基线，且在大规模搜索中仍具可处理性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 语义 token 与空间区域表示的结合可有效提升细粒度检索；寄存器 token 具有互补的细粒度价值，少量 token 的多向量检索在性能与存储/检索成本之间取得良好折中，为大规模检索提供可行方案。

Abstract: Image retrieval methods often rely on a single global semantic descriptor extracted from an image, e.g., the [CLS] token in vision transformers. However, trying to squeeze all the semantic information of an image into a single descriptor can hurt downstream retrieval performance, especially for fine-grained retrieval tasks. In this work, we augment the semantic tokens in the newer visual transformers, the global [CLS] token and the four register tokens, with a carefully selected collection of spatial tokens, aiming to capture the spatial region representation that characterizes the contents captured in each of the semantic tokens. We leverage the DINOv2-reg model, which includes register tokens that emergently learn object and part-based representations. For each "cue" token ([CLS] and each register token), we find a "buddy" image patch token and extract an N x N patch region to produce a set of localized ROI tokens. Our approach automatically captures important regions of interest without any external bounding boxes or saliency modules, purely by matching semantic tokens with their spatial representation regions. Furthermore, we incorporate these tokens into a multi-vector retrieval framework inspired by ColBERT, enabling fine-grained matching via a per-token alignment mechanism while avoiding the large storage cost of keeping all patch embeddings. Through extensive experiments, we find that (1) register tokens encode useful fine-grained details that can complement the [CLS] token; (2) automatically pooled ROI tokens further improve fine-grained discrimination; and (3) multi-vector retrieval with a small set of tokens improves over a DINOv2-reg single-vector baseline while remaining tractable for large-scale search. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [18] [PCAsplat: Gaussian Splatting with Local PCA Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11011)
*Vitor Matias,Filipe Nascimento,Kiyohiro Nakayama,João Paulo Lima,Márcus Lobo,Gordon Wetzstein,Leonidas Guibas,Afonso Paiva,Tiago Novello*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PCAsplat，用可微局部主成分分析（PCA）对高斯泼溅进行几何感知正则化，使不参与当前视角的光栅化梯度的高斯也能获得几何梯度，从而贴合表面、减少漂浮物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯泼溅方法主要依赖光栅化损失，仅当高斯对采样光线有贡献时才被监督；被遮挡或贡献极小的高斯几何梯度弱或缺失，会偏离真实表面形成漂浮物（floaters）。作者希望引入与视角无关的几何正则，使这些高斯也能被有效更新。

Method: 设计基于可微局部 PCA 的几何感知正则框架：直接在高斯中心邻域上做局部 PCA，因而能更新未贡献于当前训练视角的高斯。其一是对 PCA 特征值做正则，促使高斯移动到潜在表面并获得各向同性的切平面覆盖；其二是将每个高斯的法向与 PCA 估计的邻域法向对齐，保证朝向一致。

Result: 在 DTU、Tanks and Temples 与 NeRF Synthetic 上，PCAsplat 生成的泼溅更接近参考表面采样，显著减少漂浮物；所得表面对齐的泼溅支持下游几何处理任务，如点云分割与直接泊松重建；在常规新视角合成与网格提取任务上仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过视角无关的局部 PCA 正则化，可有效约束高斯几何使其贴合表面，兼顾几何质量与渲染性能，代码将开源。

Abstract: Gaussian splatting has emerged as a flexible representation for 3D reconstruction from posed images. However, existing methods are optimized primarily using rasterization-based losses, which supervise a splat only when it contributes to sampled camera rays. Gaussians that are occluded or contribute little to the sampled view therefore receive weak or no geometric gradients and may drift away from the underlying surface, producing undesired floaters. We introduce PCAsplat, a geometry-aware regularization framework for Gaussian splatting based on differentiable local principal component analysis (PCA). Our PCA regularizer acts directly on neighborhoods of Gaussian centers and can therefore update Gaussians that do not contribute to the current training view. We regularize the PCA eigenvalues to encourage Gaussians to move to the underlying surface with isotropic tangent-plane coverage. We also align each Gaussian normal with the PCA-estimated neighborhood normal to enforce consistent orientation. Experiments on DTU, Tanks and Temples, and NeRF Synthetic show that the splats produced by PCAsplat better approximate samples of the reference surface while substantially reducing undesired floaters. These surface-aligned splats enable downstream geometry-processing tasks, including point cloud segmentation, and direct Poisson reconstruction. Additionally, PCAsplat remains competitive under conventional novel view synthesis and mesh extraction tasks. Code will be released.

</details>


### [19] [Mid-Training Language Models on Raw Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11019)
*Jaedong Hwang,Xiaoqian Shen,Ernie Chang,Changsheng Zhao,Chong Zhou,Saksham Suri,Qi Qian,Zechun Liu,Lemeng Wu,Qinsi Wang,Raghuraman Krishnamoorthi,Wei Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究用无标注原始视频作为预训练语言模型的中期训练数据：将视频帧编码为连续视觉token，让LLM预测下一视觉token，在Qwen3-1.7B上中期训练后，视频与图像基准均有提升且文本能力未退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型主要依赖图文配对数据或标注视频，原始网络视频很少被用于继续训练语言模型。作者想验证无字幕、无文本损失的原始视频能否作为中期训练数据，从而以纯自监督方式注入视觉-时序知识。

Method: 将视频帧编码为连续视觉token，语言模型以预测下一视觉token为目标进行自监督训练；在YT-Temporal-1B的原始片段上对Qwen3-1.7B做中期训练，随后对中期训练模型与未中期训练模型施加完全相同的图文指令微调，使两者仅差在中期训练；并对比"预测字幕"与"预测下一视觉token"两种目标。

Result: 中期训练模型在4个视频基准上平均高2.9分，在10个图像基准（感知、文档、图表）上平均高5.1分；文本能力未受损，14个文本基准平均48.9，对照组48.0。训练过程分析显示图像与视频增益在30%训练量内出现后趋于平台，波动小于0.5分。预测字幕未优于预测下一视觉token。

Conclusion: 原始视频可作为纯自监督的中期训练数据有效提升视觉-语言模型能力，无需自动字幕，避免标注噪声和额外计算开销，且不损害文本性能。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models learn mostly from paired image-text data or annotated video, and raw web video is rarely used to further train an existing language model. We study whether raw video, with no captions and no text loss, can serve as mid-training data for a pretrained language model. Frames are encoded into continuous visual tokens, and the language model learns to predict the next visual token. We mid-train Qwen3-1.7B on raw clips from YT-Temporal-1B and then apply the same image-text instruction tuning to it and to the model without mid-training, so that the two differ only in mid-training. The mid-trained model scores 2.9 points higher on average across four video benchmarks and 5.1 points higher across ten image benchmarks, spanning perception, document, and chart tasks. Text performance is preserved even though mid-training includes no text, with an average of 48.9 across 14 text benchmarks compared with 48.0 for the model without mid-training. Analyses across training show that the image and video gains emerge within 30% of training and plateau thereafter, varying by less than 0.5 points. Predicting captions fails to outperform next-visual-token prediction, demonstrating that video mid-training can remain purely self-supervised without the computational overhead or labeling noise of automated captioning.

</details>


### [20] [Expression-Diverse References for Identity-Preserving Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11023)
*Tianwen Fu,Wenbin Teng,Gonglin Chen,Junyi Ouyang,Haolin Xiong,Yajie Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从生成与评测两个角度研究身份保持视频生成中"表情强度依赖的面部外观变化"问题：先用受控照片与MEAD视频量化人脸识别相似度随表情强度的变化，再构建紧凑且具表情多样性的参考图库以获得更稳健的身份相似度度量，并按轻/强/极强三档表情强度分别报告身份相似度；生成方面将Stand-In扩展为以表情多样参考集为条件，并设计数据筛选流程从训练视频中提取一致且多样的面部裁剪，单张肖像场景下用预训练面部重演模型合成额外表情，受控基准上真实与合成参考集在三个强度区间均超过基线，极强表情提升最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有身份保持视频生成通常仅以单张中性参考肖像作为身份条件，而该肖像只捕获一种面部构型；表情变化会以高度身份特异的方式改变面部外观，使未见表情下的外观在参考条件不足时欠定。同时，以中性参考计算相似度的评测方式会在强表情下低估真实同一人的身份一致性，即存在评测偏差。作者因此希望同时解决"生成时参考信息不足"与"评测时度量不稳"这两个问题。

Method: 1）用受控拍摄照片与MEAD视频量化人脸识别相似度随表情强度的变化规律；2）构建紧凑但具表情表达力的参考图库，覆盖多样的表情相关面部构型，以图库匹配替代单参考匹配来度量身份相似度；3）按mild/intense/extreme三档表情强度分层报告身份相似度，以暴露性能退化；4）将Stand-In扩展为条件于表情多样参考集，并设计数据筛选流程从训练视频中提取一致且多样的面部裁剪；5）单肖像可用场景下，用预训练面部重演模型合成额外表情构建参考集。

Result: 在自建受控基准上，真实参考集与合成参考集在轻、强、极强三档表情强度下的身份相似度均优于所评估的基线，且在极强表情区间提升幅度最大。同时，基于表情多样图库的匹配比单中性参考匹配提供更稳健的表情化运动条件下的身份相似度度量。

Conclusion: 身份保持视频生成中的身份条件应显式覆盖表情依赖的面部构型，而非依赖单张中性肖像；评测也需摆脱单中性参考的相似度偏差，改用表情多样的参考图库并分层报告表情强度。该思路在生成端与评测端均可带来一致收益，且可在仅有单张肖像时通过合成表情实现，具有较好的实用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Identity-preserving video generation aims to maintain a subject's identity while synthesizing realistic videos. Yet a single reference portrait captures the subject's appearance under only one facial configuration. As expressions change, facial appearance can vary in highly identity-specific ways, leaving the subject's appearance under unseen expressions underdetermined by the reference alone. This expression-dependent variation also complicates evaluation: similarity to a neutral reference may decrease under strong expressions even for real images of the same person. We investigate this limitation from both generation and evaluation perspectives. First, we quantify how face-recognition similarity varies with expression intensity using controlled photographs and MEAD videos. We then construct a compact yet expressive reference gallery that captures diverse expression-dependent facial configurations. Matching against this gallery provides a more robust measure of identity similarity under expressive motion. To further expose performance degradation with expression intensity, we report identity similarity separately for mild, intense, and extreme expressions. For generation, we extend Stand-In to condition on our expression-diverse reference sets and develop a data-curation pipeline that extracts consistent yet diverse face crops from training videos. In practical settings where only a single portrait is available, we construct the reference set by synthesizing additional expressions with a pretrained facial reenactment model. On our controlled benchmark, both real and synthesized reference sets outperform the evaluated baselines in identity similarity across all three expression-intensity regimes, with the largest improvements for extreme expressions.

</details>


### [21] [Transforming Image Editors into Video Editors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11037)
*Feng Wang,Zijie Li,Ceyuan Yang,Alan Yuille,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Anchor-based Video Editing (AVE)：不做端到端视频编辑训练，而是先用强图像编辑器编辑稀疏关键帧，再以运动引导的图像到视频扩散模型将编辑后的关键帧作为固定锚点生成最终视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频编辑相比图像编辑成本高、难度大；端到端训练单体视频编辑器昂贵。作者认为视频编辑可分解为「编辑稀疏关键帧」与「跨时间传播编辑」两个子问题，因此可用强图像编辑器直接迁移到视频。

Method: 两阶段框架 AVE：阶段一由图像编辑器在选定关键帧上完成组合式编辑；阶段二使用运动引导的 image-to-video 扩散模型，将编辑后的关键帧作为固定锚点，生成时间一致的最终视频。无需视频编辑端到端训练。

Result: 在 IVEBench 与 VIE-Bench 上，AVE 在指令跟随、时间一致性和内容保真度方面取得较强表现。消融显示最终视频编辑质量与图像编辑器质量强相关。

Conclusion: 视频编辑的进展可能来自更强的图像编辑底座与轻量化向视频的迁移，而非昂贵端到端视频编辑训练；关键帧锚点式传播是有效替代方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent image editing systems have achieved impressive semantic understanding, visual fidelity, and instruction-following ability, while video editing remains substantially more difficult and costly. In this paper, we present a simple alternative to end-to-end video editing: instead of training a monolithic video editor, we transform a strong image editor into a video editor through anchor-based generation. Our key insight is that video editing can be decomposed into two subproblems: editing a sparse set of keyframes and propagating those edits across time. Based on this observation, we propose Anchor-based Video Editing (AVE), a two-stage framework in which a powerful image editor first performs composed editing on selected keyframes, and a motion-guided image-to-video diffusion model then generates the final video by treating the edited keyframes as fixed anchors. This design directly inherits the strengths of modern image editors while avoiding expensive end-to-end video editing training. Experiments on IVEBench and VIE-Bench show that AVE achieves strong performance in instruction following, temporal consistency, and content fidelity. Further ablations reveal that final video editing quality is strongly correlated with the quality of the image editor, suggesting that future progress in video editing may come from stronger image editing foundations and lightweight transfer to video. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [22] [Rendering-Free Lookahead for Question-Guided Active Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11039)
*Koya Sakamoto,Daichi Azuma,Shuhei Kurita,Naoya Chiba,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Taiki Miyanishi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RFL 通过"可回答性"这一代理目标，用特权 3DGS 渲染的离线 lookahead 蒸馏出免渲染的相机动作选择策略，在 E3VS-Bench 上提升 43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视角依赖的问答需要主动移动相机去暴露当前视角看不到的视觉证据（如升高相机看进盒子里）。VLM 能解读已见图像，但无法预判未见视角是否有用；而在线渲染候选视角代价高、部署时不可行。因此需要一种训练时利用"特权未来视角"、部署时无需渲染的动作选择策略。

Method: 提出 Rendering-Free Lookahead (RFL)。将"可回答性"定义为冻结 VLM 对某视角是否足以回答问题的估计，作为动作价值的代理。训练阶段：在 3D Gaussian Splatting 场景中由特权教师渲染候选未来视角，用冻结 VLM 计算一步与两步的可回答性目标。通过两阶段蒸馏，学生模型以问题、近期视觉观测和候选相机动作作为输入，预测动作价值。部署阶段只前向预测价值，渲染被完全省去。

Result: 在未见环境的 377 个 E3VS-Bench 测试回合上，使用同一 VLM 时，RFL 的平均 judge score 比直接动作（direct-action）基线提升 43%。

Conclusion: 结果表明可以借助特权视觉 lookahead 从离线数据中学习相机控制策略，用于视角依赖的问答；该方法把昂贵的前瞻渲染转移到训练期，部署期仅做价值预测，具备实用性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Active robot vision requires controlling the camera to reveal task-relevant information that is hidden from the current viewpoint. For example, determining what is inside a box may require raising the camera and looking down into it. For viewpoint-dependent question answering, the challenge is to select camera motions that expose the visual evidence needed to answer the question. Although vision-language models (VLMs) can interpret observed images, selecting such motions requires anticipating the usefulness of unseen views. We quantify this usefulness as answerability, a VLM's estimate that a view suffices to answer the question, and present Rendering-Free Lookahead (RFL), a viewpoint-selection policy that ranks candidate camera motions by predicted future answerability. RFL transfers visual lookahead from deployment to offline training. At training, a privileged teacher renders candidate future views in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) scenes and uses a frozen VLM to compute one- and two-step answerability targets. Through two-stage distillation, a student learns to predict these action values from the question, recent visual observations, and a candidate camera motion. At deployment, RFL uses these predicted values to select camera motions without rendering future views. On 377 E3VS-Bench test episodes in unseen environments, RFL improves the mean judge score by 43\% over a direct-action baseline using the same VLM. These results support learning camera-control policies from privileged visual lookahead for viewpoint-dependent question answering.

</details>


### [23] [SatFix: Absolute Visual Localization of UAVs in Satellite Maps from a Single Oblique Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11049)
*Jiarui Zeng,Kun Shi,Chiman Vong,Zhedong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SatFix：基于VGGT-Ω的前馈式无人机-卫星绝对定位框架，利用卫星栅格特征作为查询聚合无人机视觉证据，由两个轻量头回归卫星地图坐标系下的3自由度位姿（连续2D位置+航向），支持单视图与多视图输入且无需3D地图、渲染鸟瞰图或测试时位姿对齐；同时提出University-Metric基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视图地理定位方法依赖从图库中检索最相似卫星瓦片并报告Recall@K，存在三方面缺陷：检索结果依赖图库采样密度与方式；不提供航向估计；返回瓦片索引而非连续坐标。且现有评估（如University-1652）覆盖区域过小，难以体现真实绝对定位需求。

Method: 在VGGT-Ω骨干上构建前馈框架：卫星栅格特征作为query，通过交叉注意力聚合无人机视觉证据；两个轻量回归头输出卫星地图坐标系下的连续2D位置与航向（3-DoF位姿）。单一模型同时支持单视图与多视图输入，多视图训练中引入轨迹约束。无需显式3D地图、渲染鸟瞰图像、辅助传感器或测试时位姿对齐。评估方面构建University-Metric，重采集边长最多达原University-1652的10.7倍（约114倍地面面积）的卫星影像区域，并为原始无人机轨迹提供连续位置与航向标注。

Result: 单视图：52.08%测试帧定位误差在50 m内，17.34%在10 m内；位置误差中位数45.66 m，航向误差中位数20.81°。九视图：位置误差中位数降至21.96 m，航向误差中位数降至8.73°。单视图查询在NVIDIA RTX 4090上推理耗时低于0.1 s。相比微调后的VGGT-Ω基线，位置误差中位数降低34.0%，九视图航向误差中位数由25.43°降至8.73°。

Conclusion: SatFix以纯前馈方式实现连续、带航向的无人机绝对地理定位，避免检索式方法的图库依赖与离散瓦片输出，并单模型统一单/多视图输入；配合更大范围的University-Metric基准，验证了在多视图输入下的显著精度提升与实时的推理效率。

Abstract: We study absolute metric UAV localization within a provided geo-referenced satellite region, recovering continuous map position and viewing heading from a single oblique image or a short multi-view clip. Existing cross-view geo-localization methods retrieve the most similar satellite tile from a gallery and report Recall@K, but retrieval depends on gallery sampling, provides no heading estimate, and returns a tile index rather than a continuous coordinate. We propose SatFix, a feed-forward UAV--satellite localization framework built on VGGT-$\Omega$. Satellite-grid features act as queries that aggregate UAV visual evidence, and two lightweight heads regress a 3-DoF pose in the satellite-map frame: continuous 2D position and heading. SatFix requires no explicit 3D map, rendered bird's-eye image, auxiliary sensor, or test-time pose alignment. A single model supports both single- and multi-view inputs, with trajectory constraints used during multi-view training. For metric evaluation, we introduce University-Metric, where satellite imagery is re-collected over a region up to 10.7$\times$ longer on a side (about 114$\times$ the ground area) than the original University-1652 tiles, with continuous position and heading labels for the original UAV tours. With one UAV view, SatFix localizes 52.08% of test frames within 50 m and 17.34% within 10 m, with median position and heading errors of 45.66 m and $20.81^\circ$, respectively. Inference takes under 0.1 s per single-view query on an NVIDIA RTX 4090. With nine UAV views, the median position error falls to 21.96 m and the median heading error to $8.73^\circ$. Compared with a fine-tuned VGGT-$\Omega$ baseline, SatFix reduces median position error by 34.0% and nine-view median heading error from $25.43^\circ$ to $8.73^\circ$.

</details>


### [24] [Learning What to Trust in Multimodal Learning under Noisy Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11057)
*Jiashuo Zou,Xiaobo Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 REFINE，一种多模态标签噪声检测框架，通过理论分析表示结构与噪声检测能力的关系，联合使用融合表示与单模态表示，为每个类别选取判别性特征向量与可信表示空间，据此筛选干净样本以提升多模态分类器泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态分类依赖高质量标注，但真实场景中标签噪声普遍存在；已有的噪声样本选择方法多针对单模态设计，未能充分利用多模态信息，因此需要构建更可靠的多模态噪声检测器。

Method: 首先从理论上分析表示结构与噪声检测能力之间的关系；据此提出 REFINE：对目标类别与背景类别做判别性分析以构造判别性特征向量，为每个类别挑选噪声检测能力更强的可信表示空间；在每个可信空间内度量实例表示与判别性特征向量的一致性，并将各空间筛选出的子集进行组合。

Result: 在多种任务上的大量实验表明 REFINE 优于基线方法；组合得到的干净样本集合为多模态分类器提供更可靠的监督信号，减少了错误标注样本的影响并提升模型泛化性能。源代码将公开。

Conclusion: REFINE 通过表示结构与噪声检测能力的理论关联，将融合与单模态表示统一用于标签噪声检测，为多模态鲁棒学习提供了有效方案，但其验证主要在通用多模态分类任务上，尚缺乏具体机器人或具身场景的实验证据。

Abstract: Multimodal classification processes and relates information from multiple modalities to achieve more accurate predictions. However, existing methods typically rely on high-quality ground-truth labels, which are difficult to obtain in real-world scenarios. While sample-selection methods for learning with noisy labels aim to identify correctly labeled examples from noisy data, traditional methods primarily focus on unimodal settings and fail to exploit multimodal information fully. This motivates us to build a more reliable noise detector in multimodal learning. To this end, we theoretically analyze the relationship between representation structure and noise detection capability. Based on this analysis, we propose REFINE, which is a multimodal label-noise detection framework that jointly uses fused and unimodal representations for label-noise detection. Specifically, REFINE constructs discriminative eigenvectors through discriminative analysis of the target and background classes and selects trusted representation spaces with better noise detection capability for each class. Within each trusted space, REFINE measures the alignment between each instance representation and the discriminative eigenvectors. It then combines the subsets selected from these spaces. The combined set provides cleaner supervision for updating the multimodal classifier, thereby reducing the influence of mislabeled examples during training and improving model generalization. Extensive experiments across diverse tasks demonstrate REFINE's superiority compared to baseline methods. The source code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [25] [Refine Connections, Close the Gap: A Reliable Enhancement Framework for Driving Scene Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11058)
*Xiaoqi Wang,Dingyi Zhaung,David Paz,Wenbin He,Yucai Bai,Peng Zhou,Rui Zhang,Jinhua Zhao,Liu Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TopoEnhance，将自动驾驶场景拓扑图的连通性推理建模为去噪式重建任务，从随机破坏的 GT 图中恢复结构一致性，从而把连续拓扑分数转为更可靠的离散决策图，无需重训练即可即插即用地提升多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地图元素检测方法在连通性推理上远未达理论上界；连通性多由连续分数阈值化得到，分数本身不反映真实逻辑连通概率，导致误连/漏连；现有基准也只评测连续指标，忽视决策所需的离散连通性。

Method: 把拓扑增强形式化为基于去噪的重建过程：对真值图施加随机扰动（损坏/增删边），训练模型恢复结构一致性，从而纠正逻辑不一致与不可靠连接，输出稳健的离散拓扑图；框架与来源无关（source-agnostic），可套在已有方法输出之上。

Result: 在多种基线上一致提升连续拓扑指标（TOP score）与新增的离散连通性指标 Topology Jaccard Similarity（TJS），性能接近理论上界；作为通用后处理模块，无需重训练即带来显著增益。

Conclusion: TopoEnhance 以去噪重建视角统一解决拓扑连通性的逻辑一致性与离散可靠性问题，是可插拔的通用增强框架，并指出未来基准应纳入离散连通性评测。

Abstract: In autonomous driving, understanding scene topology - the connectivity between lanes and traffic elements - is critical for safe path planning and motion control. While current methods excel at detecting individual map elements, their connectivity reasoning often falls short of its theoretical potential, leaving a significant performance gap relative to the theoretical upper-bound achievable given the underlying detections. Furthermore, the decision-ready topology graphs passed to downstream tasks often remain unreliable. Current approaches typically derive connectivity by thresholding continuous topology scores; however, these scores often fail to reflect the true logical likelihood of connectivity, resulting in false positives or missing connections. Existing benchmarks further overlook this issue by primarily evaluating continuous metrics, rather than assessing the discrete connectivity required for decision-making. To bridge these gaps, we propose TopoEnhance, a novel topology enhancement framework designed to unlock the latent potential of existing methods and improve the reliability of decision-ready topology. We formulate topology enhancement as a denoising-based reconstruction process, where the model learns to recover structural consistency from stochastically corrupted ground-truth graphs. This formulation enables the model to resolve logical inconsistencies and rectify unreliable connections, producing robust discrete topology graphs that closely approach theoretical maximum performance. Extensive experiments across different baselines show that TopoEnhance consistently improves both continuous topology metrics (TOP score), and discrete connectivity measured by our adapted Topology Jaccard Similarity (TJS) metric. As a flexible, source-agnostic framework, TopoEnhance delivers substantial gains across diverse state-of-the-art baselines without requiring retraining.

</details>


### [26] [AffordDrive3D: Affordance-Aware World-Action Modeling with Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11060)
*Tianhui Cai,Xinglong Sun,Chao Fang,Zhenxin Li,Rui Song,Jose M. Alvarez,Yunxiang Mao,Jiaqi Ma,Langechuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AffordDrive3D：一种可承受性与几何感知的世界-动作模型，在 VLM 骨干上联合预测可行驶区域/碰撞关键区域与未来几何，NAVSIM 上达 91.3 PDMS / 89.9 EPDMS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 world-action 模型多以 RGB 外观预测未来，近期虽引入几何预测以增强空间理解，但稠密几何只描述全场景布局，无法指出哪些区域与自车动作最相关。驾驶需同时识别"可安全行驶区域"与"潜在碰撞风险区域"，因此需将动作相关区域与未来几何联合建模，为策略同时提供驾驶相关线索及其空间结构。

Method: 提出 AffordDrive3D：以 VLM 为骨干捕捉场景语义与驾驶上下文，预测直接影响自车运动的可行驶区域（drivable areas）与碰撞关键区域（collision-critical regions）；同时从 RGB world-model latents 预测未来几何。以 affordance + geometry 双路联合监督训练世界-动作模型。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 基准上取得 SOTA：91.3 PDMS、89.9 EPDMS。消融表明联合建模未来 affordance 与几何对轨迹规划有效。

Conclusion: 将"动作相关区域预测"与"未来几何预测"联合嵌入世界-动作模型，可为规划提供更直接可用的空间-语义线索，是自动驾驶 world-action 建模的有效方向。

Abstract: World-action models have recently improved autonomous driving by jointly learning future scene prediction and trajectory generation. Most existing approaches model the future primarily through RGB appearance, and recent works have begun to incorporate geometric prediction to improve spatial understanding. However, dense geometry describes the spatial layout of the entire scene without indicating which parts are most relevant to the ego vehicle's action. For driving, the model must also identify and anticipate where it can safely move and which regions may pose collision risks. Jointly modeling action-relevant regions and future geometry can provide the policy with both driving-relevant cues and their corresponding spatial structure. We therefore propose AffordDrive3D, an affordance- and geometry-aware world-action model that jointly learns future action-relevant regions and spatial structure. In order to capture the scene semantics and driving context needed for driving affordance prediction, we build AffordDrive3D on a VLM backbone to forecast drivable areas and collision-critical regions that directly affect ego motion, while predicting future geometry from RGB world-model latents. On NAVSIM, AffordDrive3D achieves state-of-the-art performance with 91.3 PDMS and 89.9 EPDMS, demonstrating the effectiveness of jointly modeling future affordances and geometry for trajectory planning.

</details>


### [27] [Diffusion Meta-Prompting and Steering for Generalizable Foundation Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11067)
*Deepak Sridhar,Yi Li,Kartikeya Bhardwaj,Shuangjun Liu,Taotao Jing,Yuan Li,Shuai Zhang,Jiancheng Lyu,Dashan Gao,Nuno Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DMP 将已学习的 prompt 集合视为数据集，用扩散模型建模其分布，并在给定自然语言任务描述时采样合成新 prompt，配合测试时锚定引导策略实现跨类、跨域与组合任务泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 prompt learning 学到的提示是任务特定的，无法泛化到新类别、新领域或任务组合；且任务专属 prompt 的检索/存储成本高。作者希望在完全不接触原始任务样本和任务损失的前提下，复用已有 prompt 仓库，按任务描述合成新 prompt。

Method: 提出 Diffusion Meta-Prompt（DMP）：在已有 prompt 仓库上训练扩散模型以建模 prompt 分布，训练与采样均不需要原始任务样本或任务损失，仅以自然语言任务描述为条件生成新 prompt；引入测试时引导（test-time steering）策略，用仓库中训练期选出的最优 prompt 作为扩散采样过程的隐空间锚点，无需重训 DMP 或访问测试类；理论上给出采样 prompt 期望任务损失的界。

Result: 在分类、检索与文本到图像生成任务上提升泛化；无需显式训练即支持概念组合与负向提示；相比 prompt 检索方法降低超过 90% 的存储与推理开销；复合分类在 55 组数据集上平均提升最高约 2.0%，特定组合（如 Eurosat 与 Flowers）最高提升 8.5%；层次分类的跨任务泛化提升约 2–9%；并给出期望任务损失的理论界。代码已开源。

Conclusion: DMP 说明 prompt 本身可作为可生成的数据分布来建模，从而在无原始任务数据条件下实现可迁移、可组合、低成本的提示合成，为跨任务泛化与 prompt 复用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Prompt learning is a popular method for adapting foundation models, but learned prompts are typically task-specific and fail to generalize to new classes, domains, or compositions of tasks. In this paper, we introduce a Diffusion Meta-Prompt (DMP) model , a framework that models the distribution of learned prompts using diffusion models. Given a repository of previously learned prompts, DMP is trained and sampled without access to the original task examples or task losses, and synthesizes new prompts conditioned on natural language task descriptions. To improve the sampling stability, we introduce a test-time steering strategy for DMP, which uses the best training-selected prompt in the repository as a latent anchor during diffusion sampling, without retraining the DMP or accessing test classes. DMP improves generalization across classification, retrieval and text-to-image generation tasks, supports concept composition and negative prompting without explicit training. It reduces storage and inference costs by over 90% compared to prompt retrieval methods. For composite classification, DMP achieves upto 2.0% average gain over prior meta-learning methods across 55 pairs of datasets with gains as high as 8.5% on specific pairs such as Eurosat and Flowers. DMP also enhances cross-task generalization with ~2-9% improvement for hierarchical classification task. We further provide a theoretical guarantee bounding the expected task loss of prompts sampled from a DMP. Code is available: this https URL

</details>


### [28] [No Distillation Needed: Single-Pass Real-Time Talking Heads via Acausal Noise Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11070)
*Yu Han,Dejan Markovic,Alexander Richard,Wojciech Zielonka,Akshay Venkatesh,Cheng-hsin Wuu,Michael Zollhoefer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FaceGAN：用单次前向的因果GAN替代扩散模型做流式音频驱动面部动画，通过"非因果噪声路径"绕开噪声谱抑制与逐步创新崩塌的矛盾，实时逐帧输出表情与头姿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频驱动的面部动画要满足在线流式需求（每帧由当前已观测音频即时生成、交互速率）。扩散模型每样本需多次网络评估，不适配流式；作者主张该领域音频条件面部运动位于较低维流形，容量不是瓶颈，单次生成即可，真正障碍是随机结构：因果、时不变的i.i.d.噪声驱动生成器无法在抑制输出频带的同时避免逐步创新崩塌。

Method: 从理论上说明因果时不变i.i.d.噪声生成器存在频谱抑制与逐步创新（per-step innovation）崩塌的权衡矛盾；提出FaceGAN：音频到表情的路径保持因果，但以"非因果方式"塑造噪声通路——由于驱动噪声是合成的，其"未来"噪声可在当前时刻预先采样，从而在不破坏因果性的前提下重构噪声结构。单次前向每帧同时输出表情与头姿，采用有界注意力窗口的纯前馈结构。

Result: 生成质量达到或超过当前最好方法；单次前向实时逐帧推理；因前馈+有界注意力窗口，可无限时长生成而不出现漂移。

Conclusion: 在音频驱动面部动画这类低维流形任务中，扩散的多次评估并非必要；通过非因果噪声路径设计，单次生成GAN兼具质量、流式实时性与长时稳定性，为实时虚拟人/化身系统提供可行方案。

Abstract: Audio-driven facial animation underpins real-time avatars, telepresence, and embodied virtual agents. And it must run online: each frame emitted from audio observed up to the current time, at interactive rates. Recent progress is dominated by diffusion models, which need many network evaluations per sample and are therefore a poor fit for streaming. We argue the cost is unnecessary in this domain. Audio-conditioned facial motion occupies a comparatively low-dimensional manifold, a regime where a single-pass GAN suffices. The obstacle is not capacity but stochastic structure. We show that a causal, time-invariant generator driven by i.i.d. noise cannot suppress its output spectrum over a band without collapsing its per-step innovation. We proposed FaceGAN, which dissolved the limitation by shaping the noise pathway acausally. Because the driving noise is synthetic, its future can be sampled now, so the audio-to-expression path stays causal, and the model supports fully causal operation. FaceGAN emits expression and head pose in a single forward pass per frame and matches or outperforms state-of-art approaches in generation quality. Being feed-forward with bounded attention windows, it generates indefinitely without drift.

</details>


### [29] [Contrast Enhancement or Noise Reduction? On Improving Cervical Cancer Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11086)
*Ach Khozaimi,Ulfatun Nahdhiyah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究系统比较了图像预处理算法（PMD降噪、CLAHE对比度增强）对三种CNN架构（ResNet-34、MobileNet-V2、DenseNet-121）在SIPaKMeD宫颈细胞涂片分类任务上的性能影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宫颈癌是全球主要致死原因之一，深度学习在医学图像分类中表现良好，但图像预处理算法对分类性能的影响在文献中研究不足。本文旨在系统评估预处理对CNN分类性能的影响。

Method: 基于SIPaKMeD数据集，训练并评估三种CNN架构（ResNet-34、MobileNet-V2、DenseNet-121）。应用两种预处理算法：PMD滤波器用于降噪，CLAHE用于对比度增强。使用混淆矩阵评估模型性能。

Result: 预处理提升了所有模型的分类性能。CLAHE显著提升ResNet-34准确率从76.73%到84.16%，DenseNet-121从76.73%到84.16%。PMD滤波器改进有限，且略微降低MobileNet-V2性能。

Conclusion: 对比度增强在提升CNN性能方面比降噪更有效。研究提供了改进宫颈癌分类的新流程，但属于医学影像领域的应用型研究，未涉及具身智能、机器人或3D重建等核心方向。

Abstract: Purpose: Cervical cancer is one of the leading causes of mortality worldwide. Deep learning has shown promising performance in medical image classification. The influence of image preprocessing algorithms on classification performance remains insufficiently investigated in the literature. This research aims to evaluate the impact of image preprocessing algorithms on the performance of CNNs for Pap smear image classification. Methods: Three CNN architectures (ResNet-34, MobileNet-V2, and DenseNet-121) were trained and evaluated using the SIPaKMeD dataset. Two preprocessing algorithms were applied: the PMD filter for noise reduction and CLAHE for contrast enhancement. The model performance was assessed using a confusion matrix. Results: Preprocessing improved the classification performance of all models. CLAHE significantly increased the accuracy of ResNet-34 from 76.73% to 84.16% and DenseNet-121 from 76.73% to 84.16%. The PMD filter yielded limited improvement and slightly reduced the MobileNet-V2 performance. Novelty: This research provides a systematic comparison of contrast enhancement and noise reduction techniques across CNN architectures. This research demonstrates that contrast enhancement is more effective than noise reduction in improving CNN performance. The research provides new pipelines for improving cervical cancer classification.

</details>


### [30] [Continuous Ground-Truth Construction and a Recovery Policy for Air--Water Robotic Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11096)
*Jiangong Xiao,Zhe Sun,Kanzhong Yao,Yuanbo Bi,Haofei Zhao,Ruixuan Hu,Guan Huang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出气-水跨介质视觉跟踪基准与跨介质恢复策略CMRP，用动捕同步标注解决视觉失明期间的真值问题，并通过置信度触发模板选择在不重训主干的情况下提升MixFormerV2性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨气-水界面的目标跟踪受水花、气泡、折射、反射和突变外观变化干扰，观测会阶段性失效。此场景带来两个耦合难题：图像标注无法准确反映视觉失明期目标的真实物理位置；在线跟踪中损坏的观测会污染运动估计与外观模板。

Method: 1）构建评估用跨介质测试集：相机帧与动捕位姿同步，投影已知目标几何，用介质门控残差校正水下投影，并经人工复核，得到22,346帧评估集。2）提出Cross-Medium Recovery Policy（CMRP），以置信度触发模板选择为核心，向MixFormerV2提供固定初始模板、窗口最优预触发模板、触发帧卡尔曼引导图像裁剪及其权重，无需重训视觉主干。

Result: 精度评估中CMRP达到49.90 Macro Success AUC，比MixFormerV2 Official高2.95点。在选定的跨介质过渡与遮挡恢复区间，CMRP将跟踪覆盖率从47.91%提升至50.43%，成功恢复视频的平均丢失到恢复延迟由55.3帧降至49.3帧。

Conclusion: 通过动捕驱动的标注管线提供可靠跨介质真值，并以置信度触发模板选择策略，可在不重训主干的情况下缓解跨介质观测污染，提升跟踪精度与恢复效率。

Abstract: Visual tracking across the air-water interface is challenged by splashes, bubbles, refraction, reflections, and abrupt appearance changes that can temporarily invalidate observations. This setting poses two coupled difficulties: first, for evaluation, image-only annotation cannot reliably describe the target's physical location during visual blindness; second, for online tracking, corrupted observations can contaminate motion estimates and appearance templates. We address the first difficulty with a construction pipeline that synchronizes camera frames with motion-capture poses, projects known target geometry, corrects underwater projection with a medium-gated residual, and subjects the annotations to manual review. This yields an evaluation-only cross-medium test set of 22,346 frames. We further introduce a Cross-Medium Recovery Policy (CMRP) centered on confidence-triggered template selection. It supplies MixFormerV2 with the fixed initial template, a window-best pre-trigger template, and a trigger-frame Kalman-guided image crop, together with their associated weights, without retraining the visual backbone. In the accuracy evaluation, CMRP achieves 49.90 Macro Success AUC, 2.95 points above MixFormerV2 Official. On selected cross-medium transition and occlusion-recovery intervals, CMRP increases MixFormerV2 tracking coverage from 47.91\% to 50.43\% relative to Official updating, while mean loss-to-recovery latency over successfully recovered videos decreases from 55.3 to 49.3 frames.

</details>


### [31] [Skeleton-Guided Progressive Test-Time Adaptation for Thin Curvilinear Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11104)
*Boa Jang,JunGyu Lee,Gwanho Lee,Jinwook Choi,Young-Gon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SGP-TTA 提出骨架引导的渐进式测试时自适应：ProgBN 按样本计数将归一化统计从源域渐进迁移到目标域，CSR 从多视角对齐预测中提取结构目标并仅更新 BN 仿射参数，从而在跨模态域偏移下保住细长曲线结构的拓扑连通性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细长曲线结构（血管、道路）的像素级微小误差即可破坏全局拓扑，域偏移尤其跨模态时极易导致灾难性断裂。现有 TTA 方法只调整特征统计量与置信度，均不直接约束连通性，因而在极端跨模态差距下退化。

Method: 提出 SGP-TTA，含两个组件：(1) Progressive Batch Normalization (ProgBN)，按样本数调度表将归一化统计由冻结的源域统计向当前目标域估计值渐进平移，使源-目标权衡随自适应阶段变化而非固定系数；(2) Consensus Skeleton Recall (CSR)，从几何对齐的多视角预测中导出结构目标（骨架），仅更新 BN 仿射参数以保留连通结构。

Result: 在多项实验中，SGP-TTA 在拓扑连通性指标上持续优于现有 TTA 方法，且在跨模态偏移场景下提升幅度最大。用户未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 以结构（骨架）连通性而非单纯特征统计为约束、并以渐进方式平衡源-目标统计的 TTA 范式，对跨模态细长结构分割更有效，为无源域自适应提供了新的结构先验思路。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of thin curvilinear structures is vital for various real-world applications, from vessel analysis to road extraction. Yet their intricate geometry makes even minor pixel-wise errors enough to break the global topology, and this structural fragility turns severe domain shifts into catastrophic failures. The difficulty is most acute under cross-modality gaps, where the imaging process itself differs fundamentally between source and target. While test-time adaptation (TTA) offers a practical source-free remedy, existing methods adapt feature statistics and confidence, neither of which constrains connectivity, and thus degrade under such extreme gaps. To address this, we propose Skeleton-Guided Progressive Test-Time Adaptation (SGP-TTA). Progressive Batch Normalization (ProgBN) shifts normalization from frozen source statistics toward current target estimates under a sample-count schedule, so that the source-target balance follows the stage of adaptation rather than a fixed coefficient. Consensus Skeleton Recall (CSR) then derives a structural target from geometrically aligned multi-view predictions and updates only the BN affine parameters to preserve connected structures. Extensive experiments show that SGP-TTA consistently outperforms existing TTA methods in topological connectivity, with the largest margins under cross-modality shift. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [32] [TKCAM: Text and Keyframe to Camera Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11105)
*Haozhe Yang,Zhiyang Dou,Zekai Gu,Cheng Lin,Wenping Wang,Yuan Liu,Taku Komura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TKCAM，首个基于生成式掩码建模的文本+关键帧条件相机运动合成框架，用12维运动学特征经RVQ离散为层级运动token，两阶段掩码Transformer重建与精修，并发布RealEstate10K-Cap数据集与跨域通用CLaTr评测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助摄影、视频合成与3D场景理解需要高质量、可控的相机运动生成；现有方法要么仅支持文本条件、缺乏时序局部视觉引导，要么缺乏统一评测标准与跨域泛化能力。

Method: 1) 用12维运动学特征（位置、速度、连续旋转表示）表征相机动态，经残差向量量化RVQ离散为层级运动token；2) 两阶段掩码Transformer架构，通过显式自注意力与交叉注意力模块实现多模态条件建模；3) 支持稀疏视觉关键帧条件：用户给出自由文本提示与若干时间戳的RGB观测，提供时序局部化视觉引导以生成连贯的中间轨迹；4) 构建RealEstate10K-Cap大规模文本-相机数据集及跨域基准与通用CLaTr评测器。

Result: 在FID（Fréchet距离）、文本-运动匹配分数与检索指标R@K上超过近期SOTA基线；额外分析评估了时序平滑性与跨域泛化能力，代码已开源。

Conclusion: TKCAM以生成式掩码建模统一了文本与稀疏关键帧多模态条件下的相机轨迹合成，并通过新数据集与评测器推进了该任务的评测标准，具备良好泛化性。

Abstract: Generating high-quality and controllable camera motion is essential for AI-assisted cinematography, video synthesis, and 3D scene understanding. We introduce TKCAM, a Text- and Keyframe-conditioned CAMera-motion synthesis framework based on generative masked modeling. We represent camera dynamics using a 12-dimensional kinematic feature comprising position, velocity, and a continuous rotation representation and discretize them into hierarchical motion tokens via a Residual Vector Quantizer (RVQ). A two-stage masked transformer architecture then learns to reconstruct and refine these tokens, utilizing explicit self- and cross-attention modules for multimodal conditioning. A central feature of our framework is sparse visual keyframe conditioning: users can provide free-form text prompts together with RGB observations at selected timestamps, which provide temporally localized visual guidance for generating coherent in-between trajectories. Furthermore, to advance evaluation standards, we curate RealEstate10K-Cap, a large-scale text-camera dataset, and establish a cross-domain benchmark with a Universal CLaTr Evaluator. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TKCAM surpasses recent state-of-the-art baselines on Fréchet distance (FID), text-motion matching scores, and retrieval metrics (R@K), while additional analyses evaluate temporal smoothness and cross-domain generalization. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [33] [DiscoVL: Unveiling Disentangled C ross-Modal Representation Learning via Orthogonal Adversarial Regularization for V ision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11113)
*Mengping Dong,Jinbao Li,Fei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiscoVL：解耦跨模态表示学习框架，结合正交对抗正则与结构化跨模态对齐，用多分支低秩残差对齐器实现双向跨模态反馈，提升视觉语言模型基类到新类泛化、跨数据集与少样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉语言模型泛化强，但高效适配下游新任务时，现有提示学习方法表示不一致、未考虑语义分布偏移，导致泛化下降。

Method: 提出解耦跨模态表示学习框架：1）多分支低秩残差对齐器将表示分解到子空间，逐层实现视觉与文本流双向跨模态反馈以增强交互；2）针对传统三元组约束过拟合类质心的问题，设计正交正则化的对抗三元组损失，防止质心坍缩并提升泛化。

Result: 在15个基准上，基类到新类泛化、跨数据集评估和少样本学习均持续优于现有最优方法。

Conclusion: 通过解耦跨模态对齐与正交对抗正则，可在参数高效适配下兼顾表示一致性与泛化能力，为视觉语言模型的稳健下游适配提供新范式。

Abstract: Pre-trained vision-language models excel across varied perception tasks, but adapting them to novel downstream settings without sacrificing generalization remains non-trivial. Existing parameter-efficient prompt learning method often yields inconsistent representations and fails to account for semantic distribution shifts. In this work, we present DiscoVL, a disentangled cross-modal representation learning framework that couples orthogonal adversarial regularization with structured cross-modal alignment for vision-language models. To address the insufficient cross-modal interaction, our DiscoVL designs a multi-branch low-rank residual aligner that decomposes representations into subspaces and enables bidirectional cross-modal feedback between visual and textual streams at each layer. Furthermore, while conventional triplet constraints overfit features to class centroids, we design an orthogonal regularization for adversarial triplet loss, which prevents centroid collapse and substantially boosts generalization. Evaluations on 15 benchmarks demonstrate that DiscoVL delivers consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods for base-to-novel generalization, cross-dataset evaluation, and few-shot learning

</details>


### [34] [MCL: Meta Convolution Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11117)
*Naim Reza,Md Al Amin,Ho Yub Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Meta Convolution Layer (MCL)，从函数视角将卷积核建模为输入条件函数 W(x)，通过高阶多项式展开（嵌套残差块构成多项式元网络）生成单一自适应卷积核，作为插件与标准卷积结合，可嵌入 CNN 与 Transformer 骨干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态卷积将有效核表达为少量基核的线性混合，表征能力受混合核数量限制，且随规模增大优化困难、训练不稳定。作者希望从函数角度重新审视动态卷积，将表征能力与显式核数量解耦。

Method: MCL 以输入为条件直接生成卷积核 W(x)，借助受深度多项式网络启发的嵌套残差块实现结构化多项式元网络，进行高阶多项式展开，输出单一输入自适应核；作为插件与标准卷积并用，兼容 CNN 与 Transformer 骨干。

Result: ImageNet 上 ResNet-18/50/101 的 Top-1 分别提升 6.61%、3.42%、3.05%；在 CIFAR-10/100 上显著提升 ResNet 与 Wide-ResNet 变体精度；在 Swin、ViT 骨干的细粒度视觉分类任务上优于此前方法。

Conclusion: 高阶多项式核生成是线性混合式动态卷积的一种强大且可扩展的替代方案，能解耦表征力与混合核数量并缓解训练不稳定，具备良好通用性与可插拔性。

Abstract: Dynamic convolution enhances convolutional neural networks (CNNs) by adapting kernels to input content, but it expresses the effective kernel as a linear mixture of a small number of basis kernels, which limits expressivity and complicates optimization as the mixture size grows. In this work, we revisit dynamic convolution from a functional perspective and propose the Meta Convolution Layer (MCL), which directly models the convolutional kernel as an input-conditioned function W(x) realized via a high-order polynomial expansion.
