To train Compo, we develop a scalable pipeline that automatically constructs supervision data for different binding types and their combinations, enabling efficient adaptation without training a specialized poster generator from scratch. We further introduce a benchmark that evaluates adherence to individual binding types and their joint composition. Experiments show that Compo achieves stronger compositional controllability than both general-purpose image generation models and dedicated poster generation systems while maintaining high visual quality. By decoupling intent specification from visual generation, our work shifts poster generation from prompting toward composing.

</details>


### [137] [Syn-Omni: Structured Specialization and Progressive Collaboration for Omnimodal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12256)
*Youngtaek Oh,Qiyu Wu,Hiromi Wakaki,Junmo Kim,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Syn-Omni 统一框架，用正交模态专家 LoRA（OME-LoRA）分离共享语义与模态专属表征，并用渐进协同路由（PSR）实现跨模态协作，在 81 个图像/视频/音频/音视频任务上超越现有多模态嵌入基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态/全模态嵌入方法通常在混合模态数据上使用单一共享参数空间，难以在结构上区分通用表征与模态特异表征，导致模态间干扰与专业化不足。

Method: Syn-Omni 框架：1）OME-LoRA 将适配分解为共享 LoRA 路径（通用语义）与各模态专家 LoRA 路径（模态专业化），并施加正交约束以保持结构分离；2）PSR 渐进式协同路由，先让专家建立模态先验，再逐步与其他模态专家交互以实现跨模态协同。

Result: 在覆盖图像、视频、音频与音视频的 81 个任务上，Syn-Omni 一致优于全模态基线，验证了结构化专业化与渐进跨模态协作的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过参数空间的显式结构化（共享+专家）与渐进路由，可在不牺牲通用性的前提下提升模态专业化与跨模态协同，为全模态嵌入适配提供通用范式。

Abstract: Omnimodal embeddings naturally involve both shared representations and modality-specific features across heterogeneous inputs. However, existing omnimodal embedding methods often rely on a single shared parameter space over mixed-modality data, limiting structural separation between universal and modality-specific representations. To address this, we propose Syn-Omni, a unified framework for structured omnimodal adaptation with modality specialization and controlled cross-modal collaboration. Specifically, we introduce Orthogonal Modality-Expert LoRA (OME-LoRA), which decomposes adaptation into a shared LoRA path for universal semantics and modality-expert LoRA paths for modality-aware specialization. Furthermore, Progressive Synergy Routing (PSR) enables experts to first establish modality-specific priors, then gradually interact with other modality-experts for cross-modal synergy. Evaluated across 81 diverse tasks spanning image, video, audio, and audiovisual modalities, Syn-Omni consistently outperforms omnimodal baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of structured specialization and cross-modal progressive collaboration.

</details>


### [138] [DVD: Dynamic Vector Decoding for Efficient MLLM-based Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12266)
*Jinghua Hou,Zhe Liu,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出动态向量解码方法DVD，将2D框、2D掩码、3D框统一转为1D向量序列并映射为紧凑离散token，配合轻量de-tokenizer与MLLM无缝对接，在RefCOCO、SUN-RGBD、KITTI、Hypersim、nuScenes上提升2D/3D感知性能并显著降低token开销与推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM感知方法依赖文本坐标表示，token开销过大；或采用固定范围量化，受量程与精度限制，难以适应3D场景中无界空间范围与高定位精度需求。需要一种统一、高效且通用的2D/3D感知表示方法。

Method: 将2D边界框、2D掩码、3D边界框等异构感知表示统一转换为1D向量序列，再在高维空间中映射为紧凑离散token；设计轻量de-tokenizer，将MLLM输出token解码回原始2D/3D感知表示，实现与MLLM的即插即用集成。属于动态向量量化式解码，兼顾量程与精度。

Result: 在RefCOCO系列、SUN-RGBD、KITTI、Hypersim、nuScenes等2D与3D感知基准上，DVD在2D和3D任务上取得更优性能，同时显著降低token开销和推理延迟。

Conclusion: DVD为MLLM融入感知能力提供了一个高效、通用的框架，克服了文本坐标表示与固定范围量化方法的固有局限，可统一支撑2D与3D感知任务。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have made remarkable progress in bridging vision and language, facilitating various perception tasks essential for human-machine interaction, robotics, and autonomous driving. However, existing MLLM-based perception methods predominantly rely on text-based coordinate representation, which suffers from excessive token overhead, or fixed-range quantization, which suffers from range and precision constraints, especially for 3D domains with unbounded spatial range and high localization accuracy requirements. To address these challenges, we propose a dynamic vector decoding method named DVD, which unifies the representation of 2D and 3D perception tasks. Specifically, we first transform diverse perceptual representation (i.e., 2D bounding boxes, 2D masks, and 3D bounding boxes) into 1D vector sequences, which are then mapped to compact discrete tokens in the high-dimensional space. Then, a lightweight de-tokenizer enables seamless integration with MLLMs by decoding output tokens back to original 2D and 3D perceptual representations. Extensive experiments on 2D and 3D perception benchmarks including RefCOCO series, SUN-RGBD, KITTI, Hypersim, nuScenes demonstrate that DVD achieves superior performance in 2D and 3D tasks and reduces significantly the token overhead and inference latency. DVD provides an efficient and general framework for integrating perception capabilities into MLLMs, overcoming the inherent limitations of existing methods.

</details>


### [139] [Slot3R: Set-Associative Spatial Memory for Streaming 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12282)
*Xiyuan Zhang,Yanming Yang,Kaiyuan Xu,Ruibo Li,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Slot3R 提出免训练、集合关联式的空间记忆改造，用槽位并存多状态代替 Point3R 的邻近融合，大幅降低点云误差与轨迹误差，并解决显存溢出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式 3D 重建需在线处理不断扩张的场景并保留每帧证据。Point3R 用空间邻近性既做关联又决定是否融合，把“共址”混同于“同一状态”：邻近指针可能编码不同表面、视角或可见性条件，平均后会破坏互补证据，导致后续帧无法消歧。

Method: 核心原则是位置决定“地址”而非是否合并。Slot3R 作为免训练、集合关联（set-associative）的改造方案，在共享地址上允许同一内存槽位并存多个状态，Point3R 预训练骨干保持冻结；并用有界稀疏读出将持久存储与逐帧解码器访问解耦。

Result: 在 300-500 采样帧下，7Scenes 点云 Acc 误差降低 57.1%-63.1%，NeuralRGBD 降低 64.0%-72.0%；三个位姿基准上 Sim(3) 对齐 ATE 全部下降；视频深度估计保持竞争力。600-1000 帧全部设置约 19 FPS 完成，而 Point3R 与 InfiniteVGGT 在 800 帧及以上显存溢出。

Conclusion: 将“地址分配”与“状态合并”解耦，可显著提升流式 3D 重建的记忆保真度与长序列可扩展性，且无需重新训练骨干。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction must preserve evidence from each frame while processing an expanding scene online. Spatial memory is a natural fit because it organizes history by reconstructed 3D location. Yet Point3R uses spatial proximity both to associate a new observation with an existing memory entry and to decide whether to fuse it, conflating co-location with state identity. Because pointers summarize image patches, nearby pointers may encode distinct surfaces, viewpoints, or visibility conditions; averaging them can destroy complementary evidence before later frames disambiguate it. We argue that location should determine address, not whether observations must merge. Slot3R realizes this principle as a training-free, set-associative retrofit that lets multiple states coexist at a shared address while keeping the pretrained Point3R backbone frozen. A bounded sparse readout further decouples persistent storage from per-frame decoder access. At 300-500 sampled frames, Slot3R reduces Point3R's point-cloud accuracy error (Acc) by 57.1%-63.1% on 7Scenes and 64.0%-72.0% on NeuralRGBD, lowers Sim(3)-aligned absolute trajectory error (ATE) on all three pose benchmarks, and remains competitive on video-depth estimation. It completes all evaluated settings from 600 to 1000 sampled frames at about 19 FPS under the same protocol, whereas Point3R and InfiniteVGGT run out of memory at 800 frames and beyond.

</details>


### [140] [Multi-Agent Egocentric World Model with Fine-Grained Embodied Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12299)
*Dahyun Chung,Siyoon Jin,Hyunwook Choi,Honggyu An,Junyoung Seo,Hyunsung Kim,Seung Wook Kim,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ME-World，将多智能体自我中心世界模型建模为共享环境中多路 ego 视频流的同步生成，通过共享 token 序列联合去噪、跨视角姿态条件与共享环境记忆实现跨视角动作一致性与交互状态传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我中心世界模型多为单智能体，多智能体工作仅使用粗略动作（运动、相机控制、离散指令），缺乏细粒度具身交互建模；真实具身场景中多智能体在共享环境中交互，需要跨视角动作一致性、共享环境一致性与交互引起状态更新的传播。

Method: ME-World 在单一共享 token 序列中联合去噪多条 ego 流；每条流以所有智能体的目标视角姿态为条件；并以共享环境记忆作为生成基础，从而建模跨视角与交互一致性。

Result: 在真实与合成多智能体数据上训练评估，并提出共享世界一致性指标（环境、更新、身份一致性）；实验显示在共享世界一致性、动作控制、身份保持与视频质量上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: ME-World 将多智能体细粒度交互的自我中心世界建模统一为同步 ego 流生成，验证了联合去噪与共享环境记忆在一致性上的有效性，为该方向提供了新任务形式与评测指标。

Abstract: Egocentric world models predict first-person observations conditioned on an agent's actions, but most focus on a single agent. Real embodied settings often involve multiple agents that act and interact within a shared environment. Existing multi-agent world models rely on coarse actions like locomotion, camera control, or discrete commands, leaving fine-grained embodied interactions underexplored. We formulate multi-agent egocentric world modeling as synchronized ego-stream generation for multiple agents interacting through fine-grained actions in a shared world. This requires cross-view action consistency, shared-environment consistency, and consistent propagation of interaction-induced state updates. We propose Multi-agent Egocentric World Model (ME-World), which jointly denoises multiple ego streams in a shared token sequence, conditions each stream on all agents' target-view poses, and grounds generation with shared environment memory. We train and evaluate on real and synthetic multi-agent data and introduce shared-world consistency metrics for environment, update, and identity consistency. Experiments show ME-World improves shared-world consistency, action control, identity preservation, and video quality over existing methods.

</details>


### [141] [From What to Which: Decoding Modifier Grounding in Frozen MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12305)
*Barbara Toniella Corradini,Caterina Gallegati,Ludovica Genovese,Vittorio Murino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OTTER，一种基于冻结MLLM表征的轻量级监督探针，用最优传输对齐生成token与图像区域，验证修饰词token（属性、空间、关系/动作）中是否含有可解码的实例级指代定位信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉定位方法多聚焦"图里有什么"（名词实体），忽略"指哪一个实例"的修饰性token（颜色、方位、关系）。作者想探究冻结MLLM表征中是否蕴含跨生成token的实例判别性定位信息。

Method: OTTER：在冻结MLLM表征上训练轻量监督探针，用最优传输（Optimal Transport）将生成token与视觉区域对齐，产出紧凑的定位图（grounding map），并测试属性/空间/关系词等修饰token的可解码性、上下文扰动鲁棒性及跨数据集迁移。

Result: (i) 修饰词token可解码出实例判别性视觉信息，空间词证据最清晰；(ii) 该信息不局限于修饰词，上下文相关的物体名词也含指代信息；(iii) 定位在上下文扰动下仍具信息量且所选区域与生成相关；(iv) OT定位可扩展到自由生成与跨数据集迁移。

Conclusion: 冻结MLLM表征中含跨token的实例级指代定位信息，OT对齐探针可有效提取并泛化，为细粒度多模态定位提供新视角。

Abstract: As Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can describe increasingly complex visual scenes, token-level grounding becomes crucial. Yet, when an MLLM generates "the yellow banana on the left", established grounding approaches focus on what is in the image ("banana"), overlooking tokens that help describe which instance is meant ("yellow", "left"). In this work, we ask whether frozen MLLM representations contain decodable grounding information about the referred instance across generated tokens, extending to modifiers such as attributes, spatial expressions, and relational/action terms. To address this question, we introduce OTTER, a lightweight supervised probe over frozen MLLM representations that uses Optimal Transport (OT) to align generated tokens with visual regions and produce compact grounding maps. Our results show that (i) instance-discriminative visual information can be decoded from modifier tokens, with the clearest evidence for spatial terms, but (ii) is not confined to them, as contextualized object nouns also carry referential information; (iii) the recovered grounding remains informative under context perturbations, while selected regions remain relevant to generation; and (iv) the learned OT-based grounding extends beyond the controlled setting to free generation and cross-dataset transfer.

</details>


### [142] [BudgetPix: Compute-Adaptive Tokenization for Pixel-Space Image Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12307)
*Ozgur Kara,Yujia Chen,Daniel Watson,David Forsyth,James Matthew Rehg,Wen-Sheng Chu,Du Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BudgetPix提出自适应tokenization框架，用熵引导四叉树按视觉复杂度与空间布局动态分配计算量，使单一像素空间扩散模型checkpoint可在推理时按多种计算预算运行，在文生图与类别条件生成上实现更优质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成模型采用均匀tokenization，对等大图像块分配相同计算预算，无法适应推理时的资源约束，且在简单背景与复杂细节上投入相同算力，导致质量-成本折中次优。作者希望实现推理时可调的计算预算。

Method: BudgetPix含三部分：(1) 自适应编码器，结合熵引导四叉树与多尺度patch embedder，将固定尺寸图像映射为可变长度token序列；(2) 尺度感知解码器，从多尺度token集合重建固定分辨率图像；(3) 灵活的训练与采样调度，使像素空间去噪器可在不同token数量下工作。该框架可与现有像素空间扩散架构无缝集成，单一checkpoint支持大范围计算预算。

Result: 在文本到图像生成上，BudgetPix以仅25%的原始计算预算在512²匹配MiniT2I-L、在1024²匹配PixelDiT的保真度；在MeanFlow骨干的类别条件生成中，仅需60%完整预算即可获得近乎无质量退化的图像，FID仅上升0.8点。人类与VLM评审确认其质量-效率权衡显著优于既有预算自适应基线。

Conclusion: 自适应tokenization可在推理时灵活分配计算预算并提升质量-效率折中，且能与现有像素空间扩散模型兼容，单一checkpoint适配多种预算设置。

Abstract: Most image generation models rely on uniform tokenization, allocating the exact same computational budget to equally-sized image patches. This static paradigm cannot adapt to different resource constraints at inference time, and yields suboptimal quality-cost tradeoff by devoting the same effort to both plain backgrounds and intricate details. We propose BudgetPix, an adaptive tokenization framework that dynamically allocates compute based on visual complexity and spatial layout, enabling flexible computational budgeting at inference time. BudgetPix comprises three key components: (1) an adaptive encoder that maps a fixed-size image to a variable-length token sequence using an entropy-guided quadtree alongside a multi-scale patch embedder; (2) a scale-aware decoder reconstructs fixed-resolution images from multi-scale token sets; and (3) a flexible training and sampling schedule that enables pixel-space denoisers to operate across variable token counts. BudgetPix seamlessly integrates with existing pixel-space diffusion architectures, enabling a single checkpoint to be operated at a wide range of compute budgets. Evaluated on text-to-image generation, BudgetPix matches the fidelity of MiniT2I-L at $512^2$ and PixelDiT at $1024^2$ using just 25% of the original compute budget. In class-conditional generation using a MeanFlow backbone, BudgetPix requires merely 60% of the full compute budget to produce images with near-zero quality degradation, observing a marginal 0.8-point increase in FID. Comprehensive assessments by human and VLM judges confirm that BudgetPix establishes a significantly improved quality-efficiency tradeoff over prior budget-adaptive baselines. More details are available at our project page: this https URL

</details>


### [143] [Is In-Domain Training Enough for Fine-Grained Industrial Anomaly Understanding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12310)
*Xingwu Zhang,Duanyang Du,Huiling Zhu,Jiayue Dai,Yixiao Liu,Guozhi Liu,Zhihan Zhang,Zijun Long*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SiGMA：多智能体框架，将异构MLLM智能体与专用视觉缺陷专家分工协作，用于多模态工业异常理解，性能接近人类专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一MLLM难以同时胜任多模态工业异常理解中的检测、定位、描述与推理任务；领域内训练无法弥补这一差距，微调专家在缺陷定位上最高仅75.5%，远低于人类专家的92.3%，且异常检测准确率甚至低于其未训练的基座模型；不同MLLM能力互补但共有细粒度感知短板，简单组合无效。

Method: 提出SiGMA空间接地多智能体框架：多模态检索器提供工业知识与正常参考样本；专用视觉缺陷专家将查询-参考对比转化为校准后的异常证据；无标签可靠性控制器依据任务级能力与查询级证据质量对各来源加权融合。

Result: 在MMAD基准上平均准确率达85.2%，比最强训练专家及Gemini-2.5-Pro高4.0%，距人类专家仅差1.5%；仅用三个不超过9B参数的智能体即达84.4%；新MLLM无需重训练即可接入。

Conclusion: 通过异构智能体分工与视觉专家分工协作、配合无标签可靠性加权，可在不重训练的前提下显著提升多模态工业异常理解性能并接近人类水平。

Abstract: A single multimodal large language model (MLLM) struggles to excel simultaneously at detection, localization, description, and reasoning in multimodal industrial anomaly understanding (MM-IAU). We show that in-domain training does not close this gap. On MMAD, a widely adopted MM-IAU benchmark, trained specialists reach at most 75.5% accuracy in defect localization, against 92.3% for human experts, and even detect anomalies less accurately than their untrained base model. Meanwhile, different MLLMs offer complementary strengths but share this weakness in fine-grained perception, so combining them alone cannot remove it. We therefore propose SiGMA, a spatially grounded multi-agent framework that divides labor between heterogeneous MLLM agents and a dedicated visual defect expert. A multimodal searcher supplies industrial knowledge and normal references, the defect expert turns query-reference comparison into calibrated anomaly evidence, and a label-free reliability controller weighs each source by task-wise competence and query-level evidence quality. SiGMA reaches 85.2% average accuracy on MMAD, 4.0% above the strongest trained specialist and Gemini-2.5-Pro and within 1.5% of human experts. Even with three agents of at most 9B parameters, it reaches 84.4%, and new MLLMs join without retraining.

</details>


### [144] [Controllable Exaggeration for Generative Motion Models via Training-Time Adaptation and Inference-Time Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12316)
*Amirhossein Zamani,Arianna Rampini,Bruno Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将动画的"夸张（Exaggeration）"原则引入文本到动作生成流程：训练阶段用自建夸张动作数据集微调预训练模型，推理阶段基于动态运动基元（DMPs）提出夸张的数学表达，并将其作为引导信号，无需再训练即可引导扩散与流匹配模型生成更夸张的动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作生成模型虽能合成物理合理的角色动作，却忽视了专业动画师用于设计动作的动画原则，导致动作缺少表现力与生命力，难以满足角色动画创作需求。

Method: 两阶段框架：阶段一在训练时对预训练文本到动作模型在自建夸张数据集上做监督微调；阶段二在推理时给出基于DMP的夸张数学形式，并以此作为引导信号，作用于扩散与流匹配生成模型，实现免训练引导。

Result: 与三个强基线动作生成模型进行定性、定量对比，生成的动作更具夸张性与表现力，同时保持中性参考动作意图与物理合理性。

Conclusion: 将动画原则（夸张）显式建模并嵌入生成流程，是连接动作生成与角色动画实践的有效路径，兼顾物理合理性与艺术表现力。

Abstract: Recent motion generative models have demonstrated strong capabilities in synthesizing physically plausible character motion, but often overlook established animation principles used by professional animators to ground and design their animation work. Understanding and incorporating these principles into motion generative pipelines is essential for producing motions that serve not only physically grounded applications but also the needs of the character animation community. This enables the creation of characters that not only move in physically plausible ways but also feel alive, expressive, and engaging. To close this gap, we focus on the Exaggeration principle of animation and investigate how it can be incorporated into modern motion generative pipelines to produce more expressive character motions. To this end, we introduce a framework that operates at two stages of existing motion generative pipelines. The first stage introduces exaggeration during training, where we perform supervised fine-tuning of pre-trained text-to-motion models on our curated exaggeration dataset. The second stage operates at inference time, where we: (i) introduce a mathematical formulation of exaggeration based on dynamic movement primitives (DMPs); and (ii) leverage this formulation as an exaggeration guidance signal to guide existing diffusion and flow-matching text-to-motion generation models toward exaggerated motion without additional training. Through qualitative and quantitative evaluations against three strong motion generation models, we show that our methods generate more exaggerated and expressive motions while preserving neutral reference motion intent and physical plausibility.

</details>


### [145] [RiCo: Neural Simulation of Rigid-Body Interactions via Local Contact Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12333)
*Ruixiang Ouyang,Guanren Qiao,Fansen Meng,Yueci Deng,Ruixing Jin,Kui Jia,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RiCo 提出以稀疏接触表面点邻域表示刚体交互，将跨物体推理限制在近邻表面，同时在同一刚体内传播接触信息，在 MOVi 基准上显著降低长时预测误差并保持高接触保真度，还展示了零样本扩展与初步 sim-to-real 迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体动力学建模（尤其端到端世界模型）在整场景或整物体层面预测交互，但刚体接触本质是局部的：只有邻近表面能直接交换接触力。全文/全物体建模既引入冗余计算，又难以刻画决定运动结果的细粒度局部接触，导致长时位置与姿态误差大、接触保真度低。

Method: 提出 Rigid-body Contact Reasoning (RiCo)：以物体表面稀疏点集为核心表示，为每个表面点编码其自身状态以及邻近表面的相对几何、相对运动与物理属性（如材质/摩擦等），在近邻表面间做跨物体接触推理以确定局部接触力，再在单个刚体内部传播这些接触信息以推断整体运动。通过"跨物体仅近邻、刚体内全传播"的分解，避免对所有场景点对建模，兼顾精细接触细节与计算可行性。

Result: 在 MOVi 基准上，相比基线，100 帧预测的位置误差降低 31–35%、朝向误差降低约 38%；接触保真度方面，相对真值的穿透时间差为 11.0%、平均穿透深度差为 2.22 mm。模型可从少量物体的小规模训练场景零样本泛化到含 270 个物体的场景；真实世界多球碰撞实验提供了初步的 sim-to-real 迁移证据。

Conclusion: 刚体交互建模应显式利用接触的局部性：以稀疏表面点近邻表征接触、并在刚体内传播，可在长时段预测精度与接触物理保真度上同时获益，且具备良好的规模泛化与初步实机迁移潜力。局限在于实机验证尚属初步，接触保真度指标仍以与真值差异衡量。

Abstract: Accurate simulation of rigid-body interactions is essential for predictive physical world models. Despite recent progress in modeling object dynamics, capturing how local contacts between surfaces shape object motion remains challenging. While end-to-end world models predict interactions across entire scenes or objects, in practice, rigid-body contact is inherently local, and only nearby surfaces can directly exchange contact forces. Motivated by this observation, we introduce Rigid-body Contact Reasoning (RiCo), which represents interactions between objects through sparse neighborhoods of contact surface points. RiCo combines each point's state with the relative geometry, motion, and physical properties of nearby surfaces, then reasons across the object's points to determine how these local contacts jointly affect its motion. By confining cross-object reasoning to nearby surfaces while propagating contact information within each rigid body, RiCo retains fine-grained interaction details without the cost of modeling every pair of scene points. Such properties enable RiCo a higher accuracy and contact fidelity. Experiments on MOVi-benchmark demonstrate that RiCo reduces 100-frame position and orientation errors by 31-35% and approximately 38%, respectively, compared with baselines. Moreover, RiCo achieves high contact fidelity, with ground-truth-relative penetration-time and mean-depth differences of 11.0% and 2.22 mm, respectively. RiCo further generalizes zero-shot from small-scale training scenarios to scenes containing 270 objects. Our real-world multi-ball collision experiments further provide preliminary evidence of sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [146] [ContiLNN: Mitigating Slice Sampling Discontinuity with Liquid Neural Networks for Medical Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12337)
*Jialei He,Enhe Liu,Sifan Song,Pengfei Jin,Jionglong Su,Hongbin Wang,Zhixiang Lu,Yanhao Huang,Anteng Cai,Zhengyong Jiang,Jiaman Ding,S. Kevin Zhou,Jinfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ContiLNN 在二维复原骨干网络上引入双向闭式连续时间（Bi-CfC）模块进行跨切片建模，利用切片索引间隔调制门控，配合参考引导一致性与冻结骨干蒸馏，在 CT 去噪、MRI 超分、低计数 PET 复原三任务上稳定提升 PSNR 并降低延迟与显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像复原中，解剖连续性可提供互补信息，但其利用需同时刻画局部解剖结构与切片采样间隔的变化（如层间距不均、稀疏/不规则采样）。现有二维骨干模型仅做平面内特征提取，缺乏对跨切片结构一致性的建模；而基于神经 ODE 的连续时间建模又面临数值积分带来的计算开销。

Method: 提出 ContiLNN：在二维复原骨干上增配双向闭式连续时间（Bi-CfC）模块实现跨切片建模，同时保留平面内特征提取。以切片索引间隔调制由局部特征与隐状态决定的门控，使信息传播能响应采样变化而无需数值 ODE 积分；采用参考引导一致性约束对齐一阶与二阶跨切片强度差以保留解剖变化；并通过冻结骨干蒸馏维持平面内保真度。对比对象包括 Bi-GRU，并考察连续训练、混合间隔训练、少样本训练与第二骨干的兼容性。

Result: 在五个训练种子下，相对 Restore-RWKV，ContiLNN 在 CT 去噪、MRI 超分、低计数 PET 复原上平均 PSNR 分别提升 0.1907、1.0176、1.2482 dB，三项任务 RMSE 均更低（CT 结果为单例留出患者的描述性结果）。PET 消融表明其增益源于有序传播而非额外的逐点容量。连续训练下，Bi-CfC 在参数量与算力相近时于所有测试采样条件下保真度均优于 Bi-GRU；七切片匹配分析显示延迟降低 52.8%、峰值 GPU 显存降低 57.0%。混合间隔训练改善两种算子的稀疏与不规则上下文表现，但各指标与上下文间无统一排序。少训练患者与第二骨干实验支持数据效率与骨干兼容性。

Conclusion: ContiLNN 以闭式连续时间模组实现高效跨切片建模，在不显著增加参数与算力的前提下提升多模态医学图像复原质量与推理效率，并具备跨骨干兼容性与一定数据效率，为利用解剖连续性进行层间建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Anatomical continuity provides complementary information for medical image restoration, but its use requires accounting for local anatomy and variations in slice sampling. We introduce ContiLNN, which augments two-dimensional restoration backbones with bidirectional closed-form continuous-time (Bi-CfC) modules for cross-slice modeling while retaining in-plane feature extraction. Slice-index intervals modulate gates determined by local features and hidden states, enabling propagation to respond to sampling variations without numerical ODE integration. Reference-guided consistency aligns first- and second-order cross-slice intensity differences to preserve anatomical variation, while distillation from a frozen backbone helps retain in-plane fidelity. Across five training seeds, ContiLNN improves mean PSNR over Restore-RWKV by 0.1907, 1.0176, and 1.2482 dB for CT denoising, MRI super-resolution, and reduced-count PET restoration, respectively, with lower RMSE in all three tasks. CT results are descriptive for one held-out patient. PET ablations support ordered propagation beyond additional pointwise capacity. Under contiguous training, Bi-CfC achieves higher fidelity than a Bi-GRU with similar parameter counts and arithmetic costs across all tested sampling conditions. Matched seven-slice profiling shows 52.8% lower latency and 57.0% lower peak GPU memory use than Bi-GRU. Mixed-gap training improves sparse and irregular-context performance for both operators, without a uniform ranking across metrics and contexts. Experiments with fewer training patients and a second backbone further support data efficiency and backbone compatibility.

</details>


### [147] [Reasoning-Informed Visual Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12343)
*Xue Yang,Peiyuan Zhang,Yilun Zhu,Qihao Yang,Mingxin Liu,Xiangyu Zhao,Ziqian Fan,Zhaokai Wang,Yan Li,Yifan Yang,Xu Yang,Xiaosong Jia,Yue Zhou,Zhihang Zhong,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RISEBench++ 是首个面向"推理驱动视觉编辑"(RISE) 的分层基准，涵盖 6 大推理维度、12 个子类、65 种细粒度任务，扩展至 1000 个中英双语人工标注样例并支持多图条件输入；同时提出免训练智能体框架 RISE-Agent，评估 58 种编辑方法，最强模型仅达 56.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型多模态模型在视觉编辑中难以遵循复杂指令、保持外观一致性并支持灵活输入格式；缺少系统性基准来诊断"推理是否真正介入编辑"这一能力缺口，因此需要构建分层、细粒度且可校准评测的 RISE 基准。

Method: 将 RISE 分类体系扩展为分层结构：Temporal、Causal、Spatial、Logical、Counterfactual 及跨多轮编辑的 Hybrid 六维推理，细分为 12 子类与 65 任务类型；输入支持多图条件；规模扩至 1000 例人工标注（中英双版）；评测框架以人类评审与 LMM-as-a-judge 评估指令推理、外观一致性与视觉合理性；并设计 RISE-Agent——免训练智能体，融合推理驱动规划、工具增强执行与验证器引导精化。

Result: 评测 58 种视觉编辑方法（34 个开源、19 个闭源、5 个智能体方法）；RISE-Agent 在多样 RISE 任务上超越多数强基线；最强方法 GPT-Image-2.5 Sunburst 仅取得 56.6% 准确率，显示推理型视觉编辑仍存在显著挑战。

Conclusion: RISEBench++ 揭示了当代编辑模型在推理感知视觉编辑上的能力上限，提供诊断性洞见与未来方向；但其贡献以基准与评测为主，方法性突破有限。

Abstract: Large Multi-modality Models (LMMs) have made significant progress in visual understanding and generation, but still face challenges in visual editing, particularly in following complex instructions, preserving appearance consistency, and supporting flexible input formats. To study this gap, we introduce RISEBench, the first benchmark for evaluating Reasoning-Informed viSual Editing (RISE), and extend it to RISEBench++, a more comprehensive and fine-grained benchmark for this emerging task. RISEBench++ extends the taxonomy into a hierarchical scheme spanning six reasoning dimensions: Temporal, Causal, Spatial, Logical, and Counterfactual Reasoning, together with Hybrid Reasoning integrating multiple reasoning types across multi-turn edits. These dimensions are further decomposed into 12 subcategories and 65 fine-grained task types. We expand input formats to include multi-image conditioning and scale the benchmark to 1000 human-annotated test cases, released in English and Chinese. We also improve our evaluation framework, assessing Instruction Reasoning, Appearance Consistency, and Visual Plausibility with human judges and an LMM-as-a-judge approach for more reliable and calibrated judgements. Beyond benchmarking, we introduce RISE-Agent, a training-free agentic framework integrating reasoning-driven planning, tool-augmented execution, and verifier-guided refinement, outperforming most strong existing approaches across diverse RISE tasks. We evaluate 58 visual editing approaches, including 34 open-source models, 19 closed-source models, and 5 agentic methods. The results reveal substantial challenges in reasoning-based visual editing, with even the strongest evaluated approach, GPT-Image-2.5 Sunburst, achieving only 56.6% accuracy. RISEBench++ highlights the limitations of contemporary editing models, provides insights, and indicates future directions for reasoning-aware visual editing.

</details>


### [148] [Distilling Routed 3D Privilege for Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12355)
*Hongxing Li,Yixin Li,Dingming Li,Zixuan Wang,Yuchen Yan,Wenqi Zhang,Weiming Lu,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GPD（Geometry-Privileged Distillation），将深度、语义、BEV 等 3D 几何线索渲染为紧凑文本作为特权信息，仅在错误轨迹上施加特权 KL 损失以增强 GRPO 的 on-policy 自蒸馏，部署模型仍保持 RGB-only，在 VSI-Bench 等空间推理基准上显著超越 GRPO 与答案特权 OPSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 的空间推理是长期短板，因为 RGB 输入无法直接提供几何证据。现有方案要么在推理时注入 3D（增加架构复杂度和延迟），要么用结果奖励只监督最终答案。作者认为空间错误的根源在感知层面——深度或方向判断错误只能靠场景真实几何纠正，而空间训练语料本身就是 3D 扫描来源，已天然携带这些几何信息，因此应把几何证据作为特权信息用于训练而非推理。

Method: GPD 在 on-policy self-distillation（OPSD）框架下将几何证据设为特权信息。对每个问题，将深度、语义和鸟瞰图（BEV）线索渲染为紧凑文本，与参考答案一起路由给教师模型；仅在错误轨迹上应用特权 KL，用于增强 GRPO 目标。训练完成后部署模型仅为 RGB-only，不引入额外架构或推理延迟。消融研究了 3D 特权与答案特权的互补性、问题条件式路由相比全上下文注入的优势，以及仅对错误轨迹蒸馏的收益。

Result: 在 4B 骨干模型上，GPD 在 VSI-Bench 取得 57.1，在 MindCube、SPARBench、MMSI-Bench、ViewSpatial 四基准上平均 37.6，全面优于 GRPO 和答案特权 OPSD。消融实验证实 3D 与答案特权的互补性、问题条件式路由优于全上下文注入，以及将蒸馏限制在错误轨迹上的收益。

Conclusion: 将场景真实几何作为训练期特权信息、并仅在错误轨迹上蒸馏，是一种无需推理开销即可提升 VLM 空间推理的有效范式；几何特权与答案特权互补，错误轨迹定向蒸馏是关键设计选择。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning remains a persistent weakness of vision-language models (VLMs), because RGB inputs do not directly provide geometric evidence. Existing remedies either inject 3D into the model at inference, paying architecture and latency costs, or train with outcome rewards that supervise only the final answer. Spatial errors originate in perception: a misjudged depth or direction can be corrected only by the scene's true geometry, which the 3D-scanned sources of spatial training corpora already provide. We propose GPD (Geometry-Privileged Distillation), which makes geometric evidence the privilege in on-policy self-distillation (OPSD). For each question, depth, semantic, and bird's-eye-view (BEV) cues are rendered as compact text and routed to the teacher alongside the reference answer; a privileged KL, applied only to incorrect trajectories, augments GRPO, and the deployed model remains RGB-only. On the 4B backbone, GPD achieves 57.1 on VSI-Bench and 37.6 average across MindCube, SPARBench, MMSI-Bench, and ViewSpatial, outperforming both GRPO and answer-privileged OPSD across spatial reasoning benchmarks. Ablations confirm the complementarity of 3D and answer privilege, the advantage of question-conditioned routing over full-context injection, and the benefit of restricting distillation to incorrect trajectories.

</details>


### [149] [AgentGarten: Code Worlds for Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12374)
*Jiawei Chi,Shangchen Miao,Zhiyuan Shi,Kailu Wu,Hanyang Wang,Weiliang Chen,Qiyu Dai,Jinshan Ren,Jun Gao,Mingsheng Long,Yueqi Duan,Jiangran Lyu,Jialong Wu,Fangfu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AgentGarten 将模拟器/游戏引擎的持久世界状态与共享神经渲染器耦合，构建实时可交互虚拟环境；通过 Adversarial Forcing 微调预训练视频模型以适应几何条件并实现实时推理，使智能体以极少轮次经验蒸馏 playbook 快速学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体通过探索学习的能力受限于其训练环境的保真度（一致状态、规则、动力学）与真实感（观测符合真实视觉分布），而在多样化世界中同时满足两者仍是瓶颈。作者希望构建可规模化、实时交互、可代码化生成的环境，使智能体可持续进化。

Method: 提出 AgentGarten 框架：仿真后端维护持久世界状态并执行程序定义的交互规则，通过统一接口导出具几何结构的条件；共享神经渲染器由预训练视频模型适配几何条件得到，采用提出的 Adversarial Forcing 蒸馏——通过精确重放使 history prefilling 可微，让后续预测损失反向更新渲染器对历史观测的编码，并加入真实数据对抗监督提升视觉质量；推理针对实时交互优化。智能体以视觉观测感知、实时交互，并将每轮经验蒸馏为可继承与精炼的 playbook。

Result: 实证显示学习效率显著提升：智能体仅需 4 轮即可学习，而传统强化学习对照需数百万轮。环境以代码书写并通过同一接口渲染，可随智能体在数量与难度上同步扩展。

Conclusion: 通过共享神经渲染器连接仿真规则与视觉生成，AgentGarten 提供了保真且真实、可实时交互、可规模化生成的环境，为智能体通过交互经验持续进化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Interactive virtual worlds allow agents to learn through exploration and interaction. What agents can learn is bounded by the environments they practice in, which must be faithful, with consistent state, rules, and dynamics, and realistic, with observations that follow the real-world visual distributions. Achieving both across diverse worlds remains a bottleneck. We introduce AgentGarten, a framework that couples simulators and game engines with a shared neural renderer to build real-time interactive environments. Its simulation backends maintain persistent world state and execute program-defined interaction rules, while the renderer generates visual observations from structured conditions exported through a common interface. To build the neural renderer, we adapt a pretrained video model to geometry conditions, distill it with our proposed Adversarial Forcing, and optimize inference for real-time interaction. Adversarial Forcing makes history prefilling differentiable through exact replay, so that losses on later predictions update how the renderer encodes prior observations, and adds real-data adversarial supervision to improve its visual quality. In AgentGarten, agents perceive the world through visual observations, interact with it in real time, and improve by distilling each round of experience into playbooks that subsequent agents inherit and refine. Our empirical study demonstrates a substantial gain in learning efficiency, with agents learning from just 4 rounds compared with millions for a conventional reinforcement learning counterpart. As new worlds can be written as code and rendered through the same interface, environments can scale in both number and difficulty alongside their agents, a step toward agents that keep evolving through interactive experience.

</details>


### [150] [WorldAlign: Decoupled 4D Reward for World-Consistent Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12382)
*Jing He,Kaixin Ding,Xingye Tian,Guibao Shen,Wenhang Ge,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Ying-Cong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldAlign 提出解耦的 4D 奖励框架，将视频中的静态区域与动态主体分离，分别对齐几何世界先验与 VLM 评判先验，实现无需人工偏好的在线后训练，在 Wan2.1/Wan2.2 上同时提升静态与动态一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模拟要求生成视频保持 4D 世界一致性（静态几何一致 + 动态运动与外观一致）。几何感知后训练有潜力提升一致性，但现有方法多假设静态场景；即便支持动态场景，静态一致性反馈仍不可靠，动态一致性常被忽视或评估不足。因此需要一种能分别对静态结构与动态主体提供可靠奖励的框架。

Method: WorldAlign 是解耦式 4D 奖励框架：语义上分离静态区域与动态主体，并各自对齐匹配其假设的世界先验。(1) 静态区域：通过语义引导的掩码重投影，将静态几何对齐到几何世界先验，从而实现更可靠的静态一致性评估；并引入辅助的相机运动奖励，抑制近似静止的退化解。(2) 动态主体：以强视觉语言模型（VLM）作为动态世界先验，基于样本特定检查表构建 VLM-as-a-judge 奖励，评估动态性、物理合理性、形状与纹理一致性。该解耦设计支持无需人工偏好标注的高效在线后训练。

Result: 在两个预训练图像到视频生成器 Wan2.1 与 Wan2.2 上，WorldAlign 相较现有方法同时提升了静态一致性与动态一致性，且未抑制整体运动或主体运动。

Conclusion: 结果表明，解耦式世界先验对齐可带来更忠实的世界模拟，为几何感知后训练提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Faithful visual world simulation requires generated videos to maintain 4D world consistency, encompassing both static and dynamic consistency. Static consistency requires coherent 3D structure in static environments across viewpoints, while dynamic consistency requires plausible subject motion and consistent appearance over time. Geometry-aware post-training offers a promising way to improve world consistency. However, existing methods often rely on a static-scene assumption. Even those that accommodate dynamic scenes struggle to provide reliable static-consistency feedback, while dynamic consistency is often overlooked or inadequately assessed. To address these limitations, we introduce WorldAlign, a decoupled 4D reward framework that semantically separates static regions and dynamic subjects and provides feedback by aligning each with a world prior suited to its assumptions. For static regions, WorldAlign aligns static geometry with a geometric world prior through semantically guided masked reprojection, enabling more reliable static-consistency evaluation; an auxiliary camera-motion reward discourages nearly static solutions. For dynamic subjects, WorldAlign uses a strong vision-language model (VLM) as a dynamic world prior and constructs a VLM-as-a-judge reward based on sample-specific checklists that assess dynamicity, physical plausibility, shape, and texture consistency. This decoupled design enables more effective online post-training without requiring human preference annotations. Across two pretrained image-to-video generators, Wan2.1 and Wan2.2, WorldAlign jointly improves static and dynamic consistency over existing methods without suppressing overall or subject motion. These results support decoupled world-prior alignment for more faithful visual world simulation. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [151] [GenIA: Generative Reconstruction with Test-Time Input Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12388)
*Stefano Esposito,Naama Pearl,Polina Karpikova,Samuel Rota Bulò,Lorenzo Porzi,Peter Kontschieder,Andreas Geiger,Jonathon Luiten*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenIA：在测试时把 SAM3D 生成先验锚定到几何与光度观测，改善位姿与外观并支持外供几何，单目/多视角/动态输入上优于现有优化法、逐帧图到3D与视频到4D方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目或稀疏多视角重建完整3D资产仍困难；生成式3D基础模型虽能补全观察外的几何，但其预测未必忠实复现观测到的几何、外观与位姿，需要在推理阶段注入观测约束。

Method: GenIA 为免重训的测试时输入对齐生成框架：由几何推导平移与尺度、保留学习到的旋转先验以改进物体位姿；通过可见性偏置注意力、跨观测融合与去噪过程中的可微渲染引导对齐外观；可选去噪后精修进一步调整外观隐变量、轻量解码器适配器与物体摆放。框架同样支持外供几何，在动态物体的时序形状下恢复共享的输入对齐规范外观与稳定世界坐标放置。

Result: 在合成与真实基准上，GenIA 提升了位姿预测与物体重建，单目、多视角与动态输入均优于近期基于优化的方法、逐帧图像到3D方法以及视频到4D方法。

Conclusion: 该工作表明不重训生成基础模型、仅在测试时以几何与光度观测锚定其先验，即可显著提升位姿与外观保真度，并自然扩展到动态时序几何与规范化表示。

Abstract: Reconstructing complete 3D object assets from monocular or sparse multi-view observations remains challenging. Generative 3D foundation models can complete object geometry beyond the observed views, but their predictions may not faithfully reproduce the observed geometry, appearance, or pose. We introduce GenIA, a framework for test-time input-aligned generation that grounds SAM3D's generative prior in geometric and photometric observations without retraining the foundation model. We improve object pose by deriving translation and scale from geometry while retaining the learned rotation prior, and align appearance through visibility-biased attention, cross-observation fusion, and differentiable rendering guidance during denoising. An optional post-denoising refinement further adapts the appearance latent, lightweight decoder adapters, and object placement to the observations. Our framework also supports externally supplied geometry; when given temporal shapes of dynamic objects, it recovers a shared, input-aligned canonical appearance and stable world-space placement. Across synthetic and real benchmarks, GenIA improves pose prediction and object reconstruction from monocular, multi-view, and dynamic inputs, outperforming recent optimization-based, per-frame image-to-3D, and video-to-4D methods. Our project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [152] [SpaceFlow: Locally Controllable 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12399)
*Neil De La Fuente,Joan Lafuente,Mukhammadali Sayfiddinov,Felicia Scharitzer,Marc Pollefeys,Ata Celen,Sayan Deb Sarkar,Elisabetta Fedele*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpaceFlow：无需训练的文本+几何基元可控3D生成，通过流过程施加局部约束、分段条件路由实现局部外观控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D生成缺乏显式局部控制：几何遵循性由全局控制强度定义，外观无法局部指定。作者希望让用户对每个部件独立指定"严格遵循形状"或"允许生成式补全"，并实现部件级外观条件化。

Method: 无训练管线：输入文本与一组几何基元，每个基元作为部件代理并被赋予局部控制等级；在生成式flow过程中强制空间约束；外观合成阶段对生成结构进行分割并与基元匹配，每个部件仅受其对应文本/图像线索条件化，抑制跨部件泄漏。

Result: 区域几何指标显示高控制区域保持指定几何、低控制区域产生合理形变；用户研究表明几何保真与生成自由的平衡在整体质量上有竞争力；固定几何下的外观评估中，文本条件路由达到SOTA的提示忠实度与颜色/材质准确度；定性展示图像线索的局部路由。

Conclusion: 局部控制等级+部件级条件路由是可行的无训练3D生成控制范式，兼顾几何保真与生成自由度，并支持文本/图像线索的局部化。可作为3D生成可控性与部件编辑的基础方案。

Abstract: Current 3D generation methods lack explicit local control: geometric adherence is often defined by a global control strength, and appearance cannot be specified locally. We present SpaceFlow, a training-free pipeline for locally controllable 3D generation from text descriptions and a collection of geometric primitives. Each primitive serves as a proxy for an object part and is assigned a local control level, enabling users to specify whether regions should strictly follow the input shape or allow generative completion. During structure generation, we enforce these spatial constraints within the generative flow process. For appearance synthesis, the generated structure is segmented and matched to the primitives. Each generated part is conditioned only on its assigned text or image cue, thereby limiting cross-part leakage. Regional geometry metrics demonstrate that SpaceFlow preserves the specified geometry in high-control regions and enables plausible shape variation in low-control areas. A user study further indicates that the resulting balance between geometric fidelity and generative freedom remains competitive in overall quality. When evaluating appearance on fixed geometry, text-conditioned routing achieves state-of-the-art prompt faithfulness and color/material accuracy. Qualitative results additionally show localized routing of image cues. The project page is available at this http URL .

</details>


### [153] [SpaceCast-Bench: Evaluating Predictive Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12402)
*Hongxing Li,Jinyue Su,Dingming Li,Wenqi Zhang,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SpaceCast-Bench，首个诊断性评测「预测性空间推理」的基准（观察-变换-推断框架），覆盖 182 个真实场景、16 类任务与静态感知/局部预测/全局预测三级难度；21 个模型评测显示最强模型仅 58.0% 对比人类 87.2%，并给出桥接视角与显式 3D 证据的关键分析及微调增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间推理基准多只测试「空间感知」，即读取输入中已可见的空间关系；而真实空间智能要求「预测性空间推理」：从观测中构建场景、预判干预带来的变化、并推理未见结果。该能力缺乏直接、可诊断的评测手段，因此需要新基准来揭示模型在场景级构建、状态更新与不可见关系推断上的真实差距。

Method: 构建 SpaceCast-Bench：以 observe-transform-infer 框架组织，含 182 个真实场景、3,862 道问题、16 种任务类型，划分静态感知、局部预测、全局预测三个层级，逐步要求场景理解、空间状态更新与对未观测结果的关系推断。评测 21 个模型并做受控分析（桥接视角、显式 3D 证据 vs 生成图像/视频）；另以程序化生成数据进行微调实验（Qwen3-VL-4B）。

Result: 最强模型仅达 58.0%，人类为 87.2%，空间专精模型接近随机水平；分析表明桥接视角对整合分布式观测至关重要，显式 3D 证据比生成的结果图像或视频更稳定地带来增益；在程序化生成数据上微调使 Qwen3-VL-4B 从 34.0% 提升至 65.7%，并在六个域外基准上取得宏平均提升。

Conclusion: 预测性空间推理是当前模型与人类差距最大、且现有基准未覆盖的能力维度；SpaceCast-Bench 提供了可直接诊断的评测框架，并证明显式 3D 证据与程序化数据微调是提升该能力的有效路径。

Abstract: Existing spatial reasoning benchmarks mainly test spatial perception: reading off relations already visible in the input. Yet real-world spatial intelligence demands predictive spatial reasoning: constructing a scene from observations, anticipating how an intervention changes it, and reasoning about the unseen outcome. We introduce SpaceCast-Bench, the first benchmark to directly and diagnostically evaluate this capability. Built around an observe-transform-infer framework, its 3,862 questions from 182 real-world scenes span 16 task types at three levels: static perception, local prediction, and global prediction, progressively requiring scene understanding, spatial state updating, and relational inference over unobserved outcomes. Evaluating 21 models exposes a stark gap: the strongest model reaches only 58.0% against 87.2% human performance, while spatially specialized models remain near random chance. Controlled analyses further reveal that bridge views are critical for integrating distributed observations, and that explicit 3D evidence benefits models more reliably than generated outcome images or videos. Fine-tuning on our programmatically generated data lifts Qwen3-VL-4B from 34.0% to 65.7% with macro-average gains across six out-of-domain benchmarks.

</details>


### [154] [ViSkill: Reinforcing VLM Agents with Evolving Visual-Native Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12403)
*Hongxing Li,Dingming Li,Yixin Li,Yong Du,Wenqi Zhang,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViSkill：将成功交互轨迹编码为"视觉技能卡"，由VLM智能体直接检索用于推理与奖励塑形，并把成功轨迹蒸馏回技能库形成闭环，在Sokoban、FrozenLake、PrimitiveSkill上达0.89（冷启动0.91）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能增强智能体以文本为中心，将空间布局与动作-状态对应线性化为语言，丢失关键几何结构；近期视觉方案又把技能构建/更新与策略优化解耦，二者互促关系未被充分探索。

Method: 视觉原生的技能学习框架：把成功交互编码为复合视觉技能卡（非文本序列化）；检索到的技能同时引导推理与奖励塑形；成功轨迹被蒸馏回技能库，形成技能积累与策略改进互相强化的闭环反馈；另提供可选冷启动机制加速早期学习。

Result: 在Sokoban、FrozenLake、PrimitiveSkill三任务上总体成功率0.89，加入冷启动初始化后0.91，优于所有对比的闭源与开源基线，且收敛快于标准PPO。

Conclusion: 以视觉技能卡替代文本技能表示，并把技能库更新与策略优化纳入同一闭环，可同时提升成功率与样本效率；冷启动进一步改善早期学习。局限在于评测集中于网格类/组合式任务，缺少真实机器人部署验证。

Abstract: Skill-augmented agents improve sample efficiency by distilling successful trajectories into reusable strategies. Yet most existing approaches remain text-centric, linearizing spatial layouts and action-state correspondences into language that loses critical geometric structure. Recent efforts have begun incorporating visual evidence, but construct and update skills separately from policy optimization, leaving their mutual improvement underexplored. We propose ViSkill, a visual-native skill learning framework that encodes successful interactions as composite visual skill cards directly accessible to VLM agents. Retrieved skills guide both inference and reward shaping, while successful trajectories are distilled back into the library, forming a closed feedback loop in which skill accumulation and policy improvement reinforce each other. An optional cold-start mechanism further accelerates early-stage learning. Evaluated on Sokoban, FrozenLake, and PrimitiveSkill, ViSkill achieves an overall success rate of 0.89, rising to 0.91 with cold-start initialization, outperforming all evaluated proprietary and open-source baselines while converging faster than standard PPO. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [155] [WorldCast: Distributed Multiplayer World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12412)
*Ziyang Ye,Junchao Huang,Evelyn Zhang,Zhihao Xie,Ruicheng Zhang,Boyao Han,Litao Ban,Ziye Wang,Xinting Hu,Shaoshuai Shi,Zhuotao Tian,Li Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldCast：分布式多人世界模型，每个玩家本地运行视频生成器+状态模型，仅交换玩家状态与场景状态，实现一致、实时、可扩展的多人视图生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人世界模型需为各玩家生成独立可控视图，且需保持玩家与共享环境表示一致。现有方法多采用联合多视图生成，成本随玩家数增长，存在中心化计算瓶颈，难以扩展。

Method: 分布式架构：每玩家本地客户端含视频生成器与状态模型。训练时用录制的玩家位置和地图几何，让状态模型从生成视频与控制输入估计玩家位置。客户端交换玩家状态，投影为相机对齐的玩家状态场（camera-aligned player state field），指导其他玩家的渲染位置与方式；共享场景状态使客户端复用彼此生成的观测，保持跨视图场景外观一致。

Result: 在 Counter-Strike 2 上验证：一致性、实时性与分布式可扩展性。相机对齐玩家状态场使玩家渲染率较联合生成方法提升一个数量级以上；共享场景状态在整局长度上改善视觉一致性。每客户端实时运行，仅交换玩家与场景状态。小时级 rollout 中图像质量稳定。

Conclusion: 通过去中心化的状态交换与相机对齐状态场/共享场景状态，WorldCast 实现了可扩展、无中心瓶颈的多人世界模型生成，兼顾实时性与长时一致性。

Abstract: Multiplayer world models must generate independently controlled views with consistent representations of both players and their shared environment. Most existing approaches coordinate multiple players through joint multi-view generation, whose cost grows with each additional player. We present WorldCast, a distributed multiplayer world model in which each player runs a local client comprising a video generator and a state model. Using recorded player positions and map geometry during training, the state model estimates the player's position from generated video and control inputs. Clients exchange player states and project them into camera-aligned player state fields that guide where and how other players are rendered. Shared scene state enables clients to reuse one another's generated observations to maintain consistent scene appearance across views. Experiments on Counter-Strike 2 demonstrate WorldCast's consistency, real-time performance, and distributed scalability. The camera-aligned player state field improves player rendering rates by over an order of magnitude over joint-generation methods, while shared scene state improves visual consistency over whole rounds. Each client runs in real time and exchanges only player and scene states, enabling scalable multiplayer generation without a centralized computational bottleneck. Image quality remains stable over hour-long rollouts.

</details>


### [156] [MAMHOI: Factorizing Scene-Aware Human-Object Interaction through Affordances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12416)
*Mingyuan Lei,Yoonchang Sung,Tat-Jen Cham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出MAMHOI，通过“可供性中介的因子化”框架解耦场景理解与HOI运动合成，在缺乏配对“人-物-场景”数据的情况下生成场景感知的人-物交互，减少物体-场景穿插并保持交互质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景感知的人-物交互（HOI）生成需同时具备两项能力：环境中的交互可行性推理与真实人-物运动合成。然而二者的大规模联合监督数据极度稀缺：人-场景数据集提供环境感知运动，人-物数据集提供交互动力学，但配对的“人-物-场景”数据几乎不存在。作者希望无需此类配对数据即可学习上述两项能力。

Method: MAMHOI将场景感知HOI生成因子化为“场景理解”与“运动合成”间的显式运动-可供性接口：先由场景条件模型预测交互可执行的位置与方式（可供性），再由可供性条件的HOI模型据此生成相应的人-物运动。该因子化使两类能力可分别从互补的监督来源中学习，而无需配对的人-物-场景数据。

Result: 在复杂室内环境中的实验表明，MAMHOI在降低物体-场景穿插（object-scene penetration）的同时更好地保持了人-物交互质量，生成更真实且物理可行的场景感知交互。

Conclusion: 通过显式的运动-可供性接口解耦场景理解与交互动力学学习，MAMHOI在无需配对三模态数据的前提下实现了更优的场景感知HOI生成，为利用异构数据集训练此类模型提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Generating realistic human-object interactions (HOI) in complex 3D scenes requires two complementary capabilities: reasoning about interaction feasibility in the environment and synthesizing realistic human-object motion. However, supervision for these capabilities is rarely available jointly at scale. Human-scene datasets provide rich information about environment-aware motion, while human-object datasets capture detailed interaction dynamics, yet paired human-object-scene data remain scarce. We present MAMHOI, an affordance-mediated factorization for scene-aware human-object interaction generation. MAMHOI factorizes scene-aware HOI generation through an explicit motion-affordance interface between scene understanding and motion synthesis: a scene-conditioned model first predicts where and how an interaction can be feasibly executed, and an affordance-conditioned HOI model then generates the corresponding human-object motion. This factorization allows scene understanding and interaction dynamics to be learned from complementary sources of supervision without requiring paired human-object-scene data. Experiments in complex indoor environments show that MAMHOI reduces object--scene penetration while better preserving human--object interaction quality, yielding more realistic and physically feasible scene-aware interactions. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [157] [WOVEN: Weaving Visual World Modeling into Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12417)
*Zheyu Fan,Yue Zhang,Mingkai Deng,Kangrui Wang,Qineng Wang,Canyu Chen,Jie Hao,Xing Fan,Chenlei Guo,Eric P. Xing,Mohit Bansal,Manling Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出WOVEN训练源与基准，将视觉状态转移推理作为多模态大模型空间、具身、物理与时间推理失败的共同根源，并用系统化训练配方验证其可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在空间、具身、物理、时间推理上系统性失败，作者假设这些失败源于共同的"视觉转移推理"能力缺陷，而现有基准只分别记录缺陷，缺乏跨场景、动作与推理操作的受控比较。

Method: 基于视频预训练生成模型产生多样真实rollout，构建WOVEN：36,076个样本，覆盖20类场景、5类动作、8类推理类型，并按场景/动作/推理类型组织监督信号；评测38个前沿MLLM，并在多规模MLLM上按不同子集训练，进行跨任务迁移与数据替代实验。

Result: 38个前沿模型均显著低于人类且随规模持续失败；约2,000条的子集训练累计在26个外部基准中的22个上提升最高27.3个百分点；WOVEN数据可替代任务自身30-50%训练数据而精度相当；得到训练配方：按推理操作而非动作/场景/领域选择监督，并偏好更大的视觉状态变化。

Conclusion: 视觉转移推理可作为MLLM视觉世界模型训练的可复用基础能力，训练配方在留出基准上前瞻性验证有效。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) struggle with spatial, embodied, physical, and temporal reasoning. We hypothesize that these failures reflect a shared deficit in visual transition reasoning, and test whether this capability can serve as a shared training primitive, one that different models can learn from different supervision sources and reuse across different tasks, with a systematic training recipe. Existing benchmarks document these deficits separately but do not support controlled comparisons across scenes, actions, and reasoning operations. We therefore introduce WOVEN, a training source and benchmark for visual transition reasoning that organizes transition supervision by scene, action, and reasoning type, using diverse, realistic rollouts from video-pretrained generative models: 36,076 examples across 20 scene types, 5 action types, and 8 reasoning types. We first evaluate 38 frontier MLLMs (e.g., GPT-5.4 and Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B) and find a substantial and systematic deficit: even the strongest models fall far below humans, and the failures recur across model families and persist with scale. We then train MLLMs at multiple scales on WOVEN and find that they learn a shared capability that transfers broadly: training subsets of only about 2,000 items each collectively improve 22 of 26 external benchmarks by up to 27.3 percentage points, and WOVEN data can replace 30-50% of a task's own training data with comparable accuracy. Controlled comparisons further yield a training recipe for visual world modeling, validated prospectively on held-out benchmarks: select supervision by the reasoning operation it teaches rather than by the actions, scenes, or domains it shows, and prefer larger changes to the visual state for robustness. Our work establishes visual transition reasoning as a reusable foundation for systematic visual world-model training in MLLMs.

</details>


### [158] [OneSearch-VL: Unified Multimodal Deep Research Agent for Image and Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12419)
*Hongyu Li,Manyuan Zhang,Kaituo Feng,Shu Chen,Dian Zheng,Hao Li,Hao Yu,Zhangquan Chen,Zoey Guo,Ray Zhang,Shaofei Huang,Tianrui Hui,Linjiang Huang,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一多模态深度研究代理 OneSearch-VL，以视觉落地证据图（VGEG）编码视觉锚点、实体关系、来源事实与推理操作的依赖，驱动数据构建、过程监督与细粒度评测，在单图/多图/视频研究任务上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图、多图与视频深度研究需要不同的视觉操作，却共享“视觉落地—外部检索—事实组合”的工作流；核心难点在于如何保持局部视觉锚点、实体关系、来源支持事实与产出答案的操作之间的依赖一致性，现有方法缺乏统一的任务级结构来同时支撑数据构造、过程监督与操作级评测。

Method: 以 Visually Grounded Evidence Graph（VGEG）为共享任务级表示；基于 VGEG 的数据引擎构建并验证多图与视频问题、筛选专家轨迹，得到 SFT 数据 OneSearch-VL-SFT-110K 与 RL 数据 OneSearch-VL-RL-10K；从 VGEG 标注导出 Evidence-aware Visual-Grounded Rubric 奖励（EVGR），在强化学习中监督证据可追溯性与视觉落地；构建 OneSearch-MI-Bench 与 OneSearch-Video-Bench，按 VGEG 所编码的研究操作组织问题。

Result: OneSearch-VL-8B 在两个新基准上分别较具备工具调用能力的 Qwen3-VL-8B 提升 20.2 与 17.6 个百分点，并在 7 个图像基准与 VideoDR 上取得显著增益。

Conclusion: 显式的、任务级的视觉落地证据图可同时作为数据构造、过程奖励与操作级评测的共享骨架，是提升多图与视频深度研究中证据可追溯性与视觉落地能力的有效范式。

Abstract: Single-image, multi-image, and video deep research require different visual operations but share a workflow of visual grounding, external retrieval, and fact composition. A key challenge is to preserve the dependencies linking localized visual anchors, entity relations, source-supported facts, and answer-producing operations. We introduce OneSearch-VL, a unified agent centered on the Visually Grounded Evidence Graph (VGEG), which encodes these dependencies as a shared task-level reference for data construction, process supervision, and operation-level evaluation. Our VGEG-based data engine constructs and verifies multi-image and video questions and filters expert trajectories. Using these data, we assemble OneSearch-VL-SFT-110K and OneSearch-VL-RL-10K for SFT and RL, respectively. We further derive the Evidence-aware Visual-Grounded Rubric reward (EVGR) from VGEG annotations to supervise evidence traceability and visual grounding during RL. For fine-grained evaluation, we construct OneSearch-MI-Bench and OneSearch-Video-Bench, organizing questions by the research operations encoded in their VGEGs. Experiments show that OneSearch-VL-8B improves over Qwen3-VL-8B with tool access by 20.2 and 17.6 percentage points on the two new benchmarks, respectively, while also achieving substantial gains across 7 image benchmarks and VideoDR. Project repository: this https URL

</details>


### [159] [Beyond Spatio-Temporal Priors: A Generalizable Approach for Dense Correspondence Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12421)
*Luping Liu,Bingyi Kang,Yifan Wang,Dong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeMatching：结合生成式与语义基础表征及异构监督（经典数据集、跟踪视频、合成场景），并通过教师引导迭代精化，实现跨越图像编辑/参考引导生成变换的身份保持稠密对应匹配，兼具定量评估身份保持的指标功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稠密对应匹配依赖平滑运动、刚性几何等时空先验，在图像编辑与参考引导生成（IEG）中失效——此类变换保持视觉身份却破坏物理连续性，因此需要新的身份保持对应范式。

Method: 提出 FreeMatching 通用框架：融合生成式与语义基础表征，利用经典数据集、跟踪视频与合成场景的异构监督；在缺乏稠密对应标注的 IEG 场景下采用教师引导的迭代精化。

Result: 单一模型在具挑战性的 IEG 图像对上显著提升对应质量，同时在经典基准上保持有竞争力性能；作为身份保持的定量指标，其评分与人类判断相关。

Conclusion: FreeMatching 将稠密对应从受物理先验约束拓展到身份保持的生成变换域，并可作为可泛化的身份保持评估指标，代码已开源。

Abstract: Dense correspondence matching has historically been bounded by simplifying spatio-temporal priors, such as smooth motion and rigid geometry. While effective for classical tasks, these assumptions break down in image editing and reference-guided generation (IEG), where transformations can preserve visual identity while breaking physical continuity. To establish identity-preserving correspondence across such transformations, we introduce FreeMatching, a generalizable framework combining generative and semantic foundation representations with heterogeneous supervision from classical datasets, tracked videos, and synthetic scenes. Teacher-guided iterative refinement further improves correspondence in IEG without dense correspondence annotations. Experimentally, a single FreeMatching model substantially improves correspondence quality on challenging IEG image pairs while retaining competitive performance on classical benchmarks. Furthermore, we demonstrate its utility as a quantitative metric for evaluating identity preservation, with scores that correlate with human judgment. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [160] [Pumpire: Unified Benchmark for Metric Distance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12423)
*Siyu Chen,Zehan Wang,Jiayang Xu,Yihan Wu,Jialei Wang,Junming Chen,Ziang Zhang,Yutong Ying,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Pumpire 统一基准，直接评估图像级与视频级 3D 基础模型的"点对点距离估计"能力，并发布含 100 个真实场景、6,400 帧、带实测点对距离标注的 pumpire-6k 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估通常将深度与相机内参分开评测，或用几何相似度指标评价点云，无法直接反映模型对重建几何中点到点物理距离（物理尺度）的估计能力，而这正是 3D 基础模型被忽视的更基础能力。

Method: 构建大规模多样化数据集 pumpire-6k（100 个真实场景、每场景 64 帧，共 6,400 帧，均含物理实测点对距离标注）；在此基础上设计覆盖图像级/视频级、含/不含深度先验的统一评估协议，直接从重建几何计算点对距离误差并支持跨设定比较；对 29 种代表性 3D 基础模型基线配置开展系统性实验。

Result: 建立了可直接度量点对距离误差并支持跨设定对比的整体评估协议，完成 29 组基线配置的广泛实验与综合分析，揭示现有 3D 基础模型在物理尺度感知与点对距离估计上的不足。

Conclusion: Pumpire 将评估焦点从分离的深度/内参指标转向重建三维空间中的物理尺度感知，为该方向提供统一、可复现的基准与分析框架。

Abstract: We present Pumpire, a unified benchmark for evaluating metric point-pair distance estimation capability of both image- and video-level 3D foundation models, with or without depth priors. In contrast to previous approaches that normally evaluate depth and camera intrinsics separately or evaluate point-clouds with geometric similarity metrics, which cannot directly reflect models' point-to-point distance estimation capability, Pumpire directly assesses point-to-point distances from the reconstructed geometry. To this end, we collect a large-scale and diverse dataset (pumpire-6k) comprising 100 real-world scenes, each annotated with physically measured point-pair distances and containing 64 frames, for a total of 6,400 frames. Building on this dataset, we establish a holistic evaluation protocol that covers both image- and video-level 3D foundation models and enables direct assessment of point-pair distance errors and cross-setting comparison. We conduct extensive experiments across 29 baseline configurations of representative 3D foundation models and provide a comprehensive analysis of the results. By offering this benchmark, we target the more fundamental ability to perceive and estimate physical scale in the reconstructed 3D space, which prior evaluation protocols have largely overlooked. The project page can be found at this https URL

</details>


### [161] [FastBench: Can Streaming VLMs Perceive High-Dynamic Real-World Streams?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12427)
*Yuxuan Hu,Weikang Shi,Yang Bo,Xudong Lu,Xintong Guo,Shuhan Li,Yuyang He,Huankang Guan,Peiwen Sun,Yunqiao Yang,Wenbo Li,Rui Liu,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FastBench 评测基准，用于评估流式视频大模型在高动态真实视频流中的感知能力，并给出免训练基线 ProactiveFrame。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频 VLM 基准多集中于低动态场景；在有限上下文预算下，模型需在时间历史、空间分辨率与时间粒度之间权衡，而 1–2 FPS 稀疏采样会漏掉快速事件，因此缺少针对高动态流式感知的评测手段。

Method: 构建轨迹锚定的数据管线：从高帧率片段生成 QA、过滤掉 2 FPS 即可回答的问题、用 SAM3 与 CoTracker3 轨迹做答案验证，并经三轮人工检查；最终包含 306 个 QA 对，覆盖 8 个领域、6 类能力与前向/瞬时/后向三种时间范围，并带人工标注的证据区间。同时提出 ProactiveFrame 免训练基线，通过文本 token 调整输入帧率，并用双层滑动窗口保留近期高帧率观测、将较早帧降采样为稀疏历史。

Result: 最强模型 Gemini-3.5-Flash 仅得 50.7%；Qwen3-VL-8B 从 2 FPS 的 32.9% 提升至 24 FPS 的 44.6%，但随历史压缩收益饱和；ProactiveFrame 比稀疏均匀采样高 5.4 与 1.5 个百分点，但仍显著低于 oracle 引导的聚焦，说明当前 VLM 难以仅凭视频流判断何时需要更细的时间感知。

Conclusion: FastBench 为高动态流式视频理解提供了评测平台，揭示现有流式视频 VLM 在时间粒度自适应分配上的根本不足。

Abstract: Streaming Video Large Language Models (VLMs) enable continuous video understanding, yet existing benchmarks focus on low-dynamic scenarios. Under bounded context budgets, models must balance temporal history, spatial resolution, and temporal granularity; sparse sampling at 1--2 FPS misses fast events. We introduce FastBench to evaluate high-dynamic perception in real-world video streams. Its trajectory-grounded pipeline combines QA generation from high-FPS clips, filtering of questions answerable at 2 FPS, answer verification using SAM3 and CoTracker3 trajectories, and three rounds of human inspection. FastBench contains 306 QA pairs across eight domains, six capabilities, and forward, instant, and backward temporal scopes, with human-annotated evidence intervals. We also present ProactiveFrame, a training-free baseline that adjusts incoming frame rates through text tokens. A dual-tier sliding window retains recent high-FPS observations while downsampling older ones into sparse history. Experiments reveal substantial limitations: the strongest model, Gemini-3.5-Flash, scores only 50.7%. Denser sampling improves Qwen3-VL-8B from 32.9% at 2 FPS to 44.6% at 24 FPS, but gains saturate as history is compressed. ProactiveFrame outperforms sparse uniform sampling by 5.4 and 1.5 percentage points, yet remains well below oracle-guided focusing, showing that current VLMs struggle to determine from the stream alone when finer temporal perception is needed. FastBench provides a testbed for high-dynamic streaming video understanding. Code and data: this https URL .

</details>


### [162] [LEGO: A Lifting-Free Approach for Exocentric-to-Egocentric Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12442)
*Suhwan Cho,Yonwoo Choi,Soongjin Kim,Jicheol Park,Taegyu Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无 lifting 的跨视角条件：微调 LVSM 风格 Transformer 直接渲染自我中心视角（无需深度、点云与重投影），以概率对应分布聚合候选源像素，并输出逐区域置信度用于掩码与早期去噪引导，与视频扩散模型协同生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由单个外视角（exocentric）视频生成自我中心（egocentric）视频是极端的新视角合成问题：两相机重叠极小，目标视角大量区域未被观测。现有 SOTA 用显式几何管线——估深度、升为点云、重投影渲染——再条件化视频扩散模型。该确定性映射把每个像素指派到唯一重投影位置，虽保持纹理，却把深度误差放大为内容错位。作者追问：扩散模型真正需要的条件是什么？

Method: 提出"无 lifting"条件：(1) 学习式视角合成器，基于 LVSM 风格 Transformer 微调，直接渲染自我中心视角，不使用深度、点云或重投影，跨视角对应在模型内部隐式求解；(2) 其映射为概率式，按学习到的对应分布对每个区域在候选源位置上加权平均，因而保留结构但会平均掉细节纹理；(3) 由该分布的集中度导出逐区域置信度，用于掩码低置信区域，并在早期成形布局的去噪步中引导生成器聚焦高置信区域。

Result: 方法持续优于当前 SOTA 显式管线，且无需重训练即可泛化到其他数据集。作者经验性论证：确定性映射（高锐度但结构错位）与概率映射（结构对齐但纹理模糊）之间存在权衡，后者更适合由去噪训练擅长恢复细节的扩散生成器。

Conclusion: 分工结论：合成器供给视角结构，扩散模型补充高频细节。有"效条件"的关键在于结构对齐而非锐度，置信度作为可解释的中间量同时服务于掩码与去噪调度。

Abstract: Generating an egocentric video from a single exocentric recording is a challenging case of novel view synthesis, as the two cameras share little overlap and much of the target view is unobserved. Current state-of-the-art methods reconstruct the scene explicitly by estimating depth, lifting the video into a point cloud, and re-rendering it from the egocentric camera to condition a video diffusion model. This deterministic mapping assigns each pixel to a single reprojected location, which preserves texture but translates depth errors into misplaced content. We ask what a video diffusion model should receive as its condition and propose a lifting-free answer: a learned view synthesizer, an LVSM-style transformer fine-tuned to render the egocentric view directly without depth, point clouds, or reprojection, resolving cross-view correspondence internally. In contrast, its probabilistic mapping averages each region over candidate source locations according to a learned correspondence distribution, preserving structure while fine texture is averaged away. We argue that this trade-off suits a diffusion generator, whose denoising training excels at restoring detail, so an effective condition should prioritize structural alignment over sharpness. This distribution's concentration also yields a per-region confidence, used both to mask low-confidence regions and to guide the generator toward high-confidence areas during early layout-forming denoising steps. Our approach consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art explicit pipeline and generalizes to other datasets without retraining. The synthesizer thus supplies view structure, and the diffusion model its detail.

</details>


### [163] [One Block, Multiple Depths: Recurrent Vision Transformers with Depth-Programmed Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12448)
*Adrian Bulat,Yassine Ouali,Georgios Tzimiropoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 reViT：将单个 Transformer block 循环复用，通过以连续归一化深度坐标编程的 FFN 专家库凸组合来恢复深度特化，在监督与 DINOv2 蒸馏两种设定下达到全深度编码器精度，且存储参数减少约 70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环/权重共享 Transformer 虽能降低参数与计算，但各循环深度共享同一 FFN，导致深度间的变换被抹平，精度不及全深度编码器；若靠中间特征蒸馏补偿又会引入额外训练开销与教师依赖。作者希望在不做中间特征蒸馏的前提下，用极小的参数预算恢复深度特定的 FFN 变换。

Method: 把每个循环深度上的 FFN 参数表示为一个小型共享专家库上的凸组合（即权重空间合并式 MoE），用一个连续归一化深度坐标对该混合进行条件编程，从而在 FFN 参数空间中定义一条可重采样的轨迹；在同一 one-FFN budget 下与该系列 MoE 的其他方案（token-dispatch、输出混合）做受控对比；并在 ImageNet-1k 监督训练与 DINOv2 教师蒸馏两种设定下验证；提出 elastic-depth 训练使单 checkpoint 在多个深度上通过重采样同一坐标区间运行；固定深度部署时可将循环块物化为常规稠密图，去除在线路由与合并。

Result: 受控实验显示，在相同单 FFN 预算下权重空间合并是最强的 MoE 家族，优于 token 派发与输出混合两类替代方案；从头训练的 reViT-B/16 在约减少 70% 存储参数的情况下达到 DeiT III 精度；8 专家蒸馏模型仅使用教师输出特征，保留了 DINOv2 教师几乎全部线性探测精度，并可迁移到分类、分割与深度预测；elastic-depth 训练支持单 checkpoint 多深度运行；固定深度部署可稠密化物化，计算仍为每深度一个 FFN，但部署存储会膨胀。

Conclusion: 循环复用单 block 配合深度条件化的 FFN 权重混合，可在无中间特征蒸馏条件下逼近全深度编码器精度并大幅压缩参数，是该循环视觉编码器族中最有效的 MoE 形式；代价是固定深度部署需物化稠密图、存储增大。

Abstract: In this work, we show that a single Transformer block, applied recurrently, can match the accuracy of a full-depth vision encoder at comparable inference FLOPs without intermediate feature distillation. reViT restores depth-specific transformations by representing the FFN at each recurrent depth as a convex combination of a small shared expert bank. A continuous normalized-depth coordinate programs this mixture, defining a resampleable trajectory through FFN parameter space. We evaluate this design in two regimes: supervised ImageNet-1k training and distillation from a DINOv2 teacher. Across both regimes, controlled adaptations identify weight-space merging as the strongest tested MoE family at a matching one-FFN budget, ahead of the token-dispatch and output-mixture alternatives. Trained from scratch, reViT-B/16 attains DeiT III accuracy with about 70\% fewer stored parameters. An 8-experts model distilled using only the teacher's output features retains nearly all of its DINOv2 teacher's linear-probe accuracy and transfers across classification, segmentation, and depth prediction. Elastic-depth training allows one checkpoint (trained model) to operate at multiple tested depths by resampling the same normalized coordinate interval. For fixed-depth deployment, the recurrent block can be materialized as a conventional dense graph, removing online routing and merging without changing the one-FFN-per-depth compute but expanding deployment storage.

</details>


### [164] [VersaCamVLA: Camera-Configurable VLA Policies for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12451)
*Boyao Han,Chen Shi,Jingjing Qian,ZhuoTan Tian,Li Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VersaCamVLA，一种相机配置可变的VLA框架，通过学习统一场景token接口将任意数量/位姿的RGB视图映射为固定大小潜变量场景token，无需3D感知或新视图渲染即可适配预训练VLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖训练时的固定相机配置，部署中相机数量或位姿变化会导致性能脆弱，缺乏对可变多视图的鲁棒泛化能力。

Method: 解耦相机集合表示与动作学习：训练统一场景token接口，用多信号目标视图预测与Wrist-Augmented Pose Sampling（WAPS，借手腕相机自然运动获取免费位姿多样性）实现；部署时用轻量空间编码器将紧凑场景token作为补充视觉条件注入预训练基座VLA。

Result: 在RoboTwin、LIBERO及真实机器人平台上，VersaCamVLA均优于既有VLA方法与直接多视图基线，在变化的相机数量和未见相机位姿下保持稳健性能。

Conclusion: 相机配置可变的场景token接口能有效提升VLA对部署时相机配置变化的鲁棒性，且不依赖显式3D传感或新视图渲染。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as powerful foundations for robotic manipulation, but their reliance on fixed camera configurations during training makes them brittle to changes in camera count or pose during deployment. To overcome these limitations, we propose VersaCamVLA, a camera-configurable framework that decouples camera-set representation from action learning. VersaCamVLA learns a unified scene-token interface that maps an arbitrary, variable set of posed RGB views into fixed-size latent scene tokens. This is achieved via multi-signal target-view prediction and Wrist-Augmented Pose Sampling (WAPS), which leverages natural wrist-camera motion for free pose diversity. At deployment, a lightweight spatial encoder injects these compact scene tokens into a pretrained base VLA as a supplementary visual condition, requiring no explicit 3D sensing or novel-view rendering. Experiments on RoboTwin, LIBERO, and a real-robot platform demonstrate that VersaCamVLA consistently outperforms prior VLA methods and direct multi-view baselines, maintaining robust performance across varying camera counts and unseen camera poses.

</details>


### [165] [OmniCapBench: A Deep-Structured Evaluation Framework for Fine-Grained Audio-Visual Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12458)
*Zhongyu Yang,Jiale Tao,Ruitao Chen,Zuhao Yang,Yingfang Yuan,Xueliang Zhao,Auden,Kai Wang,Shuai Shao,Biao Wang,Steve Yves,Qinglin Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniCapBench：将音视频字幕评测重构为“原子化结构化诊断”，跨实体引用、视觉镜头、音频事件三条轨道进行确定性约束校验＋局部LLM语义比对，用786个密集标注视频定位MLLM的时序锚定失败、身份漂移、跨模态错位与幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM正走向连续音视频推理，但缺乏能暴露能力边界的评测。现有音视频字幕基准存在耦合权衡：整体字幕打分覆盖广却无定位，局部探针有定位却无覆盖，且无约束的LLM评判器带来不稳定。

Method: OmniCapBench把预测目标从自由文本改为原子化、可验证的评测单元集合，分三条轨道：实体引用、视觉镜头、音频事件；采用确定性约束检查＋局部化LLM语义比较实现可靠且可定位的打分；构建786个密集标注视频。

Result: 基准有效区分MLLM的感知错误类型，包括时序锚定失败、身份漂移、跨模态错位与幻觉描述。对前沿MLLM的评测显示：局部感知强，长时程音视频推理弱，身份漂移和跨模态错位问题尤为突出。

Conclusion: 将字幕评测转化为深度结构化诊断，为全模态模型发展提供了细粒度路线图。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are rapidly evolving toward continuous audio--visual reasoning, creating an urgent need for evaluations that expose their capability limits. Audio--visual captioning is an ideal diagnostic task, yet current benchmarks face a coupled trade-off: whole-caption scores provide coverage without localization, local probes provide localization without coverage, and unconstrained LLM judges introduce instability. We introduce OmniCapBench (Omni-Video Caption Benchmark), a benchmark that reframes audio--visual caption evaluation as a deep-structured diagnostic framework. OmniCapBench shifts the prediction target from free-form text to sets of atomic, verifiable evaluation units across three tracks: entity references, visual shots, and audio events, enabling reliable scoring with deterministic constraint checks and localized LLM-based semantic comparisons. With 786 densely annotated videos, OmniCapBench effectively distinguishes MLLM perception errors, including temporal grounding failures, identity drift, cross-modal misalignment, and hallucinated descriptions. Evaluating frontier MLLMs reveals strong local perception but weak long-horizon audio--visual reasoning, particularly in identity drift and cross-modal misalignment, providing a fine-grained roadmap for omnimodal development.

</details>


### [166] [WorldGuide: Goal-Directed Video World Model for Procedural Task Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12459)
*Ankan Deria,Komal Kumar,Hisham Cholakkal,Fahad Shahbaz Khan,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldGuide 将程序性视频生成重构为"视觉世界空间中的闭环任务执行"：仅给初始图像与任务目标，模型预测原子动作、生成对应视频片段，并用生成结果决定下一步动作或终止；Planner 与 Executor 基于同一批步骤级演示联合训练，配合分层视觉记忆控制长程 token 开销，并发布 59K 步骤级标注视频基准 WorldGuide Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程程序性任务（如装配、烹饪流程）要求生成过程能根据"实际已生成的状态"进行适应：开环视频生成无法响应执行结果，而现有闭环系统多依赖预训练执行器或间接验证，导致"决策动作"与"成功实现动作"之间存在鸿沟。作者希望把程序性视频生成建模为闭环任务执行问题，让规划与执行在同一监督信号下耦合学习。

Method: 1) 任务形式化：给定初始图像与目标，定义为视觉世界空间中的闭环任务执行（预测动作→生成该动作视频→依据结果选择下一动作或终止）。2) 双模块架构：Planner 从视觉进展预测下一原子动作或判定任务完成；Executor 直接训练以"实现"所预测的动作（而非调用预训练执行器）。3) 分层视觉记忆：跨长程执行维护状态，历史 token 成本有界。4) 数据构建：因缺少步骤级"动作-视频"联合监督，提出 WorldGuide Bench，约 59K 步骤标注视频、245 个任务、27 个程序类别，用于 Planner-Executor 联合训练。

Result: 在 WorldGuide-Bench 上达到 33.33% Task Success，优于近期强视频模型 MiniMax-H3 的 29.90%（后者还额外获得参考动作计划）；在仅给目标的 VideoCraft-Bench 条件下达到 47.69%，对比 MiniMax-H3 的 32.73%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将规划与"学习到的执行"耦合，对于目标导向的程序性视频生成至关重要；闭环的"决策-实现"一体化设计与步骤级联合监督是缩小动作决策与动作落实之间差距的关键。

Abstract: Video generators and video-based world models can synthesize plausible visual trajectories, but long-horizon procedural tasks require generation to adapt to what has actually been produced. A model must determine the next action from its generated state, execute that action, and recognize when the task is complete. Open-loop generation cannot adapt to execution outcomes, while existing closed-loop systems often rely on pretrained executors or indirect verification. This leaves a gap between deciding an action and successfully realizing it. We formulate procedural video generation as \emph{closed-loop task execution in visual world space} and introduce \textbf{WorldGuide}. Given only an initial image and a task goal, WorldGuide predicts an atomic action, generates its corresponding video clip, and uses the generated result to select the next action or terminate. The Planner and Executor are trained on the same step-level procedural demonstrations: the Planner learns to predict the next atomic action or task completion from visual progress, while the Executor is directly trained to realize the predicted actions. Hierarchical visual memory maintains state across long-horizon execution with bounded history token cost. Due to the lack of step-level action-video supervision for joint planner-executor training, we introduce \textbf{WorldGuide Bench}: approximately 59K step-annotated videos across 245 tasks and 27 procedural categories. WorldGuide achieves a 33.33\% Task Success on \textbf{WorldGuide-Bench}, compared with 29.90\% for the strong recent video model MiniMax-H3, even though MiniMax-H3 receives reference action plans, and achieves 47.69\% on \textbf{VideoCraft-Bench} compared with 32.73\% for MiniMax-H3 under goal-only conditioning. These results demonstrate the importance of coupling planning with learned execution for goal-directed procedural video generation.

</details>


### [167] [OuroWorld: Bringing Any 3D World Alive as Diverse, Endlessly Looping 3D Cinemagraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12461)
*You-Zhe Xie,Ting-Wei Chou,Yu-Hsuan Li,Kaipeng Zhang,Zhixiang Wang,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OuroWorld 将任意静态 3D Gaussian Splatting 场景转化为可循环播放的 3D cinemagraph（动态可探索场景），无需运动掩码，通过 VLM 推断动力学 + 视频模型合成参考视频并提升为多视角视频，再用抗不一致的周期 4DGS 学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 世界模型生成的光照真实场景被"冻结在时间中"，缺乏动态。已有动态化方法多为 Eulerian 表示，仅能刻画流体类运动，难以建模一般形变、物体运动与光照变化；同时多视角监督通常不完美、跨视角不一致，且缺乏无真值的动态质量评估手段。

Method: 提出 OuroWorld 框架：用 VLM 推断合理动力学并引导视频模型合成参考视频，再提升/补全为多视角视频作为监督。核心是 Inconsistency-Robust Periodic 4DGS：傅里叶级数形变场从构造上保证周期循环无缝；Grounded Drift Field 以参考视角为锚吸收跨视角不一致。无掩码，可建模一般形变、物体运动与光照变化，并提出无需真值的评估指标（生动性、自然性、循环接缝一致性、场景质量）。

Result: 在 39 个重建与生成场景上，OuroWorld 优于所有基线；用户研究对比中胜率达 70.8%–99.0%。

Conclusion: OuroWorld 以无掩码、周期性 4DGS 表示实现了从单目参考视频到多视角动态场景的动态化，兼顾循环无缝与跨视角一致性，为静态 3D 世界模型注入时间维度，并提供可扩展的无真值评估范式。

Abstract: Recent 3D world models generate photorealistic, explorable scenes that remain frozen in time. OuroWorld is a mask-free framework that turns any static 3D Gaussian Splatting scene into a 3D cinemagraph: a dynamic scene with vivid, diverse motion looping seamlessly from any viewpoint. A vision-language model infers plausible dynamics and guides a video model to synthesize a reference video, which we lift and complete into multi-view videos. To learn from this imperfect supervision, we propose Inconsistency-Robust Periodic 4DGS: a Fourier-series deformation field guarantees looping by construction, while a Grounded Drift Field anchored at the reference view absorbs cross-view inconsistency. Unlike prior Eulerian methods limited to fluid-like motion, we capture general deformation, object motion, and illumination change. We introduce a ground-truth-free evaluation covering vividness, naturalness, loop seam coherence, and scene quality. On 39 reconstructed and generated scenes, OuroWorld outperforms all baselines and wins 70.8%-99.0% of user-study comparisons. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [168] [What 30,000 Hours of Ego-centric Video Does Not Teach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12464)
*Jiahua Dong,Anurag Bagchi,Yash Jangir,Muhammad Zubair Irshad,Sergey Zakharov,Martial Hebert,Homanga Bharadhwaj,Yu-Xiong Wang,Vitor Campagnolo Guizilini,Pavel Tokmakov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作用3万小时第一视角人类视频训练世界模型，直接评测在分布外基准上的智能体与物体交互保真度，发现数据扩展100倍主要提升智能体建模而非物体保真度；作者通过视觉条件设计让智能体保真度以极少数据饱和，从而单独测量物体保真度及其饱和点，并提出监督方案将容量从场景外观转向物体动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型被视为物理模拟器的替代方案，但距离实际部署仍很远。核心问题是：单纯扩展第一视角人类视频数据，究竟能把世界模型推到多远？作者希望摆脱不透明的下游指标，直接量化"智能体建模"与"物体交互建模"两类保真度的差异。

Method: 构建/使用3万小时、覆盖1000+场景类型、14000名贡献者的第一视角人类视频数据集；在具有挑战性的分布外基准上直接评估智能体与物体交互保真度；通过视觉条件设计解耦数据量与建模能力；提出将模型容量从场景外观转向物体动力学的监督方案。

Result: 训练数据扩大100倍后，智能体与物体保真度均有提升但不均衡：智能体建模良好，物体保真度显著更低且提升缓慢。智能体收益并非来自数据本身，精心的视觉条件设计可仅用少量数据使智能体保真度饱和，从而可单独测量物体保真度并发现其饱和点。新监督方案提升物体保真度，但仍存在明显差距。结论可迁移到下游人形建模。

Conclusion: 扩展第一视角数据使智能体建模接近上限，但对"世界"（物体交互）建模的影响远落后；缩小该差距取决于模型如何训练，而不仅在于数据规模。

Abstract: World models offer a promising alternative to physics-based simulators, yet remain far from practical deployment. We ask how far scaling ego-centric human video takes them, using a dataset of 30,000 hours spanning over 1,000 scene types and 14,000 contributors. Rather than relying on opaque downstream metrics, we directly evaluate agent and object-interaction fidelity on a challenging out-of-distribution benchmark. Increasing training data by 100x improves both, but unevenly: the agent is modeled well, while object fidelity remains far lower and improves slowly. We show that the agent gains need not come from data, and a careful visual conditioning design saturates fidelity with a fraction of it, which lets us measure object fidelity on its own and discover its saturation point. We then introduce a supervision scheme that shifts capacity from scene appearance toward object dynamics, improving object fidelity though a substantial gap remains. Finally, our conclusions transfer to downstream humanoid modeling. Overall, our results suggest that scaling ego-centric data brings agent modeling close to its limit while leaving its effects on the world far behind, and that closing this gap will depend on how models are trained, not only on how much data they see.

</details>


### [169] [Rubric-CEPR: Self-Evolving Image Editing via Reward-Verified Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12469)
*Ritesh Thawkar,Shubham Patle,Shravan Venkatraman,Rao Muhammad Anwer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Rubric-CEPR自演化框架，仅用编辑器自身生成样本（无需人工编辑目标或外部奖励模型），通过评分表增强的对比编辑-保持奖励（CEPR）与冻结Critic打分、非补偿门控筛选，将最佳验证样本以轻量适配器蒸馏回编辑器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令引导图像编辑器虽已强大，但进一步提升依赖人工编辑训练对或外部奖励模型：监督获取成本高，且可能奖励"看似合理"的失败——输出逼真却未完成所需编辑，或改动了应保持的内容。因此希望仅凭编辑器自身生成即可自提升。

Method: Rubric-CEPR自演化框架：Planner从无标注图像提出结构化编辑指令；Editor采样多个候选编辑；冻结Critic利用编辑器已暴露的内部特征，对每个候选按分解式评分表检查编辑实现、旧状态移除与内容保持，构成对比编辑-保持奖励；非补偿门控拒绝不可行候选；将最佳已验证候选通过轻量适配器训练蒸馏回编辑器。

Result: 在Qwen-Image-Edit上，ImgEdit从4.36提升至4.60（+5.5%），物体隔离子项+24.9%，并可迁移至GEdit-Bench与Complex-Edit；同一流程在Step1X-Edit上ImgEdit提升+7.8%。

Conclusion: 该方法为"从自身已验证样本中自我提升"的图像编辑器提供了可靠基线，且仅依赖内部表示与自生成数据，无需人工目标或外部奖励模型。

Abstract: Instruction-guided image editors have become highly capable, yet improving them further still depends on human-edited training pairs or external reward models. Such supervision is costly to obtain and can reward plausible failures: a realistic output may leave the requested change undone or alter content that should be preserved. In this work, we strive to improve a pretrained image editor using only its own generations, without human-edited targets or an external training-time reward model. To this end, we propose a self-evolving framework, named Rubric-CEPR, that verifies the editor's own samples with its internal representations through a rubric-augmented Contrastive Edit-Preservation Reward (CEPR). A Planner proposes structured edit instructions from unlabeled images, the Editor samples multiple candidate edits, and a frozen Critic scores each candidate with decomposed rubric checks for edit realization, removal of the old state, and content preservation, using features already exposed by the editor. Non-compensatory gates reject infeasible candidates, and the best verified candidate is distilled into the editor through lightweight adapter training. On Qwen-Image-Edit, Rubric-CEPR improves ImgEdit from 4.36 to 4.60 (+5.5%), with a +24.9% gain on object isolation, and transfers to GEdit-Bench and Complex-Edit.
