The same procedure also improves Step1X-Edit by +7.8% on ImgEdit. We hope our approach will serve as a solid baseline for image editors that improve themselves from their own verified samples. Our code is publicly available at $\href{ this https URL }{\text{this URL}}$

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Does Dynamic-Point Filtering Help When Texture Is Scarce? A Controlled Study of ORB-SLAM2 Front-Ends in Synthetic Indoor Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10564)
*Zekui Xue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文通过合成数据集受控实验，因子化变化表面纹理（4级）与场景动态（3级），系统评测动态点滤波对ORB-SLAM2在低纹理场景下的影响，发现滤波收益不随纹理单调下降，且GEOM比FLOW误删更多静态特征点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有动态点滤波系统仅在纹理丰富的基准序列上评测，缺乏对"纹理—动态"交互作用的隔离研究，导致"去除动态特征会使低纹理区域静态特征不足"这一论点缺乏受控证据。

Method: 渲染合成室内序列：在相同相机轨迹下因子化组合4级表面纹理（用FAST角点密度与图像梯度熵量化）与3级场景动态，提供双目、RGB-D、真值位姿与动态掩码。在该网格上对比ORB-SLAM2无滤波、光流+对极残差滤波（FLOW）、多视图深度一致性滤波（GEOM），报告轨迹误差、跟踪完整度与存活静态特征数（5次运行），并利用掩码计算各滤波器的动态点精确率/召回率与静态特征误删率（FRR）。不提出新滤波器。

Result: 24个序列共720次运行：滤波主要在高动态单元起作用；收益不随纹理单调下降，但在最低纹理级滤波降低了跟踪完整度，且ORB-SLAM2在静态L3场景从未初始化成功。与假设相反，GEOM比FLOW丢弃更多静态关键点（RGB-D中位FRR 6.8% vs 1.5%，双目19.6% vs 1.5%）；GEOM在RGB-D的优势与动态点召回率一致，在双目模式下消失。

Conclusion: 动态点滤波的收益依赖于动态程度与传感器模态而非纹理单调性；深度一致性滤波存在较高静态特征误删风险，其模态优势不具可迁移性。合成受控基准可作为可机制解释的评测工具，数据与代码已开源。

Abstract: Dynamic-point filters are routinely added to feature-based visual SLAM, and several recent systems argue that removing dynamic features can leave too few static features in low-texture regions. So far, these systems have been evaluated only on texture-rich benchmark sequences. We present a controlled study that isolates this interaction. We render synthetic indoor sequences in which surface texture (four levels, quantified by FAST-corner density and image-gradient entropy) and scene dynamics (three levels) are varied factorially along identical camera trajectories, with stereo, RGB-D, ground-truth poses and dynamic masks. On this grid we compare ORB-SLAM2 without filtering, with an optical-flow and epipolar-residual filter (FLOW), and with a multi-view depth-consistency filter (GEOM), and report trajectory error, tracking completeness and surviving static features over five runs. Because the masks give per-keypoint ground truth, we also measure each filter's dynamic-point precision and recall and its static-feature false-removal rate, so that mechanistic explanations can be tested directly. We do not propose a new filter. On 720 runs over 24 sequences, filtering helped mainly in the most dynamic cells; the benefit did not decline monotonically with texture, but at the lowest level filtering reduced tracking completeness, and ORB-SLAM2 never initialised in static L3 scenes. Contrary to our hypothesis, GEOM discarded more static keypoints than FLOW (median FRR 6.8% vs. 1.5% for RGB-D, 19.6% vs. 1.5% for stereo); its RGB-D advantage tracked dynamic-point recall and vanished in stereo mode. Data and code are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [171] [Teaching a Robot Dog New Tricks: Diverse Quadruped Skills via Combined Reinforcement and Imitation Learning with Adversarial Task Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10601)
*Lemon Foxmere,Anthony Furman,Yizheng Du,Oliver Chang,Leilani Gilpin,Steve McGuire*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出三阶段强化学习框架，先训练多个窄任务教师策略，再通过对抗式任务选择的多教师蒸馏联合RL与模仿学习训练单一学生策略，实现22种技能并迁移到四足机器人真机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿足机器人多技能应用（农业、太空探索）需要行走、挖掘、跳跃等多样技能，端到端多任务策略训练面临样本效率低与任务间梯度冲突问题。

Method: 三阶段方法：1) 用RL在窄定义任务上训练多个教师策略；2) 多教师蒸馏，学生策略采用RL+IL联合目标；3) 引入对抗式任务选择过程，将训练聚焦于表现最差任务。用8个教师训练覆盖22个任务的学生策略。

Result: 学生策略保留运动质量并更准确跟踪指令，优于PPO与distill-then-finetune基线；部分新任务无需显式训练即可泛化；在Unitree B1四足机器人上完成真机部署验证鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 多教师蒸馏结合对抗任务选择可有效训练单策略多技能腿足控制，并具备真机部署与任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has enabled legged robots to perform a range of skills in single-task settings. However, applications such as farm robotics or space exploration require diverse skills such as locomotion, digging, or close-range surveying. Training an end-to-end policy to address this problem remains difficult due to challenges such as sample inefficiency and gradient conflict between tasks in multi-task learning. We propose a three-stage method that trains a single policy to perform distinct tasks such as walking, digging, and hopping, and compose them into novel behaviors such as crawling. First, multiple teacher policies are trained using RL on narrowly defined tasks. Then, two additional stages train a student policy with a multi-teacher distillation setup that uses a combined RL and Imitation Learning (IL) objective under an adversarial task selection process that focuses training on the worst-performing task. With this method, we train a student policy that performs 22 tasks using 8 teachers. Evaluations show our method preserves motion quality and tracks commands more accurately than PPO and distill-then-finetune baselines, and in some cases generalizes to new tasks without explicit training. Finally, we demonstrate real-world robustness by deploying the resulting policy on a Unitree B1 quadruped. Video: this https URL

</details>


### [172] [TacHair: Tactile Contact-Distribution Guided Online Correction for Robotic Hair Stroking and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10637)
*Ruiyi Hu,Yongqiang Zhao,Daniel Bak,Yupeng Wang,Xuyang Zhang,Shan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TacHair，用高分辨率触觉观测构建空间头发接触分布作为反馈表示，结合视触觉模仿策略生成名义梳理轨迹与残差模块在线校正局部接触偏移，在真实机器人 525 次试验中将梳理成功率由 42.9% 提升至 62.3%，接触维持率由 59.4% 提升至 88.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常梳理与护发评测中头发抚触交互普遍存在，机器人需要类似能力；但现有轨迹规划、柔顺控制、力调节或触觉条件策略难以应对可变形头发"持续接触但逐渐漂移"的问题，局部接触难以调节，视觉上又被头发遮挡。

Method: 将高分辨率触觉观测表示为空间头发接触分布；视触觉模仿策略生成名义梳理运动，另训一个残差模块专门校正局部接触偏差，从而把任务推进与接触恢复解耦。真实机器人试验：525 次，覆盖 5 种头部几何、3 种头发状态；成功判据同时要求足够任务推进与接触维持。

Result: 在同一位视触觉策略基础上，成功率 42.9%→62.3%，接触维持率 59.4%→88.0%；代码、演示与数据集开源。

Conclusion: 空间触觉接触分布是面向可变形、视觉遮挡表面保持接触交互的有效反馈表示，任务推进与接触恢复分离的在线校正框架可行且证据充分。

Abstract: Hair stroking is common in daily grooming and personal care, and is also widely used in hair-product evaluation, motivating robots with similar physical interaction capabilities. Existing robotic hair-care and surface-following methods mainly rely on trajectory planning, compliance, force regulation, or tactile-conditioned policies, but deformable hair can remain in contact while gradually drifting across the end-effector, making local interaction difficult to regulate. We propose TacHair, a tactile contact-distribution guided online correction framework that represents high-resolution tactile observations as a spatial hair-contact distribution. A visuotactile imitation policy generates the nominal stroking motion, while a separately trained residual module corrects local contact deviations, separating task progression from contact recovery. We evaluate TacHair in 525 real-robot trials across five head geometries and three hair conditions. A successful stroke requires both sufficient task progression and contact maintenance; our method improves success from 42.9% to 62.3% and contact maintenance from 59.4% to 88.0% over the same visuotactile policy without correction. These results demonstrate spatial tactile contact distributions as an effective feedback representation for contact-preserving interaction with deformable and visually occluded surfaces. Demos, code, and datasets are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [173] [Masked Generative Motion Planning with Geometry-Guided Token Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10646)
*Lipeng Zhuang,Yingdong Ru,Shiyu Fan,Edmond S. L. Ho,Gerardo Aragon Camarasa,Paul Henderson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MGMP将掩码生成模型从并行轨迹生成扩展到结构化修复：用掩码生成Transformer并行生成离散轨迹候选，并以几何引导的Token搜索（GGTS）决定编辑位置与候选替代，把测试时修复变成离散运动备选的高效搜索，实现路线级重规划而非局部形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式运动规划器仅把学习到的轨迹先验用于初始生成，测试时修复依赖局部连续精修，难以进行路线级重构，遇到路径失效类场景时容易陷入局部最优。

Method: 提出Masked Generative Motion Planning（MGMP）：掩码生成Transformer并行生成离散轨迹候选；Geometry-Guided Token Search（GGTS）利用场景几何定位需要编辑的token位置，并在先验支持的离散备选中进行搜索评估，从而将精修转化为离散运动备选搜索。

Result: Ring Maze成功率96%，Kuka上Controlled Route Invalidation修复成功率82%，分别超过最强外部基线23和25个百分点；泛化到未见布局、额外障碍物、未见几何、单臂与双臂规划，以及真实Baxter任务。

Conclusion: 将掩码生成先验扩展到结构性修复，使离散token搜索成为路线级重规划的有效手段，并在多场景与真实机器人任务上验证了泛化性与实用性。

Abstract: Generative motion planners typically use learned trajectory priors for initial generation, while leaving test-time repair to local continuous refinement. We introduce Masked Generative Motion Planning (MGMP), which extends the learned prior from efficient parallel generation to structural repair. A masked generative transformer generates discrete trajectory candidates in parallel, and Geometry-Guided Token Search (GGTS) uses scene geometry to target where to edit and which prior-supported alternatives to evaluate. This turns refinement into an efficient search over discrete motion alternatives, enabling route-level restructuring beyond local trajectory deformation. MGMP achieves 96% success on Ring Maze and 82% repair success on Controlled Route Invalidation on Kuka, exceeding the strongest external baselines by 23 and 25 percentage points, respectively. It further generalizes to unseen layouts, additional obstacles, unseen geometries, single- and dual-arm planning, and real-world Baxter tasks.

</details>


### [174] [TAPNAV: Humanoid Navigation through Tactile Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10748)
*Huaze Liu,Zhenyu Wu,Jaehwi Jang,Junjie Sheng,Andrew Collins,Aaron Xie,Zhaoyuan Gu,Kaijie Zhu,Ding Jiang,Kevin Cai,Ye Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TAPNAV：无视觉环境下，人形机器人通过主动触觉探测周围结构实现导航，融合里程计/IMU/触觉的位姿信念，全局规划利用触觉修正机会约束定位不确定性，局部规划最大化信息增益选择探测动作，全身控制器协调行走与末端接触执行导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉受限环境下，本体感知里程计漂移快、定位不确定性迅速累积，人形机器人导航困难；探索能否通过主动规划与环境的物理接触来获取定位线索。

Method: 提出触觉主动感知框架 TAPNAV：由里程计、IMU 和触觉接触观测维护位姿信念；不确定性感知的全局路径规划寻找能让预测定位不确定性保持有界的路线并利用触觉修正机会；信息增益驱动的局部规划选择探测动作；全身控制器协调运动与末端接触以执行导航与探测动作。

Result: 在仿真及 Unitree G1 实机上、跨不同楼层平面与障碍几何进行评测，TAPNAV 相比基线取得更低的状态估计误差和更高的任务完成率。

Conclusion: 主动规划与环境的物理交互可为定位提供线索，从而实现无视觉条件下可靠的人形机器人导航。

Abstract: Navigation in vision-denied environments is challenging for humanoid robots because proprioceptive odometry drifts and localization uncertainty accumulates rapidly. We present TAPNAV, a tactile active-perception framework that enables humanoid navigation toward a goal by actively probing surrounding structures without relying on vision. TAPNAV maintains a pose belief from odometry, IMU, and tactile contact observations, and couples uncertainty-aware global route planning with information-gain-driven local probing. The global planner searches for routes that keep predicted localization uncertainty bounded by exploiting opportunities for tactile correction, while the local planner selects probe actions that maximize expected information gain. A whole-body controller coordinates the humanoid's locomotion and end-effector contact to execute the planned navigation and probe motions. We evaluate TAPNAV in simulation and on a Unitree G1 across different floor plans and obstacle geometries. TAPNAV achieves lower state estimation error and a higher task completion rate than baselines. These results demonstrate that actively planning physical interactions with the environment can provide localization cues for reliable humanoid navigation without vision.

</details>


### [175] [NavGPT-3: Harnessing Context in a Hierarchical Navigation Runtime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10787)
*Gengze Zhou,Yicong Hong,Jiazhao Zhang,Xunyi Zhao,Jian Zhou,Zixing Lei,Zun Wang,Chongyang Zhao,Xionghui Chen,Stephen Gould,Anton van den Hengel,Qi Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 NavGPT-3，一个将长时域智能体推理语言模型与低延迟动作策略（VLA）耦合的具身导航框架，并带有类操作系统式运行时（推理/执行/监控线程调度与中断切换）。其动作策略 NavGPT VLA 基于 19.28M 样本训练，用视觉编码器码率分配（codec allocation）按场景变化分配视觉 token；8B 模型单独在 R2R-CE 取得 74.51 SR，RxR-CE 78.19 SR。完整系统在 R2R-CE 达 81.51 SR，RxR-CE 上首次让自主智能体达到人类水平（90.43 vs 90.4 SR；78.47 vs 77.7 nDTW），每回合 1 分 22 秒（人类约 3 分钟），最小反应时间从 3–19 秒降至 0.5–1 秒（1–2 Hz）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域智能体强化学习训练的语言模型具备推理与目标追求能力，但物理交互仍依赖动作策略提供的密集、低延迟控制。如何把前沿语言模型智能与底层物理控制有效连接，即"具身接口"设计，是提升具身智能体理解与决策上限的关键问题。

Method: 两个层次：(1) NavGPT VLA 动作策略，训练于 19.28M 样本，采用 codec allocation 按场景变化比例分配视觉 token，8B 规模；(2) NavGPT-3 harness 与 OS 式运行时，将推理、执行、监控作为带独立上下文、工具与权限的线程，由运行时调度并决定哪一线程控制机器人运动，通过中断与线程切换应对突发真实世界事件。

Result: NavGPT VLA（8B）单独在 R2R-CE 达 74.51 SR，RxR-CE 以 78.19 SR 领先；完整 harness 在 R2R-CE 达 SOTA 81.51 SR；RxR-CE 首次达人类水平，成功率 90.43 vs 90.4 SR，路径保真度 78.47 vs 77.7 nDTW，耗时 1 分 22 秒/回合 vs 人类约 3 分钟。消融显示：动作策略执行路线时推理循环缩短，系统最小反应时间从每个语言模型决策 3–19 秒降至每个动作策略步 0.5–1 秒（1–2 Hz）。脚注：摘要未在自主智能体独立于人类对比之外给出真机部署细节，但与人类跟随者的对照与实时反应指标构成较强实证。

Conclusion: 设计这种具身接口（语言模型推理到物理控制的桥接、线程化运行时调度、中断与权限机制）是连接前沿语言模型智能与底层物理控制的核心；工具与动作策略共同塑造从语言推理到物理控制的路径。作者将开源全部模型、代码与评测记录。

Abstract: Language models trained with long-horizon agentic reinforcement learning can generalize knowledge through reasoning, express precise actions, and pursue goals over many steps, raising the ceiling on what an embodied agent can understand and decide. Physical interaction, however, remains the domain of action policies, which provide dense, low-latency control. We present NavGPT-3, a harness that connects the two models, with an OS-like runtime built above it: reasoning, acting, and monitoring run as threads with their own context, tools, and permissions, while the runtime schedules them and decides which thread controls the robot's motion, so that the robot can react to sudden real-world events through interruption and thread switching. Beneath it, our action policy NavGPT VLA, trained on 19.28M examples, allocates visual tokens using codec allocation, in proportion to scene change; its 8B model alone reaches 74.51 SR on R2R-CE and leads RxR-CE with 78.19 SR. With the complete harness, NavGPT-3 sets the state of the art on R2R-CE (81.51 SR) and, for the first time, brings an autonomous agent to human level: on RxR-CE it matches human followers in success (90.43 vs. 90.4 SR) and path fidelity (78.47 vs. 77.7 nDTW) at 1 min 22 s per episode, versus roughly 3 min for a human. We comprehensively ablate the harness design and the interaction between the two models, showing how tools and the action policy shape the path from language-model reasoning to physical control: when NavGPT VLA executes the route, the reasoning loop shortens and the system's minimum reaction time falls from 3-19 s per language-model decision to 0.5-1 s per action-policy step (1-2 Hz). These results show that designing this embodied interface is central to connecting frontier language-model intelligence with low-level physical control. We will release all models, code, and evaluation records.

</details>


### [176] [Same Action, Different Outcome: Variability in Dynamic Cloth Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10801)
*Mahed Dadgostar,Guillem Alenyà,Júlia Borràs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文系统量化了布料在重复快速动态操作下的结果变异性：在四项动态任务、三种不同物理属性布料、最多三种执行速度下重复执行同一轨迹共269次，用标记点与双目相机记录，定义多种变异性指标，发现变异性在所有条件下显著（比机器人重复性与传感噪声高1–3个数量级），主要由布料物理属性驱动并随速度增长；四个标定后的现代布料仿真器均无法复现该变异性的大小与排序。数据集（OptiTrack、视觉、机器人日志及仿真孪生）已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布料在相同轨迹的重复快速动态操作下结果不一致，但这种变异性从未被系统刻画。量化它对于评估学习到的操作策略可靠性、以及判断仿真能否复现真实世界行为至关重要。

Method: 在4项动态任务（2项为新任务）、3种物理属性差异很大的布料、最多3种执行速度下，各重复执行同一轨迹10次，共269条rollout；记录布料上的小标记点位置与同步双目相机；形式化定义多种变异性度量指标；并将所有rollout在4个标定后的现代布料仿真器中回放对比。

Result: 变异性在每种测试条件下都显著，多数情况下比机器人自身重复性与传感噪声高1–3个数量级；变异性主要由布料物理属性驱动，同时随执行速度增大；4个现代布料仿真器均无法复现真实布料观测到的变异性大小，也无法复现其排序。发布了含同步OptiTrack、视觉与机器人日志以及对应仿真孪生的数据集。

Conclusion: 真实布料的重复动态操作存在不可忽略、系统性的内在变异性，现有布料仿真器在变异性保真度上仍有明显缺口；该工作提供了数据集与度量框架，为布料操作策略学习与sim-to-real研究提供了量化基准与警示。

Abstract: Although cloth is known to exhibit different outcomes under repeated fast dynamic motions, even when the same trajectory is applied, this variability has not yet been systematically characterized. Quantifying it is essential to assess the reliability of learned manipulation policies and the extent to which simulation can reproduce real-world behavior. To study this, we execute the same trajectory ten times across four dynamic tasks, two of which are novel, each tested with three cloths of very different properties and at up to three execution speeds, with a total of 269 recorded rollouts. For all of them, we record small marker positions on the cloth and synchronized stereo camera. We then formalize different metrics to quantify variability, and our results show how it is significant in every test condition, in most cases one to three orders of magnitude above the repeatability inherent to the robot and sensing noise. Our results also show variability is driven mainly by the cloth physical properties but also grows with speed. By replaying all the rollouts in four calibrated modern cloth simulators, we show that none of them can reproduce the variability magnitude we observed in real cloth, nor its ordering. Together with our analysis, we publish the dataset with synchronized OptiTrack, vision and robot logs and their corresponding simulator twins.

</details>


### [177] [Diagnosing and Recovering from Observation-Space Shift at Long-Horizon Skill Seams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10810)
*Pranav Wagh,Yu Fang,Yue Yang,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文诊断长时序机器人操作中"技能接缝"失败的主导原因为观测空间漂移（场景状态位移），而非机器人关节构型或被操作物体；据此构建检测-恢复-续行闭环系统，在BOSS-44上将全链成功率从7.6%提升至26.5%，并在真实Franka上验证了部分恢复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序操作通常由独立训练的技能串接而成，各技能单独可靠，但串联后性能急剧下降：下游技能需从前序技能遗留的状态出发，偏离其训练分布。作者希望明确这种技能接缝失败的成因，并检验对应的修复路径。

Method: 利用仿真器特权重置做受控诊断，逐一分离并定位漂移来源；在此诊断基础上设计全学习式"检测-恢复-续行"系统：任务进度监视器检测卡滞，学习策略恢复被位移的场景组件，接缝鲁棒微调使技能得以续行。基线包括best-of-K重采样、Diffusion Policy与世界模型方法。

Result: 诊断结论：主导漂移来自被位移的场景状态（如打开的抽屉、前序技能遗留的次级物体），而非关节构型或下游被操作物体。BOSS-44上全链成功率7.6%→26.5%（相对基策略3.5倍，达到特权恢复oracle的51%），而各替代方案在所评估接缝状态上均无法恢复。真实Franka+微调π0.5上，同一监视器受外置相机可观测性限制，但闭环仍挽回部分原本终止的失败。

Conclusion: 部分长时序组合失败应通过"先恢复场景再续行策略"来解决，而非在离支撑状态下重试；该恢复式闭环作为诊断证据成立，但作者不主张其为通用方法，并提出腕部与夹爪感知的改进方向。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation is often built by chaining independently trained skills. Although each skill can be reliable in isolation, performance degrades sharply when skills are chained: each downstream skill must start from the state its predecessor leaves behind rather than from its training distribution. We study this failure mode, Observation-Space Shift (OSS), and ask what causes these skill-seam failures. Using privileged simulator resets, we find that the dominant shift comes from displaced scene state (e.g., an open drawer or secondary objects left behind by earlier skills), not from the robot's joint configuration or the object the downstream skill manipulates. To test this diagnosis, we build a fully learned detect-restore-resume system: a task-progress monitor detects the stall, a learned policy restores the displaced scene components, and seam-robust fine-tuning lets the skill resume. It recovers the seam where every tested alternative fails, which we treat as evidence for the diagnosis rather than as a general-purpose method. On the BOSS-44 benchmark, the system improves full-chain success from 7.6% to 26.5%, a 3.5x improvement over the base policy and 51% of a privileged restoration oracle, whereas best-of-K resampling, a Diffusion Policy, and world-model baselines fail to recover from the evaluated seam states. On a real Franka arm running a fine-tuned $\pi_{0.5}$ policy, the same monitor is limited by exterior-camera observability, yet closing the loop still recovers some otherwise-terminal failures, motivating wrist and gripper sensing. These results suggest that some long-horizon composition failures are better addressed by restoring the scene before resuming the policy than by retrying from an off-support state.

</details>


### [178] [Skill-SLM: Agent Skill-driven Small Language Models for Reliable Robot Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10812)
*Wenhao Wang,Yanyan Li,Jiawei Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Skill-SLM将小模型机器人操作重构为任务分解与技能编排问题，先用技能感知CFG抽取技能建库，再由LLM教师合成训练数据，并采用渐进式技能编排策略，在UAV任务上超过蒸馏式基线，并迁移到地面车辆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向机载机器人操作的小语言模型（SLM）方法以蒸馏为主，依赖枚举代表性任务-解决方案对，导致数据集构建困难，且难以泛化到形式快速增长的多样机器人任务。

Method: 提出Skill-SLM框架：给定自然语言任务指令，将任务分解为子任务，从技能库中选择技能并编排为可执行机器人操作。具体包括：1）提出技能感知的机器人操作上下文无关文法（CFG），用于抽取完成任务所需技能并据此构建技能库；2）配置LLM教师归纳并合成SLM训练数据集，使SLM能可靠地分解任务与编排技能；3）采用渐进式技能编排策略提升技能执行与整体操作的可靠性。

Result: 在UAV操作任务上，Skill-SLM显著优于蒸馏导向基线，尤其在需要能力泛化的未见任务上；在地面车辆任务上的额外实验表明该框架可迁移到不同机器人平台。

Conclusion: 通过任务分解+技能编排替代枚举式蒸馏，Skill-SLM提升了SLM驱动机器人操作的泛化性与可靠性，并具备跨平台适用性。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) have been increasingly adopted for onboard robot operation because they enable intelligent decision-making. However, existing approaches are mainly distillation-oriented and rely on enumerating representative task-solution pairs. This makes dataset construction difficult and limits generalization to diverse robot tasks whose possible forms grow rapidly. This paper proposes Skill-SLM, a framework that reformulates SLM-driven robot operation as a task-decomposition and skill-composition problem. Given a natural language task instruction, Skill-SLM decomposes the task into subtasks, selects appropriate skills from the skill library, and orchestrates the selected skills into executable robot operations. First, to support the skill-driven workflow, we propose a novel robot operational skill aware context-free grammar (CFG) to extract the skills required to accomplish tasks and build the skill library accordingly. Then, we configure LLM teachers to induce and synthesize training datasets for the SLMs, enabling SLMs to decompose tasks and orchestrate skills reliably. Additionally, we employ a progressive skill orchestration strategy to improve the reliability of skill implementation and overall robot operation. Experiments on UAV operation tasks indicate that Skill-SLM substantially outperforms distillation-oriented baselines, especially on unseen tasks that require generalization of capabilities. Additional experiments on ground vehicle tasks further demonstrate that Skill-SLM can be applied to different robot platforms.

</details>


### [179] [Cross-Embodiment Robot Foundation World Models with Latent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10846)
*Huang Huang,Sriram Yenamandra,Arjun Majumdar,Elie Aljalbout,Tushar Nagarajan,Tsung-Yen Yang,Akshara Rai,Michael Rabbat,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Tingfan Wu,Franziska Meier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LAC-WM（潜在动作条件机器人世界模型），在跨具身共享的统一潜在动作空间中学习，适配未见机器人时性能优于显式动作条件（EAC-WM），在灵巧操作任务上提升至多46.7%，LIBERO上提升11.7%，且随预训练具身数量增加性能正向扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人具身形态与动作空间多样，导致世界模型难以跨具身泛化；显式动作条件（运动标签）在不同具身间产生不相交的动作表示，限制了对新机器人的下游适配能力。

Method: 构建LAC-WM：在跨多样具身共享的学习型统一潜在动作空间内运行的世界模型，与基于显式运动标签的EAC-WM作对照；在灵巧操作任务及改进版LIBERO基准上评估两种模型，并考察预训练具身数量对下游性能的影响。

Result: LAC-WM相对EAC-WM在灵巧操作上提升至多46.7%、LIBERO上提升11.7%；统一潜在动作空间使下游性能随预训练具身数量增加而正向扩展，而EAC-WM的不相交动作空间随具身数量增加性能下降。

Conclusion: 统一动作空间对高效的跨具身学习至关重要，为机器人世界模型的跨具身泛化提供了有效方向。

Abstract: The diversity of robot embodiments and action spaces makes it challenging to build robot world models that generalize across different embodiments. We introduce the Latent Action-Conditioned Robot World Model (LAC-WM), which operates within a learned unified latent action space shared across diverse embodiments. This unified action space improves the world model's performance when adapted to previously unseen robot embodiments. We compare LAC-WM with an Explicit Action-Conditioned World Model (EAC-WM), which conditions on explicit motion labels. Our results show that explicit action conditioning leads to disjoint action representations across embodiments, limiting downstream performance when adapting to new robots. We evaluate both models on dexterous manipulation tasks and a modified LIBERO benchmark. LAC-WM improves downstream performance over EAC-WM by up to 46.7% on dexterous manipulation and 11.7% on LIBERO. Crucially, the unified latent action space allows LAC-WM's downstream performance to scale positively with the number of embodiments used during pretraining. In contrast, the disjoint action space in EAC-WM leads to decreased performance as the number of pretraining embodiments increases. These results highlight the importance of a unified action space for efficient cross-embodiment learning, addressing a key challenge in robotics. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [180] [OmniHOI: Dexterous Hand-Object Interaction from Monocular Human Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10855)
*Ting Mao,Yanming Shao,Ziheng Wang,Haoyu Liu,Yiqun Wang,Xuanye Wu,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniHOI 提出一个从单目 RGB 视频到多指灵巧手轨迹的流水线，通过分阶段物理一致性约束（图像证据→接触几何→动力学）实现手-物交互轨迹的忠实重建与迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目人手操作视频蕴含丰富的灵巧演示，但单视图手-物交互重建并迁移到机器人手仍很困难。现有方法要么需要任务特定的强化学习训练（扩展性差），要么假设干净的动捕轨迹（无法直接处理视频），限制了视频演示直接用于机器人执行。

Method: 核心思想是在每个阶段用该阶段可获得的证据强制物理一致性：重建阶段用图像证据，重定向阶段用接触几何，物理在环精修阶段用动力学。每个阶段直接优化对应表示，在下游误差传播之前或需被学习策略吸收之前就予以纠正。

Result: 在 150 条动捕轨迹迁移到五种灵巧手（6–22 自由度）的实验中，成功率为 39–89%，而此前迁移方法最高仅 31%。在 60 段单目视频片段上成功率达 53%，此前最佳视频到机器人流水线为 28%。其轨迹也能在真实双臂机器人上跨多类任务执行。

Conclusion: OmniHOI 无需任务特定 RL，也不依赖干净动捕，可直接从单目视频生成交互忠实的灵巧手轨迹，在仿真成功率与真机执行上均显著优于先前方法，为视频演示到机器人执行的迁移提供了可扩展路径。

Abstract: Monocular videos of human manipulation provide abundant dexterous demonstrations, yet reconstructing hand-object interaction from a single view and transferring it to robot hands remain difficult, limiting their direct use for robot execution. Prior methods either require task-specific RL training, limiting scalability, or assume clean motion-capture trajectories and thus cannot operate directly on video. We present OmniHOI, a pipeline that turns an RGB video of hand-object interaction into an interaction-faithful trajectory on dexterous hands. The key idea is to enforce physical consistency using the evidence available at each stage: image evidence during reconstruction, contact geometry during retargeting, and dynamics during physics-in-the-loop refinement. Each stage optimizes the corresponding representation directly, correcting errors before they propagate downstream or must be absorbed by a learned policy. Across 150 motion-capture trajectories transferred to each of five dexterous hands with 6 to 22 DoF, we achieve 39-89% success, compared with at most 31% for prior transfer methods. On 60 monocular video clips, we achieve 53% success, compared with 28% for the best prior video-to-robot pipeline. Its trajectories also execute on a real bimanual robot across diverse tasks.

</details>


### [181] [Informationally Decoupled Trajectory Design for Sim-to-Real System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10905)
*Sangwoo Shin,Ashvin Anilkumar,Ryan Gao,Josiah P. Hanna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IDTD 提出基于 Fisher 信息矩阵 Schur 补分数的探索目标，通过按参数归一化、择优片段与对数聚合实现信息解耦的轨迹设计，使采样式系统辨识参数可分离；在仿真与真机 K1 人形上验证，参数辨识误差平均降低 39.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样式系统辨识通过调参使仿真器复现目标系统动态，是可解释的 sim-to-real 途径；但当轨迹无法区分不同参数的影响时，多组参数均可复现同一轨迹，导致参数估计不可靠（不可辨识性）。因此需要主动设计能"分离"参数影响的激励轨迹。

Method: 提出 Informationally Decoupled Trajectory Design (IDTD) 框架：以 Fisher 信息矩阵的 Schur 补分数构造探索策略目标，反映参数在给定轨迹上的条件信息增益；为忠实体现参数可分离性，对分数按单参数信息做归一化，为每个参数挑选最有利的轨迹片段，并对各片段分数做对数聚合；优化得到的轨迹由互补区间组成，每个区间只暴露参数的一个子集，使该区间运动对这些参数的贡献可唯一归因。

Result: 在从线性动力学系统到 Go2 四足、G1 人形、Crazyflie 四旋翼的多种仿真环境中，相对最强主动探索基线，参数辨识误差平均降低 39.6%，下游策略迁移效果亦提升；并在真实 K1 人形上验证轨迹设计，辨识参数能准确刻画真实系统动力学。

Conclusion: IDTD 以信息几何视角把"参数可分离性"直接写入探索目标，显著提升采样式系统辨识的可靠性与 sim-to-real 迁移质量，并具备真机可行性。

Abstract: Sampling-based system identification estimates physically meaningful parameters by tuning a simulator to reproduce the target system dynamics, providing an interpretable approach to improving sim-to-real transfer. Yet when the collected trajectories do not distinguish the effects of different parameters, multiple parameter combinations can reproduce those trajectories, leading to unreliable parameter estimates. To address this challenge, we introduce an Informationally Decoupled Trajectory Design framework (IDTD), which formulates the objective for the exploration policy built on the Schur complement score derived from the Fisher information matrix. To faithfully reflect parameter separability in the exploration objective, IDTD normalizes the score against the per-parameter information, selects the most favorable trajectory segment for each parameter, and aggregates the resulting scores logarithmically. The optimized trajectory is therefore composed of complementary intervals, each exposing a distinct subset of parameters whose contribution to the motion over that interval can be attributed unambiguously. Across diverse simulation environments, ranging from a linear-dynamics system to the Go2 quadruped, G1 humanoid, and Crazyflie quadrotor, IDTD reduces the parameter identification error by 39.6% on average relative to the strongest prior active-exploration baseline and attains improved downstream policy transfer. Furthermore, we validate the proposed trajectory design on a real K1 humanoid, demonstrating that the resulting identified parameters accurately capture the real-system dynamics.

</details>


### [182] [When Listening Becomes Easier: Scrubbing Visual Cues for Shortcut-Free VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10912)
*Jasper Gerigk,Kenzo Aspuru-Takata,Chin-Hsuan Wu,Mohammad Mohammadi,Shuhong Zheng,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究VLA模型中的视觉捷径学习问题，发现不同VLM主干对视觉捷径的敏感度差异显著，并提出无需rollout的表征级指标"action margin"来预测该行为；进一步提出领域对抗训练方法task scrubbing，提升语言关注度、抑制视觉捷径并改善分布外泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人示范数据多样性有限，策略易利用视角、背景等与任务无关的虚假视觉关联（捷径学习）。而收集多样化示范成本高、效率低，因此需要算法层面的替代方案。作者聚焦VLA模型，试图从表征层面理解并抑制视觉捷径学习。

Method: 1) 提出表征级指标action margin，无需策略rollout即可量化模型对视觉捷径的易感性，并揭示其与模型行为的关联；2) 分析发现视觉捷径在浅层即进入动作表征，不同模型在后续层利用语言信息纠正的程度不同；3) 提出task scrubbing——一种领域对抗训练方法，通过降低模型使用视觉捷径的可能性来增强对语言指令的注意力。

Result: 在仿真与真实环境中、跨多个VLA模型和多种视觉线索的实验表明，task scrubbing提升了分布外鲁棒性，并常常消除视觉捷径学习；action margin指标与模型行为表现出一致相关性。

Conclusion: 视觉捷径是VLA模型的关键失效模式，且不同VLM主干的可纠正性存在结构性差异；通过表征级诊断（action margin）与训练层面的干预（task scrubbing）可有效提升VLA的泛化能力，为数据受限条件下的机器人学习提供算法替代路径。

Abstract: Shortcut learning is a prevalent issue in robot learning. The limited diversity of robot demonstration datasets can mislead policies into exploiting spurious correlations between tasks and irrelevant features, such as viewpoint or background. Collecting sufficiently diverse robot demonstrations is costly and inefficient, motivating algorithmic alternatives. We focus on vision-language-action (VLA) models and discover that different vision-language model backbones exhibit substantially different levels of susceptibility to visual shortcut learning. We find that model behavior correlates with our proposed representation-level metric, action margin, which requires no policy rollouts. Visual shortcuts consistently enter action representations in early layers, with models differing in the extent to which later layers correct them by incorporating language information. To boost models' attention to language, we introduce task scrubbing, a new domain-adversarial training method that decreases models' likelihood of using visual shortcuts and improves VLAs' generalization. Experiments in both simulation and the real world across multiple VLAs and visual cues show that task scrubbing improves out-of-distribution robustness and often eliminates visual shortcut learning.

</details>


### [183] [Higher-Order Morphology Priors for Quadruped Reinforcement Learning Under Actuator Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10934)
*Derek You,Zafir Shamsi,Keqin Wang,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将Unitree Go1建模为具有肢级和躯干级rank-2单元的胞腔复形，用Hodge消息传递策略学习退化条件下的全身协调补偿，在未见执行器退化上取得最高回报、更高存活率与更低速度跟踪误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行器退化使四足运动变为需关节间协调补偿的协调问题。已有工作表明形态感知图策略能提升身体扰动下的学习与泛化，作者进一步追问：显式建模高阶力学结构（面元/胞腔）能否强化这一收益。

Method: 把Unitree Go1表示为胞腔复形，构造肢级（limb-level）与躯干级（body-level）的rank-2单元，从而同时含节点、边、面三层结构；在退化训练设定下应用Hodge分解/上下同调算子进行消息传递，得到node-edge-face Hodge actor，与仅基于图（节点-边）的策略对比。

Result: 在退化训练下，node-edge-face Hodge actor在未见的执行器退化配置上取得最高回报，同时存活率更高、速度跟踪误差更低，优于仅节点-边的拓扑消息传递基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明高阶形态（面/胞腔级结构）可作为有效归纳偏置，用于执行器退化下的全身补偿控制，为形态感知策略设计提供了高阶拓扑建模方向。

Abstract: Actuator degradation turns quadruped locomotion into a coordination problem requiring joints to compensate for lost actuation. Prior work suggests that morphology-aware graph policies improve learning and generalization under body perturbations. We ask whether these benefits can be strengthened by explicitly modeling higher-order mechanical structure. We represent the Unitree Go1 as a cell complex with limb- and body-level rank-2 cells and apply Hodge-based message passing. Under degradation training, the node-edge-face Hodge actor achieves the highest return on unseen actuator degradations, with higher survival and lower velocity-tracking error. These results support higher-order morphology as a useful inductive bias for whole-body compensation under actuator degradation.

</details>


### [184] [iAm.md: Robot Skill Self-Assessment through Agentic Introspection for Unknown Open-Vocabulary Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10962)
*Vincenzo Guarino,Emanuele Musumeci,Vincenzo Suriani,Daniele Nardi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于Markdown标准与生成框架的Agentic AI方法，通过开放词汇语义建图将视觉-语言检测与目标分割锚定为持久对象记录，缓解机器人行为生成中的“grounding failure”，并在仿真TIAGo上验证技能自评与可执行任务泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于基础模型的具身智能体虽能生成可行计划，但受上下文窗口限制与下一词预测的幻觉影响，生成的行为可能未核实机器人本体与观测环境是否真正支持该操作，即grounding failure。作者希望借助基础模型推理能力的提升，把自主机器人行为生成形式化为代码生成问题，并用互补的部署证据约束该过程。

Method: 提出一种Markdown标准与生成框架，将行为生成锚定在互补形式的部署证据上。通过开放词汇语义建图，融合局部视觉-语言检测与目标分割，并将结果关联到中间标准化表示中的持久对象记录，从而支持智能体的自我内省（agentic introspection）。

Result: 在仿真TIAGo平台上针对导航与操作任务进行了研究，表明该标准化表示能够同时支持技能自评估与可执行任务泛化。

Conclusion: 该标准化表示可作为代码生成式机器人行为生成的接地中介，联合促进技能自评与任务泛化，但验证仅限于仿真环境，尚缺真实机器人部署证据。

Abstract: Agentic AI based on Large Language Model generalization capabilities offers a wide range of potential applications, including planning for embodied tasks. For example, embodied agents based on Foundation models can generate plausible plans in autonomous robotics scenarios. Due to limited context windows or hallucinatory phenomena in the next-token prediction formulation, behaviors may be generated without establishing whether the deployed robot and the observed environment actually support the requested operation, in what we call a "grounding failure". Thanks to the recent improvements in reasoning capabilities of foundation models, autonomous robot behavior generation problem can be formulated as a code generation problem. We present this http URL , a Markdown standard and generation framework, that allows anchoring this process in complementary forms of deployment evidence. Through open-vocabulary semantic mapping, we combine local vision-language detections and object segmentation and refer them to persistent object records in this intermediate standardized representation, allowing agentic introspection. We then study this new technique on a simulated TIAGo, on navigation-and-manipulation tasks, showing how this standardized representation jointly supports skill self-assessment and executable task generalization.

</details>


### [185] [ActiveReg: Information-Driven Active Regional Probing for Partial-to-Full Bone Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11003)
*Tiancheng Li,Yingyu Wang,Peter Walker,Liang Zhao,Shoudong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActiveReg：闭环主动配准框架，以"推荐探测区域"替代"指定点"，结合D-最优规划与无真值在线评估，在4个解剖场景仿真和光学/电磁追踪体模实验中用24–28个点达到与Gradient-SDF相当的配准精度（基准需771–1263点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骨科手术导航与机器人辅助中，骨配准需在有限暴露的骨面上采集大量规定点，耗时且困难；减少点数同时给出清晰探测引导可显著减轻术中采集负担。

Method: ActiveReg闭环框架：(1) D-最优（D-optimal）规划器，利用已采集点与当前配准不确定度，推荐可灵活接触的探测区域而非单点；(2) 在线评估将更新前新息一致性（pre-update innovation consistency）与预设手术任务点处的不确定度结合，在无需真值条件下判定采集是否完成。

Result: 4个解剖场景仿真显示，在点数大幅减少的情况下达到与基线配准方法相当的精度；光学与电磁追踪体模实验中，仅用24–28个采集点即取得与Gradient-SDF框架相当的平均目标配准误差（后者需771–1263点）。

Conclusion: 区域级主动探测规划配合自监督式完成判据，可有效压缩术中配准所需数据量并提升引导灵活性，具备手术机器人集成的应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate bone registration is essential for orthopedic surgical navigation and robotic assistance. In a common workflow, the surgeon needs to identify and probe a number of prescribed locations on the exposed bone surface to obtain data points for registration, which can be difficult and time-consuming under limited surgical exposure. Reducing the number of required points while providing clear probing guidance would ease the surgeon's acquisition task intra-operatively. We present ActiveReg, a closed-loop framework that recommends probing regions rather than individual points, allowing flexibility in the exact contact location. A D-optimal planner that takes into account the already acquired points and the uncertainty of current registration information helps achieve accurate registration with fewer points. An online assessment combines pre-update innovation consistency with uncertainty at predefined surgical task locations improves the reliability of completion decision, without requiring ground truth. Simulations across four anatomical scenarios demonstrated that ActiveReg can achieve competitive accuracy compared with baseline registration methods while using substantially fewer acquired points. Real phantom experiments with optical and electromagnetic tracking demonstrated comparable mean target registration error to the Gradient-SDF framework, using only 24-28 acquired points instead of 771-1263.

</details>


### [186] [A Reconfigurable Fabric Based Pneumatic Actuator with Button Fastened Constraint Modules for Multi Mode Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11054)
*Kentaro Kimura,Hiroki Ishizuka,Yusuke Sakaue,Sei Ikeda,Osamu Oshiro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可重构织物气动执行器：在带扣织物套上更换低拉伸约束模块，即可在单一执行器上实现各向同性膨胀、收缩、弯曲、扭转四种驱动模式，并附加可贴装电容式织物触觉传感器；24 kPa下伸长率1.2、弯曲约60°、扭转约90°，堵转力>10 N，传感器迟滞约12%；多执行器软臂可原位换构型抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软气动执行器的驱动模式通常由内腔结构或增强层固化决定，制造后无法低成本、无工具地重构，导致同一执行器功能单一、任务适应性差；同时织物执行器缺乏可按需布置的集成触觉感知。

Method: 结构上采用带扣（buttoned）织物套筒作为通用执行器主体，通过在其表面可拆卸、可重复贴装的低拉伸约束织物模块的排布图案来定义形变模式，无需重新设计或拆解本体即可在膨胀/收缩/弯曲/扭转间切换；感知上设计可贴附的电容式织物触觉传感模块，可安装在执行器表面任意位置进行任务相关接触测量。

Result: 24 kPa气压下伸长率1.2、最大弯曲角约60°、扭转角约90°，四种模式堵转力均超过10 N；触觉传感器电容随力单调增加，迟滞约12%，预载下循环响应稳定；由多执行器构成的软体机械臂通过更换约束图案实现原位运动重构并完成抓取。

Conclusion: 模块化约束+可贴装传感的框架在保持执行器结构简单、鲁棒的前提下提升了织物软体机器人的功能可重构性与适应性，为多模式软执行器与分布式触觉感知提供了一种低成本、免工具、可复用的实现路径。

Abstract: Soft pneumatic actuators are widely used in soft robotics. However, their actuation modes are typically fixed by internal chamber designs or reinforcements, making post fabrication reconfiguration difficult. This paper presents a reconfigurable fabric-based pneumatic actuator that enables rapid, tool free, and reusable switching among multiple actuation modes by attaching constraint fabric modules to a buttoned textile sleeve. By rearranging low-stretch constraint parts on a single actuator body, the proposed system realizes four actuation modes isotropic expansion, contraction, bending, and twisting without redesigning or disassembling the this http URL further introduce an attachable capacitive fabric-based tactile sensor module that can be mounted at arbitrary locations on the actuator surface for task dependent contact sensing. Experiments demonstrated an elongation ratio of 1.2, a maximum bending angle of approximately 60 degrees, and a twisting angle of approximately 90 degrees at 24 kPa, while achieving blocking forces exceeding 10 N across all modes. The tactile sensor exhibited a monotonic, force-dependent increase in capacitance with low hysteresis (approximately 12%) and stable cyclic responses under preload. Finally, we constructed soft robotic arms composed of multiple actuators and demonstrated in situ motion reconfiguration for grasping by changing the attached constraint patterns. These results indicate that the proposed modular constraint and sensing framework improves the functional adaptability of fabric-based soft robots while preserving a simple, robust actuator structure.

</details>


### [187] [Towards Path-Creative Navigation: Robot Navigation through Embodied Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11072)
*Haoyu Xi,Siwei Cheng,Xiangyuan Liu,Brenda Li,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Path-Creative Navigation (PCN) 范式，用无地图视觉-激光-里程计框架协调移动与具身交互，通过推门/推物/避障/请求行人让路主动创造通行空间，在仿真与两项真机任务中超越传统导航基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统杂乱受限环境导航默认自由空间固定，只在既有空闲空间内搜索路径；但通路可能被铰接结构、可移动物体或行人阻塞，必须与环境发生具身交互才能到达目标，缺少统一处理"主动创造空间"的导航范式。

Method: 形式化定义 PCN 任务并提出无地图框架：视觉-LiDAR-里程计统一导航系统融合局部观测、障碍物几何与机器人状态估计，提供稳定的语义与几何信息；可通行性感知决策模块利用视觉与 LiDAR 距离信息判断阻塞是否可被作用、是否可安全绕行，从而仅在必要时交互。

Result: 设计了仿真场景，并在仿真与两个传统导航无法单独完成的真机任务上对比多种方法；框架任务完成率高于基线方法，证明协调导航与交互的有效性，并公开代码。

Conclusion: PCN 表明将具身交互纳入导航决策可显著提升杂乱受限环境下的任务完成率，无地图统一感知-决策框架是可行且可落地的一类方案。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in cluttered and constrained environments typically assumes a fixed environment and searches only for paths within existing free space. However, a route can be blocked by an articulated structure, a movable object, or a pedestrian, and reaching the goal may therefore require appropriate embodied interaction with the environment. This paper formulates Path-Creative Navigation (PCN), a navigation paradigm in which the robot recovers free space by coordinating locomotion and embodied interaction, and addresses one class of PCN tasks with a mapless framework. The vision-LiDAR-odometry framework integrates local observations, obstacle geometry, and robot-state estimates through a unified navigation system, providing stable semantic and geometric information for navigation and interaction. The traversability-aware decision method utilizes visual and LiDAR distance information to determine whether a blockage is actionable and whether it can be safely bypassed, enabling the robot to interact only when necessary while supporting articulated structure pushing, movable object pushing, obstacle avoidance, and pedestrian requests. We design simulation scenarios and evaluate different methods in both simulation and two real-world tasks that cannot be completed by conventional navigation alone without embodied interaction. These results demonstrate that our framework achieves higher task completion than the baseline methods by coordinating navigation and interaction in cluttered and constrained environments. Code is available at:{ this https URL }.

</details>


### [188] [FOCUS: From Privileged States to RGB-D with Controlled Modality Switching and Representation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11119)
*Filip Grigorov,Kourosh Darvish,Nandita Vijaykumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FOCUS: 单阶段 PPO 框架，用特权状态训练 critic，并通过两模态动作分布 KL 散度自动调节 actor 的 rollout 来自 RGB-D 还是特权状态隐变量，配合表征对齐，逐步将 on-policy 训练转向测试时使用的 RGB-D 输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉的机器人操作强化学习因 RGB-D 观测高维、噪声大而样本效率低；仿真中可用的特权状态能加速训练，但测试时缺失，造成训练-测试模态差距（train-test modality gap）。

Method: 单阶段 PPO。critic 用特权状态训练；actor 的 rollout 采集源（RGB-D 隐变量或特权状态隐变量）由两模态动作分布间的 KL 散度自动调节；同时施加表征对齐以促使跨模态动作选择一致。KL 不一致时限制 RGB-D rollout，对齐后增加 RGB-D 曝光，使 on-policy 训练逐步迁移到测试时输入。

Result: 五个操作任务上，相对各任务最强"测试用 RGB-D"基线，平均测试成功率由 0.71 提升至 0.93；预算归一化训练-成功 AUC 由 0.47 提升至 0.65。Pick-and-Place 上测试成功率 0.47→0.86，AUC 0.12→0.61，学习效率提升 5.0 倍。

Conclusion: 通过 KL 驱动的 rollout 模态调度与跨模态表征对齐，可在单阶段 on-policy 框架内低成本弥合特权状态与视觉观测的模态差距，显著提升视觉操作策略的样本效率与最终成功率。

Abstract: Vision-based reinforcement learning for robotic manipulation is sample-inefficient because RGB-D observations are high-dimensional and noisy. Privileged state information available in simulation can accelerate training, but its absence at test time creates a train-test modality gap. We propose FOCUS, a single-stage PPO framework that trains the critic on privileged state while automatically regulating whether the actor collects rollouts from RGB-D or privileged-state latents. Regulation is driven by the KL divergence between the action distributions induced by the two modalities, while representation alignment encourages consistent action selection across them. Together, these mechanisms limit RGB-D rollouts when the actor's action distributions from RGB-D and privileged-state latents disagree. As they align, RGB-D exposure increases, shifting on-policy training toward the RGB-D inputs used at test time. Across five manipulation tasks, FOCUS raises average test success from 0.71 to 0.93 relative to the strongest RGB-D-at-test baseline on each task. When accounting for each method's complete training pipeline, budget-normalized training-success AUC increases from 0.47 to 0.65. On Pick-and-Place, test success rises from 0.47 to 0.86, while AUC increases from 0.12 to 0.61, a 5.0x improvement in learning efficiency over the fixed interaction budget.

</details>


### [189] [Distributed Relative Localization Based on Ultra-WideBand and LiDAR for Multi-robot with Limited Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11141)
*Zhiqiang Cao,Ran Liu,Billy Pik Lik Lau,Chau Yuen,U-Xuan Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于机载UWB与LiDAR，构建UWB连接图与LiDAR连接图，将匿名目标识别与相对位姿估计建模为公共子图匹配问题，并设计误匹配机器人识别方法以处理遮挡，实现无GPS、通信受限下同构多机器人团队的分布式相对定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同构机器人在GPS拒止、通信受限环境下外观相似，难以区分身份，而多机器人协同探索与编队又依赖相对定位，因此需要一种仅依赖机载UWB测距与LiDAR观测的完全分布式相对位置估计方法。

Method: 利用LiDAR获取视距内匿名目标位置，UWB进行机器人间测距；构建UWB连接图与LiDAR连接图表示机器人与障碍物间的空间关系；将识别与相对位姿估计转化为公共子图匹配问题；针对LiDAR视场遮挡导致的误匹配，设计误匹配机器人识别方法，并借助匹配正确的机器人与UWB测距图对其重新定位。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在仅交换有限信息的分布式条件下，可为机器人团队取得令人满意的定位精度。

Conclusion: 图匹配框架结合UWB与LiDAR的互补性（UWB测距不受遮挡、LiDAR提供视距方位），可有效缓解同构机器人匿名性与遮挡误匹配问题，实现低成本、可扩展的分布式相对定位。

Abstract: Relative localization is crucial for a multi-robot system to collaboratively perform tasks, such as exploration and formation. However, this is highly challenging for homogeneous robots with similar appearance in GPS-denied and communication-limited environments. In this paper, we propose a fully distributed relative position estimation approach for a team of robots based on onboard UWB and LiDAR sensors, in which LiDAR is utilized to obtain the position of anonymous objects in Line-of-Sight (LOS), and UWB is used for ranging between robots. We construct two graphs, namely UWB connection graph and LiDAR connection graph, to represent the spatial relationship among objects (robots and obstacles) based on UWB and LiDAR measurements. Identification and relative position estimation are formulated as a common subgraph matching problem. A falsely-matched robot identification approach is designed to recognize the falsely-matched results caused by obstacle blockage in LiDAR field of view. These robots are then localized by leveraging the well-matched robots and the UWB ranging measurements in the UWB connection graph. We conducted experiments to evaluate the performance of our approach. The results show that the proposed approach is capable of achieving satisfactory positioning accuracy for a team of robots in a distributed manner with only exchanging limited information.

</details>


### [190] [PMTRM: Pseudo-Memory Temporal Re-encoding Module for Embodied Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11168)
*Changchuan Yang,Haoxuan Xu,Wenbo Chen,Shuai Ren,Jianlong Zheng,Huarui Zhang,Tianfu Li,Guanzhong Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出轻量插件模块PMTRM（7.61M参数），将已执行状态-动作的历史编码为潜在序列，缓解机器人操作中"相位歧义"导致的动作重复/相位切换错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中常存在重复性动作，不同相位下局部观测高度相似但所需动作不同，仅依赖当前观测的策略容易重复已完成动作或错误切换相位，即"相位歧义"问题。

Method: PMTRM将有限长度的已执行状态-动作历史编码为潜在序列供已有策略使用；设计时间异质性目标惩罚序列远距离位置的相似性以区分相位，并用锚点损失与重建损失保留动作预测所需信息；重建解码器仅训练时使用。采用合成序列→机器人数据→与策略联合训练的三阶段渐进训练，配合时间掩码处理不完整历史；保留原动作头与动作空间，仅在原策略损失上加入辅助损失。

Result: 在仿真中多种策略主干以及真实机器人上实验，相位歧义任务的成功率提升，额外计算开销很小。

Conclusion: 通过显式编码动作历史并以时间异质性约束区分相位，可作为即插即用模块提升现有策略在重复性相位歧义任务上的表现，且推理阶段无额外解码器负担。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation often contains repeated motions whose local observations look similar at different phases. When these phases require different actions, a policy that relies mainly on the current observation may repeat completed motions or switch phases at the wrong time. To address this phase ambiguity, we present the Pseudo-Memory Temporal Re-encoding Module (PMTRM), a lightweight plug-in module with only 7.61M parameters that encodes a bounded history of executed states and actions into a latent sequence for existing policies. To help distinguish phases, a temporal heterogeneity objective penalizes positive similarity between distant positions in this sequence, while anchor and reconstruction losses preserve information needed for action prediction. The reconstruction decoder is used only during training, leaving the temporal re-encoder to supply history to the policy at inference. We train the module progressively on synthetic sequences and robot data, then jointly with the policy, using temporal masking to accommodate partial histories. This integration retains the original action head and action space and adds auxiliary losses to the original policy loss. Experiments with multiple policy backbones in simulation and on a real robot show improved task success on tasks with phase ambiguity, with little additional computation.

</details>


### [191] [Higher-Order Action Supervision Makes A Strong Policy Class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11175)
*Peng Cheng,Yunxian Hou,Zhi Zhou,Qian Zhang,Chang Huang,Xianyuan Zhan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种带理论保证的损失方案，让任意现成策略（确定/随机/流）同时监督零阶与一阶动作，无需改结构即可作为即插即用模块嵌入离线RL框架，在OGBench、D4RL上显著提升性能、鲁棒性与低数据OOD泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习与强化学习在机器人、自动驾驶等真实场景中普遍存在控制不稳定与鲁棒性差的问题。作者认为根源在于现有方法仅监督和优化零阶动作（动作标签），忽略了高阶动作动态（如动作的一阶导数/时序一致性）。

Method: 在策略学习目标中引入高阶动作监督：同时监督零阶与一阶动作。设计一种新的损失方案，并给出形式化理论保证，使任意现成策略模型（确定性、随机、流策略）无需结构性改动即可具备高阶动作监督能力；该模块轻量、即插即用，可集成到多种离线RL框架中。

Result: 在OGBench与D4RL的广泛连续控制环境中，方法在性能与鲁棒性上均取得显著提升；在低数据情形下还增强了策略的分布外（OOD）泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过同时监督零阶与一阶动作，可为通用策略模型提供高阶动作监督并带来控制稳定性与鲁棒性的大幅改善，作为轻量即插即用模块适用于广泛的离线RL框架，是应对真实世界控制问题的有效工具。

Abstract: Modern data-driven decision-making methods, such as imitation learning (IL) and reinforcement learning (RL), have achieved great success in solving many complex tasks. However, these methods often suffer from serious control instability and robustness issues when applied in real-world applications such as robotics and autonomous driving, posing notable challenges for their practical deployment. We argue that this instability issue stems largely from their limitations in solely supervising and optimizing zeroth-order actions (i.e., the action labels), failing to account for higher-order action dynamics and temporal consistency. In this paper, we show that simultaneously supervising both zeroth- and first-order actions can dramatically enhance policies' performance and control robustness. To achieve this, we introduce a novel and elegant loss scheme supported by formal theoretical guarantees that can equip any off-the-shelf policy model (e.g., deterministic, stochastic, or flow policies) with the capability for higher-order action supervision, without requiring any structural modifications. Moreover, our proposed method can serve as a lightweight plug-and-play module that seamlessly integrates with a broad spectrum of existing offline RL frameworks. Extensive evaluations on OGBench and D4RL demonstrate that our approach yields substantial performance and robustness improvements across a wide range of continuous control environments. Notably, our method can also enhance policies' out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization capability in the challenging low-data regime, making it an ideal tool in tackling many real-world control problems.

</details>


### [192] [OmniDex: Scaling Dexterous Hand Grasping to Diverse Cluttered Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11194)
*Naiyu Fang,Zhongjin Luo,Yuxin Mo,Siyuan Huang,Jianbo Liu,Yufei Liu,Zheyuan Zhou,Chenkai Jin,Xiaogang Wang,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 seed-and-filter 场景生成策略，构建含 260 万+杂乱场景、4 亿抓取真值的大规模灵巧抓取基准，并提出 OmniDex 生成式模型（Soft WTA + 物理约束 + 物理驱动排序），实现免后优化的高精度灵巧抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱场景最贴近真实应用，但场景级数据采集与仿真优化成本极高，导致大规模杂乱场景灵巧抓取数据严重稀缺；同时现有生成式抓取模型存在抓取多模态性与"最后一毫米"精度误差问题。

Method: 1) 筛选高质量 3D 物体与承载基座，以 seed-and-filter 可扩展策略绕过耗时的场景级优化，批量合成杂乱场景及场景特定抓取真值；2) OmniDex 模型：训练中结合 Soft Winner-Takes-All 学习与类人物理约束以建模抓取多模态，推理时用物理驱动排序筛选，无需后优化即可保证精度。

Result: 构建超 260 万场景、4 亿场景特定抓取真值的基准，含多样真实布局与丰富语义/几何观测；OmniDex 在多种场景、视角与未见物体上达到 SOTA 性能与强泛化，且无后优化延迟。

Conclusion: 大规模杂乱场景数据可由高效合成策略低成本获得，配合多模态建模与物理约束的生成式策略，可在无后优化条件下实现鲁棒、可泛化的灵巧抓取。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping is the foundational primitive in embodied AI, demanding massive data to train robust models. As real-world data collection is expensive, simulation has become the mainstream paradigm. Yet, while cluttered scenes best reflect real-world applications, learning to grasp within them is bottlenecked by a critical scarcity of large-scale data. To resolve this, we curate high-quality 3D objects and supporting bases, proposing a scalable seed-and-filter strategy that bypasses sluggish scene-level optimization. This yields an unprecedented benchmark comprising over 2.6 million scenes and 0.4B scene-specific grasp ground truths, featuring diverse realistic layouts paired with rich semantic and geometric observations. Furthermore, we introduce the OmniDex model to overcome the grasp multimodality and last-millimeter precision errors plaguing current generative models. By coupling Soft Winner-Takes-All learning with human-inspired physical constraints during training, and utilizing physics-driven ranking, our approach achieves robust dexterous grasping without the latency of post-optimization. Experimental results show that OmniDex model achieves state-of-the-art performance and strong generalization across diverse scenes, views, and unseen objects.

</details>


### [193] [Demonstrating Arena 5.0: A Photorealistic ROS2 Simulation Framework for Developing and Benchmarking Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11220)
*Volodymyr Shcherbyna,Linh Kästner,Duc Anh Do,Hoang Tung,Huu Giang Nguyen,Maximilian Ho-Kyoung Schreff,Tim Seeger,Eva Wiese,Ahmed Martban,Huajian Zeng,An Tran,Nguyen Quoc Hung,Jonas Kreutz,Vu Thanh Lam,Ton Manh Kien,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Arena 5.0：集成 NVIDIA Isaac Gym 的机器人社会导航开发与基准测试框架，提供 SOTA 策略基准与场景/任务生成模块，并通过用户研究验证可用性与效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有社会导航研究缺乏统一、可复现且高保真度的开发与评测平台；前代 Arena 在仿真真实感、训练效率与场景定制能力上存在不足，需要兼顾物理真实感、可扩展基准与多样化场景生成。

Method: 将 Isaac Gym 完整集成进 Arena 平台，复用随机环境生成、评测工具、ROS2 支持及规划器/机器人模型/API 模块；构建涵盖多难度生成与定制世界的社会导航策略基准，配套社会导航指标体系；新增场景生成与任务规划模块（如应急与救援场景）。

Result: 在生成的多场景基准上完成多种 SOTA 社会导航策略的系统性评测，并结合用户研究显示相较前代版本在可用性与效率上有显著提升。

Conclusion: Arena 5.0 提供了开源、可定制的社会导航仿真与基准平台，兼具高保真仿真、高效训练与丰富场景生成能力，为社区提供统一评测基准。

Abstract: Building upon the foundations laid by our previous work, this paper introduces Arena 5.0, the fifth iteration of our framework for robotics social navigation development and benchmarking. Arena 5.0 provides three main contributions: 1) The complete integration of NVIDIA Isaac Gym, enabling photorealistic simulations and more efficient training. It seamlessly incorporates Isaac Gym into the Arena platform, allowing the use of existing modules such as randomized environment generation, evaluation tools, ROS2 support, and the integration of planners, robot models, and APIs within Isaac Gym. 2) A comprehensive benchmark of state-of-the-art social navigation strategies, evaluated on a diverse set of generated and customized worlds and scenarios of varying difficulty levels. These benchmarks provide a detailed assessment of navigation planners using a wide range of social navigation metrics. 3) Extensive scenario generation and task planning modules for improved and customizable generation of social navigation scenarios, such as emergency and rescue situations. The platform's performance was evaluated by generating the aforementioned benchmark and through a comprehensive user study, demonstrating significant improvements in usability and efficiency compared to previous versions. Arena 5.0 is open source and available at this https URL .

</details>


### [194] [How Firm Should a Grasp Be?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11221)
*Matthew Beveridge,Shree K. Nayar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出视觉-触觉实时估计物体材质属性（质量、弹性、摩擦）的方法，据此计算最小抓取力，并用平行夹爪机器人验证轻柔抓取多种果蔬。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想抓取需兼顾牢固与不损伤物体，这依赖物体的质量、弹性、表面摩擦等材质属性，而这些属性通常无法精确先验获知。

Method: 构建含真实物体（果蔬）已测物理属性（形状、质量、弹性、摩擦）的新数据集；基于仿真构建抓取力估计模型；采用视觉-触觉融合在抓取过程中实时估计材质属性并据此确定最小抓取力。

Result: 在配备平行夹爪的机器人上完成抓取力控制的实验验证，系统能够轻柔抓取多种物体并自适应其各自物理属性。

Conclusion: 基于视觉-触觉的实时材质属性估计可有效指导最小抓取力控制，实现兼具稳健与轻柔的自适应抓取。

Abstract: An ideal robot grasp is firm enough to securely handle an object, yet gentle enough to avoid damaging it. Achieving this balance requires knowledge of the object's material properties, such as its mass, elasticity, and surface friction. These properties, however, are seldom precisely known a priori. In this work, we propose a visuotactile approach to estimating material properties in real time, during the process of grasping. Our method uses these estimated properties to determine the minimum grasp force required to handle the object. We contribute a new dataset of real-world objects (fruits and vegetables) with measured physical properties (shape, mass, elasticity, and friction), which we use to construct our force estimation model via simulations. We experimentally validate our approach to grasp force control using a robot with a parallel-jaw gripper. We demonstrate our system's ability to gently grasp a wide variety of objects, in each case adapting to their unique physical properties.

</details>


### [195] [SafeInferCom: Safe Inference-Time Compute via Verifier-Guided Mid-Generation Intervention for Robotic Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11223)
*Weizhe Xu,Jialiang Fan,Mengyu Liu,Fanxin Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出推理时监控器与形式验证引导框架 SafeInferCom，在不干扰解码轨迹的前提下验证中间规划并引导纠错，提升 LRLM 机器人任务规划的可靠性并降低 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理语言模型（LRLM）用于机器人任务规划时存在两个问题：持续推理会覆盖有效的中间计划，或使约束违例无法解决，导致规划可靠性下降并浪费推理算力。

Method: 设计一个推理时监控器，暴露并验证中间计划而不打断原始解码轨迹；在其上构建 SafeInferCom——形式验证器引导的框架，保留有效中间计划并在生成过程中进行定向纠错。

Result: 在多个 LRLM 与规划域上实验，揭示了一次性推理下的推理-回答不一致与自纠错能力有限；SafeInferCom 相比一次性推理提升规划成功率并加快纠错。与迭代精化结合时，成功率进一步提升且 token 用量低于单独精化。另在 VirtualHome 评测并给出真实机械臂演示。

Conclusion: 形式验证引导的推理时干预可有效提升 LRLM 规划可靠性并减少推理开销，具备仿真与真实机器人验证。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Language Models (LRLMs) enable multi-step reasoning for robotic task planning, but continued reasoning can overwrite valid intermediate plans or leave constraint violations unresolved, reducing planning reliability and wasting inference-time computation. We develop an inference-time monitor that exposes and verifies intermediate plans without disrupting the original decoding trajectory. Building on this monitor, we propose SafeInferCom, a formal verifier-guided framework that preserves valid intermediate plans and directs error correction during generation. Experiments across multiple LRLMs and planning domains reveal reasoning-response inconsistency and limited self-correction under one-shot inference. SafeInferCom improves planning success and accelerates error correction relative to one-shot inference. When combined with iterative refinement, it further improves success while reducing token usage compared with refinement alone. We additionally evaluate SafeInferCom in VirtualHome and provide a real-world robotic-arm demonstration.

</details>


### [196] [SimVLA: Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real VLA Learning for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11248)
*Kyoungin Baik,Youngwoon Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimVLA 提出一个完全基于仿真数据、无需遥操作的端到端 VLA 框架，通过 SimAction 与 SimVQA 预训练并结合 SimDeploy 策略 rollout 后训练，在补货、倒水、清洁等任务上实现零样本真机迁移，性能超过用 50 条域内真机演示训练的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迈向可扩展的移动操作 VLA 学习：真实世界数据采集成本高、复杂度大，而仿真在移动操作 sim-to-real VLA 学习中的潜力尚未被充分挖掘，尤其是无需遥操作、纯合成数据的端到端训练路径缺乏系统探索。

Method: 端到端框架 SimVLA：(1) 预训练于两个互补仿真数据集——SimAction（由原子技能组合生成的 35 类移动操作任务大规模机器人动作数据）与 SimVQA（利用仿真器特权状态提供空间、几何与子任务级视觉-语言监督）；(2) 在 SimAction 与 SimDeploy（多样仿真环境下策略 rollout 采集的数据）的混合数据上后训练。

Result: 在补货、倒水、清洁等任务上评测，实现到真实世界移动操作（含真实家庭环境）的零样本迁移；性能超过基于 50 条域内真机演示训练的策略，并验证了多种互补监督形式对有效利用仿真的价值。

Conclusion: 仿真可以支撑可扩展的 sim-to-real 移动操作 VLA 学习，纯合成数据 + 多层次互补监督（动作、空间几何、子任务语言）是替代昂贵真机数据采集的可行路径。

Abstract: Large-scale, diverse datasets have driven the success of LLMs and VLMs. But VLAs for robotics remain limited by the cost and complexity of real-world data collection. While simulation offers a scalable alternative, its potential for sim-to-real VLA learning in mobile manipulation remains largely underexplored. We introduce SimVLA, an end-to-end framework that trains VLAs entirely on synthetic simulation data without teleoperation for mobile manipulation. SimVLA is first pre-trained on two complementary simulation-derived datasets: SimAction, a large-scale robot action dataset spanning 35 diverse mobile manipulation tasks, generated by composing atomic skills, and SimVQA, which leverages privileged simulator state to provide spatial, geometric, and subtask-level visual-language supervision. We further post-train SimVLA on a mixture of SimAction and SimDeploy, a dataset collected from policy rollouts across diverse simulated environments. We evaluate SimVLA on tasks including restocking, pouring, and cleaning, and show zero-shot transfer to real-world mobile manipulation, including real home environments. SimVLA outperforms policies trained on 50 in-domain real-world demonstrations, suggesting that simulation can enable scalable sim-to-real mobile manipulation. We further demonstrate the value of multiple complementary forms of supervision for effectively leveraging simulation in VLA training.

</details>


### [197] [Being-M0.7: A Latent World-Action Model for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11283)
*Junpeng Yue,Boyuan Li,Yuxuan Wang,Zepeng Wang,Yuhui Fu,Feiyang Xie,Yu Zhang,Jing Zhang,Xianqi Zhang,Weibo Li,Xiaofei Zheng,Yuming Fang,Jiangxing Wang,Zongqing Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出隐空间世界-动作模型 Being-M0.7，利用混合模态人类数据预训练、机器人中期训练与动作后训练，实现人形机器人全身移动操作控制，并在仿真与真机上取得领先成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形移动操作需协调运动与操作，且需预判场景演化与全身运动，但机器人示教数据稀缺。人类视频与运动数据可提供可扩展监督，然而现有数据多为单一模态（仅视频或仅运动），缺乏配对视频-运动数据；同时人类运动并不直接对应可执行的机器人动作。因此需要一种能从混合模态人类数据中迁移视觉-运动先验到机器人控制的建模范式。

Method: 1) 数据：从超1万小时以人为中心的原始数据中整理语料，融合仅视频、仅运动与配对视频-运动三类流，学习互补的视觉动态与全身运动学结构。2) 预训练：联合预测未来隐空间视觉状态与运动，促使视觉表征编码未来运动学。3) 机器人中期训练：将粗粒度先验适配到机器人视角与机体动力学。4) 动作后训练：冻结已适配先验，用动作专家通过门控交叉注意力融合视觉预测表征、当前图像与本体感觉，将预测上下文落地为可执行全身指令。

Result: 在 SIMPLE 基准上取得所比较基线中最高的总体成功率；在真机 Unitree G1 移动操作任务上与最强基线持平。

Conclusion: 混合模态人类数据预训练 + 隐空间视觉-运动联合预测 + 分阶段适配，是将人类先验迁移至人形全身移动操作的有效路径，具备仿真与真机验证。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation requires coordinated locomotion and manipulation informed by future scene evolution and whole-body motion, yet learning these capabilities is constrained by scarce robot demonstrations. Human video and motion datasets offer scalable supervision, but many contain only video or motion rather than paired video-motion data. Moreover, human motion does not directly specify executable robot actions. We present Being-M0.7, a latent world-action model that transfers visual-motion priors learned from mixed-modality human data to humanoid control through pre-training, robot mid-training, and action post-training. We curate a corpus from more than 10,000 hours of raw human-centric data, integrating video-only, motion-only, and paired video-motion streams to learn complementary visual dynamics and whole-body kinematic structure. Joint prediction of future latent visual states and motion encourages visual representations to encode future kinematics. Robot mid-training adapts this coarse-grained prior to robot viewpoints and body dynamics. During action post-training, an action expert combines visual predictive representations from the frozen, adapted prior with current images and proprioception through gated cross-attention, grounding predictive context in executable whole-body commands. Being-M0.7 achieves the highest aggregate success rate among the compared baselines on SIMPLE and matches the strongest baseline on real-world Unitree G1 loco-manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [198] [Distributed Relative Localization for Homogeneous Multi-Robot Systems through UWB Ranging and Limited Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11308)
*Zhiqiang Cao,Ran Liu,Billy Pik Lik Lau,Chau Yuen,U-Xuan Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种完全分布式的多机器人相对位姿估计方法，融合LiDAR、UWB与里程计，通过动态跟踪匿名聚类＋联合匹配实现队友识别与精确定位，仅需经由机载UWB交换里程计数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人探索、搜索救援等任务依赖准确可靠的相对定位。LiDAR方案对周围物体定位精度高，但外观相似的同构机器人缺乏可区分特征，难以数据关联；同时多数方法依赖外部基础设施（WiFi路由、mesh网络）或集中式计算，限制部署场景。

Method: 1）对LiDAR扫描中的潜在匿名队友聚类使用动态跟踪器进行时序跟踪；2）设计联合匹配策略，在这些匿名跟踪聚类中识别队友，保证机器人与聚类之间可靠的数据关联；3）融合对应LiDAR观测、UWB测距与里程计，实现分布式相对位姿估计，并最小化数据交换——仅通过机载UWB交换里程计，无需额外通信基础设施。

Result: 大量仿真与真实世界实验验证了所提相对定位方法的有效性与可靠性，证明在无需外部基础设施条件下可实现连续、准确的队友定位。

Conclusion: 该工作为同构多机器人系统提供了一种无外部基础设施、低通信开销的分布式相对定位方案，对探索、搜索救援等协同任务具有实用价值。

Abstract: Accurate and reliable relative localization is crucial for multi-robot applications like exploration, search, and rescue missions. LiDAR-based solutions offer high accuracy in localizing surrounding objects; however, distinguishing homogeneous robots with similar appearances remains challenging due to the lack of distinctive identification features. In this paper, we propose a fully distributed relative pose estimation approach by integrating LiDAR, UWB, and odometry measurements, allowing each robot to accurately and continuously localize its teammates without external infrastructure. Specifically, potential anonymous teammate robot clusters from LiDAR scans are tracked by a dynamic tracker. We then identify teammate robots from these tracked anonymous clusters using a joint matching strategy, ensuring reliable data association between robots and clusters. Finally, by combining the corresponding LiDAR observations, UWB ranging, and odometry measurements, each robot precisely localizes others while minimizing data exchange. The system requires only odometry data exchange through onboard UWB, eliminating the need for additional communication infrastructure like WiFi routers or mesh networks. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and reliability of the proposed relative localization approach.

</details>


### [199] [USDCraft: Geometrically Grounded Programmatic Modeling of Articulated 3D Assets for Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11322)
*Chuanrui Zhang,Zaijia Yang,Duomin Wang,Lu Shi,Daquan Zhou,Ruihua Zhang,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: USDCraft 将铰接式资产重建表述为基于部分几何证据的程序化建模，让预训练 LLM 直接编写并迭代修订可执行程序，无需任务特定训练即可生成可仿真、带显式物理属性的铰接式 USD 资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向 real-to-sim 机器人操作时，策略需从仿真迁移到真实物体，要求几何忠实且功能可动的铰接式 3D 资产。现有基于网格的方法依赖标注资产学习铰接推断，当真实物体超出训练分布、或网格不完整/损坏时部署困难。

Method: 提出源几何分析：将源网格转成度量化的文本描述，区分已观测表面与未知空间；提出迭代几何复查：将每个候选资产重新编码为同一表示，使差异指向程序编辑，而未观测区域保持开放以待补全；辅以视觉反馈与物理撰写指导，最终产出带显式物理属性、可直接载入 Isaac Sim 免手动调整的铰接式 USD 资产。

Result: 在两个基准上取得领先的铰接恢复性能，并验证 USDCraft 对 real-to-sim-to-real 机器人操作的有效性。

Conclusion: 将铰接式资产重建转化为以部分几何证据为条件的 LLM 程序化建模，无需任务特定训练即可生成仿真就绪资产，为 real-to-sim-to-real 机器人操作提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Geometrically faithful and functional articulated 3D assets are essential for real-to-sim robot manipulation, where policies trained in simulation must transfer to physical objects. Recent mesh-based methods learn to infer articulation from annotated 3D assets, but deployment remains challenging when real-world objects fall outside the training distribution or their meshes are incomplete or corrupted. To address these limitations, we formulate articulated asset reconstruction as programmatic modeling grounded in partial geometric evidence and introduce USDCraft, a framework in which a pretrained LLM writes and revises executable programs for simulation-ready articulated assets without task-specific training. We propose source geometry analysis, which converts the source mesh into a metric textual description that distinguishes observed surface from unknown space, and iterative geometric rechecking, which re-encodes each candidate in the same representation so that discrepancies point to program edits while unobserved regions remain open to completion. Visual feedback and physical authoring guidance complete the modeling process, which produces articulated USD assets with explicit physical properties that load into Isaac Sim without manual adjustment. Experiments demonstrate leading articulation recovery on two benchmarks and validate USDCraft's effectiveness for real-to-sim-to-real robot manipulation.

</details>


### [200] [PlanWAM: Planning-Shaped Future Representations for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11382)
*Jinchang Xu,Hongda Yu,Fengwei Dong,Wenhui Huang,Xi Wei,Yongzhi Liu,Sunan Zhang,Jirao Wang,Chen Lv,Bingbing Li,Guodong Yin,Weichao Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PlanWAM，一种"规划塑造"的世界-动作模型：用轨迹规划目标塑造未来隐表示（planning-shaped future latent），再通过 hindsight-to-foresight 蒸馏训练仅依赖历史信息的先验分支来预测该表示，作为规划上下文指导轨迹生成与选择，在 NAVSIM-v1/v2 与闭环 HUGSIM 上取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶世界模型主要研究"如何预测未来"和"如何利用未来"，但较少探讨哪种未来表示对规划最有用。作者希望让规划任务反过来塑造未来状态的表示，使其保留对规划最有价值的信息。

Method: 1) Temporal Register Pyramid：以 recency-aware 方式压缩多帧历史，学习面向未来推理与规划的紧凑历史表示；2) 特权未来后验分支：观测真值未来帧，用轨迹规划目标塑造其后验未来隐表示；3) Hindsight-to-Foresight Distillation：训练仅依赖历史的先验分支去预测该未来隐表示；4) 预测的未来隐表示作为规划上下文，指导轨迹生成与选择，实现前瞻规划。

Result: NAVSIM-v1/v2 navtest 上达到 93.8 PDMS / 90.9 EPDMS；闭环 HUGSIM 零样本设置下达到 38.7 HD-Score，在开环与闭环评测中均展现领先的规划性能；消融实验表明规划塑造的未来表示是有效且可部署的前瞻形式。

Conclusion: 让规划任务塑造未来表示、再用历史先验去预测该表示，是world-action模型中一种有效且可部署的前瞻（foresight）范式，兼具开环与闭环性能优势。

Abstract: World models in end-to-end autonomous driving predict future scene evolution to provide foresight for trajectory planning. Existing methods mainly study how to predict the future and how to use it, but less often ask which future representation is actually most useful for planning. To this end, we propose PlanWAM, a Planning-Shaped World Action Model. The key idea is to let the planning task shape the future-state representation, so that it retains the information most useful for planning. A latent world model then predicts this planning-shaped future latent representation from historical observations and uses it for planning, enabling foresighted planning. Specifically, we first use a Temporal Register Pyramid to compress multi-frame historical information in a recency-aware manner, learning a compact history representation oriented toward future reasoning and planning. We then introduce a privileged future posterior branch that observes ground-truth future frames, and shape its future latent representation with trajectory-planning objectives to obtain a planning-shaped future latent representation. Hindsight-to-Foresight Distillation trains a prior branch that depends only on history to predict this future latent representation. The predicted future latent representation serves as planning context and guides trajectory generation and selection. PlanWAM achieves 93.8 PDMS / 90.9 EPDMS on NAVSIM-v1/v2 navtest and reaches 38.7 HD-Score on closed-loop HUGSIM in a zero-shot setting, demonstrating leading planning performance across both open-loop and closed-loop evaluations. Extensive experiments further demonstrate that planning-shaped future representations provide an effective and deployable form of foresight for world-action models.

</details>


### [201] [WAM-Cache: Staleness-Bounded KV Reuse for Efficient World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11401)
*Kai Ding,Yang He,Ruijie Quan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WAM-Cache：免训练、跨chunk复用视频DiT的layerwise KV，仅对稀疏刷新集重算；刷新集由action expert交叉注意力与视觉latent surprise联合决定并加年龄上界，prefill FLOPs降32-42%，性能仅掉0.7-1.8pt（实机2.5pt）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Model靠预训练视频DiT表示驱动action expert，闭环下每个chunk都要跑一遍视频DiT做prefill，产生layerwise KV，成为每chunk计算瓶颈。已有免训练加速仍保持全稠密prefill。作者想在不训练的前提下压缩这一prefill开销。

Method: 1) 跨chunk缓存视频DiT的layerwise KV，仅重算一个稀疏"刷新token集"；2) 关键观察：按"视觉漂移"挑token会早熟饱和，即便用oracle给出真值KV漂移也远低于稠密基线；决定下游动作精度的是action expert的注意力位置，而非视觉变化；3) 刷新集=action expert跨注意力 ∪ 视觉latent surprise，并加严格age bound抑制误差累积；4) 完全免训练，无参数更新。

Result: 在Fast-WAM上，RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO及真实世界实验中视频DiT prefill FLOPs降低32-42%；仿真中与稠密策略差距仅0.7-1.8个百分点，真机为2.5个百分点。

Conclusion: WAM的prefill可稀疏化，且刷新准则应由action expert注意力驱动而非视觉漂移；免训练KV缓存策略能在几乎不损性能下显著减少视频DiT prefill计算，适用于真实机器人操作。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enable generalist robot manipulation by conditioning an action expert on representations from a pretrained video Diffusion Transformer (DiT). In closed-loop control, the video DiT runs at every chunk to encode the current observation into layerwise key-value (KV) pairs that the action expert queries. This prefill dominates the per-chunk computational cost, yet existing training-free accelerations leave it fully dense. We present WAM-Cache, a training-free framework that retains layerwise key-value representations across chunks and recomputes only a sparse refresh set of tokens. Crucially, we find that the intuitive heuristic of refreshing visually drifted tokens plateaus far below the dense baseline, even with an oracle predicting ground-truth KV drift. Downstream action accuracy is instead governed by where the action expert attends, not by what moved. WAM-Cache therefore selects the refresh set by uniting the action expert's cross-attention with visual latent surprise, complemented by a strict age bound that suppresses compounding error. On Fast-WAM, WAM-Cache cuts video DiT prefill FLOPs by 32-42% across RoboTwin 2.0, LIBERO, and real-world experiments, while staying within 0.7-1.8 percentage points of the dense policy in simulation and 2.5 points on a real robot.

</details>


### [202] [Rewiring Semantics, Dynamics, and Control: A Simple yet Effective Action-Centric Tri-Stream Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11416)
*Shuang Luo,Yilun Kong,Yunpeng Qing,Yihang Jiao,Zhi Hou,Shunyu Liu,Xiaogang Wang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Action-Centric Tri-Stream Transformer（ACT³），让动作专家通过分层注意力分别访问 VLM 与 WM 表征，两骨干各自仅在自身流内前向，从而在保持上下文流独立性的同时融合语义与动力学信息用于动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型依赖预训练 VLM，语义理解强但缺乏物理动力学先验，导致机器人策略泛化不足；已有工作将视频生成世界模型（WM）引入策略，但如何把语义理解与动力学预测作为互补指导用于动作生成仍具挑战。

Method: ACT³ 采用三流 Transformer 架构：VLM 流、WM 流与专用动作专家流。动作专家通过分层注意力（layerwise attention）访问两个上下文流的表征，而 VLM 与 WM 骨干只在各自流内做注意力，保持独立的上下文前向传播；两骨干均可通过控制监督信号被更新。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界机器人操作基准上，ACT³ 的性能优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 将语义理解与动力学预测以“动作中心”的三流结构解耦融合，是一种简单有效的 VLA 增强范式，可在不破坏各骨干自身表征角色的前提下提升机器人操作策略。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a prominent framework for complex robotic manipulation, building on the strong semantic understanding of pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs). However, such VLM backbones offer insufficient physical dynamics priors, which limits the generalization capabilities of robot policies. Recent efforts therefore integrate video-generation World Models (WMs) into robot policies through various strategies, using predictive dynamics to facilitate action generation. Despite these advances, harnessing semantic understanding and dynamics prediction as complementary guidance for action generation remains challenging. In this paper, we introduce $\mathrm{ACT}^3$, a simple yet effective Action-Centric Tri-Stream Transformer that fuses semantic and dynamics information into control actions while preserving the distinct roles of context streams. Specifically, $\mathrm{ACT}^3$ enables the dedicated action expert to access VLM and WM representations through layerwise attention, with each backbone attending only within its own stream. This straightforward interaction design maintains independent forward propagation in the context streams while allowing both backbones to be updated through control supervision. Experiments on both simulated and real-world robotic manipulation benchmarks show that the proposed $\mathrm{ACT}^3$ yields results superior to its counterparts.

</details>


### [203] [Experience-Guided Initiation Search for Learned Skills in Skill Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11418)
*Qixuan Li,Yanhong Zhao,Jincheng Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EVIS 提出一种"经验引导 + 行为验证"的初始配置搜索框架，在有限目标环境交互预算下为冻结的 VLA 策略寻找可靠的可执行初始状态，兼顾单技能与多技能组合任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署冻结的 VLA 策略到新环境时，需要可靠的初始配置。但在技能组合中，初始配置不仅影响当前技能，还决定传递给后续技能的状态，因此单技能成功不等于组合任务成功。穷举 rollout 代价过高，直接复用历史经验在环境变化下又不可靠。

Method: EVIS 使用历史执行经验对候选初始配置进行优先级排序（历史引导），再用目标环境上的实际行为来验证候选是否仍然有效（目标侧行为验证），从而在有限交互预算下完成初始配置搜索。

Result: 在冻结 VLA 策略的单技能与两阶段操作任务上评测，EVIS 降低了目标环境平均查询次数，并在小交互预算下提升了可靠候选的发现率。

Conclusion: 将历史经验引导与目标侧行为验证相结合，能有效降低冻结学习技能在新环境中部署的交互成本。

Abstract: Deploying frozen learned skills, such as Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies, in new environments requires identifying initiation configurations that support reliable execution. In skill composition, an initiation configuration affects not only the current skill but also the physical state passed to subsequent skills, so successful execution of an individual skill does not necessarily imply successful completion of the composed task. Estimating target-specific capability through extensive rollouts is costly in real-world deployment, while directly reusing historical experience can be unreliable under environment changes. We propose EVIS, an Experience-Guided and Behavior-Validated Initiation Search framework for discovering reliable initiation configurations under limited target interaction. EVIS uses historical execution experience to prioritize promising candidates and target-environment behavior to validate whether they remain effective. We evaluate EVIS on single-skill and two-stage manipulation tasks with frozen VLA policies. EVIS reduces mean target-environment queries and improves reliable candidate discovery under small interaction budgets. These results show that combining historical guidance with target-side behavioral validation can reduce the interaction cost of deploying frozen learned skills in new environments.

</details>


### [204] [RoboAware: Learning to Coordinate Embodied Skills from Counterfactual Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11480)
*Bohan Zhou,Xingbei Chen,Emily Huang,Weilin Ruan,Haojian Huang,Yehang Zhang,Zexi Li,Wenqian Li,Qize Yu,Zetian Song,Leyi Wu,Jinghao Li,Mingxuan Song,Xinrun Xu,Zongyang Qiu,Yangkai Wei,Tianyi Zhang,Kaiwen Zhou,Yinchuan Li,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboAware：面向具身编码智能体的状态条件责任协调器，通过 $P^5$ 五阶段模式与状态锁定反事实分支（SCB）采集分支结果，再用执行感知学习（EAL，MCTS+Q-learning）蒸馏出策略族价值，部署时按可观测上下文选择策略族，由冻结编码智能体生成局部代码块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身编码智能体可组合模块化机器人技能与冻结的端到端策略，但有效组合的前提是预判在当前物理状态下哪一策略族会成功，而现有工作缺乏反事实分支结果这一监督信号。

Method: 提出 $P^5$ 模式将技能统一组织为五个语义阶段，据此构建分层 MDP 以界定责任可比范围；引入状态锁定反事实分支（SCB），把同一训练状态复原后分别让每个可采纳族生成并执行代码块，从而暴露被选中分支经验所掩盖的结果；再以执行感知学习（EAL）将 MCTS 与 Q-learning 结合，把结果蒸馏为以族为条件的价值函数；部署时协调器依据可观测上下文选族，冻结编码智能体生成下一段局部代码。

Result: 在 100 个任务的单回合评测中总体成功率 77.0%；RoboSuite 平均 90.0%、LIBERO-Pro 多样任务簇 73.8%、RoboTwin 双臂任务 90.0%，均达 SOTA，优于现有 code-as-policy 与 VLA-harness 基线。

Conclusion: 通过学习状态条件的策略族责任协调，可在不改动冻结策略与编码智能体的前提下显著提升技能编排成功率，反事实分支是获取组合监督的有效途径。

Abstract: Embodied coding agents can combine modular robot skills with frozen end-to-end policies, yet effective composition requires anticipating which policy family will succeed in the current physical state. We present RoboAware, which builds on coding agents' skill orchestration by learning only a state-conditioned responsibility coordinator from counterfactual outcomes. Inspired by the success of REPL, we propose the $P^5$ schema and formulate a hierarchical MDP based on it. $P^5$ organizes skills uniformly into five semantic stages, defining where responsibility can be compared.
