To address the lack of counterfactual branch outcomes in existing work, we introduce State-Locked Counterfactual Branching (SCB), which restores the same training state to generate and execute a code block from each admissible family, exposing outcomes that selected-branch experience leaves unobserved. Building on this, we propose Execution-Aware Learning (EAL), which combines Monte Carlo tree search with Q-learning to distill these outcomes into family-conditioned values. At deployment, the coordinator selects the policy family according to observable context, and the frozen coding agent generates the next local code block. Comprehensive single-episode evaluations on 100 tasks show that RoboAware reaches a 77.0% overall success rate, with SOTA averages of 90.0% on RoboSuite, 73.8% on diverse LIBERO-Pro task clusters, and 90.0% on challenging RoboTwin bimanual tasks, outperforming existing code-as-policy and VLA-harness baselines.

</details>


### [205] [DAMP: Humanoid Locomotion via Denoised Belief Learning and Adversarial Motion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11505)
*Puying Shen,Wenhao Cui,Huaxing Huang,Bangyu Qin,Shengtao Li,Ziyang Dong,Guoteng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DAMP——一个基于循环神经网络的强化学习框架，在无外部感知信息的假设下，实现人形机器人穿越复杂地形的稳健且自然的运动，并成功从仿真迁移到真实世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人具备穿越复杂地形的结构潜力，但在无感知信息（盲行）条件下保持稳定行走仍具挑战性。现有方法多依赖视觉/外部感知，在感知失效或缺失场景中鲁棒性不足。

Method: 端到端强化学习框架 DAMP：用循环神经网络（RNN）捕捉时间依赖，隐式推断特权信息及其他任务相关隐变量；通过将学习到的表征与任务目标对齐，使策略在无感知输入下仍保持目标一致性与鲁棒性。

Result: 实现仿真到真实环境的迁移学习，在复杂地形上完成真机演示，展示方法的鲁棒性与泛化能力（附有真机演示视频）。

Conclusion: 隐式时序表征与任务对齐可替代显式感知，为无感知人形运动控制提供可行路径，并具备 sim-to-real 泛化能力。

Abstract: Humanoid robots possess the structural capability to traverse complex terrains. However, achieving stable t raversal without relying on perceived information remains challenging, particularly in complex environments. This paper introduces DAMP, a reinforcement learning framework aimed at achieving robust and naturalistic humanoid locomotion over challenging terrains, with the assumption that no perceived information is available. The framework leverages recurrent neural networks to capture temporal dependencies and implicitly infer privileged and other task-relevant latent information. By aligning the learned representations with the task objective, the method enables robust and goal-consistent policy learning. This end-to-end framework achieves transfer learning from simulation to real-world environments, demonstrating the proposed method's robustness and generalization capabilities. The video of the real-world demonstration can be found at the following link: this https URL .

</details>


### [206] [WARP-VLA: Wrist-Camera Adaptation for View-Robust Policy Execution in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11508)
*Junmyeong Lee,Dongmin Shin,Min-Gyu Park,Wooseok Jeon,Inho Chang,Hae-Gon Jeon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WARP-VLA 提出一种相机视角鲁棒的 VLA 策略，采用 Mixture-of-Experts 架构让不同专家学习视角特定的特征变换，由路由器依据隐式视角信息进行组合，从而在腕部相机配置变化下无需外参输入即可部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 对相机配置变化敏感，跨设备部署时几乎无法复现训练时的相机位姿。腕部相机随机械臂运动，微小安装差异即会改变细粒度几何线索，问题比固定外视角更严重。

Method: WARP-VLA 采用 MoE 架构：各专家学习视角特定的特征变换，路由器基于隐式视角信息组合专家输出；部署时不需要相机外参作为额外输入。特征级适配在仿真中学习并迁移到真实部署。同时发布腕部视角鲁棒性基准与即插即用实现。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准上，腕部视角扰动下将 pi-0.5 的平均成功率从 39.2% 提升至 78.3%；真机实验表明仿真中学到的特征级适配能有效迁移到多样部署设置。

Conclusion: 隐式视角路由的 MoE 特征适配能显著缓解腕部相机配置差异带来的性能退化，且仿真到真机可迁移；所发布的基准与即插即用实现有助于后续研究。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) for robotic manipulation, their performance remains sensitive to changes in camera configuration. The problem becomes more evident in cross-setup deployment, as reproducing the exact camera pose used for training is nearly impossible. Unlike fixed external views, wrist views are more challenging because the camera moves with the robot, causing even small mounting variations to alter fine-grained geometric cues. To address this, we propose WARP-VLA, a camera-view robust VLA for diverse wrist camera configurations. WARP-VLA adopts a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture where individual experts learn view-specific feature transformations, and a router combines them based on implicit view information. This allows the policy to be deployed without requiring camera extrinsic parameters as additional input. Through experiments on the LIBERO benchmark, WARP-VLA improves the average success rate of pi-0.5 from 39.2% to 78.3% under wrist-view perturbations. The real-robot experiments further show that the feature-level adaptation learned in simulation successfully transfers to diverse deployment settings. To facilitate reproducibility and future research, we release our wrist viewpoint robustness benchmark and a plug-and-play implementation.

</details>


### [207] [RAGNAROK: Radar-Aided Gravity-Normalized Alignment for Robust Open Keyframe-based Radar-Visual-Kinematic-Inertial SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11531)
*Hanjun Kim,Chiyun Noh,Sangwoo Jung,Jaehyung Jung,Simon Boche,Cedric Le Gentil,Stefan Leutenegger,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAGNAROK：首个雷达-视觉-运动学-惯性融合SLAM系统，用于崎岖地形下足式机器人鲁棒状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人在非结构化环境中机动性强，但依赖本体感知的估计器在崎岖地形易失效；引入雷达虽可提供速度观测，但偏航可观测性弱导致漂移，且缺乏面向恶劣环境的失效感知融合机制。

Method: 提出雷达-视觉-运动学-惯性SLAM框架，包含：滑移与滚动接触感知的腿部速度估计、运动学感知雷达因子、退化感知图像增强；并构建B样条雷达辅助本体感知主干网络、自适应权重与在线外参标定。

Result: 在公开数据集与自采集数据集上均取得鲁棒表现，优于当前最优基线方法。开源代码与数据集。

Conclusion: 多模态雷达-视觉-惯性-运动学融合可显著提升足式机器人在恶劣地形下的状态估计鲁棒性，退化感知与自适应融合是关键设计。

Abstract: Legged robots offer superior mobility in unstructured environments, but reliable operation in such conditions requires robust state estimation. To address the vulnerability of proprioceptive estimators in rough terrain, recent methods have incorporated radar to provide velocity measurements. However, their limited yaw observability still leads to drift, and failure-aware fusion for adverse environments remains underexplored. In this letter, we present RAGNAROK, the first radar-visual-kinematic-inertial SLAM designed for robust operation in challenging environments. It integrates slip- and rolling-contact-aware leg velocity estimation, a kinematics-aware radar factor, and degradation-aware image enhancement. We further incorporate a B-spline-based radar-aided proprioceptive backbone, adaptive weighting, and online extrinsic calibration. Extensive experiments on public and self-collected datasets demonstrate that RAGNAROK achieves robust performance under challenging conditions and outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. The source code and dataset are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [208] [CoCam4D: Geometry-Aware Cooperative 4D Perception for Camera-Only Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11577)
*Soham Pahari,Sudip Das,Arindam Das,Ujjwal Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoCam4D：纯视觉协同感知的贝叶斯框架，用VGGT前馈网络生成带不确定性的3D高斯场景表示，多车共享压缩高斯原语以降低彼此深度不确定性，并引入35字节动态物体原语用于C-V2X通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶单车感知受遮挡、盲区、传感器距离限制，纯相机方案又受单目深度估计的距离相关不确定性制约，因此希望在不依赖LiDAR的前提下实现几何上可靠的协同感知。

Method: 提出CoCam4D：基于VGGT的前馈网络直接输出带不确定性估计的3D高斯场景表示；多个智能体交换紧凑高斯原语，用高置信观测校正其他智能体的深度不确定性；并设计Dynamic Object Primitives（DOPs），35字节紧凑表示以适配C-V2X带宽约束。

Result: 在OPV2V+上提升11.48%，在DAIR-V2X-C上提升10.62%，持续优于近期纯视觉方法，验证了几何约束协同感知在无LiDAR自动驾驶中的潜力。

Conclusion: 显式建模几何不确定性并结合前馈3D高斯表示，可在纯视觉条件下实现高效协同感知与低带宽通信，为无LiDAR自动驾驶提供可行路径。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles often suffer from limited perception due to occlusions, blind spots, limited sensor range, and the complex nature of surrounding environments. Multi-agent collaborative perception (CP) addresses these challenges by allowing vehicles to share sensory information and reconstruct the scene cooperatively. However, camera-only perception remains fundamentally limited by the uncertainty of distance-dependent monocular depth estimation. We propose CoCam4D, a Bayesian framework for collaborative perception that explicitly models geometric uncertainty. It uses a VGGT-based feedforward network to generate 3D Gaussian scene representations with associated uncertainty estimates, enabling multiple vehicles or agents to efficiently combine their observations. By sharing compact Gaussian primitives, reliable observations from one agent can reduce the depth uncertainty of another without requiring LiDAR sensors. To support real-world deployment, we introduce Dynamic Object Primitives (DOPs), a compact 35-byte representation designed for efficient C-V2X communication. Extensive experiments show that our proposed method consistently outperforms recent vision-only methods, achieving improvements of 11.48% on OPV2V+ and 10.62% on DAIR-V2X-C, demonstrating the potential of geometrically grounded collaborative perception for LiDAR-free autonomous driving.

</details>


### [209] [SDPAD: A Fully Spike-Driven Pipeline for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11583)
*Chengjun Zhang,Yuhao Zhang,Jie Yang,Mohamad Sawan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SDPAD：全脉冲驱动的端到端自动驾驶规划流水线，通过量化ANN2SNN转换、Spike-3D-Lift与Spike-QFormer实现高精度低能耗规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶要求轨迹规划既高精度又适合边缘部署。现有ANN规划器精度高但计算密集、能耗大；SNN虽具备稀疏事件驱动、数量级省能的潜力，但规划精度仍远落后。作者希望弥合这一精度—能耗鸿沟。

Method: SDPAD为完全脉冲驱动流水线：将预训练ANN感知栈经量化ANN2SNN转换变为整型脉冲形式；用脉冲驱动的SDM深度分布（Spike-3D-Lift）将多视图图像提升到BEV空间；再经Spike-QFormer——一种脉冲查询Transformer，由BEV场景蒸馏出的自车、智能体、地图查询与可学习路点查询通过交叉注意力融合，并用可变形脉冲交叉注意力精化。所有算子均由整型脉冲门控，推理为单次前馈，无时间步仿真循环。

Result: nuScenes开环基准：平均L2误差0.40 m、碰撞率0.12%，与强ANN规划器持平，能耗仅69.9 mJ，低于近期ANN基线2%以内。NAVSIM navtest闭环：86.3 PDMS，超此前SNN规划器SAD 4.3分，并以远低能耗匹配主流ANN规划器。

Conclusion: SDPAD是首个在端到端自动驾驶中评测的全脉冲驱动规划器，证明SNN可在复杂驾驶任务中与密集ANN相媲美，为边缘部署提供了高精度低能耗的规划方案。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving demands trajectory planners that are both highly accurate and cheap enough for edge deployment. State-of-the-art artificial neural network (ANN) planners meet the accuracy requirement at the cost of heavy dense computation, while spiking neural networks (SNNs)---though promising orders-of-magnitude energy savings through sparse, event-driven arithmetic---still lag far behind in planning accuracy. We present \textbf{SDPAD}, a fully spike-driven end-to-end planning pipeline that closes this gap. SDPAD converts a pre-trained ANN perception stack into integer-spike form via quantized ANN2SNN conversion, lifts multi-view images into the bird's-eye-view (BEV) space with a spike-driven-max (SDM) depth distribution (Spike-3D-Lift), and plans through the Spike-QFormer, a spiking query transformer in which ego, agent, and map queries distilled from the BEV scene are fused by learnable waypoint queries via cross-attention, followed by deformable spike-cross-attention refinement. Every operation is gated by integer spikes and inference is a single feed-forward pass without temporal simulation loops. On the nuScenes open-loop benchmark, SDPAD achieves an average $L_2$ error of 0.40\,m and a collision rate of 0.12\%, on par with strong ANN planners while consuming 69.9\,mJ---less than 2\% of recent ANN baselines. In closed-loop evaluation on the NAVSIM navtest split, SDPAD reaches 86.3 PDMS, surpassing the previous SNN planner SAD by 4.3 points and matching mainstream ANN planners at a fraction of their energy. To our knowledge, SDPAD is the first fully spike-driven planner evaluated in end-to-end autonomous driving, demonstrating that SNNs can rival dense ANNs in complex driving tasks.

</details>


### [210] [Acting from Belief, Looking When Needed: A Bayesian Spatial World Model for Navigation under Intermittent Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11591)
*Feihong Yang,Xiang Long,Jincheng Yu,Jianfei Zhang,Guangjun Ge,Chao Wang,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ALONE：一种贝叶斯空间世界模型，支持无人机在间歇性感知（按需触发单目深度观测）下导航，10Hz决策率下两个仿真场景族闭环成功率98%/97%，仅需深度观测的决策步中位数占0.9%/1.3%，真机室内飞行10/10成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规导航依赖宽覆盖、高频传感以缓解部分可观测性；但当共享传感器被其他任务临时征用时，导航观测会被打断。作者研究"间歇性感知"下的导航：仅在执行需要新观测时才再次感知，从而在两次导航观测之间把共享传感器让给其他任务。

Method: 提出ALONE贝叶斯空间世界模型：用已执行动作传播结构化空间信念，并用选择性获取的观测进行校正；通过学习的常见几何结构先验，从已有观测历史推断未观测结构。将信念解码为空间估计供运动规划模块使用，并预测表达该估计精度置信度的可靠性图。仅当可靠性不足阻碍导航、且新证据能使相关区域空间信息更可靠时，才请求一次观测，否则继续基于传播后的信念行动。实例化于无人机+间歇性单目深度图像。

Result: 两个仿真场景族在10Hz决策率下闭环成功率分别为98%和97%；成功试验中需要新深度观测的决策步中位数占比仅0.9%和1.3%。真机室内飞行试验10次全部成功。

Conclusion: ALONE表明通过结构化空间信念传播与可靠性驱动的主/被动观测调度，可在显著降低观测需求的同时保持高导航成功率，并在真实室内飞行中得到验证。

Abstract: Robot navigation commonly uses wide-coverage, high-frequency sensing to reduce partial observability; this reliance becomes restrictive when another task temporarily redirects a shared sensor from navigation, interrupting navigation-relevant observations. We study navigation under intermittent perception: acting from an internal spatial belief and looking again only when execution needs a new observation, potentially freeing the shared sensor for other tasks between navigation observations. ALONE, a Bayesian spatial world model, propagates a structured spatial belief using executed actions and corrects it with selectively acquired observations; learned priors over common geometric structures infer unobserved structure from available observation history. It decodes the belief into a spatial estimate for the motion-planning module and predicts a reliability map expressing confidence in the estimate's accuracy. ALONE requests an observation only if insufficient reliability hinders navigation and new evidence should make relevant-region spatial information more reliable; otherwise, it continues acting from the propagated belief. We instantiate ALONE for drone navigation with intermittent single-camera depth images. Across two simulated scene families, it achieves 98% and 97% closed-loop success at a 10 Hz decision rate. Among successful trials, median fractions of decision steps requiring a new depth observation are only 0.9% and 1.3%, respectively, demonstrating high navigation success with substantially reduced observation demand. Real-world indoor flight experiments further validate navigation under intermittent depth observations, with all 10 trials successful.

</details>


### [211] [Learning Language-Conditioned Traversability Representations for Adaptive Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11622)
*Senda Chen,Changxu Cheng,Fangdi Li,Tao Wang,Wuyue Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LaTraNav 提出语言条件下的可通行性表征学习框架，用异步"慢 VLM + 快流匹配规划器"实现自适应视觉导航，并配套可控轨迹的仿真数据生成与照片级真实感图像翻译管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可通行性依赖机器人能力与用户偏好，传统方案依赖预定义代价地图或分割掩码，需手工规则与调参；且掩码与视角耦合，在感知延迟下难以异步规划。

Method: 异步双频架构：慢速 VLM 输出可通行性与导航目标的隐表征，快速 flow-matching 规划器以该隐表征为条件做像素空间路径规划；训练数据由可控轨迹仿真生成（观测-语言指令-可通行性地图-目标位置-多样轨迹配对），并用照片级图像翻译提升视觉真实感。

Result: 在多来源数据集上验证了语言引导的可通行性分割与目标定位，以及自适应像素空间路径规划；隐条件优于显式分割掩码；异步调度在相同语义更新率下将路径更新率提升 6.05 倍。

Conclusion: 语言条件隐式可通行性表征结合异步快慢架构，可替代显式掩码管线，兼顾自适应性与实时性，为视觉导航提供可扩展范式。

Abstract: Traversability is essential for visual navigation but varies with robot capabilities and user preferences. Conventional pipelines often rely on explicit costmaps or segmentation masks with predefined criteria, requiring hand-crafted rules and careful tuning. Moreover, viewpoint-dependent segmentation masks complicate asynchronous planning under perception latency. We present LaTraNav, a framework that learns language-conditioned traversability representations for adaptive visual navigation. Its asynchronous architecture combines a slow vision-language model that produces latent representations of traversability and navigation goals, with a fast flow-matching planner conditioned on these representations. To train the system, we develop a simulation-based data generation pipeline with controllable trajectories, producing observations paired with language instructions, traversability maps, goal locations, and diverse trajectories. Photorealistic image translation further enhances visual realism. Evaluations on datasets from multiple sources demonstrate effective language-guided traversability segmentation and goal localization by the slow VLM, alongside adaptive pixel-space path planning by the fast planner. Latent conditioning improves planning performance over explicit segmentation masks, while asynchronous scheduling increases the path-update rate by $6.05\times$ at the same semantic-update rate.

</details>


### [212] [Neural Networks for Temporal Pattern Recognition and Dynamic Arm Gesture Speed Estimation for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11631)
*Milán Zsolt Bagladi,László Gulyás*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 对10个抽象时序任务、18种网络架构、250+配置进行统一基准测试，选出BiGRU、TCN、Conv1D、GRUReLU四类轻量最优模型，并将其用于从骨骼关键点估计动态手势执行速度，最佳平均绝对误差0.198（约5%相对误差）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手势交互机器人需要能识别多样时序模式的神经网络，但不同架构（循环、卷积、注意力、集合函数）在时序任务上的系统对比缺失，且"手势速度"这一对机器人控制有价值的物理量能否从骨骼数据可靠回归尚不清楚。

Method: 构建10个抽象时序任务（5个置换不变的集合任务、5个顺序相关序列任务）×18种架构的基准网格，加上多种预处理与目标变量变换共250+实验配置，全部在固定随机种子、共享超参、共享数据划分下训练测试；据此选出BiGRU、TCN、GRUReLU三种架构相异的最优模型，在自建8类交通相关手势、256710帧OpenPose骨骼关键点数据上回归三种速度解释（峰值计数、周期时间、平均尖峰间隔）。

Result: 排名显示BiGRU、TCN、Conv1D、GRUReLU四种架构在全部10个任务中稳定领先，参数量均<2000，适合实时部署；在真实手势速度估计中，最佳配置峰值计数解释MAE=0.198（约5%相对误差），周期时间解释约4%相对误差，平均尖峰间隔约8%相对误差。

Conclusion: 轻量神经网络可依据骨骼关键点序列可靠估计手势速度，为速度感知型手势控制机器人系统提供了可行路径；但评测以合成基准与自建数据集为主，尚缺真实机器人闭环验证。

Abstract: Deploying intelligent robotic systems that interact with humans through gestures requires neural networks capable of recognizing diverse temporal patterns. We present a systematic benchmark of ten abstract sequential tasks--five permutation-invariant (set) and five order-dependent (sequence) problems--evaluated across eighteen neural network architectures spanning recurrent, convolutional, attention-based, and set-function families. Beyond the core architecture-task grid, we explore numerous preprocessing and target-variable transformations, yielding more than 250 distinct experimental configurations. All variants are trained and tested under strictly identical conditions (fixed random seeds, shared hyperparameters, shared data splits) to ensure fair and reproducible comparison. Ranking across all ten tasks reveals four consistently top-performing architectures--BiGRU, TCN, Conv1D, and GRUReLU--all compact enough for real-time deployment (under 2,000 parameters in the benchmark setting). Based on this ranking, we apply three architecturally diverse top models (BiGRU, TCN, and GRUReLU) to a practical robotics problem: estimating the execution speed of dynamic arm gestures from skeletal keypoint sequences. Three speed interpretations (peak count, period time, and mean spike spacing) are evaluated on a custom dataset of eight traffic-related gesture classes comprising 256,710 frames recorded via OpenPose. The best configuration achieves a mean absolute error of 0.198 on the peak-count interpretation, corresponding to roughly 5% relative error, while the period-time interpretation reaches approximately 4% relative error, and the mean spike spacing interpretation approximately 8% relative error. These results demonstrate that neural networks can reliably estimate gesture speed from skeletal data, opening a path toward speed-aware gesture-controlled robotic systems.

</details>


### [213] [Scalable LEO Conjunction Screening using Adaptive Synthetic-Covariance Thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11645)
*Vedant Srinivas,Grace Ra Kim,Duncan Eddy,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出不确定性感知的轨道交会筛选方法，用基于传播径向不确定度的对象特定近地点阈值替代固定阈值，并配合二分搜索高度扫描实现对数线性复杂度的高效评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道目标数量增长使交会筛选需处理更多对象对。现有流程的早期过滤器（如近地点过滤器）对所有对象使用统一固定阈值：对轨道约束良好的对象过于保守，对预测不确定性大的对象又可能不足，导致下游交会分析负担过重。

Method: 用传播得到的径向不确定度推导每个对象、每个预测时刻特有的近地点高度阈值，替代单一固定阈值；阈值随不确定度小则收窄、随不确定度增大而放宽。将该自适应筛选规则与二分搜索高度扫描结合，使异构区间可在目录规模下以对数线性复杂度高效评估。

Result: 在Space-Track GP目录的33,791个对象上，使用前8个公开星历快照与7天预测时域评估：自适应筛选保留1.628亿候选对，相比固定50 km近地点阈值减少28.78%，相比暴力全配对筛选减少71.48%；保留了两个独立传播参考集中的全部近距离接近事件；在最大测试目录规模下，二分搜索扫描比穷举区间评估快27倍且候选集相同。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知筛选能显著减少传递给下游交会分析的候选负担，为管理日益庞大且异质的轨道目标群体提供可扩展路径。

Abstract: As orbital populations grow, conjunction screening must search more object pairs for potential collision risks. Existing pipelines use early-stage filters, such as an apsis filter, to eliminate pairs whose orbital altitude ranges cannot produce a close approach. However, these filters apply the same fixed threshold to every object, making the screening unnecessarily conservative for well-constrained objects while potentially insufficient for objects with greater orbit-prediction uncertainty. In this work, we present an uncertainty-aware conjunction screening method that replaces a single fixed apsis threshold with object-specific thresholds derived from propagated radial uncertainty. The resulting thresholds vary across both objects and prediction time, becoming narrower when propagated uncertainty is small and wider when uncertainty grows. We pair this adaptive screening rule with a binary-search altitude sweep that makes resulting heterogeneous intervals efficient to evaluate at catalog scale with log-linear search complexity. We evaluate the method on 33,791 objects from the Space-Track General Perturbations (GP) catalog using the preceding eight public ephemeris snapshots and a seven-day prediction horizon. The adaptive sieve retains 162.8 million candidate pairs, reducing the candidate set by 28.78% relative to a fixed 50 km apsis threshold and by 71.48% relative to brute-force all-pairs screening. It retains all close approaches identified in two independently propagated reference sets. At the largest tested catalog size, the binary-search sweep is 27 times faster than exhaustive interval evaluation while returning the same candidate set. These results demonstrate that uncertainty-aware screening can substantially reduce the candidate burden passed to downstream conjunction analysis, providing a scalable path toward managing increasingly large and heterogeneous orbital populations.

</details>


### [214] [YOCO: You Only Calibrate Once! Fast Mocap Calibration for Dexterous Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11657)
*Yu Zhang,Yunqi Li,Yushi Du,Yi Ma,Yanchao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: YOCO是一个快速少样本、免微调的手部姿态标定框架，通过学习校准HyperNet为冻结的MANO估计模块预测LoRA式更新，仅用少量配对样本即可修正运动捕捉手套的偏置，提升重定向与遥操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本动捕手套与无标记跟踪器的手部状态估计常存在随用户、佩戴方式和录制会话变化的偏置，导致重定向和示教质量下降。传统标定需为每个操作者或会话单独优化模型，效率低且难以泛化。

Method: 提出YOCO：以少量配对的原始-目标姿态为条件，训练校准HyperNet预测针对冻结MANO手部估计模块的LoRA风格参数更新，将逐用户标定转化为轻量前馈自适应步骤，在保留MANO几何先验与紧凑估计器效率的同时实现免微调标定。训练时在InterHand2.6M上使用合成漂移增强。

Result: 在增强的InterHand序列、离线真实手套数据以及灵巧遥操作任务上进行评测，YOCO在标定效率、手部状态估计质量和遥操作性能上均优于未标定输入和标准标定基线。

Conclusion: YOCO表明将逐用户标定建模为对冻结手部模型的前馈参数适配，可高效吸收用户/会话偏置，是一种实用、免微调的少样本手部姿态标定方案，有望提升灵巧遥操作与示教数据质量。

Abstract: Dexterous teleoperation requires reliable human-hand state estimations. However, common low-cost motion-capture gloves and markerless trackers often exhibit biases that vary across users, glove fit, and recording sessions, degrading retargeting and demonstration quality. We present YOCO, a fast few-shot, fine-tuning-free calibration framework that corrects biased hand-pose streams from a small set of paired raw and target poses. Instead of optimizing a separate model for every operator or session, YOCO conditions a calibration HyperNet on the paired examples and predicts LoRA-style updates for a frozen MANO hand-estimation module, turning per-user calibration into a lightweight feed-forward adaptation step while preserving the geometric prior of MANO and the efficiency of a compact estimator. We train YOCO with synthetic drift augmentations on InterHand2.6M and evaluate on augmented InterHand sequences, offline real glove data, and dexterous teleoperation tasks. Across these settings, YOCO improves calibration efficiency, hand-state estimation quality and teleoperation performance compared with uncalibrated input and standard calibration baselines.

</details>


### [215] [From Solo to Ensemble: A Hierarchical Framework for Composable Multi-Agent Human-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11722)
*Zekai Deng,Kangyi Chen,Ye Shi,Jingya Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层框架，将单智能体人-物交互(HOI)策略重解释为"对象导向动作"监督，蒸馏出可复用的低层"对象导向运动技能"，并在下游冻结为执行器，由高层策略以区域级对象导向动作协调多智能体，实现跨交互类型、物体几何与团队规模的可组合多智能体HOI学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理仿真中的HOI已在单智能体操作上稳定，但拓展到多智能体协作仍困难。现有方法靠任务特定微调适配交互策略，把低层密集接触执行与高层协调耦合在一起，难以在物体几何、交互类型、团队规模间复用。

Method: 把教师策略的rollout重新解释为对象导向动作监督：从执行轨迹中提取短时域"对象代理(Object-proxy)"运动；将任务特定教师蒸馏为在对象导向动作空间中运行的低层技能。下游任务中该技能冻结为可复用执行器，高层策略以共享物体、任务目标、各智能体状态与局部操作区域为条件，生成区域级对象导向动作，从而把多智能体HOI学习从全身密集接触控制转为紧凑的对象级代理运动协调。

Result: 在多种HOI任务上，蒸馏出的对象导向运动技能可稳定执行代理运动，并支持跨不同交互类型、物体几何与团队规模的可组合策略学习。

Conclusion: 以对象导向动作空间作为单-多智能体HOI之间的接口，解耦低层接触执行与高层协作，提升了技能复用性与组合泛化能力，为多智能体HOI提供一种可扩展的分层学习范式。

Abstract: Physics-based human-object interaction has achieved robust single-agent manipulation skills, yet extending them to multi-agent cooperative tasks remains challenging. Existing approaches typically adapt interaction policies through task-specific fine-tuning, which entangles low-level contact-rich execution with high-level coordination and limits reuse across object geometries, interaction types, and team sizes. We propose a hierarchical framework that converts a single-agent HOI policy into a reusable Object-oriented Motion Skill. Specifically, we reinterpret teacher rollouts as object-oriented action supervision by extracting short-horizon object-proxy motions from executed trajectories, and distill task-specific teachers into a low-level skill operating in an Object-oriented Action Space. For downstream tasks, the distilled skill is frozen as a reusable executor, while a high-level policy coordinates multiple agents by generating region-wise object-oriented actions conditioned on the shared object, task goal, agent states, and local manipulation regions. This formulation shifts multi-agent HOI learning from direct contact-rich full-body control to compact object-level proxy-motion coordination. Experiments on diverse HOI tasks show that the distilled Object-oriented Motion Skill supports robust proxy-motion execution and enables composable policy learning across different interaction types, object geometries, and team sizes.

</details>


### [216] [2DGS-Planner: Rasterization-based Path Planning in 2D Gaussian Splatting Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11752)
*Jiwon Park,Dong-Uk Seo,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 2DGS-Planner，将 2D 高斯泼溅（2DGS）地图的栅格化渲染结果作为几何查询接口，而非把单个高斯基元当作障碍物，用于地面机器人路径规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯基元经 alpha 合成渲染联合优化，单个基元不能可靠表示障碍物；直接用基元做规划几何不稳健。因此需要一种能直接从高斯地图读出的、与规划相关的几何接口。

Method: 离线阶段：多视角归因把渲染法线离散度转成非地面圆盘的结构分数，引导地面自适应节点采样；用路径对齐的正交查询验证候选边，用圆柱查询估计局部间隙场并缓存到边上。在线阶段：图搜索初始化路径，路径精化复用缓存间隙场，并考虑机器人尺寸与地面约束。

Result: 实验显示路线图连通性更好、间隙估计更准确、规划成功率高于所对比基线，支持将栅格化作为直接在高斯地图上规划的有效几何查询接口。

Conclusion: 栅格化渲染可作为一种有效的几何查询界面，实现直接在 2DGS 高斯地图上进行地面机器人规划；代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Gaussian splatting provides an explicit and efficiently rasterizable scene representation for robot navigation. However, individual Gaussian primitives may not reliably represent obstacles as they are jointly optimized through alpha-composited rendering from a finite set of reconstruction views. We propose 2DGS-Planner, a path planner for ground robots that reads planning-relevant geometry from a 2D Gaussian splatting (2DGS) map through rasterization, rather than treating individual Gaussian primitives as obstacles. During offline roadmap construction, multi-view attribution converts rendered normal dispersion into structural scores for non-ground disks supported by the reconstruction views. These scores guide adaptive node sampling on the ground. Path-aligned orthographic queries validate candidate edges, while cylindrical queries estimate local clearance fields that are cached on the edges. During online planning, graph search initializes a route, and path refinement reuses the cached fields while accounting for the robot's dimensions and ground constraints. Experiments demonstrate improved roadmap connectivity, more accurate clearance estimation, and higher planning success compared with the tested baselines. These results support rasterization as an effective geometric query interface for planning directly on Gaussian maps. Code and data are available at this https URL

</details>


### [217] [UltraLight Luma: A Novel Edge-Deployable Perception Network for Crop-Row Segmentation in Agricultural Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11764)
*Dhanushka Balasingham,Shamod Ginigaddarage,Hanojhan Rajahrajasingh,Nuwan Kodagoda,Rajitha de Silva*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出超轻量编码器-解码器分割网络 UltraLight Luma（仅 13.1k 可训练参数），结合渐进式学习策略实现作物行检测与导航线提取，单次推理能耗仅 25.23 mJ，面向低成本农业机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主农业机器人需要可靠的作物行感知，但低成本部署受计算、内存、功耗与推理时间限制，现有大模型（U-Net、YOLO 系列）难以在资源受限平台上落地。

Method: UltraLight Luma：紧凑的编码器-解码器分割网络，仅 13.1k 可训练参数，采用渐进式学习（progressive learning）策略提升训练效率与精度；对预测的作物行掩码使用后处理算法估计行对齐方向，用于下游视觉伺服控制。

Result: 在保持可靠作物行检测性能的同时，参数效率优于 U-Net、YOLOv8 与 YOLOv26；单次推理能耗仅 25.23 mJ，验证了其在资源受限农业机器人上的适用性。

Conclusion: 极轻量化分割网络可在极端参数预算下完成作物行感知与导航线提取，为低成本农业机器人视觉导航提供了可行的节能方案；但缺乏真实机器人田间部署与多场景泛化验证。

Abstract: Reliable crop-row perception is essential for autonomous agricultural robots, but low-cost deployment is constrained by computation, memory, power, and inference time. This paper addresses crop-row detection and navigation-line extraction using UltraLight Luma, a compact encoder-decoder segmentation network with a progressive learning strategy and only 13.1k trainable parameters. The predicted crop-row masks are processed using a post-processing algorithm to estimate row alignment for downstream visual servoing. UltraLight Luma improves parameter efficiency over U-Net, YOLOv8, and YOLOv26 while maintaining reliable crop-row detection performance. The model required only 25.23 mJ per inference, demonstrating its suitability for resource-constrained agricultural robots.

</details>


### [218] [PathTime-VLA: Path-Time Decoupling for Factorized Post-Training of Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11771)
*Qing Huang,Yifei Yang,Ziqing Zou,Anzhe Chen,Zhenjie Zhu,Yufei Wei,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PathTime-VLA 将经典运动规划中的路径-时间参数化引入 VLA 动作表示，用进度索引路径 X(s) 与单调时间律 t(s) 解耦几何路径与执行速度，并通过分阶段后训练（BC/DAgger + Speed-DQN + Path-AWR）实现可变速执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略在固定时间间隔预测动作，将机器人所走路径与执行节奏耦合。这使遥操作数据迁移困难：几何指导信息有用，但其时序受界面延迟与操作者行为影响。作者希望把时间与路径解耦，从而在不改变几何预测目标的前提下自由选择执行速度。

Method: 提出路径-时间表征：运动被表示为进度索引交互路径 X(s) 与正的时间间隔剖面，后者定义单调时间律 t(s)，控制器命令为 X(s(t))。同一路径下不同执行由时间剖面表达，可在不改变几何预测目标的情况下做 chunk 级速度选择。分阶段后训练：示教与 DAgger 干预建立目标域先验；Speed-DQN 从机器人交互学习执行倍率；Path-AWR 用 rollout 结果精炼扩散路径生成器；路径条件动作专家实现运动，同时保持路径生成与执行时序两类学习接口分离。

Result: 在三个任务上，完整方法取得 58/60 成功次数，而 BC+DAgger 固定 1× 速度的 PathTime-VLA 为 57/60；成功试次的平均完成时间缩短约 39%–52%。

Conclusion: 将路径-时间参数化引入 VLA 动作表示，可在基本不损失成功率的前提下显著提升执行效率，并支持路径生成与执行时序的解耦学习与后训练。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies typically predict actions at fixed time intervals, coupling the route a robot follows with its execution pace. This coupling complicates adaptation from teleoperation: useful geometric guidance comes with timing shaped by interface delays and operator behavior. Our key insight is to bring the path-time parameterization of classical motion planning into the learned action representation of a VLA. We introduce PathTime-VLA, which represents motion as a progress-indexed interaction path $X(s)$ and a positive interval-time profile. The latter defines a monotone time law $t(s)$, yielding controller commands $X(s(t))$. For a given path, alternative executions are expressed through the time profile, allowing chunk-wise speed choices without changing the geometric prediction target. This representation supports a staged post-training procedure: demonstrations and DAgger interventions establish a target-domain prior, Speed-DQN learns execution multipliers from robot interaction, and Path-AWR uses rollout outcomes to refine the diffusion path generator. A path-conditioned action expert realizes the resulting motions while maintaining distinct learning interfaces for path generation and execution timing. Across three tasks, the complete method achieves $58/60$ successes versus $57/60$ for PathTime-VLA under BC + DAgger at fixed $1\times$, with approximately $39$-$52\%$ shorter mean completion times over successful trials.

</details>


### [219] [WAND: Learning Robust Navigation under Complex Wind Disturbances and Dense Obstacles for Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11809)
*Zhonghan Tang,Chenhui Li,Shuai Liang,Zhongrui You,Jianan Li,Bin Zhao,Zhigang Wang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 WAND 强化学习框架，用 TCN 从本体感受历史估计风扰加速度，经零初始化 WindAdapter 注入策略并同时为底层控制提供前馈补偿，实现密集障碍中风扰下的鲁棒导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式导航策略仅依赖障碍感知与本体征，需隐式推断时变风扰，在部分可观测下鲁棒性受限；风扰会扰动动力学、限制控制权限并显著提高碰撞风险。

Method: 用 TCN 从历史本体感受状态回归风致扰动加速度；估计量通过零初始化残差模块 WindAdapter 融入策略网络，形成扰动条件化导航；同时将同一估计用于低层控制的前馈补偿，实现估计的"双用"耦合。

Result: 12 种风扰仿真设置下，相较仅前馈补偿平均成功率提升 8.3 个百分点；对照侧风实验显示轨迹随风向自适应；室内风扇扰流飞行测试 20 次成功 18 次，验证实时机载可行性。

Conclusion: 显式估计并将扰动信息同时用于策略条件化与前馈补偿，可有效提升四旋翼在时变风扰与密集障碍下的导航鲁棒性，且具备实机部署可行性。

Abstract: Robust navigation in cluttered environments remains a fundamental challenge for quadrotors, particularly when strong wind disturbances arise, which perturb vehicle dynamics, limit control authority, and substantially increase collision risk. Existing learning-based navigation policies typically rely on obstacle perception and proprioceptive observations, requiring the policy to infer time-varying disturbance effects implicitly and thereby limiting robustness under partial observability. This paper proposes WAND (Wind-Aware Navigation with Disturbance Estimation), a reinforcement learning framework for navigation under time-varying wind disturbances in dense obstacle fields. Specifically, WAND estimates wind-induced disturbance acceleration from historical proprioceptive states using a Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN). This estimation is integrated into the policy via a zero-initialized residual module, \emph{WindAdapter}, while simultaneously providing feedforward compensation for low-level control. The dual use of the estimate couples disturbance-conditioned navigation with feedforward disturbance rejection. Across 12 wind-disturbed simulation settings, WAND improved the observed success rate by 8.3 percentage points on average relative to feedforward compensation alone. Controlled opposite-crosswind experiments further showed wind-direction-dependent trajectory adaptation. In indoor fan-induced flight tests, WAND succeeded in 18 of 20 trials, demonstrating the feasibility of real-time onboard navigation.

</details>


### [220] [STAG: A Sparse Traversability-Aware Graph Representation from Grid-Based Costmaps for Robotic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11943)
*Gabriel Manuel Garcia,Stéphanie Aravecchia,Miguel Angel Olivares-Mendez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STAG 将稠密栅格代价地图转换为稀疏可通行性感知图以实现高效全局规划：保留中轴拓扑骨架、同质可通行区域代表节点与强梯度处的过渡节点，边编码几何与可通行性代价；在合成洞穴、矿井及 DARPA CERBERUS 数据集共 203 张地图、101,200 次查询上，中位规划时间降低 3.4–9.9 倍、峰值查询内存降低 2.1–15.4 倍，路径长度中位差异为 -2.9%/ +7.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大范围非结构化环境中的自主漫游需要兼顾地形可通行性的全局规划，但基于稠密栅格的代价地图搜索随已建图区域增长而计算代价急剧上升，内存与时间开销成为规模化部署瓶颈，因此需要一种紧凑且保留地形难度的全局规划表示。

Method: 提出 STAG（Sparse Traversability-Aware Graph）：1）以中轴（medial-axis）拓扑骨架作为图的骨架，保证连通性与拓扑完整性；2）对可通行性同质区域插入代表节点以减少冗余采样；3）在可通行性梯度强的位置插入过渡节点以保留地形难度变化；4）边同时编码几何信息（路径长度）与可通行性（地形代价）；在 STAG 与稠密栅格上分别运行 A* 进行对比。

Result: 在合成洞穴地图、矿井地图及 DARPA CERBERUS 数据集五个基准类别、203 张地图实例、101,200 次查询上：中位规划时间降低 3.4–9.9 倍，峰值查询内存降低 2.1–15.4 倍，路径长度中位相对差异为 -2.9% 与 +7.6%（即部分场景略优于栅格最优，部分场景略长）。

Conclusion: STAG 为全局规划提供了紧凑的图表示，以牺牲稠密栅格上的可通行性最优性为代价换取更快、更省内存的搜索，适用于大尺度非结构化环境中的全局规划。局限在于未展示实机验证与增量更新/动态重规划能力。

Abstract: Autonomous rovers navigating large unstructured environments need efficient global planning that accounts for terrain traversability. However, searching dense grid-based costmaps becomes computationally expensive as the mapped area grows. We introduce STAG, a Sparse Traversability-Aware Graph that converts costmaps into compact graphs. STAG combines a medial-axis topological backbone, representative nodes for homogeneous traversability regions, and transition nodes near strong traversability gradients. Edges encode geometry and traversability to account for path length and terrain difficulty. We compare A* on STAG and dense grids using synthetic cave maps, mine maps and the DARPA CERBERUS dataset. Across five benchmark categories comprising 203 map instances and 101,200 queries, STAG reduces median planning time by 3.4x to 9.9x and peak query memory by 2.1x to 15.4x, with median relative path-length differences of -2.9% and +7.6%. STAG offers a compact representation for global planning, trading dense-grid traversability optimality for faster, less memory-intensive search.

</details>


### [221] [TACROSS: An Efficient and Low-Cost Scalable Human Touch System Across Heterogeneous Tactile Sensors for Dexterous Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11945)
*Bo Chen,Huanzhang Hu,Junyang Ma,Bo Yue,Fangdi Yu,Haijier Chen,Xianxin Lai,Shuyu Pan,Zhen Yang,Xiaoquan Sun,Wenze Cui,Zhongliang Jiang,Shaopeng Liu,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TACROSS 通过低成本压阻手套（五层、285 个传感点、成本约 10.86 美元）采集人类触觉示范，在"接触事件"语义层面而非原始传感通道层面进行跨本体对齐，并用时序 Transformer 将异构触觉信号映射到 256 维共享触觉潜空间，从而实现从人类触觉到机器人策略的可扩展迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人上采集触觉示范成本高、速度慢，人类触觉手套是更可扩展的替代方案；但人类电容/压阻手套与机器人触觉传感器在换能原理、传感器布局、空间分辨率和动态响应上存在本质差异，直接做原始通道对齐是病态问题，因此需要新的对齐范式。

Method: 硬件：五层压阻式手套，285 个传感点，成本 10.86 美元。算法：设计规范化器（canonicalizer）与残差适配器（residual adapter），通过跨手指注意力的时序 Transformer 将异构信号映射到 256 维共享触觉潜空间以实现接触语义对齐；提出"以机器人为基"的策略学习框架——机器人示范是 ground-truth 动作监督的唯一来源，人类示范仅用于触觉表征学习并经有效重定向手部目标提供置信度加权辅助监督。

Result: 在四个接触密集型操作任务上评估；相比传统遥操作，效率提升 3.5 倍，示范采集设备成本降低 95.7%。将开源软硬件系统，并公开超过 150 小时触觉数据集的记录。

Conclusion: 以接触事件为对齐单位可有效弥合人类手套与机器人触觉传感器之间的本体异构性，兼顾低成本数据采集与策略迁移可行性；开源硬件、软件与大规模触觉数据集将支撑触觉具身学习的数据规模化。

Abstract: Collecting tactile demonstrations on robots is costly and slow, motivating the use of lower-cost human tactile gloves for scalable data collection. However, human capacitive/piezoresistive gloves and robotic tactile sensors differ fundamentally in transduction principle, sensor layout, spatial resolution, and dynamic response, making alignment of raw sensor channels ill-posed. To address this problem, we present TACROSS, a scalable system for learning from human touch and transferring it to robots that bridges this heterogeneity by aligning tactile streams at the level of contact events rather than raw sensor values. The hardware component of TACROSS integrates a piezoresistive glove with five layers and a cost of USD 10.86 with 285 sensing points. To align contact semantics, we design canonicalizers and residual adapters that map heterogeneous signals into a shared tactile latent with 256 dimensions via a temporal Transformer with attention across fingers. We further introduce a robot-grounded policy learning scheme in which robot demonstrations provide the sole source of ground-truth action supervision, while human demonstrations support tactile representation learning and provide confidence-weighted auxiliary supervision through valid retargeted hand targets. We evaluate our system on four contact-rich manipulation tasks. Compared to conventional teleoperation, our proposed system achieves a 3.5-fold efficiency improvement while reducing demonstration acquisition equipment cost by 95.7%. We will open-source the TACROSS hardware and software system and publicly release a tactile dataset comprising over 150 hours of recordings. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [222] [Tell Robot What Not to Do: A Negation Understanding Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11952)
*Fazeng Li,Gan Sun,Hao Cheng,Weihong Ren,Yang Cong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NegaAlign：一种参数高效、即插即用的框架，通过图像-语言监督（无需动作标签）让预训练 VLA 模型遵循否定指令；引入 Negation Transformation Layers 重塑指令表示，并用教师引导对齐机制对齐指令相关视觉 token，仅训练 11.6M 参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 只关注"做什么"，缺乏对"不要做什么"这类否定约束的遵循能力；而人类交互中显式排除约束（如"别碰花瓶"）对安全与任务成功至关重要。

Method: 在视觉-语言骨干的部分层插入 Negation Transformation Layers 以重塑中间指令表示；设计教师引导对齐机制，对齐指令相关视觉 token，将满足否定约束后动作相关 grounding 迁移过来；训练仅更新插入层，冻结全部预训练参数（含动作生成器），监督信号由已有演示构造；同时提出 NegaBench 仿真基准（5 领域 10 场景）。

Result: 在 GR00T、π0、π0.5 上一致提升否定指令遵循；仅 11.6M 可训参数下，π0.5 在 NegaBench 上否定指令成功率从 2.60%→88.45%，真实任务从 12.4%→88.8%，同时保持肯定指令性能。

Conclusion: NegaAlign 以极小训练开销、即插即用地补齐了 VLA 的否定指令遵循能力，并兼顾真实机器人验证与肯定指令不退化，为约束感知的机器人交互提供可推广方案。

Abstract: Instruction following enables robots to perform diverse tasks specified in natural language, making it a fundamental capability for human-robot interaction. Beyond communicating desired outcomes, users also need to specify constraints on what not to do. We investigate how to enable vision-language-action models (VLAs) to follow negated instructions, where robots must accomplish task goals while respecting explicit exclusions. To this end, we propose NegaAlign, a parameter-efficient, plug-and-play framework that extends pretrained VLAs to follow negated instructions through image-language supervision alone. Specifically, we introduce Negation Transformation Layers into selected layers of the vision-language backbone to reshape intermediate instruction representations. Meanwhile, a teacher-guided alignment mechanism is designed to align instruction-relevant visual tokens, transferring action-relevant grounding from instructions that satisfy the negated constraint. The training phase uses supervision constructed from existing demonstrations and updates only the inserted layers, keeping all pretrained parameters frozen, including the action generator. We further introduce NegaBench, a simulation benchmark spanning 10 scenarios across five domains for systematically evaluating manipulation under negated constraints. Experiments across GR00T, $\pi_0$, and $\pi_{0.5}$ demonstrate consistent improvements in negated instruction following. With 11.6M trainable parameters, NegaAlign increases the negated-instruction success rate of $\pi_{0.5}$ from 2.60% to 88.45% on NegaBench and from 12.4% to 88.8% on real-world tasks, while retaining performance on affirmative instructions.

</details>


### [223] [Reliability-Aware Future Conditioning for Temporally Robust Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11956)
*Mohammad Khoshnazar,Mohammad Dehghani Tezerjani,Zhiyuan Gao,Deyuan Qu,Max Gandyra,Yanxiang Zhan,Mehreen Naeem,Andrew Melnik,Jeroen Schafer,Qing Yang,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 指出生成未来视频与机器人实际阶段的时间错位会带来负面引导，提出RAFC以"控制"方式在线估计信任度与时间偏移假设，在CALVIN及Franka上显著提升时序错配下的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式未来视频只有在描绘机器人当前所处阶段时才有指导价值。已有方法默认生成片段与当前时刻对齐，忽视时序错位（提前/滞后、帧率不匹配）会把"任务一致"的未来变成有害引导，作者希望量化并解决这一问题。

Method: 先给出FEC未来经验条件化：任务grounding、无机器人digital-twin rollout与mask-free视频扩散一次性构建片段；再提出RAFC，把时序可靠性当作控制问题，每步估计对收到片段的信任程度与偏好的邻近时间假设，两者都不合适时退回静态分支，仅用任务奖励学习，无需偏移标签或对齐监督；配合候选集成。

Result: CALVIN上仅提前5帧就把成功率从81.3%降到54.8%（无未来基线54.0%），强制时序错位进一步跌至34.2%（低于无未来策略19.8点）；RAFC在off-grid相位偏移与速率失配下大幅恢复性能，候选集成负责近对齐区间的主要回复，学习可靠性在off-grid偏移下比同候选库均匀平均再高7.0个百分点；Franka自然时序错配下总成功率26.7%提升至56.7%。

Conclusion: 生成未来的时序对齐是独立且关键的控制问题，适度回退与可靠性估计可将未来条件化从脆弱变为鲁棒；无需对齐标注、仅凭任务奖励即可学习。资源将公开。

Abstract: A generated video of a task the robot is about to perform is useful guidance only if it depicts the phase the robot is actually in. We show that temporal misalignment can turn a task-consistent generated future into actively harmful guidance. On CALVIN, a five-frame early shift nearly erases the benefit of generated futures, reducing success from 81.3% to 54.8% against 54.0% without futures; imposed timing shifts reduce it even further to 34.2%, 19.8 points below the future-free policy. We introduce Reliability-Aware Future Conditioning (RAFC), which treats this as a control problem rather than a generation problem. At every step, RAFC estimates how far to trust the received clip and which nearby temporal hypothesis to prefer, falling back toward a static branch when neither fits, and it learns both from task reward alone without shift labels or alignment supervision. RAFC sits on top of Future-Experience Conditioning (FEC), which builds the clip once from task grounding, a robot-free digital-twin rollout, and mask-free video diffusion. Under deliberately off-grid phase shifts and rate mismatch, RAFC substantially improves success under temporal mismatch. Candidate ensembling accounts for most of the recovery near alignment, while learned reliability adds a further 7.0 percentage points over uniform averaging of the identical candidate bank under off-grid shifts. The gain holds on the evaluated task sets and survives on a Franka under natural timing mismatch nobody imposed, where aggregate success rises from 26.7% to 56.7%. All resources will be made publicly available. this https URL .

</details>


### [224] [CAPABLE: Capability-Aware Policy Adaptation via Behavioral Latent Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11971)
*Mohammad Khoshnazar,Mohammad Dehghani Tezerjani,Deyuan Qu,Zhiyuan Gao,Yanxiang Zhan,Jeroen Schafer,Andrew Melnik,Qing Yang,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAPABLE：面向冻结VLA的统一"能力感知"自适应框架，用自监督能力推断+残差强化学习，在线估计各关节实际执行能力（含对末端贡献），据此为VLA手臂动作叠加有界修正，无需故障标签或故障关节标识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略隐含"训练机型即部署机型"的假设，一旦关节故障改变了指令动作的物理执行方式，策略就会失效。已有故障恢复方法依赖任务级重训、故障标签、显式诊断或特权本体信息，泛化与部署成本高。

Method: 冻结VLA不动，仅学习残差策略。核心是自监督能力推断：从指令-响应历史与运动学在线推断"能力"（每个关节实际实现的运动比例及其对末端执行器行为的贡献）。结构上共享跨关节的时序编码器、雅可比接地、跨关节注意力与自监督物理预测；所得表示条件化一个残差策略，输出有界修正项。全程不使用故障标签与故障关节标识。

Result: 28个LIBERO任务上，对于被排除在故障训练之外的一个执行器，成功率从24.8%提升到59.3%，比参数规模匹配的全局历史基线高17.4个百分点，同时保持健康状态性能。对六个关节做留一执行器实验，证明迁移不局限于单一执行器；额外评估显示可迁移到未见故障族，并在实体Franka Panda上实现恢复。

Conclusion: 将"能力"作为可在线自监督推断的隐变量，配合残差RL，可让冻结VLA在无需标签、诊断与特权信息下获得跨执行器、跨故障族的通用故障恢复能力，并有真机验证。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies assume the embodiment on which they were trained and can fail when a joint fault changes how commanded actions are physically executed. Existing fault-recovery methods often require task-specific retraining, fault labels, explicit diagnosis, or privileged embodiment information. We introduce CAPABLE, a unified capability-aware adaptation framework for frozen VLAs that integrates self-supervised capability inference with residual reinforcement learning. CAPABLE infers capability, how much of the commanded motion each joint actually realizes and how that motion contributes to end-effector behavior, online from command-response history and kinematics using a temporal encoder shared across joints, Jacobian grounding, cross-joint attention, and self-supervised physical prediction. The resulting representation conditions a residual policy that adds bounded corrections to the VLA arm action without fault labels or faulty-joint identifiers. Across 28 LIBERO tasks, CAPABLE raises success on an actuator excluded from fault training from 24.8% to 59.3%, outperforming a parameter-matched global-history baseline by 17.4 points while preserving healthy performance. Leave-one-actuator-out experiments across six joints show that this transfer is not specific to one actuator, and additional evaluations characterize transfer to unseen fault families and demonstrate recovery on a physical Franka Panda. this https URL

</details>


### [225] [Predicting Cable Dynamics with Physical Attention Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11975)
*Avihai Giuili,Rotem Atari,Avishai Sintov,Maya Bechler-Speicher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究可变形线性物体（DLO，如线缆）学习型模拟器中的注意力几何偏置问题，提出在注意力 logits 上加入带可学习速率的物理偏置项，并系统比较"无偏置""弧长距离""欧氏距离""两者分头"四种设置，发现物理偏置能提升未见线缆的预测精度，弧长偏置在注意力为唯一远端连接机制时误差降低 15%、段长漂移减半以上，两者分头分配在各项指标上最优或近优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线缆等 DLO 学习型模拟器的主要误差集中在自接触或与地面接触处，且需在长时滚动中保持稳定、泛化到未训练线缆。全对注意力可建模沿长度相距较远的段间接触，但注意力本身缺乏几何感知。线缆存在两种成对距离：弧长距离（决定弹性力）与空间欧氏距离（决定接触），仅在伸直时一致，因此需要研究注意力该使用哪种距离作为物理偏置。

Method: 在注意力 logits 上加入加性物理偏置项，偏置速率可学习；在模型其余部分与训练流程完全固定的前提下，对比四种设置：无偏置、仅弧长距离、仅欧氏距离、以及在不同注意力头上分别使用两种距离。

Result: 物理偏置改善了对未见线缆的预测。增益在注意力是连接远端线段的唯一机制时最大：弧长偏置使预测误差降低 15%，段长漂移减少一半以上。仅欧氏偏置的效果接近无偏置注意力，而将两种距离分配到不同头上在报告的所有指标上均为最优或接近最优。

Conclusion: 为注意力引入与几何相关的物理偏置（尤其是弧长偏置）可有效提升 DLO 学习型模拟器的泛化性与长时稳定性；将弧长与欧氏两种距离解耦到不同注意力头是一种简单且普遍有效的设计。

Abstract: Learned simulators for deformable linear objects (DLOs) such as cables have to predict the motion of cables they were not trained on and stay stable over long rollouts. Most of their error occurs where the cable touches itself or the floor. Attention over all pairs of cable segments can represent contact between parts of the cable that are far apart along its length, but attention has no notion of geometry. A cable has two pairwise distances, which agree only while it is straight: the arc-length distance along the cable, which governs elastic forces, and the Euclidean distance in space, which governs contact. We add a physical attention bias, an additive term on the attention logits with a learned rate, and ask which distance it should use. We compare no bias, each distance alone, and both distances on disjoint sets of heads, keeping the rest of the model and the training protocol fixed. A physical bias improves prediction on unseen cables. The gain is largest when attention is the only mechanism that connects distant segments: there, the arc-length bias reduces prediction error by 15% and more than halves the drift in segment length. The Euclidean bias alone stays close to unbiased attention, while assigning both distances across heads is best or near-best on every metric we report. Code and per-run records: this https URL .

</details>


### [226] [REACT: Rolling Denoising and Dual Decoupling for Reactive Robot Control with VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12007)
*Houlong Xiong,Zhenqi Qiu,Zechen Wang,Suohang Zhang,Yiyu Ren,Wanting Xu,Hongfei Niu,Chengyang He,Ge Sun,Ran Cheng,Qian Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REACT 提出滚动去噪框架，让基于流的 VLA 在保持长时程上下文的同时提升反应性：维护带交错流时间步的持久动作缓冲区，每步用最新观测对整个时域去噪，执行最干净的块、前移部分精炼的未来块、尾部补新噪声，并配合双解耦实现实时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流匹配的 VLA 以动作块（chunk）形式输出动作，存在闭环权衡：块越长执行越平滑，但重规划频率低导致反应性差；频繁重规划虽提升反应性，却带来动作不连续与抖动。需要一种既能保持长时程平滑、又能高频响应新观测的控制机制。

Method: REACT 采用滚动去噪：不复用整段重生成，而是维护一个持久动作缓冲区，各块处于交错的流时间步（噪声程度不同）。每个控制步用最新观测对整个时域进行去噪，执行最干净（最成熟）的动作块，将部分精炼的未来块向前平移，并在尾部追加新噪声；因此每个被执行的动作块在部署前已跨多个近期观测被反复精炼。此外提出双解耦，将感知、VLM 编码、DiT 去噪与动作执行分离，在高频观测更新下实现动作流式输出，满足实际算力下的实时约束。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 仿真基准以及跨多机器人平台的双臂操作与动态控制真机任务上，REACT 相比频繁重规划与异步基线提高了任务成功率、降低了反应延迟，并产生更平滑的轨迹。

Conclusion: 通过滚动去噪与交错时间步的动作缓冲区，可在不牺牲长时程上下文的前提下显著提升流式 VLA 的闭环反应性；双解耦使该方案在真实算力约束下可实时运行，兼具仿真与真机验证，是 VLA 闭环控制层面较具实用价值的改进。

Abstract: Flow-based vision-language-action (VLA) models generate action chunks for temporally coherent robot motion, but chunked control creates a fundamental closed-loop trade-off: long chunks provide smooth execution, whereas frequent replanning improves reactivity at the cost of action discontinuities. We introduce REACT, a rolling-denoising framework that makes flow-based VLAs more reactive while preserving long-horizon context. Instead of regenerating entire action chunks from scratch, REACT maintains a persistent action buffer with staggered flow timesteps. At each control step, the full horizon is denoised using the latest observation, the cleanest action block is executed, partially refined future blocks are shifted forward, and fresh noise is appended to the tail. As a result, each executed action block is refined across multiple recent observations before deployment. To support real-time control, we further introduce dual decoupling, which separates sensing, VLM encoding, DiT denoising, and action execution, enabling high-frequency observation updates and action streaming under practical compute constraints. Across the RoboTwin 2.0 simulation benchmark and real-world tasks spanning bimanual manipulation and dynamic control on multiple robot platforms, REACT improves task success and reduces reaction latency while producing smoother trajectories than frequent-replanning and asynchronous baselines.

</details>


### [227] [Humanoid World Action Model With Joint State--Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12026)
*Yan Yang,Jikun Rong,Minzhao Zhu,Zheyi Zhao,Qirui Hu,Zihan Lan,Weixin Mao,Yinhao Li,Zhen Fu,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HWAM（人形机器人世界动作模型），将执行后的本体感知状态作为显式预测目标，通过策略路径、前向动力学建模（FDM）与逆向动力学建模（IDM）三条互补条件路径联合生成参考动作与真实执行状态，缓解分层人形系统中"动作—执行鸿沟"；在 LimX OLI 真机三项任务上成功率最高，Candy Picking 达 70.6%（对比 Fast-WAM 的 43.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层人形机器人中，VLA/WAM 输出的是参考动作，需经全身控制、动力学、平衡与接触才能实现，导致参考动作与真实执行运动之间存在"动作—执行鸿沟"。若不对实际执行的身体状态显式建模，未来视觉预测必须同时解释场景演化与参考—执行偏差，难以将动作与其物理结果关联。

Method: HWAM 将机器人执行后的本体感知状态作为显式联合预测目标，联合生成参考动作及其实现的身体状态。训练含三条互补条件路径：Policy 路径仅以当前观测为条件，联合去噪状态—动作轨迹，匹配部署条件；Forward Dynamics Modeling（FDM）以动作与执行后状态为条件预测未来视觉观测；Inverse Dynamics Modeling（IDM）从视觉转移重建关节轨迹。三者共同连接策略参考、真实身体运动与视觉结果。

Result: 在 LimX OLI 人形机器人三项真机任务上，HWAM 在所评估基线中取得最高成功率；Candy Picking 任务成功率为 70.6%，Fast-WAM 为 43.3%。

Conclusion: 显式建模执行后本体状态可有效弥合人形分层系统中的动作—执行鸿沟，提升真机操作任务成功率，为世界动作模型与全身控制的衔接提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are a promising platform for general-purpose manipulation. Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies learn actions directly from multimodal observations, while World Action Models (WAMs) further incorporate future visual prediction to improve action generation. However, in hierarchical humanoid systems, VLA and WAM policies output reference actions that are subsequently realized through whole-body control, robot dynamics, balance, and contact. This hierarchy creates an action--execution gap: the reference produced by the policy can differ from the motion realized by the robot. Without explicitly modeling the realized body state, future visual prediction must jointly explain scene evolution and discrepancies between reference actions and executed motion, making it difficult to associate an action with its physical outcome. We propose HWAM, a Humanoid World Action Model with joint state--action generation, which makes the robot's post-execution proprioceptive state an explicit prediction target. By jointly generating reference actions and their realized body states, HWAM directly incorporates supervision of executed motion into action learning. HWAM is trained through three complementary conditional paths. The Policy path jointly denoises state--action trajectories conditioned only on current observations, matching deployment conditions. Forward Dynamics Modeling (FDM) predicts future visual observations conditioned on actions and post-execution states, while Inverse Dynamics Modeling (IDM) reconstructs the joint trajectory from visual transitions. Together, these paths connect policy references, realized body motion, and visual outcomes. HWAM achieves the highest success rate among evaluated baselines on three real-robot tasks on the LimX OLI humanoid. On Candy Picking, HWAM achieves a 70.6% success rate, compared with 43.3% for Fast-WAM.

</details>


### [228] [Traceable World State: A Provenance-Aware State Representation and Deterministic Replay Framework for Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12033)
*Zoe Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TWS 是一种面向机器人世界状态的可溯源语义表示与参考运行时，通过不可变快照、确定性重放和 SHA-256 哈希链实现可审计的世界状态管理，并在 BEHAVIOR-1K 与 Unitree G1 仿真轨迹上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人长期任务中的世界状态由不同时刻、不同置信度的观测拼装而成，且需要不断修订；传统表示只保留最新估计，导致事实来源不可追溯、决策不可复现、执行过程无法审计。作者希望提供一种支持溯源的世界状态表示与运行时。

Method: 提出 Traceable World State（TWS）：一种中间件中立的语义表示与参考运行时。TWS 快照捕获实体、关系、观测、置信度与修订元数据；经校验的更新操作以不可变方式变换快照；有序更新支持确定性重放；规范 SHA-256 哈希链保证日志防篡改。评估包括模式一致性、完整状态生命周期、确定性重放与故障注入。

Result: 在 Python 3.10-3.14 上通过 38 项测试，可检测记录损坏、哈希链断裂、序列不连续与世界不匹配。在十个公开 BEHAVIOR-1K 任务定义中导入 153 个实体与 146 个关系并通过校验。在 103 条 NVIDIA Unitree G1 仿真轨迹（78,369 帧）上，所有回合均实现精确终态重放，检出 412/412 次注入损坏，相比 Plain JSONL 仅增加 1.72% 存储开销。

Conclusion: TWS 提供了一种可溯源、可确定性重放且防篡改的机器人世界状态表示与运行时，适用于需要审计与复现的长期机器人任务。

Abstract: Robotic systems operating over extended tasks must maintain a world state assembled from observations arriving at different times, with varying confidence and potential revisions. Conventional representations emphasize latest estimates, hindering fact provenance, decision reproduction, or execution auditing. We present Traceable World State (TWS), a middleware-neutral semantic representation and reference runtime for provenance-aware robot world state. A TWS snapshot captures entities, relations, observations, confidence, and revision metadata. Validated update operations transform snapshots immutably, ordered updates support deterministic replay, and a canonical SHA-256 hash chain ensures tamper-evident logs. We evaluate TWS through schema conformance, complete state lifecycles, deterministic replay, and fault injection. Passing 38 tests across Python 3.10-3.14, the framework detects record corruptions, broken hash links, sequence discontinuities, and world mismatches. Across ten public BEHAVIOR-1K task definitions, TWS imported 153 entities and 146 relations with successful validation. On 103 NVIDIA Unitree G1 simulated trajectories containing 78,369 frames, TWS achieved exact terminal-state replay in all episodes and detected 412/412 injected corruptions with a 1.72% storage overhead over Plain JSONL.

</details>


### [229] [SkillWeave: Weaving Heterogeneous Demonstrations into Long-Horizon Manipulation Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12046)
*Ryosei Tamura,Xiaoxiang Dong,Uksang Yoo,Yuemin Mao,Romina Mir,Jonathan Francis,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkillWeave 提出异构示教框架：粗粒度移动用遥操作、精细接触技能用动觉示教，并配合物体掩码条件扩散策略与后继感知交接引导，在三个真实长时域灵巧操作任务上实现 27% 端到端成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域灵巧操作同时需要大范围任务推进与精细接触交互，单一示教模态难以兼顾：遥操作利于粗略到达与搬运但接触精度不足，动觉示教利于精细接触但采集时人类遮挡造成视觉-动作不匹配；同时独立训练的兄弟子任务策略之间存在分布漂移，导致交接失败。

Method: 采用异构示教：遥操作采集粗粒度到达/搬运，动觉示教采集精细接触技能。针对动觉数据中示教者遮挡引起的视觉不匹配，提出物体掩码条件扩散策略——训练时用离线物体分割作监督，部署时用轻量学得的掩码预测器，避免在线分割与图像修复。针对子任务策略间分布漂移，提出后继感知终止引导（successor-aware terminal steering），从前驱策略采样的动作中挑选将系统导向后继策略初始状态分布支持区域的行动。

Result: 三个真实世界长时域任务上平均端到端成功率 27%；掩码条件动觉策略将灵巧子任务成功率提升至平均 65%；后继感知交接平均组合效率达 87%。已公开视频与代码。

Conclusion: 将示教模态与交互范式相匹配、显式处理动觉示教的视觉不匹配、并把策略交接引导至后继策略支持的状态，可显著提升长时域灵巧操作性能。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation requires both large-scale task progression and precise contact-rich interaction, making it challenging to collect demonstrations that effectively support both regimes. We present SkillWeave, a heterogeneous demonstration framework for long-horizon dexterous manipulation that combines teleoperation for coarse reaching and transport with kinesthetic teaching for precise, contact-rich skills. To address the visual mismatch introduced by the demonstrator's presence during kinesthetic data collection, we propose an object-mask-conditioned diffusion policy that uses offline object segmentation for training supervision and a lightweight learned mask predictor at deployment, avoiding online segmentation and image inpainting. To mitigate distribution shift between independently trained sub-task policies, we introduce successor-aware terminal steering, which selects among actions sampled from the predecessor policy to guide the system toward states supported by the successor's demonstrated initial-state distribution. Across three real-world long-horizon tasks, SkillWeave achieves 27% average end-to-end success. Mask-conditioned kinesthetic policies improve dexterous sub-task success to an average of 65%, while successor-aware handoffs achieve an average composition efficiency of 87%. These results show that matching demonstration modality to interaction regime, explicitly addressing kinesthetic visual mismatch, and steering policy handoffs toward successor-supported states substantially improves long-horizon dexterous manipulation. Videos and code are available at this http URL .

</details>


### [230] [A Minimal Optical-Flow Representation for Vision-Based Tactile Rotation Classification in Robotic Manipulation Across Gravity Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12073)
*Oscar Martinez-Bernal,Mario Cavero-Vidal,Francesco Grella,Carol Martinez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作用仿真GelSight Mini的稠密光流聚合为126维（可压缩至40维）触觉运动特征，研究不同重力条件下物体旋转方向的分类，发现重力会引入小而显著的域偏移，单一跨重力域模型可达96.3%总体精度，而仅用地球数据训练的模型在轨道重力下退化到75.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉触觉传感器信息丰富但高分辨率图像处理对空间机器人等资源受限平台代价高；同时地球重力下验证的触觉感知能否迁移到火星、月球、轨道等不同重力环境尚不明确，需要验证重力引起的域偏移是否影响触觉运动表征的可迁移性。

Method: 从仿真GelSight Mini获取稠密光流，在7x9网格上聚合为126维特征；在Earth、Mars、Moon、Orbital四种重力域下分类载荷诱导旋转的方向；使用XGBoost，分别训练单域（仅Earth）与四域联合模型（不以重力为输入），并做特征压缩实验与推理耗时测量。

Result: 重力引起的特征偏移虽仅解释1.6%方差（R2=0.016），但影响显著：仅Earth训练模型精度从Earth的94.4%降至轨道的75.9%；四域联合训练单模型总体精度96.3%，各域95.1%-97.0%；特征降至40维仍保持95.7%精度，XGBoost单次推理仅0.14 ms。

Conclusion: 仅凭地球重力下的性能不足以证明触觉感知在空间机器人操作中的可迁移性，训练与验证阶段需显式考虑重力诱导的域偏移；紧凑触觉运动表征可支持跨重力域的高效鲁棒分类。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensors provide rich contact information, but processing high-resolution images can be costly for resource-constrained platforms such as space robots. This work investigates whether a compact representation of tactile motion can classify object rotation across different gravity conditions. Dense optical flow from a simulated GelSight Mini is aggregated over a 7x9 grid into 126 features and used to classify the direction of load-induced rotation under Earth, Mars, Moon, and orbital gravity. Gravity causes a small but significant shift in these features, accounting for 1.6% of their variance (R2 = 0.016). Despite its small magnitude, this shift affects models trained only on Earth data: XGBoost accuracy decreases from 94.4% on Earth to 75.9% in orbit. In contrast, a single model trained across all four gravity domains achieves 96.3% overall accuracy and 95.1%-97.0% across individual domains, without using gravity as an input. The representation can also be reduced to 40 features while retaining 95.7% accuracy, with XGBoost requiring only 0.14 ms per inference. These findings show that Earth-gravity performance alone is insufficient to establish the transferability of tactile perception for space robotic manipulation, highlighting the need to account for gravity-induced domain shifts during training and validation.

</details>


### [231] [ManiUnit: A Manipulation Skill Dataset and Benchmark for Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12089)
*Guoting Wei,Dawei Yan,Xia Yuan,Gengming Zhang,Yelin He,Guodong Du,Jiaquan Ye,Heng Zhang,Xinming Wei,Xianbiao Qi,Chunxia Zhao,Haokui Zhang,Rong Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ManiUnit 是一个基于 BEHAVIOR-1K 的移动操作技能数据集与基准（13.7 万段、21 类技能、417 子任务、1260 测试实例），通过子任务级指令、起始状态扰动敏感度与可恢复中间仿真状态，实现技能级独立评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程移动操作需在单一自然语言指令下完成多房间导航与多技能序列，但现有学习与评测存在三难：固定任务指令下相似观测导致技能选择歧义；前序技能成功但继承的机器人状态偏离演示起始状态；任务级指标无法定位具体技能失败，且早期失败使后续技能无法被测试。

Method: 从 50 个 BEHAVIOR-1K 活动中构建 ManiUnit：为每段数据配显式子任务指令；定义对机器人基座位置或关节构型扰动的敏感度度量；恢复仿真中间状态并定义局部成功条件，使每个技能可脱离前序阶段独立评测。随后对代表性 VLA 策略做系统评测，并对比任务策略与技能策略，及通过规划器协调两者的完整任务执行。

Result: 相似总体得分会掩盖显著的技能间差异；关节扰动使全基准成功率相对演示起始状态下降约 56%。在两个长时程活动上，基于 ManiUnit 段训练的技策取得 78.7% 局部操作成功率，而基于完整演示训练的任务策略为 49.3%；通过规划器协调任务与技能策略，完整任务成功率从 4.0% 提升到 18.0%。

Conclusion: 子任务级监督、起始状态鲁棒性度量与可恢复中间状态评测，能揭示被聚合指标掩盖的技能级差异，并显著提升长时程移动操作的成功率；ManiUnit 为技能级诊断与策略组合提供了可复用的基准基础设施。

Abstract: Long-horizon mobile manipulation requires a robot to navigate multi-room environments and execute a sequence of manipulation skills under a single natural language instruction. Learning and evaluating these skills present three challenges: similar observations under a fixed task instruction may make skill selection ambiguous; even when a preceding skill succeeds, the robot state inherited by the next skill may deviate from its demonstrated starting states and affect execution; and task-level metrics hinder skill-specific diagnosis, while early failures leave later skills untested. We therefore introduce ManiUnit, a manipulation skill dataset and benchmark built from 50 BEHAVIOR-1K activities. Its dataset contains 137,899 segments across 21 skill types and 417 subtasks, and its benchmark contains 1,260 test instances. Correspondingly, ManiUnit pairs each segment with an explicit subtask instruction; measures sensitivity to perturbations of the robot's starting base position or joint configuration; and restores intermediate simulator states and defines local success conditions so that each skill can be evaluated without executing preceding stages. Evaluations of representative vision-language-action (VLA) policies show that similar aggregate scores can hide substantial per-skill differences. The tested starting-state perturbations also degrade execution: on the full benchmark, joint perturbations reduce success rates by approximately 56% relative to those from demonstrated starting states. On two long-horizon activities, a skill policy trained on ManiUnit segments achieves 78.7% local manipulation success, compared with 49.3% for a task policy trained on complete demonstrations. The trained skills further support complete-task execution on these activities, as coordinating the task and skill policies through a planner raises full-task success from 4.0% to 18.0%.

</details>


### [232] [UNITAS: A 3D-Native World Action Model for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12099)
*Ruixiang Wang,Yongyi Su,Wenlve Zhou,Bo Yue,Hengyan Liu,Dekun Lu,Yuxin Tian,Yihan Fang,Zerui Wu,Xing Hu,Jietao Chen,Yong Guo,Ziyan He,Junbin Yuan,Guiliang Liu,Xiaofen Xing,Kui Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UNITAS: 3D原生世界动作模型，在统一度量3D坐标系中用点轨迹表示动作流与场景流，1.7B参数实现强场景预测与操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型多基于预训练视频生成器，以图像或视觉隐变量表示世界演化；但机器人交互发生在度量三维空间中，图像是视角相关的投影，像素距离不直接对应物理距离。作者希望构建一个在统一度量3D框架内联合建模观测、动作与场景动力学的3D原生WAM，并用统一表示跨越机器人与人类手。

Method: 提出UNITAS，声称首个3D原生世界动作模型。动作流将人手与机器人夹爪表示为3D点轨迹，场景流在此基础上描述场景点位移；世界对齐3D位置嵌入在有无深度输入时均能对视觉token进行定位；物理时间轨迹分词器将每条点轨迹编码为锚定在当前3D位置的单个token。该接口同时支持直接动作执行与动作条件场景预测。

Result: 在1.7B参数规模下，UNITAS在RoboTwin上取得对比方法中最佳的动作条件场景预测，位移误差较PointWorld最高降低49%；操作成功率方面在LIBERO达99.8%，真实世界任务平均85%。

Conclusion: 用统一度量3D点轨迹表示动作与场景动力学是可行且有效的，能在跨具身（人手/机器人）条件下同时提升场景预测精度与操作成功率，为3D原生世界动作模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) aim to answer a coupled physical question: given a task instruction, what motion should the robot execute, and how will that motion change the surrounding world? Most existing WAMs build on pretrained video generators and represent world evolution through images or visual latents. Robotic interaction, however, takes place in metric three-dimensional space, while images are view-dependent projections whose pixel distances do not directly encode physical distances. We introduce UNITAS, to our knowledge the first 3D-native world action model that unifies observations, actions, and scene dynamics in a shared metric 3D frame within each interaction, using a common representation across robot embodiments and human hands. Action flow represents human hands and robot grippers as 3D point trajectories, while scene flow describes scene-point displacements conditioned on these trajectories. World-aligned 3D positional embeddings ground visual tokens with or without depth input, and a physical-time trajectory tokenizer encodes each point trajectory as one token anchored at its current 3D position. This interface supports both direct action execution and action-conditioned scene prediction. With 1.7B parameters, UNITAS achieves the best action-conditioned scene prediction on RoboTwin among the compared methods, with up to 49% lower displacement errors than PointWorld, and state-of-the-art manipulation success, including 99.8% on LIBERO and an average of 85% across real-world tasks. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [233] [PIER: An Evidence-Gated Execution Interface for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12123)
*Zoe Li,Anze Wang,Zhongyu Chen,Jingran Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PIER：一个将证据校验、决策溯源与阶段级再观测同硬件控制解耦的机器人执行授权接口，用1600组阈值网格与1200条合成轨迹评测其输入契约的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成合理的机器人动作并不等于当前观测支持其执行；作者由"严重遮挡下视觉输出仍高置信"这一探索性观察出发，希望显式分离"证据是否充分"与"动作是否执行"，使授权过程可审查而非隐藏在策略隐状态中。

Method: 设计确定性门控（deterministic gate）：对声明的视觉与触觉输入做阈值式证据检查，与硬件控制解耦；调用方维护每阶段至多一次再观测（re-observation）的预算；用1600组阈值网格案例与1200条配对合成轨迹，覆盖分数噪声、触觉缺失、陈旧观测、虚假安抚性分数四类扰动，并以匹配的布尔基线做对照。

Result: 有限阈值网格上零声明不变量违反，匹配布尔基线复现全部非恢复决策；合成分数噪声下再观测将有效状态拒行由57/120降至18/120，同时将无效状态放行由5/120升至7/120；陈旧观测与虚假安抚性分数暴露出纯阈值检查无法解决的局限。

Conclusion: 工作刻画了一个可审查的执行授权接口及其输入契约边界，明确不主张优于等价规则逻辑、标定后触觉精度或经过认证的物理安全；探索性视觉/触觉/机器人装置记录仅提供语境，未建立真实任务性能。

Abstract: Generating a plausible robot action does not establish that current observations justify its execution. Motivated by exploratory observations of high-confidence visual outputs under severe occlusion, we present PIER, an execution-authorization interface that separates evidence checks, decision provenance, and stage-scoped re-observation from hardware control. The deterministic gate evaluates declared visual and tactile inputs, while its caller maintains a budget of at most one re-observation per stage. We evaluate the implementation using 1,600 threshold-grid cases and 1,200 paired synthetic traces spanning score noise, missing tactile inputs, stale observations, and falsely reassuring scores. The finite grid yields zero declared invariant violations, and a matched Boolean baseline reproduces all non-recovery decisions. Under synthetic score noise, re-observation reduces valid-state denials from 57/120 to 18/120 while increasing invalid-state proceeds from 5/120 to 7/120. Stale and falsely reassuring inputs expose limitations that threshold checks alone cannot resolve. Exploratory visual, tactile, and robot setup records provide context but do not establish physical task performance. These results characterize an inspectable authorization interface and its input-contract limitations, without claiming superiority over equivalent rule logic, calibrated tactile accuracy, or certified physical safety.

</details>


### [234] [SuperNav: An Agentic Navigation System for Any Task in Any Scene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12126)
*Jinkai Zhang,Jingyi Xu,Yuanhong Yu,Jiarui Guo,Ruizhen Hu,Hujun Bao,Xiaowei Zhou,Sida Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SuperNav: 不微调 MLLM,而是通过 agent harness(导航技能、交互工具、进度/上下文管理、统一视觉点接口)实现任务与环境通用的机器人导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用服务机器人需同时具备任务通用性与场景通用性,但现有方法微调 MLLM 预测导航动作,行为受训练数据覆盖度限制,难以泛化到新请求与新环境。

Method: 保留预训练 MLLM 的通用能力,让其专注于请求理解、场景理解与决策,将运动执行委托给导航工具。构建 SuperNav harness:Navigation Skills 支持决策、面向 agent 的物理交互 Tools、任务进度与上下文管理;统一视觉点接口(image-point interface)允许模型直接在图像上指定目标点,并依据执行反馈修正决策。

Result: 在 instance-level、multi-object、demand-driven 任务上优于 4 个基线;HM3D 上的 category-level 评估以及真实四足机器人部署进一步验证跨环境适用性。

Conclusion: 无需导航专用微调的解耦式 agent harness 方案,可同时获得任务与场景通用性,并在仿真与真实机器人上成立。

Abstract: General-purpose service robots need navigation systems that can handle diverse human requests in unfamiliar environments, combining task generality with scene generality. Some existing methods fine-tune multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to predict navigation actions, making their behavior dependent on the coverage of navigation training data and potentially limiting generalization to new requests and environments. Our key insight is to let the MLLM focus on interpreting requests, understanding scenes, and making decisions while preserving its general-purpose capabilities and delegating motion execution to navigation tools. To realize this idea, we introduce SuperNav, which equips a pretrained MLLM with a specialized agent harness without navigation-specific fine-tuning of the MLLM. Our harness supports these decisions with Navigation Skills, agent-oriented Tools for physical interaction, and task-progress and context management. A unified visual-point interface connects decision-making to motion by allowing the model to specify destinations directly in images and revise its decisions from execution feedback. Together, these components support sustained navigation across different task requirements and environments. SuperNav outperforms four evaluated baselines on instance-level, multi-object, and demand-driven tasks. Category-level evaluation on HM3D and deployment on a real quadruped robot further demonstrate its applicability across environments. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [235] [Leveraging Human-In-The-Loop Demonstrations in Reinforcement Learning for Digital Twin-Driven Robot Flexibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12140)
*Yuzhu Sun,Mien Van,Nguyen Minh Nhat,Stephen McIlvanna,Sean McLoone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种人在回路的在线训练框架，将实时同步的数字孪生、强化学习与人类演示结合，用"双Actor"结构在不向RL actor添加模仿损失的前提下利用演示引导适应；在Xarm5真机上验证了物理工作空间变化后可续训，且成功率显著优于两种模仿损失方法（83-100% vs 0-17%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作机器人工作环境日益多变，传统数字孪生主要用于任务执行前生成合成数据，无法在部署后随物理系统变化持续适应；同时重编程/人工示教成本高，而直接在RL actor中加入模仿损失又容易损害策略性能。

Method: （1）数字孪生通过摄像头视频流与物理系统实时同步，使虚拟机器人能根据真实反馈更新观测与策略，实现人在回路的在线训练；（2）双Actor框架：将模仿学习分支与RL actor解耦，不在RL actor上直接加模仿损失，从而让非最优/未达成目标的演示用于引导适应而非主导策略；（3）在Ufactory Xarm5协作机械臂上执行末端到达目标并避障任务，对比两种将模仿损失加到actor上的基线方法。

Result: 在物理工作空间发生变化后框架能够恢复训练；在固定的一组非最优演示下，双Actor框架的最终成功率远高于两种模仿损失方法；使用VR采集的真实人类演示（且演示从未达成目标）时，双Actor平均确定性评估成功率为83-100%，而两种模仿损失方法仅为0-17%。

Conclusion: 实时同步的数字孪生配合解耦的双Actor设计，可在不依赖高质量演示的条件下实现真机上的持续适应，说明"避免在RL actor上直接加模仿损失"是提升演示引导在线适应效果的关键设计选择。

Abstract: Growing automation makes collaborative robots work in more variable environments, increasing the need for adaptation. We propose a human-in-the-loop online training framework combining a digital twin (DT), reinforcement learning (RL), and human demonstrations. Unlike DTs used mainly to generate synthetic data before task execution, our DT is synchronized with the physical system in real time through camera feeds, allowing the virtual robot to update its observations and policy from real-world feedback. A dual actor framework integrates imitation learning (IL) without adding a direct imitation loss to the RL actor, so demonstrations can guide adaptation instead of manual reprogramming. The proposed framework is demonstrated on the Ufactory Xarm5 collaborative robot, where the robot's end-effector aims to reach the target position while avoiding obstacles. The experiments show that the framework can resume training after a change in the physical workspace and that, with a fixed set of non-optimal demonstrations, the dual actor framework achieves a much higher final success rate than two methods that add an imitation loss to the actor. The same pattern holds with real human demonstrations collected in virtual reality (VR): with demonstrations that never reach the goal, the dual actor framework reached 83-100% mean deterministic evaluation success, against 0-17% for the two imitation-loss methods.

</details>


### [236] [Instance-anchored interaction evidence: Grounding robot plans in human pointing and handling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12157)
*Xinliang Xiao,Bowen Yang,Wenjing Zhang,Li Yang,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"实例锚定交互证据"(IAE)：将最终场景每个物体绑定公开标识，用反向掩码传播在视频中保持身份，用"手-前臂-实例"几何描述每帧；证据网络仅由任务结果训练来打分实例；指向类任务用语法约束动态规划+结构化损失解码"物体-目的地"程序。在 WatchAct 1,255 请求上，隐式意图任务计划成功率 64.2%（32B VLM 27.5%，严格成功 49.7% vs 15.4%）；无任务特定训练下在恢复/反转/模仿任务上 46.4% vs 27.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人助手常需依据人的"展示动作"而非语言指令行动（如指向哪个同款纸箱、拿了哪个盒子）。证据出现在动作早期，且可能涉及后来已移动的物体，而计划却要在最终场景执行；现有端到端 VLM 难以将早期交互证据可靠地锚定到最终场景中的具体实例（隐式意图/指代消解）。

Method: 1) 实例锚定交互证据（IAE）：最终场景每个物体注册到公开标识，通过反向掩码传播跨帧保持身份，以手、前臂与实例间的几何关系描述每帧；2) 仅用任务结果训练的证据网络对实例打分；3) 指向任务用语法约束动态规划 + 结构化损失解码物体-目的地程序；4) 符号程序做指代消解与免学习的情节式任务（恢复/反转/模仿）。

Result: WatchAct 1,255 条请求上：隐式意图任务计划成功率 64.2% vs 32B VLM 27.5%，严格成功 49.7% vs 15.4%；恢复/反转/模仿 46.4% vs 27.0%（无任务特定训练）。相同感知叠加、相同 32 帧、前向跟踪、同标签关系模型等对照均无法解释增益；将 IAE 证据以带解释的文本交给同一 LLM 达 57.4%，说明增益主要来自实例锚定证据，显式程序以极小代价再加 6.8 点；指向仍最难，严格成功仅 16.9%。

Conclusion: 实例锚定的交互证据是性能提升的主要来源，显式符号程序以低成本带来额外增益；该方法可免任务特定训练迁移到情节式任务，但指向类隐式意图仍是瓶颈（严格成功 16.9%），代码已开源。

Abstract: A robot that assists people must often act on what a person has shown rather than said: which of several identical cartons was pointed at, or which box was handled. The plan is executed from the final scene, whereas the evidence occurs earlier, possibly on objects that have since moved. We propose instance-anchored interaction evidence (IAE), which registers every object of the final scene to its public identifier, keeps each identity through the video by backward mask propagation, and describes every frame by the geometry between hands, forearms and these instances. An evidence network trained only from task outcomes scores the instances. For pointing tasks, a grammar-constrained dynamic program trained with a structured loss decodes object-destination programs; symbolic programs handle reference disambiguation and, without learning, episodic tasks. On 1,255 WatchAct benchmark requests, scored by symbolic execution, IAE reaches 64.2% plan success on implicit-intent tasks against 27.5% for a 32B vision-language model (strict success 49.7% against 15.4%), and 46.4% against 27.0% on restoration, reversal and imitation without task-specific training. Controls with the same perception overlays, the same 32 frames, forward tracking, or a relation model trained on the same labels do not explain the gain. Given IAE's evidence as text with its meaning explained, the same language model reaches 57.4%: most of the gain comes from the instance-anchored evidence, and the explicit programs add 6.8 points at a fraction of the cost. Pointing remains the hardest case, with 16.9% strict success. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [237] [Unifying Policy Learning and State Prediction through Spatial Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12172)
*Minye Wu,Zehao Wang,Tinne Tuytelaars*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出空间语言建模（Spatial Language Modeling），用离散坐标与语义token的统一词表表示场景轮廓、目标、动作目标与未来状态，以任务语法组织成空间序列，用单一自回归Transformer通过下一token预测联合学习动作生成与动作条件状态预测；在Push-T仿真与真机上验证，成功率和目标覆盖率优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习动作如何改变场景几何可为目标导向操作提供互补监督；现有策略学习通常只监督动作，未显式建模动作到状态几何变化的因果预测。

Method: 统一离散坐标+语义token词表；任务特定语法将轮廓、目标、动作目标、未来状态组织为空间序列；单一自回归Transformer共享下一token目标同时学动作生成与动作条件状态预测；随机游玩转场预训练（动作坐标条件化后续状态预测但不计入损失）+专家演示联合动作与状态训练；控制时仅解码可执行动作目标并用新观测状态更新历史。

Result: Push-T仿真性能具竞争力（competitive）；真机任务成功率与目标覆盖率高/优于所评估的真实机器人策略基线；消融显示联合动作-状态序列提升控制，随机游玩预训练进一步增益；给定动作轨迹时同一模型可预测连续场景状态，捕捉推动的几何效应。

Conclusion: 以空间语言序列统一动作与状态建模，可在单一自回归框架下同时实现策略生成与前向动力学预测，真机验证有效；联合训练与随机游玩预训练是关键增益来源。

Abstract: Learning how actions change scene geometry can provide complementary supervision for goal-directed manipulation. We introduce Spatial Language Modeling, which represents scene contours, goals, action targets, and future states with a shared vocabulary of discrete coordinates and semantic tokens. A task-specific grammar organizes these elements into spatial sequences, allowing one autoregressive Transformer to learn action generation and action-conditioned state prediction through a common next-token objective. We train the model from scratch using random-play transition pretraining followed by joint action and state training on expert demonstrations. During pretraining, recorded action coordinates condition subsequent state predictions and are excluded from the prediction loss. During control, the model decodes only executable action targets and updates its history with newly observed states. We evaluate the approach on Push-T in simulation and on a real robot. The model achieves competitive simulation performance and higher task success and target coverage than the evaluated real-robot policy baselines. Training ablations show improved control with joint action and state sequences, with further gains from random-play pretraining. Given supplied action trajectories, the same model also predicts successive scene states, capturing the geometric effects of pushing.

</details>


### [238] [RESETTLE: Robotic Recovery through Disagreement-Triggered Retrieval and Efficient Corrective Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12185)
