*Yuxin Chen,Senqiao Yang,Zixuan Wang,Jinhui Ye,Changsheng Lu,Pengguang Chen,Shu Liu,Zhuotao Tian,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出模型无关框架RESETTLE，通过双采样动作一致性检测触发恢复，检索同任务演示并用状态伺服+视觉残差执行单步纠正，无需在线优化或额外VLM推理，在仿真和真机上提升多种基础策略成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中执行偏差与错误需要及时干预，但基于重复视觉语言推理或迭代在线优化的恢复方法延迟高，导致干预不及时。

Method: 在冻结策略的动作执行接口处加入恢复机制：以同一条件下独立采样的两个动作提案持续不一致作为触发信号；用适配的V-JEPA编码器检索同任务演示参考；结合状态伺服先验与带门控的视觉残差生成单个纠正动作，不进行在线轨迹优化或额外VLM推理，随后交还控制权给基础策略。

Result: 六种基础策略仿真中，LIBERO-Plus、Meta-World、RoboCasa Tabletop上绝对成功率提升最高达8.70%、6.28%、6.83%；真实世界四项任务两种策略进一步提升；QwenPI对比中监控与恢复计算延迟比VoLoAgent低74.04%–93.57%；Harness VLA的LIBERO-Pro Swap成功率从42%升至50%。

Conclusion: RESETTLE以低成本触发与检索式纠正实现高效恢复，兼容多种冻结策略与高层智能体规划，兼顾实时性与有效性。

Abstract: Reliable robotic manipulation requires timely intervention to correct emerging deviations and restore progress after execution errors. However, recovery methods based on repeated vision-language reasoning or iterative online optimization can incur substantial latency, delaying intervention. To address these challenges, we introduce RESETTLE(Robotic rEcovery through diSagrEement-Triggered reTrievaL and Efficient Corrective Control), a model-agnostic framework that provides computationally efficient recovery at the action-execution interface of frozen robot policies. RESETTLE triggers recovery when two action proposals independently sampled under identical conditioning persistently disagree. It retrieves a same-task demonstration reference using an adapted V-JEPA encoder and combines a state-servo prior with a guarded visual residual to execute one corrective action without online trajectory optimization or additional vision-language reasoning, then returns control to the base policy. Across six base policies in simulation, RESETTLE achieves up to 8.70%, 6.28%, and 6.83% absolute success-rate gains on LIBERO-Plus, Meta-World, and RoboCasa Tabletop, respectively, with further improvements on four real-world tasks using two policies. In QwenPI-based comparisons, its monitoring-and-recovery computation latency is 74.04%--93.57% lower than VoLoAgent's monitoring-and-planning latency for grasp and place tool calls. It also raises Harness VLA's LIBERO-Pro Swap success from 42% to 50%, demonstrating compatibility with high-level agentic planning. Code available at: this https URL

</details>


### [239] [MiniWAM: Learning Compact Future Targets for Efficient World-Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12194)
*Jie Chen,Ruofei Bai,Yuxin Cai,Yifeng Zhang,Chengyang He,Jun Li,Wei-Yun Yau,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MiniWAM 提出用紧凑的预测性表征替代原生视觉特征作为世界动作模型（WAM）的预测目标：通过 PRISM（逆动力学监督+特征重建）学习当前-未来转移的紧凑表征，冻结编码器后联合预测该目标与动作，在减少 65 倍未来 token、最高 8 倍训练加速下仍优于 DINOv3 与 WAN2.1 VAE 原生特征预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 WAM 大多在预训练视觉骨干的原生表征空间中预测未来状态，目标维度高、训练开销大。作者认为有效世界动作建模不一定需要预测原生视觉未来，可用更紧凑、控制相关的预测表征作为学习目标。

Method: 提出 PRISM：结合逆动力学监督（强调控制相关转移信息）与特征重建（保留有用未来状态信息），学习紧凑的当前-未来预测表征。冻结 PRISM 编码器后训练 MiniWAM，从当前观测联合预测该紧凑目标与机器人动作。模型规模 0.25B。

Result: 相比原生未来特征预测，未来特征 token 减少 65 倍，WAM 训练最高提速 8 倍；在 DINOv3 与 WAN2.1 VAE 特征上均持续更优。0.25B 参数下在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 2.0 仿真基准上与更大规模 WAM 具有竞争力。表征分析显示 PRISM 在特征重建之外额外引入行为结构。

Conclusion: 有效世界动作建模无需预测原生视觉未来，紧凑预测表征是更强且更高效的学习目标。

Abstract: World modeling has emerged as an effective co-training objective for robot policies, giving rise to World Action Models (WAMs) that jointly predict actions and future states. However, most WAMs predict future states in the native representation space of pretrained visual backbones, resulting in high-dimensional targets with substantial training cost. We introduce MiniWAM, which instead predicts compact future representations learned from privileged current-future transitions. To construct these targets, we propose Predictive Representations via Inverse Spatiotemporal Modeling (PRISM), which combines inverse-dynamics supervision with feature reconstruction to emphasize control-relevant transition information while preserving useful future-state information. With the learned PRISM encoder frozen, MiniWAM is trained to jointly predict the resulting targets and robot actions from current observations. With 65$\times$ fewer native future feature tokens, MiniWAM consistently outperforms native future-feature prediction with both DINOv3 and WAN2.1 VAE features, while achieving up to an 8$\times$ speedup in world-action training. At 0.25B parameters, MiniWAM is already competitive with substantially larger WAMs on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin 2.0 simulation benchmarks. Representation analyses further show that PRISM contributes behavioral structure beyond feature reconstruction alone. These results demonstrate that effective world-action modeling does not require predicting native visual futures, and that compact predictive representations provide a strong and substantially more efficient target for policy learning. The project page is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [240] [MAP2: Model- and Acceleration-Based Pursuit with MPC and Gaussian Process Residual Learning for Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12196)
*Weizhan Huang,Cheng Hu,Edoardo Ghignone,Nicolas Baumann,Yiqin Wang,Hongye Su,Lei Xie,Michele Magno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAP2：曲率运动学MPC + 稀疏高斯过程残差校正的模型式pursuit控制器，实车实验将横向跟踪误差比MAP降低37.99%、比PP降低44.65%，圈速至少提升1.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主赛车需要在车辆操控极限附近实现高精度轨迹跟踪，同时保持低计算延迟。几何控制器（Pure Pursuit等）计算高效，但Ackermann转向几何在极限工况下失效；MAP虽引入轮胎动力学，本质上仍是依赖Ackermann几何的几何控制器，其轮胎模型难以完全刻画车辆真实动态响应。

Method: 提出MAP2模型式pursuit控制器：1）基于曲率的运动学MPC在预测时域内优化运动学控制输入；2）通过带SGP残差校正的轮胎动力学模型，将优化结果映射为转向指令；SGP用于补偿轮胎模型与真实动力学之间的残差。

Result: 实车实验表明横向跟踪误差与圈速显著下降：相比MAP与Pure Pursuit，平均横向跟踪误差分别降低37.99%与44.65%，平均圈速至少缩短1.5%。

Conclusion: 以轻量运动学MPC+GP残差学习的组合替代纯几何控制，能在保留低延迟的同时提升极限工况下的跟踪精度，为极限驾驶控制提供了可行的模型式方案；但其在3D感知/世界模型主线上的外溢价值有限。

Abstract: Autonomous racing requires accurate trajectory tracking near the handling limits of a vehicle while maintaining low computational latency. Geometric controllers are computationally efficient, but the Ackermann steering geometry becomes invalid under limit-handling conditions. Model- and Acceleration-based Pursuit (MAP) preserves the simplicity of geometric approaches while leveraging tire dynamics. Yet MAP remains fundamentally a geometric controller that relies on Ackermann steering geometry, and its tire model may not fully capture the vehicle's actual dynamic response. This paper presents MAP2, a model-based pursuit controller that combines a curvature-based kinematic MPC and a Sparse Gaussian Process (SGP) residual correction. The proposed algorithm uses MPC to optimize kinematic control inputs over a prediction horizon and maps them to steering commands through a tire dynamics model augmented with SGP residual correction. Real-world vehicle experiments demonstrate substantial reductions in lateral tracking error and lap time. Compared with MAP and Pure Pursuit (PP), MAP2 reduces the average lateral tracking error by 37.99% and 44.65%, respectively, while reducing average lap time by at least 1.5%.

</details>


### [241] [Sim-to-Real RL for ASVs using SysID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12202)
*Cody Sheltraw,Tsimafei Lazouski,Maani Ghaffari,Alan Papalia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个ASV（自主水面载具）仿真器与配套流程，可在未知水动力与推进器参数条件下进行并行RL训练，并实现零样本sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASV仿真器很少支持RL并行训练；且通常需要CFD或拖曳水池试验获得精确水动力参数以缩小sim-to-real差距，门槛高、成本大。作者希望从"未知动力学"出发，仅用CAD模型与少量实测轨迹即可训练策略。

Method: 构建支持并行环境的ASV仿真器及训练流程；以CAD模型和少量开放水域现场轨迹为输入，同时近似并迭代精修水动力参数与推进器参数；在此基础上进行强化学习策略训练。

Result: 在BlueBoat ASV上完成真实部署，在路径跟随与定点保持任务中实现无需先验水动力与螺旋桨信息的零样本sim-to-real迁移，验证了参数辨识+仿真训练的可行性。

Conclusion: 通过"少量真实轨迹驱动的参数估计+并行仿真RL"路线，降低了ASV强化学习对昂贵水动力建模的依赖，实现了实际水面载具的零样本部署，为海洋机器人RL训练提供了可复用的仿真与部署管线。

Abstract: Autonomous Surface Vehicles (ASVs) operating in dynamic marine environments require robust control policies for tasks such as path following and station keeping, making reinforcement learning (RL) a promising alternative to classical controllers. However, existing ASV simulators rarely support parallel environments for RL training. Such existing simulators require accurate hydrodynamic modeling from computational fluid dynamics solvers or towing tank tests for setting hydrodynamic parameters to address the sim-to-real gap. To address these challenges, we present an ASV simulator and accompanying pipeline that enables training policies starting from unknown vehicle dynamics. Our framework uses only a CAD model and brief set of open-water field trajectories for approximating and refining both hydrodynamic and thruster parameters. Real-world deployments on a BlueBoat ASV demonstrate successful zero-shot sim-to-real transfer in path following and station-keeping tasks without prior hydrodynamic and propeller information.

</details>


### [242] [Residual Modeling Closes the Regression and Generative Policy Gap in Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12231)
*Yuchen Zhou,Jiacheng You,Weikang Wan,Weijun Dong,Yang Gao,Jiayuan Mao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文从统计建模角度分析模仿学习中扩散/流匹配策略（Flow-Policies）优于MSE直接回归策略（MSE-Policies）的原因，发现真实机器人示教数据的动作残差具有状态依赖的尺度变化和重尾特性，并提出异方差Student-t动作回归（HT-Policies），在保持单次前向推理效率的同时达到与生成式策略相当的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow-Policies（扩散/流匹配）通常优于MSE直接回归策略，这一差距常被归因于演示数据的多模态性，但缺乏从统计建模角度的深入解释。作者希望理解动作预测残差的分布特性如何影响策略优化，从而找到一个既高效又性能可比的替代方案。

Method: 首先对真实机器人示教数据的动作残差进行统计分析，发现残差尺度存在显著的状态依赖性且尾部重于高斯分布。对比两类策略的训练梯度行为：MSE-Policies将更多梯度幅值分配给动作残差较大的观测，损害优化；Flow-Policies虽同样残差重尾但梯度行为不同。基于此提出异方差Student-t动作回归（HT-Policies），学习输入相关的残差尺度以降低重尾影响，单次前馈预测动作块，并可直接复用预训练的流匹配策略网络作为骨干。

Result: 在四个仿真基准和真实机器人评估中，HT-Policies无论从头训练还是基于预训练VLA与世界动作模型微调，成功率均与生成式策略基线相当，同时训练和推理更快。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了生成式目标在机器人模仿学习中的实际优势来源（重尾残差下的梯度分配差异），并提供了一种高效的直接回归替代方案，可适配多种架构与任务。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration has enabled impressive robot behaviors. A common choice for policy learning is to use diffusion or flow matching (Flow-Policies), which often outperforms direct action regression trained with mean squared error (MSE-Policies). This gap is commonly attributed to multimodal demonstrations. We revisit this gap from the perspective of statistical modeling: how action-prediction residuals shape policy optimization. Our analysis of real-world robot demonstration data reveals substantial state-dependent variation in residual scales and heavier-than-Gaussian tails. While both MSE-Policies and Flow-Policies exhibit heavy-tailed action residuals, their training gradients behave differently: MSE allocates more gradient magnitude to observations with large action residuals, which hurts optimization. Motivated by these findings, we introduce heteroscedastic Student-t action regression (HT-Policies), which learns input-dependent residual scales and reduces the influence of heavy tails. HT-Policies predict action chunks with a single feed-forward pass and can reuse pretrained flow-matching-based policy networks as the backbone. Across four simulation benchmarks and real-robot evaluations, HT-Policies achieves success rates competitive with generative policy baselines, both when trained from scratch and from pretrained vision-language-action and world-action models, despite being faster in training and inference. Together, these findings shed light on the practical advantages of generative objectives in robot learning from demonstrations and offer an efficient direct-regression alternative for a range of architectures and tasks. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [243] [From Language to Motion: Task-Conditioned Focal-Stack Trajectory Integration for Microscopic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12241)
*Junjie Xie,Chuxuan He,Junkai Huang,Heng Zhang,Angen Ye,Yujia Song,Yuqing Li,Pengsong Zhang,Dapeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出语义到物理框架，将指令映射为几何算子，复用冻结开放词汇感知，按置信度加权+动态规划融合焦平面轨迹，实现少标注的显微机器人微操作高精度轨迹执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显微机器人任务中语言、零件和焦点不断变化，标定与轨迹精度难以保持；传统按零件训练的U-Net需大量标注且耗时长，需要零新增标注、跨零件泛化的几何执行方案。

Method: 语义到物理框架：指令→受限几何算子；复用冻结开放词汇感知；对局部可靠的焦平面轨迹做置信度加权与动态规划融合；标定多视图几何将2D路径映射到物理执行。

Result: 提示、未见零件、几何重构测试RMSE 6.30-6.59像素；零新增标注配置15分钟完成，对比20-100标签的U-Net需72-165分钟；九种零件-光照条件下轨迹空间融合将RMSE从14.41降至6.28像素（56.4%），P95从20.07降至8.13（59.5%）；消融验证置信度与路径选择作用；机器人实验目标区域覆盖率由83.5%提升到92.9%。

Conclusion: 轨迹空间（而非图像优先）的融合与置信度加权是精度提升关键，方法可零标注快速部署并泛化到未见零件与变化焦点，具备实际显微机器人执行可行性。

Abstract: Microscopic robots require accurate task geometry despite changes in language, parts, and focus. We present a semantic-to-physical framework that maps instructions to constrained geometric operators, reuses frozen open-vocabulary perception, and integrates locally reliable focal-plane trajectories by confidence weighting and dynamic programming. Calibrated multi-view geometry connects 2-D paths to physical execution. Prompt, unseen-part, and geometry reconfiguration tests yield 6.30-6.59-pixel RMSE. Relative to part-specific U-Net training with 20-100 labels, the proposed zero-new-label configuration takes 15 rather than 72-165 min. Across nine part-illumination conditions, trajectory-space integration reduces RMSE from 14.41 to 6.28 pixels (56.4%) and P95 error from 20.07 to 8.13 pixels (59.5%) compared with image-first multi-focus fusion. An ablation isolates the roles of confidence and path-wise selection. In representative robot experiments, target-region coverage improves from 83.5% to 92.9%. Dispensing provides a measurable physical trace, not a task-specific limitation of the method.

</details>


### [244] [Fixed-Reference Pose Residuals for Measuring Cross-Dataset Cue Transfer in Human-Robot Interaction Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12245)
*Bowen Yang,Xinliang Xiao,Wenjing Zhang,Li Yang,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出固定参考姿态残差(FRPR)模型，将预测显式分解为几何项与姿态项，并用两台不同机器人在不同场地采集的公开数据集(HUI360、SSUP-A)检验跨机器人、跨场地的"即将被触碰"预判迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共空间中的社交与服务机器人需要在接触前预判哪位附近的人即将靠近并触摸自己，以便提前准备响应；但当模型在一个机器人上训练、迁移到另一台机器人、另一场地时，哪些线索能支撑这种预判仍基本未知。

Method: 构建FRPR模型：先用人框与分割掩码训练一个几何预测器并冻结，再由时序网络从人体姿态学习对其logit的加性修正，使每次预测精确拆分为几何项与姿态项；所有选择(阈值、参照模型等)均在源域数据上确定，再在HUI360与SSUP-A之间双向迁移，并与联合训练、简单几何基线、树集成及头部朝向残差进行对比。

Result: 在SSUP-A→HUI360方向，姿态修正将AP从0.277提升至0.321；反方向无可测增益，且使用更强的源域几何参照后该不对称性依然存在。冻结无AP优势，简单几何基线与树集成仍具竞争力或更优。头部朝向残差在两个方向均有小幅增益。"人是否面向相机"的判别方向跨数据集保持一致，而头部俯仰方向发生反转。在源域阈值下，使用几何信息的神经模型最多只检测到17%的目标交互。

Conclusion: FRPR的核心价值在于提供可分解的测量工具而非单纯提升预测精度；跨机器人、跨场地的预判线索迁移存在明显方向性不对称，姿态残差收益有限，几何基线难以被显著超越，实际检测率偏低，说明该问题在真实部署条件下仍远未解决。

Abstract: Social and service robots in public spaces need to anticipate which nearby person is about to approach and touch them, so that a response can be prepared before contact. It is largely unknown which cues support this anticipation when a model trained with one robot is used on another robot at a different site. We study this question with a fixed-reference pose residual (FRPR) model: a geometry predictor built from the person's bounding box and mask is trained and frozen, and a temporal network then learns from body pose an additive correction to its logit, so that every prediction splits exactly into a geometry term and a pose term. Between two public egocentric datasets recorded by different robots, HUI360 and SSUP-A, with every choice made on source data, the pose correction raised average precision (AP) from 0.277 to 0.321 from SSUP-A to HUI360 and gave no measurable gain in the opposite direction; the same asymmetry held over a stronger, source-selected geometry reference. Freezing gave no AP advantage over joint training, and simple geometric baselines and tree ensembles remained competitive or better, so the construction serves measurement rather than prediction accuracy. A head-orientation residual added small gains in both directions. Post hoc, whether a person faces the camera kept its discriminative direction across datasets, whereas head pitch reversed. With thresholds chosen on source data, the neural models that use geometry detected at most 17% of target interactions. Code and processed data are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [245] [Real-Time Motion Planning with Dynamic Hazards: Classical vs. Learning-Based Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12249)
*Eran Iceland,Alexander Tuisov,Oren Gal,Ariel Barel,Alfred M. Bruckstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文构建统一基准，在动态危险场（旋转喷淋式时变禁区）中对比经典规划与学习式规划；确定性环境下经典法近完美但耗时，随机障碍动态下在线搜索预算敏感，PPO策略在时延、成功率与路径质量上占优，主要瓶颈是障碍演化不确定性而非部分可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态障碍场中的实时运动规划缺少公平对比：经典规划与学习式方法通常在不同环境、约束、信息假设与指标下评测，难以判断各自适用区间，尤其是不确定性来源（障碍随机演化 vs. 部分可观测）对范式选择的影响未被分离。

Method: 不提出新规划器，而是构建统一基准：面域环境含类喷淋旋转危险源，通过扫掠角扇区生成时变禁行区；代表性经典规划器与学习式方法（PPO策略）共享同一场景分布、运动约束、信息假设与评测指标；控制变量对比确定性/随机障碍动力学、在线搜索不同规划预算以及部分可观测设置。

Result: 确定性环境下经典规划器成功率接近100%且路径质量更高，但规划/重规划时间开销大；随机障碍动力学下在线搜索强预算敏感——低预算频繁失败，高预算提升成功率但增加时延与轨迹长度；同分布训练的PPO策略在该随机场景下于时延、成功率、路径质量上均稳定更优。

Conclusion: 决定规划范式实际有效性的主导因素是障碍演化的不确定性，而非部分可观测性；实际部署应按不确定性水平选择：低不确定性用经典规划，高随机性用学习式策略。

Abstract: We study real-time motion planning in dynamic hazard fields through a controlled comparison between classical planning and learning-based methods. Rather than introducing a new planner, we construct a unified benchmark in which representative classical and learning-based methods face the same environments, motion constraints, information assumptions, and evaluation metrics. The test environment consists of planar domains populated with rotating sprinkler-like hazards that generate time-varying forbidden regions via sweeping angular sectors. Our results show a clear regime shift. In deterministic environments, classical planners achieve near-perfect success and higher-quality paths, though sometimes at the cost of substantial planning or replanning time. Under stochastic obstacle dynamics, however, online search becomes strongly budget-sensitive: low budgets lead to frequent failure, while high budgets improve success at the cost of latency and longer trajectories. PPO-based policies, trained under the same scenario distribution, consistently outperform in latency, success rate, and path quality in these stochastic regimes. Overall, the results indicate that uncertainty in obstacle evolution, more than partial observability, is the dominant factor determining which planning paradigm is practically effective for the problem at hand.

</details>


### [246] [Walking on Roofs: Exploring the Potential of Walking Robots for Construction Work on Roofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12272)
*Bjoern-Felix Dettmar,Arne Roennau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估商用四足机器人Unitree Go2在屋顶环境中的运动可行性:自建屋顶坡度试验台,发现标准球形足端在斜坡上打滑随坡度二次增长,25°中等坡度即出现关键失效,指出需要任务导向足端设计、接触力学与RL步态控制相结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人虽已展示多领域通用性,但大规模部署受限于缺乏应用场景专用设计;屋顶作业对人类工人存在高安全风险且任务重复繁重,是一个新颖且未被探索的机器人应用场景,需要先弄清现有商用平台在该环境下的基线能力。

Method: 设计并搭建专用屋顶表面试验台,以商用四足机器人Unitree Go2为对象,在不同倾斜角度下开展运动学与稳定性实验,系统测量打滑、起卧动作可行性与失效阈值。

Result: 标准球形足端在倾斜屋顶上本质上不适用:滑移量随坡度呈二次增长;起卧序列仅能在小坡度完成;在25°中等坡度上已频繁出现关键失效。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了四足机器人在屋顶环境中运动的首个系统性基线评估,明确了应用潜力与当前局限,并指出后续研究需融合任务导向足端设计、先进接触力学建模以及环境特定控制策略(如面向屋顶的强化学习步态)。

Abstract: This paper investigates the feasibility of deploying quadruped walking robots for the automation of work in roof environments. While quadrupeds have demonstrated versatility across various domains, their large-scale deployment remains limited, partly due to lack of application-specific designs. Roof environments represent a novel and unexplored use case, combining high safety risks for human workers with repetitive, strenuous tasks that could benefit from robotic assistance. A dedicated test rig of a roof's surface was designed to evaluate the baseline performance of a commercial quadruped, the \emph{Unitree Go2}, in this new environment. Experiments revealed that standard ball feet are inherently inadequate for locomotion on sloped roofs: slippage increased quadratically with incline angle, get-up and lie-down sequences were only possible on small inclines, and critical failures already occurred regularly on moderate inclines of 25°. This work provides the first systematic assessment of quadruped locomotion in roof environments, highlighting both the potential and the current limitations of this application and establishes a baseline for future research. Effective solutions will require a combination of task-oriented foot designs, advanced contact mechanics and environment-specific control strategies, such as reinforcement learning for roof-adapted gaits.

</details>


### [247] [Toward Lunar Legged Robots: Field Deployment Lessons at LUNA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12276)
*Adrian Fuhrer,Joseph Church,Oliver Fischer,William Talbot,Nicolas Faesch,Yannic Hofmann,Hendrik Kolvenbach,Yusuke Tanaka,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 2025 LUNA模拟活动中，ANYmal-D与Magnecko在模拟月壤和类陨石坑地形上完成长时导航与视觉惯性数据采集，验证四足机器人可穿越松散风化层，但性能受下沉、打滑、扬尘以及过曝、阴影、低纹理导致的感知失效影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人有望胜任未来月球表面任务，因其可穿越陡坡、松散和崎岖地形，而轮式漫游车难以应对。然而月面部署的成熟度受限于足-风化层交互不确定性、扬尘、光照驱动的感知退化与任务级运行约束等未解问题。

Method: 基于2025年LUNA模拟（analogue）活动，使用ANYmal-D与Magnecko两台四足机器人在松散月壤模拟物和类陨石坑地形上开展长时程穿越实验，采集导航与视觉-惯性数据，覆盖挑战性光照条件，属于实地模拟场景下的经验性评测与案例分析。

Result: 四足机器人能够穿越模拟月壤，但存在明显的下沉与滑移，接触过程产生扬尘；视觉感知在过曝、阴影和低纹理区域出现失效，长时程导航与视觉-惯性数据为上述失效模式提供了直接证据。

Conclusion: 未来月球腿式机器人需要更紧密地整合：具备风化层感知的运动策略、光照鲁棒的感知模块、可重复的模拟测试流程，以及任务级运行验证。

Abstract: Legged robots are promising candidates for future lunar surface missions because they can traverse steep, loose, and obstacle-rich terrain that challenges conventional wheeled rovers. However, readiness for lunar deployment is limited by uncertainties in foot-regolith interaction, dust generation, illumination-driven perception degradation, and operational constraints. This paper reports lessons from the 2025 LUNA analogue campaign, where ANYmal-D and Magnecko traversed loose regolith simulant and crater-like terrain and collected long-horizon navigation and visual-inertial data under challenging lighting. We show that quadrupedal robots can traverse regolith simulant, but performance is affected by sinkage and slip, dust-generating contacts, and perception failures caused by overexposure, shadows, and low-texture regions. These results motivate tighter integration of regolith-aware locomotion policies, illumination-robust perception, repeatable analogue testing, and mission-level operational validation for future lunar legged robots.

</details>


### [248] [PLaW-VLA: Predictive Latent World Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12285)
*Yu Liu,Hetian Guo,Tianlv Huang,Ziyi Cai,Wudi Chen,Hantang Wang,Qiutong Liu,Yingzhi Peng,Wei Han,Peijun Tang,Jianan Wang,Zipei Fan,Zhiyuan Zha,Xuan Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PLaW-VLA 在预训练的预测导向表征空间中建模任务相关未来状态，用 Mixture-of-Transformers 与结构化因果注意力将动作生成条件化于观测历史、任务语义和预测未来，在长程控制与分布偏移下取得增益，并将推理延迟降至生成式世界-动作建模的约 1/19。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略进行长程控制时需要关于世界未来演化的预测性上下文，但效果取决于两点：建模何种未来表征，以及如何用它条件化动作生成。既有方案若在像素/重构导向潜空间预测未来，需承担控制无关视觉细节的预测负担，效率低且泛化受限。

Method: 提出 PLaW-VLA：(1) 在预训练的预测导向表征空间中建模任务相关未来状态，避免低层视觉重构；(2) 基于 Mixture-of-Transformers 架构，通过结构化因果注意力，将动作生成条件化于观测历史、当前任务语义与预测未来状态；(3) 轻量级潜空间世界模型并行预测未来，降低预测负担与推理延迟。

Result: 在 RoboTwin Hard Horizon III 上相比反应式策略提升 +11.8 个百分点；在零样本 LIBERO-Plus 上相比重构导向潜预测提升 +1.77 个百分点；推理延迟约为生成式世界-动作建模的 1/19，同时保持可比策略性能。

Conclusion: 将未来预测放在预测导向的语义表征空间而非像素重构空间，并以结构化注意力注入动作生成，可在长程控制与分布偏移泛化上取得一致增益，同时以轻量潜世界模型实现高推理效率，为世界模型驱动的 VLA 提供了一条高效路径。

Abstract: Learning to predict how the world evolves can provide vision-language-action (VLA) policies with predictive context for long-horizon control, but its effectiveness depends on what future representation is modeled and how it conditions action generation. We introduce PLaW-VLA, which models task-relevant future states in a pretrained prediction-oriented representation space, reducing the need to predict control-irrelevant visual details. Built on a Mixture-of-Transformers architecture, PLaW-VLA conditions action generation on observation history, current task semantics, and predicted future states through structured causal attention. Experiments show a +11.8 percentage-point (pp) gain over reactive policies on RoboTwin Hard Horizon III and a +1.77 pp gain over reconstruction-oriented latent prediction on zero-shot LIBERO-Plus, supporting improved long-horizon control and generalization under distribution shift, respectively. By avoiding low-level visual reconstruction, PLaW-VLA lowers the burden of future prediction, enabling a lightweight latent world model with parallel future prediction and about 1/19 the inference latency of generative world-action modeling at comparable policy performance.

</details>


### [249] [LiteNWM: Efficient Latent World Models for Onboard Visual Navigation in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12368)
*Linkai Liu,Yuntian Zhang,Zhenshan Bing,Chen Chen,Lingjuan Lyu,Shangguang Wang,Mengwei Xu,Dongqi Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LiteNWM 是一种潜在导航世界模型，通过共享视觉编码、联合预测多时域动作条件未来表征，并用学习到的评分器选择轨迹，从而将世界模型的前瞻能力以低成本引入视觉导航策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接视觉导航策略效率高但缺乏对未来后果的显式评估；生成式导航世界模型虽可通过视觉推演提供前瞻，但在评估多个候选轨迹时计算代价高昂，难以部署。

Method: 在潜在空间中构建导航世界模型：候选轨迹共享视觉编码，联合预测其动作条件下的多时域未来表征；训练一个学习型评分器基于这些预测对候选轨迹打分并选择。

Result: 离线评测（RECON、SCAND、SACSoN）：宏平均轨迹误差较 NoMaD+NWM-XL 降低 17.56%，端到端在 RTX 5090 上加速 128 倍；同一评分器无需针对提议器重训即可从 NoMaD 迁移到 MBRA，使 MBRA 误差降低 16.2%；真实机器人在未见室内外环境中导航成功率从 43.3% 提升到 83.3%。

Conclusion: LiteNWM 表明低成本潜在世界模型可支撑未来感知规划与闭环导航，并具备在实体机器人上部署的可行性。

Abstract: Direct visual navigation policies generate trajectories efficiently but do not explicitly evaluate their future consequences. Generative navigation world models provide this foresight through visual rollouts, which are costly when evaluating multiple candidates. We present LiteNWM, a latent navigation world model that shares visual encoding across candidates and jointly predicts their action-conditioned future representations at multiple horizons, while a learned scorer uses these predictions to select trajectories. In offline evaluations on RECON, SCAND, and SACSoN, LiteNWM reduces macro-averaged trajectory error by 17.56% relative to NoMaD+NWM-XL and achieves a 128.00-fold end-to-end speedup on an RTX 5090. The same evaluator transfers from NoMaD to MBRA without proposer-specific retraining, reducing MBRA's macro-averaged trajectory error by 16.2%. In real-robot experiments in unseen indoor and outdoor environments, LiteNWM improves navigation success from 43.3% to 83.3% relative to NoMaD. These results demonstrate that LiteNWM can be deployed for future-aware planning and closed-loop navigation on a physical robot.

</details>


### [250] [Embodied Turing Machines: Stateful Code for Robot Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12369)
*Kairui Hu,Siyuan Hu,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Code-Only-as-Policy (COAP) 范式：将具身任务视为图灵机，状态由代码显式表示与跟踪，决策完全由代码完成，闭环中不含 VLM/VLA。在 RoboDojo 42 项双臂任务上达到 70.24% 成功率，并提出其作为递归自改进 (RSI) 媒介与 VLA 数据引擎的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流机器人策略在控制回路中保留模型（VLA 映射观测到动作，Agent Harness 在运行时查询 VLM 做决策），带来延迟、成本与不可控性问题。作者认为若具身状态可被准确表示，决策即可完全用代码写出，从而摆脱测试时的模型依赖。

Method: 核心思想是把具身世界建模为 Embodied Turing Machine：纸带是机器人与环境状态，规则是策略。COAP 用代码从相机图像与本体感受中测量并跟踪机器人、环境与任务状态，并完全基于该状态做决策；同一份代码跨 episode 复用，不同任务共享同一代码库，测试时不使用 VLM/VLA。文中从三方面论证优势：显式状态（状态可存入代码）、执行（可控制、可灵活恢复失败、在线快且廉价）、可扩展性（新任务复用/继承/扩展共享库，能力可累积），并据此将其作为编码智能体闭环迭代开发代码库的递归自改进 (RSI) 载体。

Result: 在 RoboDojo 的 42 项双臂任务上，所得代码库在测试时无模型参与的情况下达到 70.24% 成功率。作者指出 COAP 的上界取决于状态表示的准确性与代码逻辑的鲁棒性，并且由于跨 episode 适用，可兼作 VLA 与 Agent Harness 的高效数据引擎。

Conclusion: COAP 被提出作为一种新的具身任务范式：以显式状态表示 + 纯代码决策替代控制回路中的 VLM/VLA，具备可控、可解释、可累积与可递归自改进的特性，同时可作为 VLA/Agent Harness 的数据引擎；其性能瓶颈在于状态表示精度与代码鲁棒性。

Abstract: Most robot policies keep a model in the control loop: a VLA maps observations to actions, and an Agent Harness, such as Agent-as-Policy or Harness VLA queries a VLM for decision making at run time. We propose a different view: the embodied world is an Embodied Turing Machine, whose tape is the robot and environment state and rules are the policy. If this state can be represented accurately, the decision making can be written entirely in code. We therefore propose Code-Only-as-Policy (COAP): code measures and tracks the robot, environment, and task state from camera images and proprioception, and makes every decision from it. The same code applies across episodes, and different tasks share one library without a VLM or VLA in the loop. Compared with VLAs and Agent Harnesses, we analyze three advantages of COAP: (i) Explicit State: the state can be stored in code; (ii) Execution: code makes decision making controllable, recovers from failures flexibly, and runs fast and cheaply online; (iii) Extensibility: new tasks reuse, inherit, or extend the shared library, so capabilities can accumulate over tasks. These advantages make COAP a suitable medium for recursive self-improvement (RSI): coding agents develop the library in a closed loop, and each change is explicit and controllable. On RoboDojo's 42 bimanual tasks, the resulting library reaches a success rate of 70.24% without a model at test time. The upper bound of COAP lies in how accurately the state is represented for decision making and how robust the code logic is. We thus propose COAP as a new paradigm for embodied tasks; since it applies across episodes, it can also serve as an efficient data engine for VLAs and Agent Harnesses.

</details>


### [251] [ARC: A Reasoning Recipe for Robot Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12386)
*Gokul Puthumanaillam,Tao Sun,Elie Aljalbout,Moritz Reuss,Zhaoshuo Li,Fabio Ramos,Ankit Goyal,Jenai Xuning Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ARC——一套"推理配方"，通过将动作锚定的因果推理轨迹自动标注并注入预训练机器人基础模型（VLA/WAM），在不采集新机器人数据、不扩大模型规模的前提下大幅提升零样本任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前提升机器人基础模型的主流路径依赖更大模型、更多机器人演示与昂贵的规模化训练，成本高且边际收益受限；作者希望找到一种高效、互补的方法来提升现有 SOTA 模型的零样本性能。

Method: ARC 包含三部分：(1) 推理轨迹设计——轨迹须锚定机器人的下一动作并解释其因果结构（为何该动作合适、应产生何种效果）；(2) 可扩展的自动标注流水线——从已有演示中自动生成这些轨迹，构建出 ARC-Trace-DROID（无需新采集机器人数据）；(3) 将预训练 RFM 适配为以轨迹控制——针对 π0.5 等 VLA 与 Cosmos3-Nano-Policy 等 WAM 的架构与能力，定制微调与推理方式。

Result: 在无额外机器人演示、无基础规模训练的情况下取得前所未有的零样本提升：在 RoboLab-120 与 MolmoSpaces 上刷新 SOTA，RoboLab-Reasoning-50 上提升最高达 50 个百分点；真实机器人上使 π0.5 任务成功率提升 82.2 个百分点。

Conclusion: "正确的推理配方"可作为一种高效、可扩展的补充路径，替代对规模化的单一依赖，为现有机器人基础模型带来显著的零样本泛化增益，并已在仿真基准与真实机器人上验证。

Abstract: The prevailing approach to improving robot foundation models (RFMs) relies on larger models, more robot demonstrations, and costly training at scale. We show that there exists an effective and efficient complementary approach: the right reasoning recipe can substantially improve the zero-shot task performance of existing state-of-the-art RFMs. We refer to this recipe as ARC. It consists of three key ingredients: a reasoning trace, a scalable automatic labeling pipeline, and a strategy for adapting pretrained RFMs to use these traces for control. First, we find that effective reasoning traces should be grounded in the robot's next action and explain its causal structure: why the action is appropriate and what effect it should produce. Second, we show that these traces can be generated automatically from existing demonstrations, enabling us to construct ARC-Trace-DROID from DROID without collecting new robot data. Third, we show how state-of-the-art VLAs such as $\pi_{0.5}$ and WAMs such as Cosmos3-Nano-Policy can learn to use these traces for control, with fine-tuning and inference tailored to each model's architecture and capabilities. Using ARC, we obtain gains in zero-shot RFM performance that, to our knowledge, are unprecedented without additional robot demonstrations or foundation-scale training. The adapted models establish a new state of the art on RoboLab-120 and MolmoSpaces, with gains of up to 50 percentage points on RoboLab-Reasoning-50. On real robots, ARC improves $\pi_{0.5}$'s task success by 82.2 percentage points. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [252] [A Physics-Informed Collision Learning Framework for Collaborative Robot Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12404)
*Chen Cai,Steven Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PI-UDF是一个物理信息引导的统一可微框架，用解析正运动学+可学习连杆几何嵌入+共享残差网络直接由机器人构型预测双臂间距，并嵌入非线性MPC实现无碰撞双臂协同运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近距离双臂操作需要既几何精确又可微的碰撞模型用于实时优化。经典几何检测器距离可靠但难以嵌入基于梯度的MPC，而保守代理模型会过度限制紧耦合运动，因此需要一个兼顾精度与可微性的体-体碰撞距离预测方法。

Method: PI-UDF结合解析正运动学、可学习连杆几何嵌入和共享残差网络，直接从关节构型预测成对臂间距离；训练上采用配额驱动的边界挖掘与非对称越界惩罚，重点惩罚碰撞边界附近将危险误判为安全的符号错误；学习到的距离场作为可微的臂间间隙项集成进非线性MPC。

Result: 在真实双Franka平台上验证：14自由度双臂高速近距离换位、持续单臂动态规避，以及将运动臂按冻结/预测/目标切换处理的动态规避规划；硬件实验与Drake/FCL离线回放表明PI-UDF提供可微的臂间间隙估计，支持闭环避碰协同运动生成。

Conclusion: 将物理先验与可微学习结合的距离场可作为梯度型MPC中的实时避碰约束，在真实双臂平台上支撑高速紧耦合协同操作，是可微碰撞建模与双臂控制融合的可行方案。

Abstract: Close-proximity multi-arm manipulation requires collision models that are both geometrically accurate and differentiable enough for real-time optimization. Classical geometry checkers provide reliable distances but are difficult to use inside gradient-based model predictive control, while conservative proxy models can restrict tightly coupled motion. We present PI-UDF, a physics-informed unified differentiable framework for body-to-body collision distance prediction between articulated robots. PI-UDF combines analytical forward kinematics with learnable link-geometry embeddings and a shared residual network to predict pairwise inter-arm distances directly from robot configurations. To improve safety-critical fidelity, we combine quota-driven boundary mining with an asymmetric boundary-crossing penalty that emphasizes false-safe sign errors near the collision boundary. The learned distance field is integrated into nonlinear MPC as a differentiable inter-arm clearance term. We validate the framework on a real dual-Franka platform through high-speed close-proximity 14-DoF dual-arm swapping, sustained single-arm dynamic evasion, and dynamic-evasion planning configurations with frozen, predicted, and target-switching treatments of the moving arm. Hardware experiments and offline Drake/FCL replay show that PI-UDF provides a differentiable inter-arm clearance estimate suitable for closed-loop collision-aware collaborative robot motion generation.

</details>


### [253] [LeWAM: A JEPA World Action Model with Diffusion-Steering-Based MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12407)
*Shashank Hegde,Alexander Popov,Elie Aljalbout,Nikolai Smolyanskiy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LeWAM 是一个双向 Transformer 世界动作模型，在无解码器的 JEPA 潜空间上端到端训练前向、后向、逆动力学与策略四种预测模式，兼顾表征对齐、闭环控制与规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）多基于重建式表征，潜空间含噪声与冗余，干扰下游动作与观测预测；同时世界模型与策略往往分离，缺乏统一且对齐良好的潜表征。

Method: 采用无解码器的 JEPA 潜空间（避开像素重建），用双向 Transformer 同时进行四种模式预测：前向动力学、后向动力学、逆动力学、策略预测；四种模式的梯度端到端回传以塑造共享潜空间；规划时在策略头的噪声空间而非原始动作空间采样，交由 MPC 使用。

Result: 1) 对齐性：线性探针从 LeWAM 潜空间读取机器人/物体状态优于常规 Le World Model（纯前向 JEPA），且对视觉干扰物的忽略程度与 LeWM 相当、远优于重建式 WAM；2) 行动：闭环评测中 LeWAM 与相同编码器、同规模训练的流匹配策略持平，同时额外提供世界模型；3) 规划：直接在原始动作空间采样会让 MPC 利用动力学模型误差，改为在策略头噪声空间规划可提升闭环性能。

Conclusion: 将世界模型与策略统一在无解码器的 JEPA 双向潜空间中，并改在策略噪声空间做规划，可同时获得更对齐的表征、与专用策略相当的闭环表现以及更稳健的 MPC 规划。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) predict actions and future observations, typically from a reconstruction-based representation that carries noisy, redundant information which can complicate downstream predictions. We introduce LeWAM, a bidirectional transformer for forward, backward, inverse dynamics and policy prediction, on a decoder-free JEPA latent trained end-to-end through all four modes. We see the following benefits: 1) Alignment: linear probes read robot and object state from LeWAM's latent better than from a regular Le World Model (a forward-only JEPA world model), while the latent ignores visual distractors as well as LeWM does and far better than a reconstruction-based WAM. 2) Acting: Closed-loop evaluations of LeWAM match a regular flow-matching policy trained on the same encoder at matched size, while also providing a world model. 3) Planning: Sampling raw actions when planning with WAMs lets MPC exploit dynamics-model inaccuracies; planning in the noise space of the policy head instead improves the closed-loop performance of these WAMs.

</details>


### [254] [GLIO2: A GPU-Parallelized Tightly-Coupled LiDAR-Inertial-GNSS System for Robust and Real-Time Global Localization and Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12411)
*Qi Zhang,Xikun Liu,Qijun Qin,Xiangru Wang,Junzhe Wang,Naigui Xiao,Jianhao Jiao,Weisong Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GLIO2 提出紧耦合 LiDAR-惯性-GNSS 融合系统，在 GPU 并行前端中以单滑动窗口因子图联合优化 scan-to-multiscan 激光、IMU 预积分与原始 GNSS 观测，并配以复用同一因子缓存的离线批处理后端，实现全局一致实时状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LiDAR-惯性-GNSS 融合方法共用 scan-to-map 前端，存在两个失效模式：一是扫描对齐到增量构建且会漂移的地图，退化场景下估计发散后不可恢复；二是即便未发散，受动态物体或错误对应影响的配准结果以单一位姿约束、过度自信协方差传播，其对应关系无法被 GNSS 重新加权或线性化。

Method: GPU 并行前端在单一滑动窗口因子图中联合优化 scan-to-multiscan LiDAR 配准、IMU 预积分与原始 GNSS 测量，可在边缘硬件上实时运行；离线后端复用同一缓存因子对整条轨迹做批量精化。

Result: UrbanNav Whampoa 30 分钟 4.51 km 序列离线精化约 24 秒完成。在 UrbanNav、MARS-LVIG、M3DGR 三个公开基准及自采 UAV/车辆数据上总体精度最优。5.66 km 桥梁、最高 96 km/h 场景下所有对比基线在 LiDAR 退化时发散，本方法保持 1.6 m 水平精度。NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX 上完整流程约 25 Hz（39.60 ms/帧）。

Conclusion: 通过前端多扫描联合优化与可复用因子缓存，GLIO2 在退化环境中避免了 scan-to-map 前端的漂移与不可恢复发散问题，兼顾实时性与全局一致性，并将在开源代码与数据集。

Abstract: Globally consistent, real-time state estimation in large-scale, perceptually degraded environments is essential for autonomous vehicles and aerial robots, and requires fusing LiDAR, inertial, and GNSS measurements. Existing fusion methods, however, share a scan-to-map front-end with two failure modes. First, each scan is aligned to an incrementally built map that drifts under degeneracy, and once the estimate diverges the error is irrecoverable. Second, even without divergence, a registration biased by dynamic objects or wrong correspondences is propagated as a single pose constraint with an over-confident covariance, leaving its correspondences unavailable for GNSS to re-weight or relinearize. We propose GLIO2, a tightly-coupled LiDAR-Inertial-GNSS system whose GPU-parallel front-end jointly optimizes scan-to-multiscan LiDAR, IMU pre-integration, and raw GNSS measurements in a single sliding-window factor graph, sustaining real-time operation on edge hardware. A complementary offline back-end reuses the same cached factors to refine the entire trajectory in batch, completing the 30-min, 4.51-km UrbanNav Whampoa sequence in about 24 s. Across three public benchmarks (UrbanNav, MARS-LVIG, M3DGR) and self-collected UAV and vehicle data, GLIO2 attains the best overall accuracy among evaluated systems. On a 5.66-km bridge traversed at up to 96 km/h, where every competing baseline diverges under LiDAR degeneracy, it maintains 1.6 m horizontal accuracy. On an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX, the full pipeline runs at about 25 Hz (39.60 ms per scan). The source code and datasets will be released.

</details>


### [255] [RoboRSI: Stable, efficient, and reusable robot self-evolution in complex real-world environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12424)
*Zimo Wen,Yijin Chen,Yuxuan Cao,Wendi Chen,Yanwen Zou,Wenye Yu,Fuhang Kuang,Han Xue,Jun Lv,Chuan Wen,Cewu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboRSI 提出基于自顶向下技能细化（TSR）的机器人自我改进系统，通过把任务分解为复合/原子/基础技能并赋予明确输入输出契约，将执行反馈归因到对应技能分支并受限于该分支修订，配合 Manager/Planner/Engineer/Reviewer 多智能体闭环实现经校验的新技能发布；实机移动操作完成 104 轮多物体清理，仿真在 LIBERO、LIBERO-PRO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 上以 2.7–11.0 个百分点超越最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人不仅要完成多样任务，还需从执行经验中持续改进，并把学到的东西转化为后续任务可复用的能力。以代码为动作空间的机器人智能体已能利用执行反馈修复程序，但核心挑战在于如何围绕赋予经验意义的任务结构来组织经验：使每次修复归因到真正的责任能力、有执行证据支撑、并在复用前经过验证。

Method: 提出 RoboRSI 自改进系统，核心为 Top-Down Skill Refinement（TSR）：将任务分解为具有作用域职责与显式输入输出契约的复合技能、原子技能与基础技能；把每次执行结果归因到负责的技能分支，并将修订限制在该分支内。在此结构上由 Manager、Planner、Engineer、Reviewer 四类角色分工协调规划、执行、诊断与新技能的经验证发布，人类通过目标与纠正进行干预；稳定的技能序列进一步固化为可复用的复合技能。

Result: 在移动操作平台上经过 104 轮迭代开发出多物体家庭清理能力；仿真中在 LIBERO、LIBERO-PRO、LIBERO-Plus 与 RoboTwin 上取得最高成功率，较最强基线高出 2.7 至 11.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: 以带契约的技能层级作为经验组织骨架，可实现有归因、有证据、有校验的技能级自我改进与复用，兼顾长程实机任务与多仿真基准的泛化表现，为通用机器人持续自我提升提供了可操作范式。

Abstract: A generalist robot should not only perform diverse tasks but also improve through experience, turning what it learns during execution into capabilities that later tasks can reuse. Robot agents that act through code can already repair programs from execution feedback, yet it remains a central challenge to organize this experience around the task structure that gives it meaning, so that each repair is attributed to the responsible capability, supported by execution evidence, and validated before it is reused. We introduce RoboRSI, a robot self-improvement system built on Top-Down Skill Refinement (TSR). TSR decomposes tasks into compound, atomic, and base skills with scoped responsibilities and explicit input--output contracts, attributes each execution outcome to the responsible branch, and confines revision to that branch. Building upon this structure, a Manager, Planner, Engineer, and Reviewer coordinate planning, execution, diagnosis, and the validated release of new skills, while people steer the process through objectives and corrections; stable skill sequences are further consolidated into reusable compound skills. On a mobile manipulator, RoboRSI develops multi-object household cleanup over 104 rounds. In simulation, it achieves the highest success rate on LIBERO, LIBERO-PRO, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin, exceeding the strongest baseline by 2.7 to 11.0 percentage points.

</details>


### [256] [Control-Ready Uncertainty for Trajectory Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12431)
*Zhiwei Xue,Jia Yue Kam,Jinhang Qiu,Yifeng Cheng,Ege Gursoy,Jiaming Wang,Vincent Bonnet,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCOPE 通过蒸馏扩散模型的 score-curvature 信息，为每条名义轨迹学习结构化精度矩阵，从而在无需 Monte Carlo 采样的情况下，低成本地在线给出校准过的逐时刻高斯“管道”不确定性，用于行人预测与机器人自适应控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散轨迹模型能表达复杂的多模态分布，但从采样中提取不确定性代价高昂（需要大量 Monte Carlo 采样），难以满足机器人在实时控制中对风险快速评估与安全裕度维持的需求。因此需要一个轻量、控制友好的在线不确定性估计模块。

Method: 提出 SCOPE 模块：在每条名义轨迹周围学习一个结构化精度矩阵（协方差逆矩阵），通过从扩散模型的 score 场曲率（Hessian 相关）中蒸馏信息来监督学习，直接输出校准的高斯管道（Gaussian tube），无需重复 Monte Carlo 采样，低开销即可给出逐时间步协方差估计；该协方差既可用作运动体的预测占据（occupancy），也可作为机器人控制中的自适应探索引导。

Result: 在行人预测、人群导航、Maze2D 控制以及真实 Franka Panda 机械臂操作四类任务上评估，均采用 mode-conditioned 多模态扩散主干；SCOPE 提供快速的不确定性估计，并带来更好的闭环控制性能。

Conclusion: SCOPE 表明扩散轨迹模型的 score-curvature 可被压缩为结构化协方差预测器，实现快速在线不确定性建模，从而改善机器人的闭环安全与性能，实用性强且具有真实机器人验证。

Abstract: Diffusion models can represent complex, multimodal trajectory distributions, but extracting uncertainty from them typically requires costly Monte Carlo sampling. This limits their use in real-time control, where robots must rapidly assess risk and maintain safety margins. We introduce Score-Curvature for Online Precision Estimation (SCOPE), a lightweight module that augments diffusion trajectory models with control-ready uncertainty. SCOPE learns a structured precision matrix around each nominal trajectory by distilling score-curvature information and producing calibrated Gaussian tubes with low overhead and without repeated Monte Carlo sampling. These tubes provide per-timestep covariance estimates that can be used both as predicted occupancy for moving agents and as adaptive exploration guides for robot control. We evaluate SCOPE with mode-conditioned multimodal diffusion backbones in pedestrian forecasting, crowd navigation, Maze2D control, and real-world Franka Panda manipulation. Across these settings, SCOPE provides fast uncertainty estimation, which leads to better closed-loop performance. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [257] [FAITH: Feasibility-Aware Safety-Filtered RL for High-Dimensional Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12432)
*Songyuan Zhang,Baljeet Singh,Sarthak Ranjeet Kaingade,Chuchu Fan,Bryan Trinh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FAITH: 无模型的可行性感知安全过滤框架,离线学习状态-动作安全价值并以前馈网络摊销最小干预过滤,任务策略经过滤动力学优化以恢复可行约束问题;无可行动作时退化为最小峰值伤害动作。双积分器、Safety Gym、29-DoF 人形及 Unitree G1 上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全强化学习把安全与任务性能放在同一目标中会产生冲突更新;安全过滤器虽在动作执行层分离二者,但经典方法依赖解析安全函数与动力学模型,标准最小干预过滤器只最小化瞬时动作偏差而对长时程回报短视,且不存在安全动作时硬投影无定义。

Method: FAITH 无模型、可行性感知:近似最优状态-动作安全价值(可行条件下的安全价值),用前馈网络摊销最小干预过滤;任务策略在过滤后的动力学上优化任务回报,从而在不含竞争安全项的情况下恢复可行约束问题;当无动作满足学习到的安全条件时,同一过滤器选取预测峰值伤害最小的动作作为退路。

Result: 双积分器与 Safety Gym 上,在无可行的初始违反前提下取得最高回报,并在不可行起始条件下匹配最低伤害;29-DoF 人形在 Walking-Avoid 达到 99.95% 安全率并保留 97% 未过滤回报,Push-Avoid 通过学习牺牲平衡、向远离保护区方向倒地取得最高实测安全率;同一策略在真实 Unitree G1 人形上演示。

Conclusion: 将安全过滤与任务策略更新解耦,并用学习到的安全价值替代解析安全函数与动力学模型,可在高维人形与真实硬件上兼顾安全率与任务回报;峰值伤害退路机制处理了不可行状态下的投影未定义问题。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning commonly places safety and task performance in the same policy objective, where they can introduce competing updates. Safety filters separate them at action execution, but classical designs require an analytic safety function and dynamics model, and standard minimal-intervention filters are myopic to long-horizon task return because they minimize only instantaneous action deviation. Hard projections are also undefined when no safe action exists. We present FAITH, a feasibility-aware, model-free framework that approximates the optimal state-action safety value and amortizes minimal-intervention filtering with a feedforward network. The task policy optimizes the task return through the filtered dynamics, which recovers the feasible constrained problem without a competing safety term in the task-policy update. When no action satisfies the learned safety condition, the same filter approaches the action with minimum predicted peak harm. On a double integrator example and a Safety Gym environment, FAITH achieves the highest return among methods with no feasible-start violations and matches the lowest harm from infeasible starts. On a 29-DoF humanoid, it reaches a 99.95% safety rate while retaining 97% of the unfiltered return in Walking-Avoid, and obtains the highest measured safety rate in Push-Avoid by learning to sacrifice balancing and fall away from the protected region. The same policies are also demonstrated on a real-world Unitree G1 humanoid.

</details>


### [258] [VioLA: Learning Generalist Humanoid Control Policies from Human Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12435)
*Mert Albaba,Jens Beißwenger,Anna Manasyan,Daniel Marta,Michael J. Black,Wieland Brendel,Andreas Krause,Georg Martius,Martin Riedmiller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VioLA将人形通用策略的动作空间改为预测身体与手部运动潜变量，由预训练控制器执行，从而把人类动作数据直接转化为策略可用的监督信号，实现零样本全身指令跟随。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身指令跟随面临两个障碍：一是动作空间大且强耦合（腿、臂、手指需协调并保持平衡），关节级动作难以学习；二是人形演示稀缺，现有通用策略无法零样本执行新指令，需对每个任务遥操作微调。人类演示数据量远大于机器人数据，但人体运动并非机器人指令，两者动作空间不匹配。

Method: 提出VioLA通用人形策略，不预测关节命令，而是预测身体与手的运动潜变量。由预训练的身体控制器和手部控制器在真机上执行这些潜变量；对应的运动编码器将人类运动与机器人运动映射到同一潜空间。由此人类录像可直接在策略的动作空间中被标注，训练数据池达1.406亿帧，其中93.2%为人类数据。方法可跨两个VLA骨干与一个world-action model骨干使用。

Result: VioLA在真实机器人上零样本跟随移动指令，无需任务级微调，成功率达100%，而GR00T N1.7为16.7%、Ψ0为0%；无需任务级微调的操纵成功率为88.6%。仅用人类演示训练的通用策略也能在真机上零样本完成移动任务。方法与骨架无关，适用于两种VLA与一种world-action model骨干。代码与检查点将开源。

Conclusion: 通过将策略输出从关节命令改为跨人机共享的运动潜变量，可打通人类演示到人形策略的学习通路，在不做任务级微调的前提下实现全身零样本指令跟随，并显著优于现有通用策略。

Abstract: Teaching a humanoid to follow instructions with its whole body runs into two obstacles. Its action space is large and tightly coupled: legs, arms, and fingers must move together while the robot keeps its balance, which makes joint-level actions hard to learn. And humanoid demonstrations are scarce, so current humanoid generalist policies do not follow new instructions out of the box and are fine-tuned on teleoperated demonstrations of each task before deployment. Human demonstrations exist in far larger numbers, but a person's motion is not a robot command. We remove both obstacles by changing what the generalist policy predicts. We introduce VioLA, a generalist humanoid policy that predicts body and hand motion latents instead of joint commands. A pretrained body- and hand-controller execute these latents on the robot. Their corresponding motion encoders map human and robot motion into the same latent spaces. A human recording is therefore labeled in the policy's action space, and the training demonstration pool contains 140.6 million frames, 93.2% of them human. As a result, VioLA follows locomotion instructions on the real robot zero-shot, without task-specific fine-tuning, reaching 100% success where GR00T N1.7 and $\Psi_0$ reach 16.7% and 0%, respectively. It also reaches 88.6% manipulation success without task-specific fine-tuning. The same approach works across two VLA and one world-action model backbones. A generalist policy trained on human demonstrations alone performs locomotion tasks on the real robot zero-shot. Code and checkpoints will be released.

</details>


### [259] [Generative Neural Retargeting for Human-to-Robot Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12440)
*Dechen Gao,Yue Yang,Ben Abbatematteo,Nathan Godwin,Pengcheng Wang,Roger Boldu,Steven Man,Zhiyang Dou,Chuan Qin,Sho Nakagome,Eric Whitmire*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出生成式神经重定向（GNR），用 flow matching 学习跨演示共享的低维可行轨迹流形，以人类运动为条件直接采样动力学可行轨迹，替代逐条求解的 RL/MPC，并用于构建大规模稠密接触力标注数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示是可扩展的灵巧操作数据来源，但人机具身差异导致人类运动无法直接迁移。IK 重定向高效却忽略动力学，常产生不可行运动；RL 与采样型 MPC 虽能保证动力学可行，但样本效率低、超参敏感、奖励工程繁琐，且 MPC 对每条轨迹独立求解，采样成本随数据集规模与任务难度快速增长。作者假设动力学可行轨迹集中于跨演示共享的低维流形。

Method: GNR 将重定向重新表述为从该共享可行流形上的条件采样问题：以人类运动为条件，训练 flow matching 生成模型直接采样动力学可行轨迹，一次性学习跨演示的结构，而非为每条演示重新求解优化问题。

Result: GNR 仅需 MPC 8.5% 的采样量即超过 MPC，成功率 56.20% vs. MPC 27.20%。进一步嵌入 real-to-sim 数据引擎，生成含稠密接触力标签的灵巧操作数据集，覆盖 223k 条演示与 3.3k 个物体几何。

Conclusion: 将运动重定向从逐条优化转为条件生成采样，可在大规模、长时域、毫米级精度的人类演示上实现可扩展且高效的重定向，并支撑带接触力标注的灵巧操作数据生成。

Abstract: Human demonstrations are a scalable data source for learning dexterous manipulation, but the embodiment gap prevents human motion from being executed directly on robots. Inverse kinematics (IK) retargets human motion to robots efficiently but ignores dynamics, often producing infeasible motions. Reinforcement learning (RL) and sampling-based model predictive control (MPC) are commonly employed to yield dynamically feasible motions, but both are sample-inefficient and sensitive to hyperparameters. RL suffers from costly and unstable training and tedious reward engineering; MPC avoids policy optimization, yet retargets each trajectory in isolation, and solving one does not make the next easier. Sampling cost grows rapidly with dataset size and task difficulty. We hypothesize that dynamically feasible trajectories concentrate near a low-dimensional manifold shared across demonstrations, so that retargeting can be reduced to sampling from that manifold, conditioned on human motion, rather than solving a fresh optimization problem for every demonstration. We propose \textbf{Generative Neural Retargeting} (GNR), which uses a flow matching model to sample feasible trajectories. GNR outperforms MPC with only $8.5\%$ of the samples required by MPC, achieving a success rate of $56.20\%$ compared to $27.20\%$ for MPC. GNR can be used for scalable and efficient retargeting of large-scale, long-horizon, and millimeter precision human demonstrations: by applying GNR within a real-to-sim data engine, we produce a dexterous manipulation dataset with dense contact-force labels, spanning $223$k demonstrations and $3.3$k object geometries.

</details>


### [260] [SpatialHarness: Test-Time Spatial Scaffolding for Fine Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12457)
*Jiayu Wang,Yue Yu,Bin Zhu,Zhiyao Yang,Jingjing Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SpatialHarness：一种测试时具身增强框架，通过在线仿真场景同步与互补虚拟视角渲染，为冻结的多模态基础模型提供空间可观测性支架，无需微调策略或改动物理传感器即可提升精细操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿多模态基础模型（如 GPT-6 Astra）已展现直接机器人控制的潜力，但在精细操作上性能受限。作者认为失败的主因并非策略能力不足，而是空间可观测性不足——任务关键的空间关系在既有物理相机布局下难以被充分呈现。

Method: SpatialHarness 是测试时具身支架（test-time embodied harness）：1）维护与真实执行同步的在线仿真场景；2）识别任务关键空间关系；3）渲染互补虚拟视图并输入冻结的多模态策略。为保持交互过程中的场景对齐，提出交互感知场景同步，区分静态、抓持与过渡三种模式。

Result: 在四类真实机器人操作任务（精确几何对齐、物体相对放置、铰接物体交互）上，使用同一冻结的 GPT-6 Astra 策略，成功率显著提升：插头插入 26.7%→66.7%，汉诺塔 0%→100%。

Conclusion: 在测试时提升空间可观测性，可以释放强多模态基础模型中已具备但未被触发的精细操作能力；无需策略微调或传感硬件改动。

Abstract: Frontier multimodal foundation models (e.g., GPT-6 Astra) have recently shown strong potential for direct robotic control, yet their performance on fine manipulation remains limited. We argue that an important source of failure is not necessarily insufficient policy capability, but insufficient spatial observability, where task-critical spatial relationships may be poorly revealed by the existing physical camera setup. We introduce SpatialHarness, a test-time embodied harness that provides test-time spatial scaffolding for fine robotic manipulation without policy fine-tuning or changes to the physical sensing setup. SpatialHarness maintains an online simulated scene synchronized with real-world execution, identifies task-critical spatial relationships, and renders complementary virtual views that expose them to a frozen multimodal policy. To keep the simulated scene aligned during interaction, we develop interaction-aware scene synchronization that distinguishes static, held, and transition modes. We evaluate SpatialHarness on four real-robot manipulation tasks spanning precise geometric alignment, object-relative placement, and articulated-object interaction. Using the same frozen GPT-6 Astra policy, SpatialHarness substantially improves task success, including from 26.7% to 66.7% on plug insertion and from 0% to 100% on Tower of Hanoi. These results indicate that improving spatial observability at test time can unlock fine-manipulation capabilities already present in strong multimodal foundation models. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [261] [A Balanced Data Diet: Addressing the Exploration Bottleneck in Mega-Scale RL for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12465)
*Octi Zhang,Mateo Guaman Castro,Patrick Yin,Ignacio Dagnino,Abhishek Gupta,Rosario Scalise,Byron Boots*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Success Guided Sampling (SGS) 自适应采样器，将大规模并行 RL 训练集中到策略能力前沿附近的任务配置上，在 2^20 并行环境下解决多地形四足运动与接触密集装配任务，并将操作策略蒸馏为 RGB 策略实现真机零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 sim-to-real RL 依赖人工设计的奖励塑形与示教等结构性先验，工程负担重；靠多样化仿真初始状态 + 大规模并行仿真虽能缓解，但在更精确或高动态任务上均匀采样会浪费大量学习经验——策略已掌握或尚无法尝试的配置占据过多 batch 信号，导致并行规模扩展收益不明显。

Method: SGS 是一种轻量自适应采样器：依据策略在任务配置上的成功情况，动态调整配置采样分布，把训练经验集中到策略能力边界（frontier）附近，从而提升单个 batch 中有效学习信号比例，使大规模并行仿真（最多 2^20 个并行环境）的扩展更高效；随后将学到的操作策略蒸馏为基于 RGB 观测的策略用于真机部署。

Result: 在最多 2^20（超百万）并行环境下，SGS 使 RL 能够求解此前方法失败的多地形四足运动与接触密集装配任务；蒸馏得到的 RGB 策略在真实硬件上对若干高难度装配任务实现零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 通过自适应地将训练集中在能力前沿而非均匀采样任务配置，可显著提高大规模并行 RL 的学习效率，使 sim-to-real 流程在减少人工先验工程的同时扩展到高精度、高动态任务，并具备真机零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: General-purpose robots must perform a wide range of tasks from agile locomotion to dexterous manipulation. While sim-to-real reinforcement learning (RL) has proven to be a useful tool for this goal, current RL pipelines depend on engineering-heavy, per-task structural priors such as shaped rewards and demonstrations. Recent work has shown that diverse simulator resets, combined with massively parallel simulation, can alleviate much of this engineering burden on several manipulation problems. However, we find that naively scaling this paradigm to more precise or dynamic problems remains non-trivial. While simulator resets can help with exploration, uniformly sampling over this distribution wastes a growing fraction of learning experience on task configurations the policy has already mastered or cannot yet attempt. This makes it challenging to see the expected benefits of scaling parallel environments for RL, since much of the learning signal in a batch is wasted during learning. To mitigate this, we introduce Success Guided Sampling (SGS), a simple adaptive sampler that concentrates RL training on task configurations around the frontier of the policy's capabilities. Doing so allows large-scale simulated RL to make the most out of the experience in a batch, enabling much more effective scaling to large-scale parallel simulation. Across experiments using up to $2^{20}$ (over one million) parallel environments, SGS enables RL to solve challenging multi-terrain quadruped locomotion and contact-rich assembly tasks that prior methods fail to solve. Finally, we distill the learned manipulation policies into RGB-based policies and demonstrate zero-shot transfer to several challenging assembly tasks on real hardware. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [262] [CSF: Contextual Safety Filtering for Motion Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12467)
*Lizhi Yang,Yiling Hou,Yao Tang,Junheng Li,Daniel Weng,Blake Werner,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练的情境安全过滤(CSF)：以生成器产出的安全/不安全参考轨迹为锚，将自然语言安全规则落地为仿射安全值并用参考跟踪CBF-QP强制约束，在四个预训练运动生成器及Unitree G1真机上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本条件运动生成可产生可跟踪的全身动作，但缺乏场景依赖的安全意识（同一动作指向物体或人时含义不同）。现有防护或检查提示词、或依赖标注运动数据、或施加几何约束，均未直接建模场景上下文如何改变动作语义。

Method: CSF为免训练过滤模块：每条安全规则对应一组由生成器采样的安全与不安全参考轨迹，据此拟合仿射安全函数（即CBF约束的仿射近似）；在线时用参考跟踪CBF-QP在生成动作上求解最小修正，使安全值非负，从而在不重训生成器的情况下结合场景语义实施规则。

Result: 在四种不同架构的预训练生成器上，CSF在全部显式与场景触发的不安全案例中激活预期规则，将危险事件率最多降低90%，同时保留88%–100%的良性动作；在Unitree G1真机上完整系统演示，成功阻止包括人机与物体交互在内的多类不安全运动。

Conclusion: 以参考轨迹定义安全值、用CBF-QP做在线过滤，是一种有效的免训练情境安全机制，可跨生成器架构泛化并落地真机；局限是参考轨迹质量与规则标注需依赖采样与人工规则设计。

Abstract: Text-conditioned motion generators produce trackable whole-body motion, but they have no notion of scene-dependent safety: the same action may target an object or a person. Existing safeguards either inspect the prompt, require labeled motion data, or enforce geometric constraints; therefore, they do not directly account for how scene context changes a motion's meaning. We introduce contextual safety filtering (CSF), a training-free filter that grounds natural-language safety rules in safe and unsafe reference trajectories produced by the generator. For each active rule, safe and unsafe reference trajectories define an affine safety value that a safe reference tracking CBF-QP enforces. Across four pretrained generators with different architectures, CSF activates the intended rules in all explicit and scene-triggered unsafe cases and reduces the danger-event rate by up to 90%, while preserving 88-100% of benign motions. We demonstrate the complete system on a real-world Unitree G1, where it successfully prevents unsafe motions in a variety of scenarios, including interactions with humans and objects.

</details>


### [263] [DreamTrue: Action-Faithful Robot World Model with Counterfactual Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12468)
*Junyan Li,Ruizhi Li,Yu Liu,Xiangshuo Liu,Mingchao Sun,Hongyu Pan,Mu Xu,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamTrue：多视角、跨具身机器人世界模型，通过图像空间动作渲染+离线几何标定提升动作跟随，用反事实后训练扩展交互覆盖，并用人类标注的具身视频奖励模型引导强化学习后训练，在AgiBot上将人工评估的交互缺陷率从48.12%降至6.25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人世界模型训练面临两大障碍：标定不精确导致动作跟随差；数据集缺乏失败交互样本，使预测偏向成功结果。需要一个动作忠实且物理合理的视频预测模型。

Method: 1) 将动作轨迹渲染为图像空间条件，并引入离线几何标定，使条件与目标视频对齐，提升跨具身动作跟随；2) 反事实后训练：修改记录的动作轨迹，在更广的动作与接触构型下生成未来视频，扩大交互覆盖；3) 构建涵盖机器人、物体、交互缺陷的人工标注视频数据集，训练具身视频奖励模型，用其评分引导强化学习后训练以获得更物理合理的交互结果。

Result: 在AgiBot上达到SOTA动作跟随效果，人工评估的交互缺陷率从48.12%降至6.25%，并在AgiBot World Challenge 2026世界模型赛道排名第一。

Conclusion: 跨具身世界模型可通过图像空间动作条件、几何标定、反事实数据扩展与奖励模型引导的RL后训练，同时提升动作忠实度与物理合理性，具备真实机器人平台上的强证据。

Abstract: We present DreamTrue, a multi-view, cross-embodiment robot world model for action-faithful and physically plausible video prediction. Training such a model on existing robot datasets faces two obstacles: imprecise calibration can impair action following, while limited coverage of unsuccessful interactions can bias predictions toward successful outcomes. To improve action following across embodiments, we render action trajectories into image-space conditions and introduce offline geometric calibration to align these conditions with the target videos. To broaden interaction coverage, we introduce counterfactual post-training, modifying recorded action trajectories and generating future videos under a wider range of actions and contact configurations. To provide feedback on these predictions without paired ground-truth futures, we construct a human-annotated video dataset covering robot, object, and interaction defects and use it to train an embodied video reward model. Its scores guide reinforcement-learning post-training toward more physically plausible interaction outcomes. On AgiBot, DreamTrue attains state-of-the-art action following, while reducing the human-assessed interaction defect rate from from 48.12% to 6.25%. Notably, our model ranks first in the world model track of the AgiBot World Challenge 2026. The project page can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [264] [Dex-One2Many: Learning Dexterous Manipulation from a Single Human Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12470)
*Jusuk Lee,Sungha Kim,Yeonsoo Park,Jonguk Cheon,Yoonkyo Jung,Yongjun You,H. Jin Kim,Jia-Bin Huang,Furong Huang,Youngseok Jang,Seungjae Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Dex-One2Many：从单个人类视频出发，构建 real-to-sim-to-real 框架，将视频抽象为序列化场景图，用作 RL 探索的生成式约束（采样多样重置状态）与稠密奖励，训练出可零样本迁移到真实多指手的通用灵巧操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视频模仿学习泛化性差（仅匹配演示轨迹，难以适应新初始位姿、目标位姿与抓取），而纯 RL 又因缺乏先验在复杂多阶段任务中探索困难。作者希望同时解决"泛化性"与"探索效率"这两个耦合难题。

Method: 核心洞见是把人类视频抽象为一组序列化场景图（sequential scene graphs）。场景图作为生成式约束，用于采样超越视频之外的多样化重置状态（覆盖新物体位姿与抓取），并为每个阶段提供稠密奖励以缩短并引导探索。全过程在仿真中训练，随后零样本迁移到真实多指灵巧手（real-to-sim-to-real）。

Result: 在五项工具使用与操作任务上，Dex-One2Many 在已见配置上比基线高出 6.5%；在未见场景中凭借稳健泛化将优势扩大到 71%。策略完全在仿真中训练即可零样本部署到真实多指手。

Conclusion: 将视频抽象为约束"关系"而非"精确位姿"的场景图，是兼顾泛化与高效探索的有效手段，为单视频驱动的通用灵巧操作与 sim-to-real 部署提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: While learning dexterous manipulation from a single human video offers a promising alternative to costly robot demonstrations, many recent methods predominantly imitate demonstrated motions. Such strict motion matching often limits generalization to initial object poses, goal poses, and grasps not shown in the video. Alternatively, discovering a policy via reinforcement learning (RL) allows for broad generalization, but without prior guidance, it struggles with high-dimensional exploration in complex, multi-stage tasks. To address these coupled generalization and exploration challenges, we present Dex-One2Many, a real-to-sim-to-real framework that learns a generalizable dexterous manipulation policy from a single human video. Our key insight is to abstract the video into sequential scene graphs that guide RL, enabling efficient exploration while preserving broad generalizability. The graphs serve as generative constraints for sampling diverse reset states and provide dense rewards for each stage. Because the graphs constrain relations rather than exact poses, these reset states cover object poses and grasps beyond the video, while initializing each stage from them with dense rewards keeps exploration short and guided. Trained entirely in simulation, Dex-One2Many transfers zero-shot to a real multi-fingered hand. Across five tool-use and manipulation tasks, Dex-One2Many exceeds baselines by 6.5% in seen configurations, while its robust generalization widens this gap to 71% in unseen scenarios.

</details>
